(* ---- theories/D001/D001_01__Carryless_Pairing.v ---- *)

(*@file@*)
(*@copyright@*)

(*@H.1@[[Proofcase / D001 / Carryless Pairing]]@*)
(*@H.2@[[Overview]]@*)
(*@p.l@[[This construction defines the local carryless pairing interface used by the arithmetic floor of D001.]]@*)
(*@p.l@[[It contains the abstract carryless device later needed by `D001_03__RA`: the parameter pack, the `pair` and `unpair` operators, the decoder helpers, the generic Fibonacci and arithmetic lemmas, and the abstract correctness consequences `unpair_pair` and `pair_inj`.]]@*)

From D001 Require Export D001_00_Premises.

(*@c.unicode@[[Paired Fibonacci recursion: `fib_pair n = (F_n, F_{n+1})`, computed by a single structural pass.]]@*)

Fixpoint fib_pair (n : nat) : nat * nat :=
  match n with
  | 0 => (0, 1)
  | S n' =>
      match fib_pair n' with
      | (a, b) => (b, a + b)
      end
  end.

(*@c.inline@[[We expose the first component of `fib_pair` as the local Fibonacci sequence. This lets the later lemmas reason through the paired recursion while downstream files use an ordinary one-argument function.]]@*)

Definition fib (n : nat) : nat := fst (fib_pair n).

(*@c.inline@[[We interpret a list of indices as a Fibonacci-weighted support by summing the corresponding values. This is the semantic evaluation map used for Zeckendorf supports and for the carryless bands built from them.]]@*)

Fixpoint sum_fib (xs : list nat) : nat :=
  match xs with
  | [] => 0
  | k :: xs' => fib k + sum_fib xs'
  end.

(*@c.inline@[[Double: `two n = n + n`.]]@*)

Definition two (n : nat) : nat := n + n.

(*@c.inline@[[`2j - 1`, with `Nat.pred 0 = 0`.]]@*)

Definition two_j_minus1 (j : nat) : nat := Nat.pred (two j).

(*@c.inline@[[Boolean even test by structural recursion.]]@*)

Fixpoint is_even (n : nat) : bool :=
  match n with
  | 0 => true
  | 1 => false
  | S (S k) => is_even k
  end.

(*@c.inline@[[Boolean odd test.]]@*)

Definition is_odd (n : nat) : bool := negb (is_even n).

(*@c.inline@[[Integer half, rounding down.]]@*)

Fixpoint div2 (n : nat) : nat :=
  match n with
  | 0 => 0
  | 1 => 0
  | S (S k) => S (div2 k)
  end.

(*@c.inline@[[Parameters of the device: `Z` is the Zeckendorf support extractor, and `r` is the rank given by the first Fibonacci index strictly above `x`. We keep them abstract here; the corresponding specification appears in `R02`.]]@*)

Inductive Params : Type :=
| Build_Params : (nat -> list nat) -> (nat -> nat) -> Params.

(*@c.unicode@[[Projection helpers: `Z P : ℕ → list ℕ` extracts the support map, and `r P : ℕ → ℕ` extracts the rank map from the packed parameter record.]]@*)

Definition Z (P : Params) : nat -> list nat :=
  match P with
  | Build_Params z0 _ => z0
  end.

(*@c.unicode@[[Second projection: `r P : ℕ → ℕ` reads the rank function from the packed carryless parameters.]]@*)

Definition r (P : Params) : nat -> nat :=
  match P with
  | Build_Params _ r0 => r0
  end.

(*@c.inline@[[The boundary `B P x` is twice the rank bound of `x`. It marks the point above which the odd-band contribution for `y` begins, keeping the two coordinates separated without carries.]]@*)

Definition B (P : Params) (x : nat) : nat := 2 * r P x.

(*@c.inline@[[The even band carries the support of `x` onto even indices, while the odd band carries the support of `y` onto odd indices strictly above the boundary `B P x`. This parity-and-boundary split is the structural core of the carryless device.]]@*)

Definition even_band (P : Params) (x : nat) : list nat :=
  map (fun e => two e) (Z P x).

(*@c.inline@[[Odd-support transport: each `j ∈ Z(P,y)` is sent to `B(P,x) + (2j - 1)`, placing the right coordinate strictly in the odd band above the boundary.]]@*)

Definition odd_band (P : Params) (x y : nat) : list nat :=
  map (fun j => B P x + two_j_minus1 j) (Z P y).

(*@c.inline@[[The pairing map evaluates the combined even/odd support as a single Fibonacci sum. Because the two bands occupy disjoint index regions, the encoded number still remembers both coordinates separately.]]@*)

(*@c.unicode@[[pair(P,x,y) = Σfib(even_band(P,x) ∪ odd_band(P,x,y))]]@*)

Definition pair (P : Params) (x y : nat) : nat :=
  sum_fib (even_band P x ++ odd_band P x y).

(*@c.inline@[[To recover the left coordinate, we filter out the even support indices and halve them back to the original Zeckendorf indices of `x`.]]@*)

Definition half_even_indices (zn : list nat) : list nat :=
  map div2 (filter is_even zn).

(*@c.inline@[[This boolean test recognizes exactly the odd-band indices belonging to the right coordinate: they must be odd and lie at or above the first odd slot past `Bx`.]]@*)

Definition odd_ge_B1 (Bx k : nat) : bool :=
  match is_odd k with
  | false => false
  | true => Nat.leb (S Bx) k
  end.

(*@c.inline@[[An odd-band index has the form `Bx + (2j - 1)`. After removing the boundary offset and undoing the odd shift, `decode_odd_index` recovers the original index `j`.]]@*)

Definition decode_odd_index (Bx k : nat) : nat :=
  div2 (S (k - Bx)).

(*@c.inline@[[To recover the right coordinate, we keep exactly the odd indices above the boundary and decode them back to the support indices of `y`.]]@*)

Definition y_indices (Bx : nat) (zn : list nat) : list nat :=
  map (decode_odd_index Bx) (filter (odd_ge_B1 Bx) zn).

(*@c.inline@[[Unpairing runs the abstract support extractor on `n`, reconstructs `x` from the even band, recomputes the corresponding boundary, and then reconstructs `y` from the surviving odd band.]]@*)

Definition unpair (P : Params) (n : nat) : nat * nat :=
  let zn := Z P n in
  let x := sum_fib (half_even_indices zn) in
  let Bx := B P x in
  let y := sum_fib (y_indices Bx zn) in
  (x, y).

(*@c.section@[[Generic Fibonacci Infrastructure]]@*)

(*@c.inline@[[The paired Fibonacci recursion advances by shifting the current pair and summing its entries.]]@*)

Lemma fib_pair_S : forall n a b,
  fib_pair n = (a, b) -> fib_pair (S n) = (b, a + b).
Proof.
  intros n a b H.
  simpl. rewrite H. reflexivity.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[`fib_S` identifies the second component of `fib_pair n` with the next Fibonacci value. It is the basic bridge between the paired recursion and the ordinary one-dimensional sequence used throughout the later arithmetic estimates.]]@*)

Lemma fib_S : forall n, fib (S n) = snd (fib_pair n).
Proof.
  intro n. unfold fib. simpl. destruct (fib_pair n) as [a b]. reflexivity.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[`fib_step` is the usual Fibonacci recurrence expressed in the local `fib_pair` presentation. Most later monotonicity and growth estimates reduce to this identity and then finish by linear arithmetic.]]@*)

Lemma fib_step : forall n, fib (S (S n)) = fib (S n) + fib n.
Proof.
  intro n.
  destruct (fib_pair n) as [a b] eqn:Hn.
  assert (H1 : fib_pair (S n) = (b, a + b)) by (apply fib_pair_S; exact Hn).
  assert (H2 : fib_pair (S (S n)) = (a + b, b + (a + b))) by (apply fib_pair_S; exact H1).
  unfold fib. rewrite H2. rewrite H1. rewrite Hn. simpl.
  rewrite Nat.add_comm. reflexivity.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[The first monotonicity lemma records that Fibonacci values do not decrease under successor. It is the main one-step comparison fact used in later search and support estimates.]]@*)

Lemma fib_S_ge : forall n, fib (S n) >= fib n.
Proof.
  induction n as [|n IH].
  - unfold fib. simpl. lia.
  - rewrite fib_step. lia.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[Every positive-index Fibonacci value is at least `1`, with the successor form packaged separately for repeated use.]]@*)

Lemma fib_pos_S : forall n, fib (S n) >= 1.
Proof.
  induction n as [|n IH].
  - unfold fib. simpl. lia.
  - rewrite fib_step. lia.
Qed.

(*@c.unicode@[[Indexed positivity: from `n ≥ 1` we recover `fib n ≥ 1` by reducing to the successor case.]]@*)

Lemma fib_pos : forall n, n >= 1 -> fib n >= 1.
Proof.
  intros n H.
  destruct n as [|n].
  - lia.
  - apply fib_pos_S.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[Fibonacci values are monotone with respect to their index.]]@*)

Lemma fib_monotone_le : forall a b, a < b -> fib a <= fib b.
Proof.
  intros a b Hlt.
  induction b as [|b IH]; [lia|].
  destruct (Nat.eq_dec a b) as [Heq|Hne].
  - subst. apply fib_S_ge.
  - assert (a < b) by lia.
    specialize (IH H).
    apply Nat.le_trans with (m:=fib b); [exact IH |].
    apply fib_S_ge.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[Advancing the Fibonacci index by at least two gives a strict increase.]]@*)

Lemma fib_gap2_gt : forall a, fib a < fib (S (S a)).
Proof.
  intro a.
  rewrite fib_step.
  assert (Hpos : fib (S a) >= 1) by apply fib_pos_S.
  lia.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[Any index gap of at least two yields strict growth. This packages the previous local step and ordinary monotonicity into the exact comparison later used for rank bounds and band separation.]]@*)

Lemma fib_monotone_gap2 : forall a b, a + 1 < b -> fib a < fib b.
Proof.
  intros a b Hlt.
  assert (Hle : S (S a) <= b) by lia.
  apply Nat.lt_le_trans with (m:=fib (S (S a))).
  - apply fib_gap2_gt.
  - destruct (Nat.eq_dec (S (S a)) b) as [Heq|Hne].
    + subst. apply Nat.le_refl.
    + apply fib_monotone_le. lia.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[These arithmetic rearrangement lemmas keep later Fibonacci band proofs free of repeated `lia` boilerplate.]]@*)

Lemma add_sub_cancel_r : forall a b, a <= b -> a + (b - a) = b.
Proof.
  intros a b Hle. lia.
Qed.

(*@c.unicode@[[Left-associated regrouping over `+`: `a + b + c = a + (b + c)`.]]@*)

Lemma add_assoc_l : forall a b c, a + b + c = a + (b + c).
Proof.
  intros a b c. lia.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[We now assume an abstract parameter pack together with the three semantic laws needed for correctness: `Z` evaluates supports faithfully, and the support of a paired value splits exactly into the even band for `x` and the odd band for `y`. The rest of the section derives decoding and injectivity from those hypotheses alone.]]@*)

Section Carryless_Correctness.

  Variable P : Params.

  Opaque B.

  (*@c.inline@[[Zeckendorf specification: `Z` represents numbers as Fibonacci sums.]]@*)

  Hypothesis Z_sound : forall n, sum_fib (Z P n) = n.

  (*@c.inline@[[Even/odd split of the Zeckendorf support for a paired value. These two laws capture the semantic fact that the indices used by the carryless encoding can be recovered by filtering the support of the encoded number.]]@*)

  Hypothesis Z_even_split :
    forall x y,
      filter is_even (Z P (pair P x y)) = even_band P x.

  Hypothesis Z_odd_split :
    forall x y,
      filter (odd_ge_B1 (B P x)) (Z P (pair P x y)) = odd_band P x y.

  (*@c.inline@[[This unfolds the doubling helper through successor. It is the small arithmetic normalization lemma needed when we convert encoded even or odd indices back to their original parameters.]]@*)

  Lemma two_S : forall n, two (S n) = S (S (two n)).
  Proof.
    intro n.
    unfold two.
    simpl.
    rewrite Nat.add_succ_r.
    reflexivity.
  Qed.

  (*@c.inline@[[Halving an even index produced by `two` returns the original index. This is the basic decoding fact behind recovery of the left-coordinate support.]]@*)

  Lemma div2_two : forall n, div2 (two n) = n.
  Proof.
    induction n as [|n IH].
    - simpl. reflexivity.
    - rewrite two_S. simpl. rewrite IH. reflexivity.
  Qed.

  (*@c.inline@[[We cancel a fixed left summand after subtraction. The odd-band decoder uses this to remove the boundary offset `Bx` before halving.]]@*)

  Lemma add_sub_cancel_l : forall a b, a + b - a = b.
  Proof.
    induction a as [|a IH]; intro b; simpl.
    - rewrite Nat.sub_0_r. reflexivity.
    - apply IH.
  Qed.

  (*@c.inline@[[Mapping `div2` across the even band exactly inverts the index-doubling step, so the even band decodes back to the original support `Z P x`.]]@*)

  Lemma map_div2_even_band :
    forall x, map div2 (even_band P x) = Z P x.
  Proof.
    intro x.
    unfold even_band.
    rewrite map_map.
    rewrite <- map_id.
    apply map_ext.
    intro a.
    apply div2_two.
  Qed.

  (*@c.inline@[[Each odd-band index is constructed from `j` by adding the boundary offset and replacing `j` with `2j - 1`. The decoder reverses that construction pointwise.]]@*)

  Lemma decode_encode_odd :
    forall Bx j,
      decode_odd_index Bx (Bx + two_j_minus1 j) = j.
  Proof.
    intros Bx j.
    unfold decode_odd_index, two_j_minus1.
    rewrite (add_sub_cancel_l Bx (Nat.pred (two j))).
    destruct j as [|j'].
    - simpl. reflexivity.
    - rewrite two_S. simpl. rewrite div2_two. reflexivity.
  Qed.

  (*@c.inline@[[Pointwise inversion on odd-band elements lifts to the whole odd support list, so decoding the odd band reconstructs `Z P y`.]]@*)

  Lemma map_decode_odd_band :
    forall x y,
      map (decode_odd_index (B P x)) (odd_band P x y) = Z P y.
  Proof.
    intros x y.
    unfold odd_band.
    rewrite map_map.
    rewrite <- map_id.
    apply map_ext.
    intro a.
    apply decode_encode_odd.
  Qed.

  (*@c.inline@[[Filtering the support of `pair P x y` to its even indices, halving them, and re-evaluating the resulting support yields exactly the left coordinate `x`.]]@*)

  Lemma sum_fib_half_even_pair :
    forall x y,
      sum_fib (half_even_indices (Z P (pair P x y))) = x.
  Proof.
    intros x y.
    unfold half_even_indices.
    rewrite Z_even_split.
    rewrite map_div2_even_band.
    apply Z_sound.
  Qed.

  (*@c.inline@[[Filtering the support of `pair P x y` to the odd indices above `B P x`, decoding them, and re-evaluating the result yields exactly the right coordinate `y`.]]@*)

  Lemma sum_fib_y_indices_pair :
    forall x y,
      sum_fib (y_indices (B P x) (Z P (pair P x y))) = y.
  Proof.
    intros x y.
    unfold y_indices.
    rewrite Z_odd_split.
    rewrite map_decode_odd_band.
    apply Z_sound.
  Qed.

  (*@c.inline@[[Abstract correctness of the carryless device. Under the support-evaluation and split hypotheses, unpairing the code `pair P x y` recovers both coordinates without loss.]]@*)
  
  (*@c.unicode@[[Correctness: ∀x,y, unpair(P, pair(P,x,y)) = (x,y)]]@*)

  Theorem unpair_pair :
    forall x y, unpair P (pair P x y) = (x, y).
  Proof.
    intros x y.
    unfold unpair. cbn.
    rewrite !sum_fib_half_even_pair.
    rewrite sum_fib_y_indices_pair.
    reflexivity.
  Qed.

  (*@c.inline@[[Injectivity is the immediate corollary of `unpair_pair`: equal paired codes have equal decoded coordinates, hence equal original pairs.]]@*)
  
  (*@c.unicode@[[Injectivity: pair(P,x₁,y₁)=pair(P,x₂,y₂) ⇒ (x₁=x₂ ∧ y₁=y₂)]]@*)

  Theorem pair_inj :
    forall x1 y1 x2 y2,
      pair P x1 y1 = pair P x2 y2 -> x1 = x2 /\ y1 = y2.
  Proof.
    intros x1 y1 x2 y2 H.
    pose proof (unpair_pair x1 y1) as H1.
    pose proof (unpair_pair x2 y2) as H2.
    rewrite H in H1.
    rewrite H1 in H2.
    split.
    - exact (f_equal fst H2).
    - exact (f_equal snd H2).
  Qed.

  (*@box.section@[[Image Membership]]@*)

  (*@c.inline@[[The checked unpairing decodes the input and then immediately re-pairs it to verify if the original number was genuinely in the image of `pair`.]]@*)
  
  Definition unpair_checked (n : nat) : option (nat * nat) :=
    let (x, y) := unpair P n in
    if Nat.eqb (pair P x y) n then Some (x, y) else None.

  (*@c.inline@[[The boolean image test simply runs the checked unpairing and returns true iff it succeeds. This implements the Delta_0 test from the specification.]]@*)
  
  Definition in_imageb (n : nat) : bool :=
    match unpair_checked n with
    | Some _ => true
    | None => false
    end.

  (*@c.unicode@[[Semantic image predicate.]]@*)
  
  Definition InImage (n : nat) : Prop :=
    exists x y, pair P x y = n.

  (*@c.unicode@[[Decidability of the image: `in_imageb n = true ↔ InImage n`.]]@*)
  
  Theorem in_imageb_correct :
    forall n, in_imageb n = true <-> InImage n.
  Proof.
    intro n.
    unfold in_imageb, unpair_checked, InImage.
    destruct (unpair P n) as [x y] eqn:Hup.
    split.
    - intro Heq.
      destruct (Nat.eqb (pair P x y) n) eqn:Hcheck; [|discriminate Heq].
      apply Nat.eqb_eq in Hcheck.
      exists x, y.
      exact Hcheck.
    - intros [x' [y' Hn]].
      subst n.
      rewrite unpair_pair in Hup.
      inversion Hup; subst.
      rewrite Nat.eqb_refl.
      reflexivity.
  Qed.

End Carryless_Correctness.


(* ---- theories/D001/D001_02__Base_Fibonacci.v ---- *)

(*@file@*)
(*@H.1@[[Proofcase / D001 / Base Fibonacci]]@*)
(*@H.2@[[Overview]]@*)
(*@p.l@[[This construction is the concrete Zeckendorf realization layer for the local carryless pairing device used by the arithmetic floor of D001.]]@*)
(*@p.l@[[The main outputs are:]]@*)
(*@plist.roman@[[the greedy support extractor `Z0`]][[the Zeckendorf rank / boundary machinery around `r0`]][[correctness and uniqueness of the support representation]][[the odd/even band split lemmas for the concrete base realization]]@*)
(*@p.l@[[Downstream, `D001_03__RA` relies on this file only through the concrete parameter pack `base_params` and the three theorems `base_Z_sound`, `base_Z_even_split`, and `base_Z_odd_split`.]]@*)

From D001 Require Export D001_01__Carryless_Pairing.

(*@c.section@[[Pairing]]@*)

(*@c.inline@[[We isolate the semantic side conditions of canonical Zeckendorf supports: strict decrease, no adjacency, and the lower bound `2` on every index. Their conjunction `zeck_valid` is the invariant preserved by the greedy constructor.]]@*)

Fixpoint strictly_decreasing (xs : list nat) : Prop :=
  match xs with
  | [] => True
  | a :: xs' =>
      match xs' with
      | [] => True
      | b :: xs'' => a > b /\ strictly_decreasing xs'
      end
  end.

(*@c.unicode@[[Adjacency exclusion on support indices: consecutive entries must satisfy `a ≥ b + 2`, ruling out neighboring Fibonacci indices in canonical form.]]@*)

Fixpoint no_adjacent (xs : list nat) : Prop :=
  match xs with
  | [] => True
  | a :: xs' =>
      match xs' with
      | [] => True
      | b :: xs'' => a >= b + 2 /\ no_adjacent xs'
      end
  end.

(*@c.inlinw@[[Lower-bound side condition for canonical supports: every chosen index is at least `2`.]]@*)

Fixpoint all_ge_2 (xs : list nat) : Prop :=
  match xs with
  | [] => True
  | a :: xs' => 2 <= a /\ all_ge_2 xs'
  end.

(*@c.inline@[[We package the three canonical Zeckendorf side conditions into one predicate. A list is `zeck_valid` exactly when its indices decrease strictly, avoid adjacency, and all start at least at `2`.]]@*)

Definition zeck_valid (xs : list nat) : Prop :=
  strictly_decreasing xs /\ no_adjacent xs /\ all_ge_2 xs.

(*@c.inline@[[Bounded linear search: starting at `k`, iterate while `fib k ≤ x`; return the first index where `x < fib k`, or stop at fuel exhaustion.]]@*)

Fixpoint find_r_aux (x k fuel : nat) : nat :=
  match fuel with
  | 0 => k
  | S fuel' =>
      if Nat.ltb x (fib k)
      then k
      else find_r_aux x (S k) fuel'
  end.

(*@c.inline@[[The rank search `r0 x` starts from index `0` and scans upward until it reaches the first Fibonacci value strictly larger than `x`. The fuel `S (S x)` is sufficient because `fib (S (S x)) >= S x > x`.]]@*)

(*@c.unicode@[[r0(x) = least k with x < fib(k), realized by bounded scan `find_r_aux x 0 (S(S x))`]]@*)

Definition r0 (x : nat) : nat := find_r_aux x 0 (S (S x)).

(*@c.unicode@[[Descending greedy constructor over candidate indices: optionally take `k` when allowed and affordable (`fib k ≤ rem`), enforce no-adjacency via `prev_taken`, and thread the residual remainder.]]@*)

Fixpoint zeck_greedy_down (k rem : nat) (prev_taken : bool)
  : list nat * nat :=
  match k with
  | 0 => ([], rem)
  | S k' =>
      match k' with
      | 0 => ([], rem)

      (*@c.inline@[[Canonical supports start at index `2`; index `1` is excluded from the representation.]]@*)

      | S k'' =>
          if prev_taken then
            zeck_greedy_down k' rem false
          else
            if Nat.leb (fib k) rem then
              let pr := zeck_greedy_down k' (rem - fib k) true in
              (k :: fst pr, snd pr)
            else
              zeck_greedy_down k' rem false
      end
  end.

(*@c.inline@[[The greedy support extractor `Z0` is obtained by running the downward constructor from the search bound `r0 x` with full remainder `x` and no previously chosen index. The resulting `base_params` package is the concrete parameter pack used by the arithmetic coding layer.]]@*)

(*@c.unicode@[[Z0(x) = fst(zeck_greedy_down(r0(x), x, false))]]@*)

Definition Z0 (x : nat) : list nat :=
  fst (zeck_greedy_down (r0 x) x false).

(*@c.inline@[[The concrete parameter pack used everywhere downstream pairs the greedy support extractor `Z0` with the search-bound function `r0`. This is the canonical base realization of the abstract carryless interface from `D001_01__Carryless_Pairing`.]]@*)

(*@c.unicode@[[base_params := ⟨Z0, r0⟩ : concrete carryless parameter pack]]@*)


Definition base_params : Params :=
  Build_Params Z0 r0.

(*@box.inline@[[Search Properties for r0]]@*)

(*@c.unicode@[[Global growth bound used by rank search fuel: `fib (S (S n)) ≥ S n` for every `n`.]]@*)

Lemma fib_ge_Sn : forall n, fib (S (S n)) >= S n.
Proof.
  induction n as [|n IH].
  - unfold fib. simpl. exact (Nat.le_refl 1).
  - rewrite fib_step.
    pose proof (fib_pos_S n) as Hpos.
    lia.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[The search-upper lemma states that any external witness bound `k + fuel` for `x` is propagated by `find_r_aux`: if `fib (k + fuel)` already exceeds `x`, then the returned search index also lies above `x`.]]@*)

Lemma find_r_aux_upper_from_witness :
  forall x k fuel,
    x < fib (k + fuel) ->
    x < fib (find_r_aux x k fuel).
Proof.
  intros x k fuel.
  revert k.
  induction fuel as [|fuel IH]; intros k Hlt.
  - simpl in Hlt. rewrite Nat.add_0_r in Hlt. exact Hlt.
  - simpl in Hlt |- *.
    destruct (Nat.ltb x (fib k)) eqn:Hk.
    + apply Nat.ltb_lt in Hk.
      exact Hk.
    + apply IH.
      replace (S k + fuel) with (k + S fuel) by lia.
      exact Hlt.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[The complementary search lemma says that every index strictly below the result of `find_r_aux` failed the stopping test. In boolean form, `Nat.ltb x (fib j)` is therefore false for each earlier candidate `j`.]]@*)

Lemma find_r_aux_before_false :
  forall x k fuel j,
    k <= j ->
    j < find_r_aux x k fuel ->
    Nat.ltb x (fib j) = false.
Proof.
  intros x k fuel.
  revert x k.
  induction fuel as [|fuel IH]; intros x k j Hkj Hlt.
  - simpl in Hlt. lia.
  - simpl in Hlt.
    destruct (Nat.ltb x (fib k)) eqn:Hk.
    + lia.
    + destruct (Nat.eq_dec j k) as [->|Hneq].
      * exact Hk.
      * assert (Hskj : S k <= j) by lia.
        exact (IH x (S k) j Hskj Hlt).
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[`r0_upper` is the forward search bound: the Fibonacci value at the chosen rank already lies strictly above the input. Together with `r0_minimal`, it characterizes `r0 n` as the first index where the Fibonacci sequence overtakes `n`.]]@*)

Lemma r0_upper :
  forall n, n < fib (r0 n).
Proof.
  intro n.
  unfold r0.
  apply find_r_aux_upper_from_witness.
  rewrite Nat.add_0_l.
  apply Nat.lt_le_trans with (m := S n).
  - exact (Nat.lt_succ_diag_r n).
  - exact (fib_ge_Sn n).
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[`r0_upper_S` packages the search result with one extra monotonicity step. It is the convenient strict upper bound used when the greedy constructor needs a Fibonacci ceiling at `S (r0 n)` rather than at `r0 n` itself.]]@*)

Corollary r0_upper_S :
  forall n, n < fib (S (r0 n)).
Proof.
  intro n.
  assert (Hup : n < fib (r0 n)) by apply r0_upper.
  assert (Hmono : fib (r0 n) <= fib (S (r0 n))) by apply fib_S_ge.
  lia.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[`r0_minimal` is the converse bound to `r0_upper`: any Fibonacci index strictly below `r0 n` is still at most `n`. Together the two results characterize `r0` as the first index whose Fibonacci value rises above `n`.]]@*)

Lemma r0_minimal :
  forall n k, k < r0 n -> fib k <= n.
Proof.
  intros n k Hlt.
  unfold r0 in Hlt.
  pose proof (find_r_aux_before_false n 0 (S (S n)) k (Nat.le_0_l k) Hlt) as Hfalse.
  apply Nat.ltb_ge in Hfalse.
  exact Hfalse.
Qed.

(*@box.section@[[Greedy Invariant — Structural Components]]@*)

(*@c.unicode@[[Uniform index ceiling predicate: `all_le m xs` means each element of `xs` is bounded above by `m`.]]@*)

Fixpoint all_le (m : nat) (xs : list nat) : Prop :=
  match xs with
  | [] => True
  | x :: xs' => x <= m /\ all_le m xs'
  end.

(*@c.inline@[[The auxiliary predicate `all_le m xs` records that every support index in `xs` lies below the current search bound. It lets the greedy invariant track support shape separately from the additive remainder equation.]]@*)

Definition bound (k : nat) (prev_taken : bool) : nat :=
  if prev_taken then Nat.pred k else k.

(*@c.unicode@[[Monotone bound transport: if every element of `xs` is `≤ m` and `m ≤ n`, then every element of `xs` is also `≤ n`.]]@*)

Lemma all_le_weaken : forall m n xs,
  all_le m xs -> m <= n -> all_le n xs.
Proof.
  induction xs as [|x xs IH]; intros Hle Hmn; simpl in *; auto.
  destruct Hle as [Hx Hxs].
  split; [lia | exact (IH Hxs Hmn)].
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[Extending a valid support by a new head index at least `2` preserves Zeckendorf validity provided the existing support already lies below the ambient bound `m`. This is the structural validity step used in the greedy take branch.]]@*)

Lemma zeck_valid_cons_ge2 :
  forall m xs,
    zeck_valid xs ->
    all_le m xs ->
    zeck_valid (S (S m) :: xs).
Proof.
  intros m xs [Hdec [Hadj Hge]] Hall.
  split; [|split].
  - destruct xs as [|x xs']; simpl; auto.
    destruct Hall as [Hx Hall'].
    split; [lia | exact Hdec].
  - destruct xs as [|x xs']; simpl; auto.
    destruct Hall as [Hx Hall'].
    split; [lia | exact Hadj].
  - simpl. split; [lia | exact Hge].
Qed.

(*@box.section@[[Greedy Invariant]]@*)

(*@c.unicode@[[Main loop invariant for `zeck_greedy_down`: additive decomposition (`sum_fib xs + rem' = rem`), structural validity of `xs`, index bound by `bound k prev_taken`, base-case emptiness at low `k`, and preservation of the Fibonacci upper remainder bound.]]@*)

Definition greedy_inv k rem prev_taken xs rem' :=
  sum_fib xs + rem' = rem /\
  zeck_valid xs /\
  all_le (bound k prev_taken) xs /\
  (k <= 1 -> xs = [] /\ rem' = rem) /\
  (rem < fib (S (bound k prev_taken)) -> rem' < fib (S (bound k prev_taken))).

(*@c.unicode@[[Take-branch arithmetic closure: if `fib k ≤ rem` and the recursive state sums to `rem - fib k`, then prefixing `k` restores total sum `rem`.]]@*)

Lemma greedy_take_step_sum :
  forall k rem xs rem',
    fib k <= rem ->
    sum_fib xs + rem' = rem - fib k ->
    sum_fib (k :: xs) + rem' = rem.
Proof.
  intros k rem xs rem' Hle Hsum.
  simpl.
  rewrite <- Nat.add_assoc.
  rewrite Hsum.
  apply add_sub_cancel_r.
  exact Hle.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[The main greedy correctness theorem establishes the full invariant for every execution of `zeck_greedy_down`: additive decomposition of the remainder, canonical support validity, index bounds, the low-index empty-support clause, and the residual top-bound clause.]]@*)

(*@todo@[[Temporary state: `zeck_greedy_down_correct_core` is still maintained as a dense recursive case script with semicolon-heavy invariant threading. If the greedy search or invariant changes again, split the take-step and skip-step transports into named helper lemmas before this proof becomes harder to stabilize.]]@*)

(*@c.unicode@[[Core induction theorem: every successful run of `zeck_greedy_down` returns `(xs, rem')` satisfying `greedy_inv k rem prev_taken xs rem'`.]]@*)

Lemma zeck_greedy_down_correct_core :
  forall k rem prev_taken xs rem',
    zeck_greedy_down k rem prev_taken = (xs, rem') ->
    greedy_inv k rem prev_taken xs rem'.
Proof.
  induction k as [|k IH]; intros rem prev xs rem' H.
  - inversion H; subst; repeat split; simpl; auto; lia.
  - destruct k as [|k']; simpl in H.
    + inversion H; subst; repeat split; simpl; auto; lia.
    + destruct prev eqn:Hprev.

      (*@c.inline@[[If the previous index was taken, the no-adjacency constraint forces the current index to be skipped.]]@*)
      * specialize (IH rem false xs rem' H) as [Hsum [Hz [Hall [Hlow Hbd]]]].
        unfold greedy_inv.
        split.
        { exact Hsum. }
        split.
        { exact Hz. }
        split.
        { simpl. exact Hall. }
        split.
        { intro Hk_le_1. exfalso. lia. }
        { intro Hrem.
          simpl in Hrem |- *.
          exact (Hbd Hrem). }

      (*@c.inline@[[If the previous index was skipped, canonicality allows either taking or skipping the current index.]]@*)
      * destruct (Nat.leb (fib (S (S k'))) rem) eqn:Hle.
        -- (*@c.inline@[[Take the current index exactly when `fib k <= rem`; recurse on the reduced remainder with adjacency lock enabled.]]@*)
           destruct (zeck_greedy_down (S k') (rem - fib (S (S k'))) true) as [xs' rem''] eqn:Hpr.
           change
             ((S (S k')
               :: fst (zeck_greedy_down (S k') (rem - fib (S (S k'))) true),
               snd (zeck_greedy_down (S k') (rem - fib (S (S k'))) true))
              = (xs, rem')) in H.
           rewrite Hpr in H.
           inversion H; subst xs rem'; clear H.
           specialize (IH _ true xs' rem'' Hpr) as [Hsum [Hz [Hall [Hlow Hbd]]]].
           apply Nat.leb_le in Hle.
           assert (Hz_new : zeck_valid (S (S k') :: xs')).
           { exact (zeck_valid_cons_ge2 k' xs' Hz Hall). }
           unfold greedy_inv; split; [|split; [|split; [|split]]].
           { exact (greedy_take_step_sum _ _ _ _ Hle Hsum). }
           { exact Hz_new. }
           { simpl; split; [lia|].
             eapply all_le_weaken.
             - exact Hall.
             - unfold bound; simpl; lia. }
           { intro Hk_le_1. exfalso. lia. }
           { intros Hrem.
             assert (Hinner : rem - fib (S (S k')) < fib (S k')).
             { simpl in Hrem. rewrite fib_step in Hrem. lia. }
             assert (Hrem'' : rem'' < fib (S k')) by (apply Hbd; exact Hinner).
             apply Nat.lt_le_trans with (m:=fib (S k')).
             - exact Hrem''.
             - simpl. apply fib_monotone_le; lia. }

        -- (*@c.inline@[[Skip the current index when it exceeds the remainder; propagate the invariant unchanged.]]@*)
           specialize (IH rem false xs rem' H) as [Hsum [Hz [Hall [Hlow Hbd]]]].
           apply Nat.leb_gt in Hle.
           unfold greedy_inv; split; [|split; [|split; [|split]]].
           { exact Hsum. }
           { exact Hz. }
           { eapply all_le_weaken.
             - exact Hall.
             - unfold bound; simpl; lia. }
           { intro Hk_le_1. exfalso. lia. }
           { intros Hrem.
             assert (Hinner : rem < fib (S (S k'))) by lia.
             assert (Hrem' : rem' < fib (S (S k'))) by (apply Hbd; exact Hinner).
             apply Nat.lt_le_trans with (m:=fib (S (S k'))).
             - exact Hrem'.
             - simpl. apply fib_monotone_le; lia. }
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[The first wrapper records the invariant specialized to the trivial search level `k = 0`. It is used later to keep case analyses on the search depth explicit rather than repeatedly unfolding the generic correctness theorem.]]@*)

Lemma zeck_greedy_down_inv_k0 :
  forall rem prev,
    greedy_inv 0 rem prev
      (fst (zeck_greedy_down 0 rem prev))
      (snd (zeck_greedy_down 0 rem prev)).
Proof.
  intros rem prev.
  apply zeck_greedy_down_correct_core.
  reflexivity.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[This is the corresponding specialized invariant at search depth `k = 1`. The low-depth cases are separated because several later remainder arguments reduce immediately at these boundary levels.]]@*)

Lemma zeck_greedy_down_inv_k1 :
  forall rem prev,
    greedy_inv 1 rem prev
      (fst (zeck_greedy_down 1 rem prev))
      (snd (zeck_greedy_down 1 rem prev)).
Proof.
  intros rem prev.
  apply zeck_greedy_down_correct_core.
  reflexivity.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[When the previous index was taken, the next recursive call is forced into the skip branch. This wrapper packages that branch of the generic invariant under the exact equation produced by the evaluator.]]@*)

Lemma zeck_greedy_down_inv_skip_prev :
  forall k rem xs rem',
    zeck_greedy_down (S (S k)) rem true = (xs, rem') ->
    greedy_inv (S (S k)) rem true xs rem'.
Proof.
  intros k rem xs rem' H.
  apply zeck_greedy_down_correct_core.
  exact H.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[This wrapper isolates the skip case caused by the current Fibonacci value exceeding the remaining budget. The greedy invariant is simply propagated through that branch.]]@*)

Lemma zeck_greedy_down_inv_skip_lt :
  forall k rem xs rem',
    fib (S (S k)) > rem ->
    zeck_greedy_down (S (S k)) rem false = (xs, rem') ->
    greedy_inv (S (S k)) rem false xs rem'.
Proof.
  intros k rem xs rem' _ H.
  apply zeck_greedy_down_correct_core.
  exact H.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[This is the corresponding wrapper for the take branch, where the current Fibonacci value is admissible. It exposes the invariant under the branch condition most later proofs naturally assume.]]@*)

Lemma zeck_greedy_down_inv_take :
  forall k rem xs rem',
    fib (S (S k)) <= rem ->
    zeck_greedy_down (S (S k)) rem false = (xs, rem') ->
    greedy_inv (S (S k)) rem false xs rem'.
Proof.
  intros k rem xs rem' _ H.
  apply zeck_greedy_down_correct_core.
  exact H.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[This theorem is the public wrapper around the core greedy invariant proof. It repackages the branch analysis into the direct statement later lemmas use, so downstream arguments can invoke greedy correctness without reopening the evaluator structure.]]@*)

Theorem zeck_greedy_down_correct :
  forall k rem prev xs rem',
    zeck_greedy_down k rem prev = (xs, rem') ->
    greedy_inv k rem prev xs rem'.
Proof.
  intros k rem prev xs rem' H.
  destruct k as [|k'].
  - apply zeck_greedy_down_correct_core. exact H.
  - destruct k' as [|k''].
    + apply zeck_greedy_down_correct_core. exact H.
    + destruct prev.
      * apply zeck_greedy_down_inv_skip_prev. exact H.
      * destruct (Nat.leb (fib (S (S k''))) rem) eqn:Hleb.
        -- apply zeck_greedy_down_inv_take.
           ++ apply Nat.leb_le. exact Hleb.
           ++ exact H.
        -- apply zeck_greedy_down_inv_skip_lt.
           ++ apply Nat.leb_gt. exact Hleb.
           ++ exact H.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[At the top-level call defining `Z0`, the residual remainder lies strictly below the next Fibonacci cutoff `fib (S (r0 n))`. This is the main global bound extracted from the greedy invariant and the defining property of `r0`.]]@*)

Lemma greedy_top_bound :
  forall n xs rem',
    zeck_greedy_down (r0 n) n false = (xs, rem') ->
    rem' < fib (S (r0 n)).
Proof.
  intros n xs rem' H.
  pose proof (zeck_greedy_down_correct (r0 n) n false xs rem' H)
    as [_ [_ [_ [_ Hbd]]]].
  simpl in Hbd.
  apply Hbd.
  apply r0_upper_S.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[If the greedy invariant already exposes a taken head index at level `S (S k)`, then the resulting partial Fibonacci sum still lies below the next Fibonacci threshold. The lemma converts the remainder bound in the invariant into a bound on the accumulated prefix sum.]]@*)

Lemma sum_fib_prefix_lt_next :
  forall k rem xs rem',
    greedy_inv (S (S k)) rem false (S (S k) :: xs) rem' ->
    rem < fib (S (S (S k))) ->
    sum_fib (S (S k) :: xs) < fib (S (S (S k))).
Proof.
  intros k rem xs rem' Hinv Hrem.
  destruct Hinv as [Hsum [_ [_ [_ _]]]].
  assert (sum_fib (S (S k) :: xs) <= rem) by lia.
  lia.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[The remainder arithmetic occasionally collapses to the observation that a natural number strictly below `1` must be `0`. We record that fact separately so the low-depth greedy cases stay readable.]]@*)

Lemma rem_lt_1_is_0 : forall r, r < 1 -> r = 0.
Proof.
  intros r Hr. lia.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[At depth `1`, the branch-bound parameter `bound 1 prev` never exceeds `1`. This tiny arithmetic fact is used to normalize the low-depth remainder bound coming from the generic greedy invariant.]]@*)

Lemma bound_k1_le_1 : forall prev, bound 1 prev <= 1.
Proof.
  intro prev. destruct prev; simpl; lia.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[We pin down the first nonzero Fibonacci value explicitly. These concrete identities for `fib 1` and `fib 2` keep the low-depth greedy cases computation-free.]]@*)

Lemma fib_2_eq_1 : fib 2 = 1.
Proof.
  vm_compute. reflexivity.
Qed.

(*@c.unicode@[[Base numeral identity: `fib 1 = 1`.]]@*)

Lemma fib_1_eq_1 : fib 1 = 1.
Proof.
  vm_compute. reflexivity.
Qed.

(*@c.unicode@[[Low-depth remainder squeeze: from `greedy_inv` at `k = 1`, the precondition `rem < fib (S (bound 1 prev))` forces `rem' < 1`.]]@*)

Lemma rem_lt_1_from_inv_k1 :
  forall rem prev xs rem',
    zeck_greedy_down 1 rem prev = (xs, rem') ->
    greedy_inv 1 rem prev xs rem' ->
    rem < fib (S (bound 1 prev)) ->
    rem' < 1.
Proof.
  intros rem prev xs rem' Hcall Hinv Hpre.
  destruct Hinv as [_ [_ [_ [_ Hbd]]]].
  specialize (Hbd Hpre).
  destruct prev.
  - simpl in Hbd. rewrite fib_1_eq_1 in Hbd. lia.
  - simpl in Hbd. rewrite fib_2_eq_1 in Hbd. lia.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[Once the available remainder already lies below the next admissible Fibonacci threshold, the greedy procedure running with `prev_taken = false` must finish with residual remainder below `1`. This is the key induction used to prove exact soundness of `Z0`.]]@*)

Lemma greedy_rem_lt_1_false :
  forall k rem xs rem',
    rem < fib (S k) ->
    zeck_greedy_down k rem false = (xs, rem') ->
    rem' < 1.
Proof.
  refine (well_founded_induction
            lt_wf
            (fun k =>
               forall rem xs rem',
                 rem < fib (S k) ->
                 zeck_greedy_down k rem false = (xs, rem') ->
                 rem' < 1) _).
  intros k IH rem xs rem' Hbound Hcall.
  destruct k as [|k1].
  - simpl in Hcall. inversion Hcall; subst. exact Hbound.
  - destruct k1 as [|k2].
    + simpl in Hcall. inversion Hcall; subst.
      rewrite fib_2_eq_1 in Hbound. exact Hbound.
    + simpl in Hcall.
      destruct (Nat.leb (fib (S (S k2))) rem) eqn:Hle.
      * destruct (zeck_greedy_down (S k2) (rem - fib (S (S k2))) true)
          as [xs1 rem1] eqn:Hrec.
        change
          ((S (S k2)
            :: fst (zeck_greedy_down (S k2) (rem - fib (S (S k2))) true),
            snd (zeck_greedy_down (S k2) (rem - fib (S (S k2))) true))
           = (xs, rem')) in Hcall.
        rewrite Hrec in Hcall.
        inversion Hcall; subst xs rem'. clear Hcall.
        apply Nat.leb_le in Hle.
        assert (Hrem1 : rem - fib (S (S k2)) < fib (S k2)).
        -- rewrite fib_step in Hbound. lia.
        -- destruct k2 as [|k3].
           ++ simpl in Hrec. inversion Hrec; subst.
              rewrite fib_1_eq_1 in Hrem1. exact Hrem1.
           ++ simpl in Hrec.
              pose proof (IH (S k3) ltac:(lia)) as IHk.
              exact (IHk (rem - fib (S (S (S k3)))) xs1 rem1 Hrem1 Hrec).
      * apply Nat.leb_gt in Hle.
        pose proof (IH (S k2) ltac:(lia)) as IHk.
        exact (IHk rem xs rem' Hle Hcall).
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[Applying the previous remainder lemma to the defining top-level call of `Z0` shows that the final residual remainder of the concrete greedy support extractor is always below `1`, hence later forced to be `0`.]]@*)

Lemma Z0_rem_lt_1 :
  forall n xs rem',
    zeck_greedy_down (r0 n) n false = (xs, rem') ->
    rem' < 1.
Proof.
  intros n xs rem' H.
  exact (greedy_rem_lt_1_false (r0 n) n xs rem' (r0_upper_S n) H).
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[`Z0_sound` is the main correctness theorem for the concrete support extractor: the Fibonacci sum of the greedy support produced for `n` is exactly `n`. The proof combines the greedy invariant with the fact that the final residual remainder vanishes.]]@*)

Theorem Z0_sound : forall n, sum_fib (Z0 n) = n.
Proof.
  intro n.
  unfold Z0.
  destruct (zeck_greedy_down (r0 n) n false) as [xs rem'] eqn:Hgd.
  pose proof (zeck_greedy_down_correct (r0 n) n false xs rem' Hgd)
    as [Hsum _].
  assert (Hrem1 : rem' < 1) by (eapply Z0_rem_lt_1; exact Hgd).
  assert (Hrem0 : rem' = 0) by (apply rem_lt_1_is_0; exact Hrem1).
  rewrite Hrem0 in Hsum.
  rewrite Nat.add_0_r in Hsum.
  exact Hsum.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[The sound greedy extractor is also canonical: the support it returns always satisfies the Zeckendorf side conditions. This theorem exposes the validity component of the invariant at the top-level call defining `Z0`.]]@*)

Theorem Z0_valid : forall n, zeck_valid (Z0 n).
Proof.
  intro n.
  unfold Z0.
  destruct (zeck_greedy_down (r0 n) n false) as [xs rem'] eqn:Hgd.
  pose proof (zeck_greedy_down_correct (r0 n) n false xs rem' Hgd)
    as [_ [Hvalid _]].
  exact Hvalid.
Qed.

(*@box.section@[[Canonical Inversion: Basic Bounds]]@*)

(*@c.unicode@[[Head lower bound for support sums: `sum_fib (k :: xs) ≥ fib k`.]]@*)

Lemma sum_fib_ge_head :
  forall k xs, sum_fib (k :: xs) >= fib k.
Proof.
  intros k xs. simpl. lia.
Qed.

(*@c.unicode@[[Tail stability of Zeckendorf validity: if `k :: xs` is valid, then `xs` is valid.]]@*)

Lemma zeck_valid_tail :
  forall k xs,
    zeck_valid (k :: xs) ->
    zeck_valid xs.
Proof.
  intros k xs [Hdec [Hadj Hge]].
  destruct xs as [|a xs'].
  - simpl. split; [trivial|]. split; trivial.
  - simpl in Hdec, Hadj, Hge.
    destruct Hdec as [_ Hdec'].
    destruct Hadj as [_ Hadj'].
    destruct Hge as [_ Hge'].
    split; [exact Hdec'|]. split; assumption.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[Any valid nonempty Zeckendorf support starts at an index at least `2`. This is the head form of the `all_ge_2` component of `zeck_valid` and is used repeatedly in Fibonacci-growth arguments.]]@*)

Lemma zeck_valid_head_ge_2 :
  forall k xs,
    zeck_valid (k :: xs) ->
    2 <= k.
Proof.
  intros k xs [_ [_ Hge]].
  simpl in Hge.
  destruct Hge as [Ha _].
  exact Ha.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[This monotonicity lemma is the non-strict comparison form for Fibonacci values. It lets later proofs move from an index inequality to the corresponding numeric bound on Fibonacci sums.]]@*)

Lemma fib_le_of_le : forall a b, a <= b -> fib a <= fib b.
Proof.
  intros a b Hle.
  destruct (Nat.eq_dec a b) as [Heq|Hneq].
  - subst. apply Nat.le_refl.
  - apply fib_monotone_le. lia.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[For valid head indices `k >= 2`, the next Fibonacci value is strictly larger than the current one. This is the strict growth fact needed to show that a canonical support sum stays below the next threshold.]]@*)

Lemma fib_lt_succ_of_ge2 : forall k, 2 <= k -> fib k < fib (S k).
Proof.
  intros k Hk.
  destruct k as [|[|k']]; try lia.
  rewrite (fib_step (S k')).
  pose proof (fib_pos_S k') as Hpos.
  eapply Nat.lt_le_trans with (m := S (fib (S (S k')))).
  - 
    apply Nat.lt_succ_diag_r.
  - rewrite <- Nat.add_1_r.
    apply Nat.add_le_mono_l.
    exact Hpos.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[This predecessor form of the Fibonacci recursion is the version needed when a proof compares `fib (S k)` with the current head contribution `fib k` and the residual tail budget below it.]]@*)

Lemma fib_step_pred : forall k, 1 <= k -> fib (S k) = fib k + fib (Nat.pred k).
Proof.
  intros k Hk.
  destruct k as [|k']; [lia|].
  destruct k' as [|k''].
  - vm_compute. reflexivity.
  - simpl. apply fib_step.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[A valid nonempty Zeckendorf support always sums to strictly less than the next Fibonacci value above its head index. This is the standard canonical-prefix bound used later in the band-splitting and uniqueness arguments.]]@*)

Lemma sum_fib_lt_next_of_valid :
  forall k xs,
    zeck_valid (k :: xs) ->
    sum_fib (k :: xs) < fib (S k).
Proof.
  intros k xs Hvalid.
  revert k Hvalid.
  induction xs as [|x xs' IH]; intros k Hvalid; simpl.
  - apply zeck_valid_head_ge_2 in Hvalid.
    rewrite Nat.add_0_r.
    apply fib_lt_succ_of_ge2.
    exact Hvalid.
  - pose proof (zeck_valid_tail k (x :: xs') Hvalid) as Htail_valid.
    pose proof (IH x Htail_valid) as Htail.
    destruct Hvalid as [_ [Hadj _]].
    simpl in Hadj.
    destruct Hadj as [Hgap _].
    assert (Hsx : S x <= Nat.pred k) by lia.
    assert (Hsx_le : fib (S x) <= fib (Nat.pred k)).
    + apply fib_le_of_le. exact Hsx.
    + assert (Hk1 : 1 <= k) by lia.
      assert (Hstep : fib (S k) = fib k + fib (Nat.pred k)).
      * apply fib_step_pred. exact Hk1.
      * rewrite Hstep.
        assert (Hlt_pred : fib x + sum_fib xs' < fib (Nat.pred k)).
        -- eapply Nat.lt_le_trans; [exact Htail | exact Hsx_le].
        -- lia.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[A valid nonempty support has positive Fibonacci sum. The lemma packages the obvious positivity consequence of `k >= 2` and `fib k > 0` in the exact form needed by later subtraction arguments.]]@*)

Lemma sum_fib_cons_pos_valid :
  forall k xs,
    zeck_valid (k :: xs) ->
    0 < sum_fib (k :: xs).
Proof.
  intros k xs Hvalid.
  simpl.
  assert (Hk2 : 2 <= k) by (apply zeck_valid_head_ge_2 with (xs:=xs); exact Hvalid).
  assert (Hf : fib k >= 1) by (apply fib_pos; lia).
  lia.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[If one valid head index lies strictly below another, then the successor Fibonacci value at the smaller index is still bounded by the Fibonacci value at the larger one. This comparison is the key inequality in the uniqueness proof.]]@*)

Lemma fib_succ_le_of_lt_ge2 :
  forall a b,
    2 <= a ->
    a < b ->
    fib (S a) <= fib b.
Proof.
  intros a b Ha Hlt.
  destruct (Nat.eq_dec (S a) b) as [Heq|Hneq].
  - subst. apply Nat.le_refl.
  - apply fib_monotone_le. lia.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[`Zeckendorf_unique_core` is the structural uniqueness argument for canonical supports: two valid supports with the same Fibonacci sum must coincide exactly. The proof compares heads using the canonical-next-threshold bound and then recurses on the tails.]]@*)

Lemma Zeckendorf_unique_core :
  forall xs ys,
    zeck_valid xs ->
    zeck_valid ys ->
    sum_fib xs = sum_fib ys ->
    xs = ys.
Proof.
  induction xs as [|k xs IH]; intros ys Hx Hy Heq.
  - destruct ys as [|l ys].
    + reflexivity.
    + exfalso.
      pose proof (sum_fib_cons_pos_valid l ys Hy) as Hpos.
      rewrite <- Heq in Hpos. simpl in Hpos. lia.
  - destruct ys as [|l ys].
    + exfalso.
      pose proof (sum_fib_cons_pos_valid k xs Hx) as Hpos.
      rewrite Heq in Hpos. simpl in Hpos. lia.
    + assert (Hk : k = l).
      * destruct (Nat.lt_ge_cases k l) as [Hkl|Hkge].
        -- pose proof (sum_fib_lt_next_of_valid k xs Hx) as Hup.
           pose proof (sum_fib_ge_head l ys) as Hlow.
           rewrite Heq in Hup.
           assert (Hf : fib (S k) <= fib l).
           ++ apply fib_succ_le_of_lt_ge2.
              ** apply zeck_valid_head_ge_2 with (xs:=xs). exact Hx.
              ** exact Hkl.
           ++ lia.
        -- destruct (Nat.lt_ge_cases l k) as [Hlk|Hlge].
           ++ pose proof (sum_fib_lt_next_of_valid l ys Hy) as Hup.
              pose proof (sum_fib_ge_head k xs) as Hlow.
              rewrite <- Heq in Hup.
              assert (Hf : fib (S l) <= fib k).
              ** apply fib_succ_le_of_lt_ge2.
                 --- apply zeck_valid_head_ge_2 with (xs:=ys). exact Hy.
                 --- exact Hlk.
              ** lia.
           ++ lia.
      * subst l.
        apply f_equal.
        apply IH.
        -- apply zeck_valid_tail with (k:=k). exact Hx.
        -- apply zeck_valid_tail with (k:=k). exact Hy.
        -- simpl in Heq. lia.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[Applying the concrete greedy extractor to the Fibonacci sum of an already valid support recovers that support unchanged. This is the reification direction of the concrete Zeckendorf normal form.]]@*)

Theorem Z0_of_sum_fib :
  forall xs, zeck_valid xs -> Z0 (sum_fib xs) = xs.
Proof.
  intros xs Hvalid.
  apply Zeckendorf_unique_core.
  - apply Z0_valid.
  - exact Hvalid.
  - apply Z0_sound.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[The uniqueness theorem is just the core uniqueness proof factored through `Z0_of_sum_fib`. It states the extensional uniqueness of valid Zeckendorf supports by their Fibonacci sum alone.]]@*)

Theorem Zeckendorf_unique :
  forall xs ys,
    zeck_valid xs ->
    zeck_valid ys ->
    sum_fib xs = sum_fib ys ->
    xs = ys.
Proof.
  intros xs ys Hx Hy Heq.
  rewrite <- (Z0_of_sum_fib xs Hx).
  rewrite <- (Z0_of_sum_fib ys Hy).
  f_equal.
  exact Heq.
Qed.

(*@c.section@[[Bridge to Carryless Pairing]]@*)

(*@c.inline@[[Evaluation across concatenated supports splits additively. This is the basic bridge from list concatenation of bands to arithmetic addition of their Fibonacci values.]]@*)

Lemma sum_fib_app : forall xs ys, sum_fib (xs ++ ys) = sum_fib xs + sum_fib ys.
Proof.
  induction xs as [|a xs IH]; intro ys; simpl.
  - lia.
  - rewrite IH. lia.
Qed.

(*@c.unicode@[[Membership form of the lower-index constraint: from `all_ge_2 xs` and `k ∈ xs`, infer `2 ≤ k`.]]@*)

Lemma all_ge_2_in :
  forall xs k, all_ge_2 xs -> In k xs -> 2 <= k.
Proof.
  induction xs as [|a xs IH]; intros k Hge Hin; simpl in *.
  - contradiction.
  - destruct Hge as [Ha Hxs].
    destruct Hin as [<-|Hin].
    + exact Ha.
    + apply (IH k Hxs Hin).
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[The predicate `all_le` yields pointwise upper bounds on every member of the support list. This membership form is used to turn invariant-wide support bounds into concrete index inequalities.]]@*)

Lemma all_le_in :
  forall m xs k, all_le m xs -> In k xs -> k <= m.
Proof.
  induction xs as [|a xs IH]; intros k Hle Hin; simpl in *.
  - contradiction.
  - destruct Hle as [Ha Hxs].
    destruct Hin as [<-|Hin].
    + exact Ha.
    + apply (IH k Hxs Hin).
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[Every index occurring in a support contributes at most the total Fibonacci sum of that support. The lemma is the obvious membership lower bound on the sum and is used to compare support indices with ambient numeric bounds.]]@*)

Lemma sum_fib_in_ge :
  forall xs k, In k xs -> fib k <= sum_fib xs.
Proof.
  induction xs as [|a xs IH]; intros k Hin; simpl in *.
  - contradiction.
  - destruct Hin as [<-|Hin].
    + lia.
    + specialize (IH k Hin). lia.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[Every index appearing in `Z0 x` lies strictly below the search cutoff `r0 x`. The lemma combines the support-bound component of the greedy invariant with the defining minimality property of `r0`.]]@*)

Lemma Z0_indices_below_r0 :
  forall x e,
    In e (Z0 x) ->
    e < r0 x.
Proof.
  intros x e Hin.
  unfold Z0 in Hin.
  destruct (zeck_greedy_down (r0 x) x false) as [xs rem'] eqn:Hgd.
  simpl in Hin.
  pose proof (zeck_greedy_down_correct (r0 x) x false xs rem' Hgd)
    as [Hsum [_ [Hall _]]].
  assert (Hele : e <= r0 x).
  - apply (all_le_in (r0 x) xs e Hall Hin).
  - destruct (Nat.eq_dec e (r0 x)) as [Heq|Hneq].
    + subst e.
      assert (Hf_le : fib (r0 x) <= sum_fib xs).
      * apply sum_fib_in_ge. exact Hin.
      * assert (Hf_le_x : fib (r0 x) <= x) by lia.
        pose proof (r0_upper x) as Hru.
        lia.
    + lia.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[The odd-band index transform `two_j_minus1` is strictly increasing. This gives the order-preservation needed when transporting strict decrease from Zeckendorf supports to their odd-band images.]]@*)

Lemma two_S : forall n, two (S n) = S (S (two n)).
Proof.
  intro n.
  unfold two.
  simpl.
  rewrite Nat.add_succ_r.
  reflexivity.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[Doubling always lands on an even index. This parity lemma is the first half of the band-separation argument showing that the even support can be recovered by filtering for even indices.]]@*)

Lemma is_even_two : forall n, is_even (two n) = true.
Proof.
  induction n as [|n IH].
  - reflexivity.
  - rewrite two_S. simpl. exact IH.
Qed.

(*@c.unicode@[[Successor-of-double parity fact: `S (2n)` is odd, encoded as `is_even (S (two n)) = false`.]]@*)

Lemma is_even_S_two_false : forall n, is_even (S (two n)) = false.
Proof.
  induction n as [|n IH].
  - reflexivity.
  - rewrite two_S. simpl. exact IH.
Qed.

(*@c.unicode@[[Closed arithmetic form of the odd-index encoder: `two_j_minus1 j = 2*j - 1`.]]@*)

Lemma two_j_minus1_formula :
  forall j, two_j_minus1 j = 2 * j - 1.
Proof.
  intro j.
  unfold two_j_minus1.
  rewrite <- Nat.sub_1_r.
  unfold two.
  lia.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[Positive Zeckendorf indices are sent by `two_j_minus1` to positive odd indices. This elementary lower bound is one of the ingredients for proving that odd-band supports stay above the even-band boundary.]]@*)

Lemma two_j_minus1_lt :
  forall a b, b < a -> two_j_minus1 b < two_j_minus1 a.
Proof.
  intros a b Hlt.
  repeat rewrite two_j_minus1_formula.
  lia.
Qed.

(*@c.unicode@[[Gap transport through odd encoding: if indices satisfy `a ≥ b + 2`, then encoded values satisfy `two_j_minus1 a ≥ two_j_minus1 b + 2`.]]@*)

Lemma two_j_minus1_gap2 :
  forall a b, a >= b + 2 -> two_j_minus1 a >= two_j_minus1 b + 2.
Proof.
  intros a b Hgap.
  repeat rewrite two_j_minus1_formula.
  lia.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[Every value of the form `two_j_minus1 j` with `j >= 1` is odd. This parity fact is used repeatedly when separating even-band and odd-band indices by filtering the combined support.]]@*)

Lemma two_j_minus1_ge_1 :
  forall j, 1 <= j -> 1 <= two_j_minus1 j.
Proof.
  intros j Hj.
  rewrite two_j_minus1_formula.
  lia.
Qed.

(*@c.unicode@[[Parity-shift invariance under even offsets: `∀ a n, is_even (two a + n) = is_even n`.]]@*)

Lemma is_even_two_plus :
  forall a n, is_even (two a + n) = is_even n.
Proof.
  induction a as [|a IH]; intro n.
  - simpl. reflexivity.
  - rewrite two_S. simpl. apply IH.
Qed.

(*@c.unicode@[[Same parity invariance in multiplicative form: `∀ a n, is_even (2*a + n) = is_even n`.]]@*)

Lemma is_even_double_plus :
  forall a n, is_even (2 * a + n) = is_even n.
Proof.
  induction a as [|a IH]; intro n.
  - simpl. reflexivity.
  - replace (2 * S a + n) with (S (S (2 * a + n))) by lia.
    simpl.
    apply IH.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[Doubling preserves strict decrease of support indices. This transport lemma is the first step in showing that the even band of a valid Zeckendorf support is itself a valid canonical support.]]@*)

Lemma is_even_two_j_minus1_false :
  forall j, 1 <= j -> is_even (two_j_minus1 j) = false.
Proof.
  intros j Hj.
  destruct j as [|j']; [lia|].
  unfold two_j_minus1.
  rewrite two_S.
  apply is_even_S_two_false.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[Doubling also preserves the no-adjacency condition. Together with the previous lemma and the lower-bound transport, it gives validity of the even band.]]@*)

Lemma strictly_decreasing_map_two :
  forall xs, strictly_decreasing xs -> strictly_decreasing (map two xs).
Proof.
  induction xs as [|a xs IH]; intro Hdec; simpl; auto.
  destruct xs as [|b xs']; simpl in *; auto.
  destruct Hdec as [Hab Htail].
  split.
  - unfold two. lia.
  - apply IH. exact Htail.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[Adding a fixed boundary offset and applying `two_j_minus1` preserves strict decrease. This is the odd-band analogue of `strictly_decreasing_map_two`.]]@*)

Lemma no_adjacent_map_two :
  forall xs, no_adjacent xs -> no_adjacent (map two xs).
Proof.
  induction xs as [|a xs IH]; intro Hadj; simpl; auto.
  destruct xs as [|b xs']; simpl in *; auto.
  destruct Hadj as [Hab Htail].
  split.
  - unfold two. lia.
  - apply IH. exact Htail.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[The odd-band transport also preserves the no-adjacency gap. This ensures that the odd support extracted from `y` remains canonical after shifting it above the boundary `B x`.]]@*)

Lemma all_ge_2_map_two :
  forall xs, all_ge_2 xs -> all_ge_2 (map two xs).
Proof.
  induction xs as [|a xs IH]; intro Hge; simpl; auto.
  destruct Hge as [Ha Htail].
  split.
  - unfold two. lia.
  - apply IH. exact Htail.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[Odd-band transport preserves the lower bound `2` on indices. The point is that every shifted odd index lies well above `1`, so canonicality is retained after offsetting.]]@*)

Lemma strictly_decreasing_map_odd :
  forall Bx xs,
    strictly_decreasing xs ->
    strictly_decreasing (map (fun j => Bx + two_j_minus1 j) xs).
Proof.
  intros Bx xs Hdec.
  revert Bx Hdec.
  induction xs as [|a xs IH]; intros Bx Hdec; simpl; auto.
  destruct xs as [|b xs']; simpl in *; auto.
  destruct Hdec as [Hab Htail].
  split.
  - apply Nat.add_lt_mono_l. apply two_j_minus1_lt. exact Hab.
  - apply IH. exact Htail.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[The even band of the concrete base realization is always a valid Zeckendorf support. This packages the three doubling-transport lemmas over the canonical support `Z0 x`.]]@*)

Lemma no_adjacent_map_odd :
  forall Bx xs,
    no_adjacent xs ->
    no_adjacent (map (fun j => Bx + two_j_minus1 j) xs).
Proof.
  intros Bx xs Hadj.
  revert Bx Hadj.
  induction xs as [|a xs IH]; intros Bx Hadj; simpl; auto.
  destruct xs as [|b xs']; simpl in *; auto.
  destruct Hadj as [Hab Htail].
  split.
  - assert (Hgap : two_j_minus1 a >= two_j_minus1 b + 2).
    + apply two_j_minus1_gap2. exact Hab.
    + lia.
  - apply IH. exact Htail.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[The odd band is likewise a valid canonical support. Its proof is the offset-odd counterpart of `even_band_valid`, using the support validity of `Z0 y`.]]@*)

Lemma all_ge_2_map_odd :
  forall Bx xs,
    all_ge_2 xs ->
    all_ge_2 (map (fun j => Bx + two_j_minus1 j) xs).
Proof.
  intros Bx xs Hge.
  revert Bx Hge.
  induction xs as [|a xs IH]; intros Bx Hge; simpl; auto.
  destruct Hge as [Ha Htail].
  split.
  - rewrite two_j_minus1_formula.
    lia.
  - apply IH. exact Htail.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[Validity of supports is preserved under concatenation when both halves are valid and every element of the left half is still strictly above every element of the right half. This version handles the lower-bound component `all_ge_2`.]]@*)

Lemma even_band_valid :
  forall x, zeck_valid (even_band base_params x).
Proof.
  intro x.
  unfold even_band, base_params.
  pose proof (Z0_valid x) as [Hdec [Hadj Hge]].
  split.
  - apply strictly_decreasing_map_two. exact Hdec.
  - split.
    + apply no_adjacent_map_two. exact Hadj.
    + apply all_ge_2_map_two. exact Hge.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[This is the concatenation lemma for the no-adjacency component. It lifts local no-adjacency on each half plus a uniform cross-gap condition to the concatenated support.]]@*)

Lemma odd_band_valid :
  forall x y, zeck_valid (odd_band base_params x y).
Proof.
  intros x y.
  unfold odd_band, base_params.
  pose proof (Z0_valid y) as [Hdec [Hadj Hge]].
  split.
  - apply strictly_decreasing_map_odd. exact Hdec.
  - split.
    + apply no_adjacent_map_odd. exact Hadj.
    + apply all_ge_2_map_odd. exact Hge.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[Every index in the odd band is genuinely odd. This is the parity counterpart of `even_band_even` and is what makes the even/odd filter split recover the two bands from the combined support.]]@*)

Lemma even_band_lt_B :
  forall x e, In e (even_band base_params x) -> e < B base_params x.
Proof.
  intros x e Hin.
  unfold even_band, base_params in Hin.
  apply in_map_iff in Hin.
  destruct Hin as [j [He Hj]].
  subst e.
  assert (Hjlt : j < r0 x).
  - apply Z0_indices_below_r0. exact Hj.
  - unfold base_params.
    unfold B.
    unfold two.
    simpl.
    replace (2 * r0 x) with (r0 x + r0 x) by lia.
    lia.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[Odd-band elements satisfy the exact boolean predicate used by carryless decoding to recognize the shifted odd support above the boundary `B x`.]]@*)

Lemma odd_band_ge_B1 :
  forall x y o, In o (odd_band base_params x y) -> S (B base_params x) <= o.
Proof.
  intros x y o Hin.
  unfold odd_band, base_params in Hin.
  apply in_map_iff in Hin.
  destruct Hin as [j [Ho Hj]].
  subst o.
  assert (Hj2 : 2 <= j).
  - apply all_ge_2_in with (xs:=Z0 y); [|exact Hj].
    destruct (Z0_valid y) as [_ [_ Hge]]; exact Hge.
  - assert (Hj1 : 1 <= j) by lia.
    assert (Hodd1 : 1 <= two_j_minus1 j) by (apply two_j_minus1_ge_1; exact Hj1).
    unfold B.
    unfold base_params.
    simpl.
    lia.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[Every odd-band index lies strictly above every even-band index built from the same left coordinate `x`. This is the clean separation fact that makes the concatenated support split unambiguously into the two carryless bands.]]@*)

Lemma odd_band_gt_even_band :
  forall x y o e,
    In o (odd_band base_params x y) ->
    In e (even_band base_params x) ->
    o > e.
Proof.
  intros x y o e Ho He.
  pose proof (odd_band_ge_B1 x y o Ho) as Hob.
  pose proof (even_band_lt_B x e He) as Heb.
  lia.
Qed.

(*@c.unicode@[[Separated-band gap lemma: every odd-band index is at least two above every even-band index, i.e. `o ≥ e + 2`.]]@*)

Lemma odd_band_gap_even_band :
  forall x y o e,
    In o (odd_band base_params x y) ->
    In e (even_band base_params x) ->
    o >= e + 2.
Proof.
  intros x y o e Ho He.
  pose proof (odd_band_ge_B1 x y o Ho) as Hob.
  pose proof (even_band_lt_B x e He) as Heb.
  lia.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[Putting the two canonical bands together yields another valid support: the odd band for `y` sits entirely above the even band for `x`, so their concatenation satisfies the full Zeckendorf validity predicate.]]@*)

Lemma all_ge_2_app :
  forall xs ys, all_ge_2 xs -> all_ge_2 ys -> all_ge_2 (xs ++ ys).
Proof.
  induction xs as [|a xs IH]; intros ys Hx Hy; simpl.
  - exact Hy.
  - destruct Hx as [Ha Hxs]. split; [exact Ha|]. apply IH; assumption.
Qed.

(*@c.unicode@[[Monotone concatenation criterion: if both lists are strictly decreasing and every `x ∈ xs` is greater than every `y ∈ ys`, then `xs ++ ys` remains strictly decreasing.]]@*)

Lemma strictly_decreasing_app :
  forall xs ys,
    strictly_decreasing xs ->
    strictly_decreasing ys ->
    (forall x y, In x xs -> In y ys -> x > y) ->
    strictly_decreasing (xs ++ ys).
Proof.
  induction xs as [|a xs IH]; intros ys Hx Hy Hcross; simpl.
  - exact Hy.
  - destruct xs as [|b xs'].
    + simpl.
      destruct ys as [|y ys'].
      * simpl. exact I.
      * simpl. split.
        -- apply Hcross; simpl; auto.
        -- exact Hy.
    + simpl in Hx.
      destruct Hx as [Hab Htail].
      simpl.
      split; [exact Hab|].
      apply IH; try assumption.
      intros x y Hinx Hiny.
      apply Hcross; simpl; auto.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[`filter_false_nil` is the standard list-filter elimination principle: if every element fails the boolean predicate, the filtered list is empty. It is used to discard the wrong band during support splitting.]]@*)

Lemma no_adjacent_app :
  forall xs ys,
    no_adjacent xs ->
    no_adjacent ys ->
    (forall x y, In x xs -> In y ys -> x >= y + 2) ->
    no_adjacent (xs ++ ys).
Proof.
  induction xs as [|a xs IH]; intros ys Hx Hy Hcross; simpl.
  - exact Hy.
  - destruct xs as [|b xs'].
    + simpl.
      destruct ys as [|y ys'].
      * simpl. exact I.
      * simpl. split.
        -- apply Hcross; simpl; auto.
        -- exact Hy.
    + simpl in Hx.
      destruct Hx as [Hab Htail].
      simpl.
      split; [exact Hab|].
      apply IH; try assumption.
      intros x y Hinx Hiny.
      apply Hcross; simpl; auto.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[Dually, `filter_true_id` states that filtering by a predicate that holds everywhere leaves the list unchanged. This is the positive half of the later even/odd band split proofs.]]@*)

Lemma odd_even_concat_valid :
  forall x y,
    zeck_valid (odd_band base_params x y ++ even_band base_params x).
Proof.
  intros x y.
  pose proof (odd_band_valid x y) as [Hdec_o [Hadj_o Hge_o]].
  pose proof (even_band_valid x) as [Hdec_e [Hadj_e Hge_e]].
  split.
  - eapply strictly_decreasing_app; eauto.
    intros o e Ho He.
    apply odd_band_gt_even_band with (x:=x) (y:=y); assumption.
  - split.
    + eapply no_adjacent_app; eauto.
      intros o e Ho He.
      apply odd_band_gap_even_band with (x:=x) (y:=y); assumption.
    + apply all_ge_2_app; assumption.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[The concrete even-split theorem recovers the even band of `x` from the support of the paired code by filtering for even indices. The proof uses the concatenation description of `Z0 (pair x y)` and the parity separation of the two bands.]]@*)

Lemma pair_base_as_odd_even_sum :
  forall x y,
    pair base_params x y =
    sum_fib (odd_band base_params x y ++ even_band base_params x).
Proof.
  intros x y.
  unfold pair.
  rewrite !sum_fib_app.
  lia.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[The concrete odd-split theorem recovers the odd band of `y` relative to `x` by filtering the paired support with `odd_ge_B1 (B x)`. Together with `Z0_even_split`, this is the main concrete bridge to the abstract carryless pairing interface.]]@*)

Theorem Z0_pair_is_concat :
  forall x y,
    Z0 (pair base_params x y) =
    odd_band base_params x y ++ even_band base_params x.
Proof.
  intros x y.
  rewrite pair_base_as_odd_even_sum.
  apply Z0_of_sum_fib.
  apply odd_even_concat_valid.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[If `x` already lies below `fib K`, then every support index appearing in `Z0 x` lies strictly below `K`. This packages the top-index control furnished by `r0` into a direct statement about concrete support members.]]@*)

Lemma even_band_even :
  forall x k,
    In k (even_band base_params x) ->
    is_even k = true.
Proof.
  intros x k Hin.
  unfold even_band, base_params in Hin.
  apply in_map_iff in Hin.
  destruct Hin as [e [He _]].
  subst k.
  apply is_even_two.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[Every concrete support index produced by `Z0` is at least `2`. This is the membership form of the canonical lower-bound property and feeds directly into the support-window estimates below.]]@*)

Lemma odd_band_even_false :
  forall x y k,
    In k (odd_band base_params x y) ->
    is_even k = false.
Proof.
  intros x y k Hin.
  unfold odd_band, base_params in Hin.
  apply in_map_iff in Hin.
  destruct Hin as [j [Hk Hj]].
  subst k.
  assert (Hj2 : 2 <= j).
  - apply all_ge_2_in with (xs:=Z0 y); [|exact Hj].
    destruct (Z0_valid y) as [_ [_ Hge]]; exact Hge.
  - assert (Hj1 : 1 <= j) by lia.
    unfold B.
    rewrite is_even_double_plus.
    apply is_even_two_j_minus1_false.
    exact Hj1.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[A translated Fibonacci band `fib_band b x` sits inside the precise interval `[b + 2, b + K)` whenever `x < fib K`. This local window estimate is the main quantitative fact used for band-separation arguments.]]@*)

Lemma odd_band_odd_ge_B1_true :
  forall x y k,
    In k (odd_band base_params x y) ->
    odd_ge_B1 (B base_params x) k = true.
Proof.
  intros x y k Hin.
  unfold odd_ge_B1.
  unfold is_odd.
  rewrite (odd_band_even_false x y k Hin).
  apply Nat.leb_le.
  apply (odd_band_ge_B1 x y k).
  exact Hin.
Qed.

(*@c.unicode@[[Even-band rejection by the odd-threshold filter: `k ∈ even_band(base_params,x) ⟹ odd_ge_B1(B(base_params,x),k) = false`.]]@*)

Lemma even_band_odd_ge_B1_false :
  forall x k,
    In k (even_band base_params x) ->
    odd_ge_B1 (B base_params x) k = false.
Proof.
  intros x k Hin.
  unfold odd_ge_B1.
  unfold is_odd.
  rewrite (even_band_even x k Hin).
  reflexivity.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[Two translated Fibonacci bands with base offsets separated by at least `K` are disjoint with a one-step gap. This no-overlap lemma is the quantitative separation fact later reused by higher-level constructions.]]@*)

Lemma filter_false_nil :
  forall (A : Type) (p : A -> bool) (xs : list A),
    (forall a, In a xs -> p a = false) ->
    filter p xs = [].
Proof.
  intros A p xs Hp.
  induction xs as [|a xs IH]; simpl.
  - reflexivity.
  - assert (Ha : p a = false) by (apply Hp; simpl; auto).
    rewrite Ha.
    apply IH.
    intros b Hb.
    apply Hp.
    simpl; auto.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[Dually to `filter_false_nil`, if every list element satisfies the boolean predicate then filtering returns the original list unchanged. This is the positive filter identity used in the concrete split theorems.]]@*)

Lemma filter_true_id :
  forall (A : Type) (p : A -> bool) (xs : list A),
    (forall a, In a xs -> p a = true) ->
    filter p xs = xs.
Proof.
  intros A p xs Hp.
  induction xs as [|a xs IH]; simpl.
  - reflexivity.
  - assert (Ha : p a = true) by (apply Hp; simpl; auto).
    rewrite Ha.
    simpl.
    f_equal.
    apply IH.
    intros b Hb.
    apply Hp.
    simpl; auto.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[The concrete even-split theorem recovers the even band of `x` from the support of the paired code by filtering for even indices. The proof uses the concatenation shape of `Z0 (pair x y)` together with parity separation of the two bands.]]@*)

Corollary Z0_even_split :
  forall x y,
    filter is_even (Z0 (pair base_params x y)) = even_band base_params x.
Proof.
  intros x y.
  rewrite Z0_pair_is_concat.
  rewrite filter_app.
  assert (Hodd_nil : filter is_even (odd_band base_params x y) = []).
  - apply filter_false_nil. intros a Ha. apply (odd_band_even_false x y a); exact Ha.
  - assert (Heven_id :
      filter is_even (even_band base_params x) = even_band base_params x).
    + apply filter_true_id. intros a Ha. apply (even_band_even x a); exact Ha.
    + rewrite Hodd_nil, Heven_id. reflexivity.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[The concrete odd-split theorem recovers the shifted odd band of `y` by filtering the paired support with `odd_ge_B1 (B x)`. This is the companion to `Z0_even_split` and completes the concrete carryless decomposition law.]]@*)

Corollary Z0_odd_split :
  forall x y,
    filter (odd_ge_B1 (B base_params x))
      (Z0 (pair base_params x y)) =
    odd_band base_params x y.
Proof.
  intros x y.
  rewrite Z0_pair_is_concat.
  rewrite filter_app.
  assert (Hodd_id :
    filter (odd_ge_B1 (B base_params x))
      (odd_band base_params x y) =
    odd_band base_params x y).
  - apply filter_true_id. intros a Ha. apply (odd_band_odd_ge_B1_true x y a); exact Ha.
  - assert (Heven_nil :
      filter (odd_ge_B1 (B base_params x)) (even_band base_params x) = []).
    + apply filter_false_nil. intros a Ha. apply (even_band_odd_ge_B1_false x a); exact Ha.
    + rewrite Hodd_id, Heven_nil.
      rewrite app_nil_r.
      reflexivity.
Qed.

(*@box.section@[[Fibonacci-Banded Support Window (R12 helper)]]@*)

(*@c.unicode@[[Support-window translation: `fib_band b x = map (λ j, b + j) (Z0 x)`, i.e. shift every support index by base offset `b`.]]@*)

Definition fib_band (b x : nat) : list nat :=
  map (fun j => b + j) (Z0 x).

(*@c.unicode@[[Rank upper-bound inversion: `∀ x K, x < fib K ⟹ r0 x ≤ K`.]]@*)

Lemma r0_le_of_lt_fib :
  forall x K,
    x < fib K ->
    r0 x <= K.
Proof.
  intros x K Hx.
  destruct (le_gt_dec (r0 x) K) as [Hle | Hgt].
  - exact Hle.
  - exfalso.
    assert (HK : K < r0 x) by lia.
    pose proof (r0_minimal x K HK) as HKmin.
    lia.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[If `x` already lies below `fib K`, then every support index occurring in `Z0 x` also lies below `K`. The lemma turns the global search bound around `r0 x` into a direct bound on individual support members.]]@*)

Lemma Z0_index_lt_of_lt_fib :
  forall x K i,
    x < fib K ->
    In i (Z0 x) ->
    i < K.
Proof.
  intros x K i Hx Hin.
  pose proof (Z0_indices_below_r0 x i Hin) as Hir0.
  pose proof (r0_le_of_lt_fib x K Hx) as Hr0.
  lia.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[Every index occurring in the concrete support `Z0 x` is at least `2`. This is the membership form of canonical lower-boundedness and provides the left edge of the later support-window estimate.]]@*)

Lemma Z0_index_ge_2 :
  forall x i,
    In i (Z0 x) ->
    2 <= i.
Proof.
  intros x i Hin.
  apply all_ge_2_in with (xs := Z0 x).
  - destruct (Z0_valid x) as [_ [_ Hge]].
    exact Hge.
  - exact Hin.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[A translated Fibonacci band `fib_band b x` lies inside the interval `[b + 2, b + K)` whenever `x < fib K`. This window estimate packages the two basic membership bounds needed for band-separation arguments.]]@*)

Lemma fib_band_support_window :
  forall b K x i,
    x < fib K ->
    In i (fib_band b x) ->
    b + 2 <= i /\ i < b + K.
Proof.
  intros b K x i Hx Hin.
  unfold fib_band in Hin.
  apply in_map_iff in Hin.
  destruct Hin as [j [Hij Hj]].
  subst i.
  split.
  - pose proof (Z0_index_ge_2 x j Hj) as Hj2.
    lia.
  - pose proof (Z0_index_lt_of_lt_fib x K j Hx Hj) as HjK.
    lia.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[Two translated Fibonacci bands whose offsets differ by at least `K` cannot overlap and are separated by at least one gap. This quantitative no-overlap lemma is the final support-window fact in the concrete base route.]]@*)

Lemma fib_band_no_overlap :
  forall b1 b2 K x y i1 i2,
    x < fib K ->
    y < fib K ->
    b1 + K <= b2 ->
    In i1 (fib_band b1 x) ->
    In i2 (fib_band b2 y) ->
    i1 + 1 < i2.
Proof.
  intros b1 b2 K x y i1 i2 Hx Hy Hsep Hin1 Hin2.
  pose proof (fib_band_support_window b1 K x i1 Hx Hin1) as [_ Hi1].
  pose proof (fib_band_support_window b2 K y i2 Hy Hin2) as [Hi2 _].
  lia.
Qed.

(*@box.section@[[CONCRETE BASE]]@*)

(*@c.inline@[[The concrete base soundness law states that the local support extractor really sums back to the encoded natural number.]]@*)

Theorem base_Z_sound :
  forall n, sum_fib (Z base_params n) = n.
Proof.
  exact Z0_sound.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[The even split law isolates the even-band support of the left component in a paired value.]]@*)

Theorem base_Z_even_split :
  forall x y,
    filter is_even (Z base_params (pair base_params x y)) =
    even_band base_params x.
Proof.
  exact Z0_even_split.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[The odd split law isolates the odd-band support of the right component relative to the left-band boundary.]]@*)

Theorem base_Z_odd_split :
  forall x y,
    filter (odd_ge_B1 (B base_params x))
      (Z base_params (pair base_params x y)) =
    odd_band base_params x y.
Proof.
  exact Z0_odd_split.
Qed.


(* ---- theories/D001/D001_03__RA.v ---- *)

(*@file@*)
(*@H.1@[[Proofcase / D001 / RA]]@*)
(*@H.2@[[Overview]]@*)
(*@p.l@[[This construction is the foundational arithmetic route of D001.]]@*)
(*@p.l@[[It builds the raw object-language layer on top of the two preceding carryless routes and then fixes the concrete coding of terms, formulas, and finite sequences used throughout the arithmetic and proof-theory folders.]]@*)
(*@p.l@[[Higher schema mapping, public theory interface, and evaluator/provability layers are intentionally placed in later routes.]]@*)
(*@H.2@[[Contents]]@*)
(*@ptoclist.roman@[[SYNTAX]][[Terms and formulas for first-order arithmetic over the symbols `0`, `S`, `+`, and `=`.]]@*)
(*@ptoclist.roman@[[CLOSURE PRINCIPLE]][[Boolean closure checking for terms and formulas together with the sentence predicate used by the package surfaces.]]@*)
(*@ptoclist.roman@[[CONSTRUCTORS]][[Readable formula builders and binder-blocking substitution on the core syntax.]]@*)
(*@ptoclist.roman@[[TERM AND FORMULA CODES]][[Carryless codes for arithmetic terms and formulas using the distinguished local pairing instance.]]@*)
(*@ptoclist.roman@[[CARRYLESS PAIRING]][[Carryless list coding for finite natural-number sequences.]]@*)
(*@ptoclist.roman@[[INJECTIVITY]][[Injectivity of term and formula codes together with tag-level contradictions used by later routes.]]@*)

From D001 Require Export D001_02__Base_Fibonacci.

Local Open Scope nat_scope.

Module BaseR.

  (*@c.inline@[[`base_params` names the concrete carryless instance inherited from the immediate predecessor, fixing the pairing/unpairing backend used in this route.]]@*)

  Definition base_params : Params := D001_02__Base_Fibonacci.base_params.
End BaseR.

(*@box.section@[[SYNTAX]]@*)

(*@c.inline@[[We introduce the raw term and formula constructors of the weak arithmetic floor.]]@*)

Inductive RATerm : Type :=
| RA_Var : nat -> RATerm
| RA_Zero : RATerm
| RA_Succ : RATerm -> RATerm
| RA_Add : RATerm -> RATerm -> RATerm.

(*@c.inline@[[`RAFormula` is the primitive formula grammar for the arithmetic floor. Equality, falsity, implication, conjunction, disjunction, and first-order quantifiers are all represented explicitly so later recursive predicates can inspect formula shape directly.]]@*)

Inductive RAFormula : Type :=
| RA_Eq : RATerm -> RATerm -> RAFormula
| RA_Bot : RAFormula
| RA_Imp : RAFormula -> RAFormula -> RAFormula
| RA_And : RAFormula -> RAFormula -> RAFormula
| RA_Or : RAFormula -> RAFormula -> RAFormula
| RA_Forall : nat -> RAFormula -> RAFormula
| RA_Exists : nat -> RAFormula -> RAFormula.

(*@c.inline@[[The generated equality schemes give structural decidable equality for terms and formulas. Later executable recognizers and injectivity arguments use these comparison principles to reason about the raw syntax.]]@*)

Scheme Equality for RATerm.
Scheme Equality for RAFormula.

(*@c.inline@[[`numeral` embeds a natural number into the arithmetic term language as the expected unary successor tower over `0`. This is the standard numeral constructor used when later schemas substitute concrete parameters into formulas.]]@*)

Fixpoint numeral (n : nat) : RATerm :=
  match n with
  | 0 => RA_Zero
  | S n' => RA_Succ (numeral n')
  end.

(*@c.inline@[[`RA_Not` and `RA_Neq` are derived abbreviations rather than new primitive constructors. Negation is represented as implication into `RA_Bot`, and disequality is then defined as negated equality.]]@*)

Definition RA_Not (phi : RAFormula) : RAFormula :=
  RA_Imp phi RA_Bot.

(*@c.unicode@[[Derived disequality constructor: `RA_Neq s t ≝ ¬(s = t)`, encoded as implication to `RA_Bot`.]]@*)

Definition RA_Neq (s t : RATerm) : RAFormula :=
  RA_Not (RA_Eq s t).

(*@box.section@[[CLOSURE PRINCIPLE]]@*)

(*@c.inline@[[We track free variables through a boolean closure checker and package sentencehood as the closed-at-empty-context predicate.]]@*)

Fixpoint term_closedb_ctx (ctx : list nat) (t : RATerm) : bool :=
  match t with
  | RA_Var x => existsb (Nat.eqb x) ctx
  | RA_Zero => true
  | RA_Succ u => term_closedb_ctx ctx u
  | RA_Add u v => term_closedb_ctx ctx u && term_closedb_ctx ctx v
  end.

(*@c.inline@[[`formula_closedb_ctx` lifts the same closure test from terms to formulas. Quantifiers extend the context by their bound variable, so the boolean check tracks exactly which variables are available as bound names at each recursive position.]]@*)

Fixpoint formula_closedb_ctx (ctx : list nat) (phi : RAFormula) : bool :=
  match phi with
  | RA_Eq s t => term_closedb_ctx ctx s && term_closedb_ctx ctx t
  | RA_Bot => true
  | RA_Imp a b => formula_closedb_ctx ctx a && formula_closedb_ctx ctx b
  | RA_And a b => formula_closedb_ctx ctx a && formula_closedb_ctx ctx b
  | RA_Or a b => formula_closedb_ctx ctx a && formula_closedb_ctx ctx b
  | RA_Forall x psi => formula_closedb_ctx (x :: ctx) psi
  | RA_Exists x psi => formula_closedb_ctx (x :: ctx) psi
  end.

(*@c.inline@[[The package-level sentence test is simply closure at the empty context. The boolean predicate `sentenceb` and its propositional wrapper `Sentence` are the closed-formula notions reused by the later schema and public-interface routes.]]@*)

Definition sentenceb (phi : RAFormula) : bool :=
  formula_closedb_ctx [] phi.

(*@c.unicode@[[Propositional sentencehood wrapper: `Sentence φ` is defined as boolean closure success, i.e. `sentenceb φ = true`.]]@*)

Definition Sentence (phi : RAFormula) : Prop :=
  sentenceb phi = true.

(*@box.section@[[CONSTRUCTORS]]@*)

(*@c.inline@[[These are the core object-language utilities used throughout the later mapping, class, and theory layers. The substitution routine here is binder-blocking only: it stops at a matching binder and does not rename binders to avoid capture.]]@*)

Definition mk_eq (s t : RATerm) : RAFormula := RA_Eq s t.

(*@c.inline@[[Implication smart-constructor alias (object-language implication).]]@*)

(*@c.unicode@[[mk_imp a b ≝ a → b]]@*)

Definition mk_imp (a b : RAFormula) : RAFormula := RA_Imp a b.

(*@c.inline@[[Negation-as-implication helper.]]@*)

(*@c.unicode@[[mk_not φ ≝ φ → ⊥]]@*)

Definition mk_not (phi : RAFormula) : RAFormula := mk_imp phi RA_Bot.

(*@c.inline@[[Derived disequality helper.]]@*)

(*@c.unicode@[[mk_neq s t ≝ ¬(s = t)]]@*)

Definition mk_neq (s t : RATerm) : RAFormula := mk_not (mk_eq s t).

(*@c.inline@[[Derived disequality helper.]]@*)

(*@c.unicode@[[mk_forall x φ ≝ ∀x. φ]]@*)

Definition mk_forall (x : nat) (phi : RAFormula) : RAFormula := RA_Forall x phi.

(*@c.inline@[[Existential binder constructor.]]@*)

(*@c.unicode@[[mk_exists x φ ≝ ∃x. φ]]@*)

Definition mk_exists (x : nat) (phi : RAFormula) : RAFormula := RA_Exists x phi.
