(*@c.inline@[[`mk_forall_chain` folds a list of variables into a left-to-right universal prefix. It is the basic quantifier builder used later when schema instances quantify a fixed parameter context before their main matrix.]]@*)

Fixpoint mk_forall_chain (xs : list nat) (phi : RAFormula) : RAFormula :=
  match xs with
  | [] => phi
  | x :: xs' => mk_forall x (mk_forall_chain xs' phi)
  end.

(*@c.inline@[[`mk_exists_chain` is the existential companion to `mk_forall_chain`. It packages a finite witness list into a nested existential prefix while preserving the list order as the quantifier order.]]@*)

Fixpoint mk_exists_chain (xs : list nat) (phi : RAFormula) : RAFormula :=
  match xs with
  | [] => phi
  | x :: xs' => mk_exists x (mk_exists_chain xs' phi)
  end.

(*@c.inline@[[`subst_term` performs structural substitution on arithmetic terms. Since terms have no binders, the routine simply replaces free occurrences of the chosen variable by the given representative term.]]@*)

Fixpoint subst_term (x : nat) (rep : RATerm) (t : RATerm) : RATerm :=
  match t with
  | RA_Var y => if Nat.eqb x y then rep else t
  | RA_Zero => RA_Zero
  | RA_Succ u => RA_Succ (subst_term x rep u)
  | RA_Add u v => RA_Add (subst_term x rep u) (subst_term x rep v)
  end.

(*@c.inline@[[`subst_formula` lifts term substitution to formulas. At quantifiers it is binder-blocking: when the bound variable matches the substituted variable, the routine leaves the quantified body unchanged instead of renaming binders.]]@*)

Fixpoint subst_formula (x : nat) (rep : RATerm) (phi : RAFormula) : RAFormula :=
  match phi with
  | RA_Eq s t => mk_eq (subst_term x rep s) (subst_term x rep t)
  | RA_Bot => RA_Bot
  | RA_Imp a b => mk_imp (subst_formula x rep a) (subst_formula x rep b)
  | RA_And a b => RA_And (subst_formula x rep a) (subst_formula x rep b)
  | RA_Or a b => RA_Or (subst_formula x rep a) (subst_formula x rep b)
  | RA_Forall y psi =>
      if Nat.eqb x y then RA_Forall y psi else RA_Forall y (subst_formula x rep psi)
  | RA_Exists y psi =>
      if Nat.eqb x y then RA_Exists y psi else RA_Exists y (subst_formula x rep psi)
  end.

(*@box.section@[[TERM AND FORMULA CODES]]@*)

(*@c.inline@[[We code terms and formulas by tagging their top constructor and packing the payload with the distinguished carryless pairing instance.]]@*)

Definition base_pair (a b : nat) : nat :=
  pair BaseR.base_params a b.

(*@c.unicode@[[Decoder wrapper for the fixed backend: `base_unpair : ℕ → ℕ × ℕ` with `base_unpair(base_pair a b) = (a,b)` proven below.]]@*)

Definition base_unpair (n : nat) : nat * nat :=
  unpair BaseR.base_params n.

(*@c.inline@[[The local pairing wrappers isolate the distinguished local carryless realization used throughout this route. The closing lemma records that decoding immediately after encoding recovers the original ordered pair.]]@*)

(*@c.unicode@[[∀x,y, base_unpair(base_pair x y) = (x,y)]]@*)

Lemma base_unpair_pair_closed :
  forall x y,
    base_unpair (base_pair x y) = (x, y).
Proof.
  intros x y.
  unfold base_unpair, base_pair.
  exact
    (unpair_pair
      BaseR.base_params
      base_Z_sound
      base_Z_even_split
      base_Z_odd_split
      x y).
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[`code_tag1` and `code_tag2` are the two basic tagged payload encoders. They reserve the outer successor for the nonzero constructor case and use the first payload component as the constructor tag inspected by later inversion lemmas.]]@*)

Definition code_tag1 (tag a : nat) : nat :=
  S (base_pair tag a).

(*@c.unicode@[[Binary tagged payload encoder: `code_tag2(tag,a,b) = S⟨tag,⟨a,b⟩⟩`, using nested carryless pairing.]]@*)

Definition code_tag2 (tag a b : nat) : nat :=
  S (base_pair tag (base_pair a b)).

(*@c.inline@[[`code_term` assigns a carryless code to each arithmetic term by pairing a constructor tag with the recursively coded payload. The tags separate variables, zero, successor, and addition at the outermost decoding step.]]@*)

Fixpoint code_term (t : RATerm) : nat :=
  match t with
  | RA_Var x => code_tag1 0 x
  | RA_Zero => code_tag1 1 0
  | RA_Succ u => code_tag1 2 (code_term u)
  | RA_Add u v => code_tag2 3 (code_term u) (code_term v)
  end.

(*@c.inline@[[`code_formula` extends the same tagged-coding discipline to formulas. Equality, falsity, binary connectives, and quantifiers each receive a fixed outer tag so later routes can recognize formula shape by arithmetic inspection of the code.]]@*)

Fixpoint code_formula (phi : RAFormula) : nat :=
  match phi with
  | RA_Eq s t => code_tag2 0 (code_term s) (code_term t)
  | RA_Bot => code_tag1 1 0
  | RA_Imp a b => code_tag2 2 (code_formula a) (code_formula b)
  | RA_And a b => code_tag2 3 (code_formula a) (code_formula b)
  | RA_Or a b => code_tag2 4 (code_formula a) (code_formula b)
  | RA_Forall x psi => code_tag2 5 x (code_formula psi)
  | RA_Exists x psi => code_tag2 6 x (code_formula psi)
  end.

(*@box.section@[[CARRYLESS PAIRING]]@*)

(*@c.inline@[[We code finite sequences by repeated head-tail pairing.]]@*)

Fixpoint code_nat_list (xs : list nat) : nat :=
  match xs with
  | [] => 0
  | x :: xs' => S (base_pair x (code_nat_list xs'))
  end.

(*@box.section@[[INJECTIVITY]]@*)

(*@c.inline@[[We prove the coding consequences used by later layers: injectivity of syntax codes and the tag-level contradictions needed to inspect coded formulas.]]@*)

Lemma pair_base_injective :
  forall a b c d,
    base_pair a b = base_pair c d ->
    a = c /\ b = d.
Proof.
  intros a b c d Hpair.
  unfold base_pair in Hpair.
  exact
    (pair_inj
      BaseR.base_params
      base_Z_sound
      base_Z_even_split
      base_Z_odd_split
      a b c d Hpair).
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[Injectivity of the underlying pairing function is the basic arithmetic fact behind all later code inversions. Once paired payloads are recoverable, the tagged syntax encodings inherit constructor-by-constructor injectivity.]]@*)

Lemma code_tag1_injective :
  forall tag a tag' a',
    code_tag1 tag a = code_tag1 tag' a' ->
    tag = tag' /\ a = a'.
Proof.
  intros tag a tag' a' Hcode.
  unfold code_tag1 in Hcode.
  inversion Hcode as [Hpair].
  exact (pair_base_injective _ _ _ _ Hpair).
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[`code_tag2_injective` refines the one-payload case to binary payloads. Equality of two binary tagged codes forces equality of the outer tag and of both recursively packed components.]]@*)

Lemma code_tag2_injective :
  forall tag a b tag' a' b',
    code_tag2 tag a b = code_tag2 tag' a' b' ->
    tag = tag' /\ a = a' /\ b = b'.
Proof.
  intros tag a b tag' a' b' Hcode.
  unfold code_tag2 in Hcode.
  inversion Hcode as [Hpair].
  apply pair_base_injective in Hpair.
  destruct Hpair as [Htag Hpayload].
  apply pair_base_injective in Hpayload.
  destruct Hpayload as [Ha Hb].
  exact (conj Htag (conj Ha Hb)).
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[A unary tagged code cannot coincide with a binary tagged code unless their exposed outer tags already agree. This contradiction lemma is used in later injectivity proofs to eliminate impossible cross-constructor equalities.]]@*)


Lemma code_tag1_code_tag2_tag :
  forall tag a tag' b c,
    code_tag1 tag a = code_tag2 tag' b c ->
    tag = tag'.
Proof.
  intros tag a tag' b c Hcode.
  unfold code_tag1, code_tag2 in Hcode.
  inversion Hcode as [Hpair].
  apply pair_base_injective in Hpair.
  exact (proj1 Hpair).
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[This is the symmetric mixed-tag contradiction for a binary code equated with a unary code. Together with the previous lemma, it lets the structural injectivity arguments discard constructor clashes in either order.]]@*)

Lemma code_tag2_code_tag1_tag :
  forall tag a b tag' c,
    code_tag2 tag a b = code_tag1 tag' c ->
    tag = tag'.
Proof.
  intros tag a b tag' c Hcode.
  unfold code_tag1, code_tag2 in Hcode.
  inversion Hcode as [Hpair].
  apply pair_base_injective in Hpair.
  exact (proj1 Hpair).
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[`code_tag1_tag` and `code_tag2_tag` project out only the outer constructor tag from a same-arity code equality. They are the discriminators used to close the off-diagonal cases in the structural injectivity proofs: once the tag equality is isolated, a single `discriminate` closes the goal.]]@*)

Lemma code_tag1_tag :
  forall tag a tag' a',
    code_tag1 tag a = code_tag1 tag' a' ->
    tag = tag'.
Proof.
  intros tag a tag' a' H.
  exact (proj1 (code_tag1_injective _ _ _ _ H)).
Qed.

Lemma code_tag2_tag :
  forall tag a b tag' a' b',
    code_tag2 tag a b = code_tag2 tag' a' b' ->
    tag = tag'.
Proof.
  intros tag a b tag' a' b' H.
  exact (proj1 (code_tag2_injective _ _ _ _ _ _ H)).
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[The term code is injective because each constructor carries a distinct outer tag and the carryless pairing payload is itself injective. We therefore recover term equality by a straightforward structural case split on both terms.]]@*)

(*@c.unicode@[[code_term(s) = code_term(t) ⇒ s = t]]@*)

Theorem code_term_injective :
  forall s t,
    code_term s = code_term t ->
    s = t.
Proof.
  induction s as [x| |u IHu|u IHu v IHv]; intros t Hcode; destruct t as [y| |w|w z].
  - destruct (code_tag1_injective _ _ _ _ Hcode) as [_ Hxy]. subst y. reflexivity.
  - discriminate (code_tag1_tag _ _ _ _ Hcode).
  - discriminate (code_tag1_tag _ _ _ _ Hcode).
  - discriminate (code_tag1_code_tag2_tag _ _ _ _ _ Hcode).
  - discriminate (code_tag1_tag _ _ _ _ Hcode).
  - reflexivity.
  - discriminate (code_tag1_tag _ _ _ _ Hcode).
  - discriminate (code_tag1_code_tag2_tag _ _ _ _ _ Hcode).
  - discriminate (code_tag1_tag _ _ _ _ Hcode).
  - discriminate (code_tag1_tag _ _ _ _ Hcode).
  - destruct (code_tag1_injective _ _ _ _ Hcode) as [_ Huw].
    f_equal. exact (IHu w Huw).
  - discriminate (code_tag1_code_tag2_tag _ _ _ _ _ Hcode).
  - discriminate (code_tag2_code_tag1_tag _ _ _ _ _ Hcode).
  - discriminate (code_tag2_code_tag1_tag _ _ _ _ _ Hcode).
  - discriminate (code_tag2_code_tag1_tag _ _ _ _ _ Hcode).
  - destruct (code_tag2_injective _ _ _ _ _ _ Hcode) as [_ [Huw Hvz]].
    f_equal.
    + exact (IHu w Huw).
    + exact (IHv z Hvz).
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[Formula coding is injective for the same reason as term coding: every outer constructor carries its own tag, and equal payload codes force equality of the recursively embedded terms or subformulas.]]@*)

(*@c.unicode@[[code_formula(φ) = code_formula(ψ) ⇒ φ = ψ]]@*)

Theorem code_formula_injective :
  forall phi psi,
    code_formula phi = code_formula psi ->
    phi = psi.
Proof.
  induction phi as
      [s t| |a IHa b IHb|a IHa b IHb|a IHa b IHb
      |x body IHbody|x body IHbody];
    intros psi Hcode;
    destruct psi as [s' t'| |a' b'|a' b'|a' b'|y psi'|y psi'].
  - destruct (code_tag2_injective _ _ _ _ _ _ Hcode) as [_ [Hs Ht]].
    apply code_term_injective in Hs. apply code_term_injective in Ht. subst. reflexivity.
  - discriminate (code_tag2_code_tag1_tag _ _ _ _ _ Hcode).
  - discriminate (code_tag2_tag _ _ _ _ _ _ Hcode).
  - discriminate (code_tag2_tag _ _ _ _ _ _ Hcode).
  - discriminate (code_tag2_tag _ _ _ _ _ _ Hcode).
  - discriminate (code_tag2_tag _ _ _ _ _ _ Hcode).
  - discriminate (code_tag2_tag _ _ _ _ _ _ Hcode).
  - discriminate (code_tag1_code_tag2_tag _ _ _ _ _ Hcode).
  - reflexivity.
  - discriminate (code_tag1_code_tag2_tag _ _ _ _ _ Hcode).
  - discriminate (code_tag1_code_tag2_tag _ _ _ _ _ Hcode).
  - discriminate (code_tag1_code_tag2_tag _ _ _ _ _ Hcode).
  - discriminate (code_tag1_code_tag2_tag _ _ _ _ _ Hcode).
  - discriminate (code_tag1_code_tag2_tag _ _ _ _ _ Hcode).
  - discriminate (code_tag2_tag _ _ _ _ _ _ Hcode).
  - discriminate (code_tag2_code_tag1_tag _ _ _ _ _ Hcode).
  - destruct (code_tag2_injective _ _ _ _ _ _ Hcode) as [_ [Ha Hb]]. f_equal.
    + exact (IHa a' Ha).
    + exact (IHb b' Hb).
  - discriminate (code_tag2_tag _ _ _ _ _ _ Hcode).
  - discriminate (code_tag2_tag _ _ _ _ _ _ Hcode).
  - discriminate (code_tag2_tag _ _ _ _ _ _ Hcode).
  - discriminate (code_tag2_tag _ _ _ _ _ _ Hcode).
  - discriminate (code_tag2_tag _ _ _ _ _ _ Hcode).
  - discriminate (code_tag2_code_tag1_tag _ _ _ _ _ Hcode).
  - discriminate (code_tag2_tag _ _ _ _ _ _ Hcode).
  - destruct (code_tag2_injective _ _ _ _ _ _ Hcode) as [_ [Ha Hb]]. f_equal.
    + exact (IHa a' Ha).
    + exact (IHb b' Hb).
  - discriminate (code_tag2_tag _ _ _ _ _ _ Hcode).
  - discriminate (code_tag2_tag _ _ _ _ _ _ Hcode).
  - discriminate (code_tag2_tag _ _ _ _ _ _ Hcode).
  - discriminate (code_tag2_tag _ _ _ _ _ _ Hcode).
  - discriminate (code_tag2_code_tag1_tag _ _ _ _ _ Hcode).
  - discriminate (code_tag2_tag _ _ _ _ _ _ Hcode).
  - discriminate (code_tag2_tag _ _ _ _ _ _ Hcode).
  - destruct (code_tag2_injective _ _ _ _ _ _ Hcode) as [_ [Ha Hb]]. f_equal.
    + exact (IHa a' Ha).
    + exact (IHb b' Hb).
  - discriminate (code_tag2_tag _ _ _ _ _ _ Hcode).
  - discriminate (code_tag2_tag _ _ _ _ _ _ Hcode).
  - discriminate (code_tag2_tag _ _ _ _ _ _ Hcode).
  - discriminate (code_tag2_code_tag1_tag _ _ _ _ _ Hcode).
  - discriminate (code_tag2_tag _ _ _ _ _ _ Hcode).
  - discriminate (code_tag2_tag _ _ _ _ _ _ Hcode).
  - discriminate (code_tag2_tag _ _ _ _ _ _ Hcode).
  - destruct (code_tag2_injective _ _ _ _ _ _ Hcode) as [_ [Hx Hbody]].
    subst y. f_equal. exact (IHbody psi' Hbody).
  - discriminate (code_tag2_tag _ _ _ _ _ _ Hcode).
  - discriminate (code_tag2_tag _ _ _ _ _ _ Hcode).
  - discriminate (code_tag2_code_tag1_tag _ _ _ _ _ Hcode).
  - discriminate (code_tag2_tag _ _ _ _ _ _ Hcode).
  - discriminate (code_tag2_tag _ _ _ _ _ _ Hcode).
  - discriminate (code_tag2_tag _ _ _ _ _ _ Hcode).
  - discriminate (code_tag2_tag _ _ _ _ _ _ Hcode).
  - destruct (code_tag2_injective _ _ _ _ _ _ Hcode) as [_ [Hx Hbody]].
    subst y. f_equal. exact (IHbody psi' Hbody).
Qed.


(* ---- theories/D001/D001_04__Mapping.v ---- *)

(*@file@*)
(*@H.1@[[Proofcase / D001 / Mapping]]@*)
(*@H.2@[[Overview]]@*)
(*@p.l@[[This construction contains the coding/mapping layer built on top of `D001_03__RA`.]]@*)
(*@p.l@[[It now owns the coded-sequence helper surface, the one-hole implication mapping machinery, and the executable code-level substitution layer.]]@*)

From D001 Require Export D001_03__RA.

(*@c.inline@[[We keep the inherited carryless realization sealed in this route. The local proofs use only its algebraic interface lemmas.]]@*)

Local Opaque base_pair base_unpair.

Local Open Scope nat_scope.

(*@c.inline@[[This layer treats formula and proof codes simply as naturals and records the minimal semantic predicate saying that a natural number actually arises as the code of some arithmetic formula.]]@*)

Definition SentenceCode : Type := nat.

(*@c.inline@[[ProofCode ≡ ℕ]]@*)

Definition ProofCode : Type := nat.

(*@c.unicode@[[FormPred(u) :≡ ∃ φ : RAFormula, u = code_formula φ]]@*)

Definition FormPred (u : SentenceCode) : Prop :=
  exists phi : RAFormula, u = code_formula phi.

(*@c.inline@[[`FormPred` is the minimal semantic predicate on sentence codes used in this route: a natural number satisfies it exactly when it is the carryless code of some arithmetic formula.]]@*)

Theorem code_formula_is_formpred :
  forall phi,
    FormPred (code_formula phi).
Proof.
  intro phi.
  exists phi.
  reflexivity.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[`coded_list_head` and `coded_list_tail` are the primitive destructors for the carryless list encoding inherited from `D001_03__RA`. They expose the first entry and the remaining coded tail, with `0` serving as the empty-list code.]]@*)

Definition coded_list_head (n : nat) : nat :=
  match n with
  | 0 => 0
  | S k => fst (base_unpair k)
  end.

(*@c.unicode@[[coded_list_tail 0 = 0` and `coded_list_tail (S k) = snd (base_unpair k)]]@*)

Definition coded_list_tail (n : nat) : nat :=
  match n with
  | 0 => 0
  | S k => snd (base_unpair k)
  end.

(*@c.inline@[[`coded_nth` iterates the coded head-tail decomposition to read the `i`th entry of a coded finite list. Later code-level constructions use it to inspect formula and proof sequences without decoding them into Gallina lists first.]]@*)

Fixpoint coded_nth (i n : nat) : nat :=
  match i with
  | 0 => coded_list_head n
  | S i' => coded_nth i' (coded_list_tail n)
  end.

(*@c.inline@[[The first destructor lemma states that coding a nonempty list and then reading its head recovers the original first entry. This is the basic computational fact behind later sequence-index arguments.]]@*)

Theorem coded_list_cons_head :
  forall x xs,
    coded_list_head (code_nat_list (x :: xs)) = x.
Proof.
  intros x xs.
  change
    (fst
      (base_unpair
        (base_pair x (code_nat_list xs))) = x).
  rewrite (base_unpair_pair_closed x (code_nat_list xs)).
  reflexivity.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[The companion tail lemma shows that the coded remainder of a nonempty list is exactly the code of its Gallina tail. Together with the head lemma, it gives a complete one-step decomposition of coded cons cells.]]@*)

Theorem coded_list_cons_tail :
  forall x xs,
    coded_list_tail (code_nat_list (x :: xs)) = code_nat_list xs.
Proof.
  intros x xs.
  change
    (snd
      (base_unpair
        (base_pair x (code_nat_list xs))) =
     code_nat_list xs).
  rewrite (base_unpair_pair_closed x (code_nat_list xs)).
  reflexivity.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[We add a code-level substitution chain for one-hole implication schemas.]]@*)

Inductive OForm : Type :=
| OBot : OForm
| OHole : OForm
| OImp : OForm -> OForm -> OForm.

(*@c.inline@[[`OForm` is the one-hole implication grammar used for code-level substitution experiments in this route. It separates the distinguished hole from the implication skeleton so that closing and substitution can be defined by structural recursion.]]@*)

Fixpoint osize (phi : OForm) : nat :=
  match phi with
  | OBot => 1
  | OHole => 1
  | OImp a b => S (osize a + osize b)
  end.

(*@c.inline@[[`osize` is a simple structural size measure on one-hole forms. It supplies a lightweight complexity parameter for later recursive arguments over the implication skeleton.]]@*)

Definition enc_form (phi : RAFormula) : nat := code_formula phi.

(*@c.inline@[[`enc_form` reuses the arithmetic formula coding from `D001_03__RA` without adding new structure. This alias keeps the one-hole mapping vocabulary separate from the raw coding vocabulary while preserving definitional transparency.]]@*)

Definition oform_tag_bot : nat := 70.

(*@c.inline@[[Reserved constructor tag for the one-hole atom `□` (hole node).]]@*)

Definition oform_tag_hole : nat := 71.

(*@c.inline@[[Reserved constructor tag for one-hole implication nodes (`OImp`).]]@*)

Definition oform_tag_imp : nat := 72.

(*@c.inline@[[The three `OForm` tags are chosen outside the arithmetic formula tag range so that one-hole skeleton codes stay disjoint from ordinary object-language formula codes. This separation is what makes later decoding and non-collision lemmas straightforward.]]@*)

Fixpoint enc_oform (phi : OForm) : nat :=
  match phi with
  | OBot => code_tag1 oform_tag_bot 0
  | OHole => code_tag1 oform_tag_hole 0
  | OImp a b => code_tag2 oform_tag_imp (enc_oform a) (enc_oform b)
  end.

(*@c.inline@[[`enc_oform` assigns a dedicated carryless code to each one-hole implication skeleton. Because the coding is structural and tag-separated, later lemmas can decode implication nodes without confusing them with arithmetic formulas.]]@*)


Fixpoint close_oform (phi : OForm) (theta : RAFormula) : RAFormula :=
  match phi with
  | OBot => RA_Bot
  | OHole => theta
  | OImp a b => RA_Imp (close_oform a theta) (close_oform b theta)
  end.

(*@c.inline@[[`close_oform` plugs an arithmetic formula into the distinguished hole of a one-hole skeleton. Semantically, it interprets `OForm` as a schematic implication context over the object-language formulas of `D001_03__RA`.]]@*)

Fixpoint subst_oform (phi : OForm) (t : nat) : nat :=
  match phi with
  | OBot => enc_form RA_Bot
  | OHole => t
  | OImp a b => code_tag2 2 (subst_oform a t) (subst_oform b t)
  end.

(*@c.inline@[[`subst_oform` is the code-level analogue of `close_oform`: it plugs a formula code into a coded one-hole skeleton by rebuilding the implication tags recursively. The closing theorem below shows that this numeric substitution agrees with syntactic closing followed by ordinary formula coding.]]@*)

Theorem close_oform_enc :
  forall phi theta,
    enc_form (close_oform phi theta) = subst_oform phi (enc_form theta).
Proof.
  intros phi theta.
  induction phi as [| |a IHa b IHb].
  - reflexivity.
  - reflexivity.
  - simpl.
    rewrite IHa.
    rewrite IHb.
    reflexivity.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[The closing theorem is the key coherence statement for the one-hole layer: syntactic closing followed by formula coding agrees exactly with direct numeric substitution into the coded skeleton.]]@*)

Definition ClosedCode (t : nat) : Prop :=
  exists theta : RAFormula, enc_form theta = t.

(*@c.inline@[[`ClosedCode` is the existential semantic predicate saying that a natural number is realized by some arithmetic formula code. It is the closure notion preserved by the one-hole substitution operation.]]@*)

Lemma subst_oform_preserves_closed :
  forall phi t,
    ClosedCode t ->
    ClosedCode (subst_oform phi t).
Proof.
  intros phi t Hclosed.
  destruct Hclosed as [theta Htheta].
  exists (close_oform phi theta).
  rewrite close_oform_enc.
  rewrite Htheta.
  reflexivity.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[`decode_oform1` is a one-step partial decoder for implication skeletons. It recognizes only the implication tag and, when successful, returns the two immediate coded subcontexts packed in the payload. One-step implication decoder contract: decode_oform1(c) = Some(l,r) iff `c` is an encoded `OImp` node whose immediate subcodes are `(l,r)`; otherwise it returns `None`.]]@*)

Definition decode_oform1 (n : nat) : option (nat * nat) :=
  match n with
  | 0 => None
  | S k =>
      let p := base_unpair k in
      let tag := fst p in
      let payload := snd p in
      if Nat.eqb tag oform_tag_imp
      then
        let q := base_unpair payload in
        Some (fst q, snd q)
      else None
  end.

(*@c.inline@[[The decoding lemma shows that `decode_oform1` is correct on genuine implication nodes: encoding an `OImp` and immediately decoding it recovers exactly the two coded immediate subcontexts.]]@*)

Lemma decode_oform1_enc_imp :
  forall a b,
    decode_oform1 (enc_oform (OImp a b)) = Some (enc_oform a, enc_oform b).
Proof.
  intros a b.
  cbn [decode_oform1 enc_oform code_tag2].
  rewrite (base_unpair_pair_closed oform_tag_imp (base_pair (enc_oform a) (enc_oform b))).
  cbn [fst snd].
  rewrite Nat.eqb_refl.
  rewrite (base_unpair_pair_closed (enc_oform a) (enc_oform b)).
  reflexivity.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[The first non-collision lemma separates the two nullary one-hole constructors. Distinct reserved tags ensure that the code for `OBot` can never be mistaken for the code for `OHole`.]]@*)

Lemma enc_oform_bot_not_hole :
  enc_oform OBot <> enc_oform OHole.
Proof.
  intro Henc.
  cbn [enc_oform code_tag1] in Henc.
  apply code_tag1_injective in Henc.
  destruct Henc as [Htag _].
  unfold oform_tag_bot, oform_tag_hole in Htag.
  discriminate Htag.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[This symmetric non-collision statement is recorded separately so later case analyses can use the direction that matches the ambient equality without an extra symmetry step.]]@*)

Lemma enc_oform_hole_not_bot :
  enc_oform OHole <> enc_oform OBot.
Proof.
  intro Henc.
  apply enc_oform_bot_not_hole.
  symmetry.
  exact Henc.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[This lemma rules out confusion between an implication skeleton and the bottom skeleton. The disjoint outer tags make the contradiction immediate once the encoded equality is reduced to a tag equality.]]@*)

Lemma enc_oform_imp_not_bot :
  forall a b,
    enc_oform (OImp a b) <> enc_oform OBot.
Proof.
  intros a b Henc.
  cbn [enc_oform code_tag2] in Henc.
  pose proof
    (code_tag2_code_tag1_tag
      oform_tag_imp
      (enc_oform a)
      (enc_oform b)
      oform_tag_bot
      0
      Henc) as Htag.
  unfold oform_tag_imp, oform_tag_bot in Htag.
  discriminate Htag.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[This is the analogous separation lemma for implication versus hole. Together with the previous non-collision facts, it shows that the three one-hole constructors remain distinguishable at the code level.]]@*)

Lemma enc_oform_imp_not_hole :
  forall a b,
    enc_oform (OImp a b) <> enc_oform OHole.
Proof.
  intros a b Henc.
  cbn [enc_oform code_tag2] in Henc.
  pose proof
    (code_tag2_code_tag1_tag
      oform_tag_imp
      (enc_oform a)
      (enc_oform b)
      oform_tag_hole
      0
      Henc) as Htag.
  unfold oform_tag_imp, oform_tag_hole in Htag.
  discriminate Htag.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[The boolean non-equality corollary packages the preceding structural separation into the `Nat.eqb` form used by later decidable case analyses over coded one-hole contexts.]]@*)

Lemma enc_oform_hole_ne_botb :
  Nat.eqb (enc_oform OHole) (enc_oform OBot) = false.
Proof.
  apply Nat.eqb_neq.
  exact enc_oform_hole_not_bot.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[This boolean corollary exposes the implication-versus-bottom separation in the decidable equality form needed by the executable substitution checker.]]@*)

Lemma enc_oform_imp_ne_botb :
  forall a b,
    Nat.eqb (enc_oform (OImp a b)) (enc_oform OBot) = false.
Proof.
  intros a b.
  apply Nat.eqb_neq.
  apply enc_oform_imp_not_bot.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[This is the corresponding boolean inequality for implication versus hole. The trio of `Nat.eqb = false` lemmas gives the evaluator-facing API for ruling out malformed one-hole codes.]]@*)

Lemma enc_oform_imp_ne_holeb :
  forall a b,
    Nat.eqb (enc_oform (OImp a b)) (enc_oform OHole) = false.
Proof.
  intros a b.
  apply Nat.eqb_neq.
  apply enc_oform_imp_not_hole.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[`subst_code_fuel` is the executable, fuel-bounded decoder-substituter for coded one-hole formulas. It inspects the outer one-hole code, rebuilds implication nodes recursively when decoding succeeds, and falls back to `RA_Bot` when the supplied fuel is exhausted or the code is malformed.]]@*)

Fixpoint subst_code_fuel (fuel code t : nat) : nat :=
  match fuel with
  | 0 => enc_form RA_Bot
  | S fuel' =>
      if Nat.eqb code (enc_oform OBot)
      then enc_form RA_Bot
      else
        if Nat.eqb code (enc_oform OHole)
        then t
        else
          match decode_oform1 code with
          | Some (left_code, right_code) =>
              code_tag2 2
                (subst_code_fuel fuel' left_code t)
                (subst_code_fuel fuel' right_code t)
          | None => enc_form RA_Bot
          end
  end.

(*@c.inline@[[`subst_code_fuel_correct` is the main adequacy theorem for the executable code-level substituter. As soon as the available fuel dominates the structural size `osize phi`, numeric substitution on `enc_oform phi` agrees exactly with the abstract structural operation `subst_oform phi`.]]@*)

(*@c.unicode@[[osize(φ) ≤ fuel ⇒ subst_code_fuel(fuel, enc_oform(φ), t) = subst_oform(φ, t).]]@*)

Theorem subst_code_fuel_correct :
  forall phi fuel t,
    osize phi <= fuel ->
    subst_code_fuel fuel (enc_oform phi) t = subst_oform phi t.
Proof.

(*@c.inline@[[We first prove the exact computation with an arbitrary amount of extra fuel. This mirrors the recursive shape of [subst_code_fuel] and keeps the later general theorem as a short arithmetic corollary.]]@*)

  assert
    (Hslack :
      forall phi extra t,
        subst_code_fuel (osize phi + extra) (enc_oform phi) t =
        subst_oform phi t).
  {
    intro phi.
    induction phi as [| |a IHa b IHb]; intros extra t.
    - cbn [osize].
      change
        (subst_code_fuel (S extra) (enc_oform OBot) t =
         subst_oform OBot t).
      cbn [subst_code_fuel subst_oform].
      rewrite Nat.eqb_refl.
      reflexivity.
    - cbn [osize].
      change
        (subst_code_fuel (S extra) (enc_oform OHole) t =
         subst_oform OHole t).
      cbn [subst_code_fuel subst_oform].
      rewrite enc_oform_hole_ne_botb.
      rewrite Nat.eqb_refl.
      reflexivity.
    - cbn [osize].
      change
        (subst_code_fuel
           (S (osize a + osize b + extra))
           (enc_oform (OImp a b))
           t =
         subst_oform (OImp a b) t).
      cbn [subst_code_fuel subst_oform].
      rewrite enc_oform_imp_ne_botb.
      rewrite enc_oform_imp_ne_holeb.
      rewrite decode_oform1_enc_imp.
      rewrite <- Nat.add_assoc.
      rewrite (IHa (osize b + extra) t).
      replace (osize a + (osize b + extra))
        with (osize b + (osize a + extra)) by lia.
      rewrite (IHb (osize a + extra) t).
      reflexivity.
  }
  intros phi fuel t Hfuel.
  replace fuel with (osize phi + (fuel - osize phi)) by lia.
  apply Hslack.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[The general correctness theorem says that enough fuel recovers the intended code-level substitution exactly on genuine one-hole contexts. The proof separates the structural recursion from the final arithmetic bound by proving a slack-fuel version first.]]@*)

Theorem subst_code_correct :
  forall phi t,
    subst_code_fuel (osize phi) (enc_oform phi) t = subst_oform phi t.
Proof.
  intros phi t.
  apply subst_code_fuel_correct.
  lia.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[The closedness corollary transports the previous correctness result back to the semantic closure predicate on codes. Substituting a closed formula code into a genuine one-hole context therefore produces another closed formula code.]]@*)

Theorem subst_code_preserves_closed :
  forall phi t,
    ClosedCode t ->
    ClosedCode (subst_code_fuel (osize phi) (enc_oform phi) t).
Proof.
  intros phi t Hclosed.
  rewrite subst_code_correct.
  apply subst_oform_preserves_closed.
  exact Hclosed.
Qed.


(* ---- theories/D001/D001_05__Classes.v ---- *)

(*@file@*)
(*@H.1@[[Proofcase / D001 / Classes]]@*)
(*@H.2@[[Overview]]@*)
(*@p.l@[[This construction owns the bounded formula-class layer for D001.]]@*)
(*@p.l@[[It stays entirely on the object-language side: bounded guards, bounded-quantifier builders, and the context-sensitive `Delta_0` / `Sigma_1` classes over `{0, S, +, =}`.]]@*)
(*@p.l@[[It is an internal class layer; the single downstream arithmetic interface is `D001_14__RA_Language`.]]@*)
(*@H.2@[[Contents]]@*)
(*@ptoclist.roman@[[BOUNDED QUANTIFIER]][[Canonical bounded-guard shape together with reusable bounded universal/existential builders.]]@*)
(*@ptoclist.roman@[[DELTA-0 ON CONTEXT]][[Context-sensitive semantic and recursive `Delta_0` predicates.]]@*)
(*@ptoclist.roman@[[SIGMA-1 ON CONTEXT]][[Context-sensitive semantic and recursive `Sigma_1` predicates, then the closed public aliases.]]@*)

From D001 Require Export D001_04__Mapping.

Local Open Scope nat_scope.

(*@box.section@[[BOUNDED QUANTIFIER]]@*)

(*@c.inline@[[A bounded guard `x <= t` is represented in the primitive language `{0, S, +, =}` by an existential witness for the additive difference between `t` and `x`.]]@*)

Definition leq_atom (x witness : nat) (bound : RATerm) : RAFormula :=
  mk_exists witness
    (mk_eq bound (RA_Add (RA_Var x) (RA_Var witness))).

(*@c.inline@[[Bounded universal macro: `bounded_forall x w t φ ≝ ∀x.(x ≤ t → φ)`, where `x ≤ t` is encoded by `leq_atom`.]]@*)

Definition bounded_forall
    (x witness : nat) (bound : RATerm) (body : RAFormula) : RAFormula :=
  mk_forall x (mk_imp (leq_atom x witness bound) body).

(*@c.inline@[[Bounded existential macro: `bounded_exists x w t φ ≝ ∃x.(x ≤ t ∧ φ)`, with the same arithmetic guard encoding.]]@*)

Definition bounded_exists
    (x witness : nat) (bound : RATerm) (body : RAFormula) : RAFormula :=
  mk_exists x (RA_And (leq_atom x witness bound) body).

(*@c.inline@[[Extending a context cannot destroy a successful boolean membership test. This is the one-step weakening fact for `existsb (Nat.eqb z)`, and it is the atomic ingredient behind all later closure-weakening lemmas.]]@*)

Lemma existsb_nat_eq_weaken :
  forall z x ctx,
    existsb (Nat.eqb z) ctx = true ->
    existsb (Nat.eqb z) (x :: ctx) = true.
Proof.
  intros z x ctx H.
  simpl.
  destruct (Nat.eqb z x); simpl; try reflexivity.
  exact H.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[Duplicating the same context variable has no effect on the membership test. The closure check therefore depends on whether a variable occurs in the context, not on how many times it occurs consecutively.]]@*)

Lemma existsb_nat_eq_dup :
  forall z x ctx,
    existsb (Nat.eqb z) (x :: x :: ctx) = existsb (Nat.eqb z) (x :: ctx).
Proof.
  intros z x ctx.
  simpl.
  destruct (Nat.eqb z x); reflexivity.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[Boolean context membership is invariant under permutation of the context list. This packages the fact that closure depends only on which variables are present, not on the order in which they are stored.]]@*)

Lemma existsb_nat_eq_perm :
  forall z ctx1 ctx2,
    Permutation ctx1 ctx2 ->
    existsb (Nat.eqb z) ctx1 = existsb (Nat.eqb z) ctx2.
Proof.
  intros z ctx1 ctx2 Hperm.
  induction Hperm; simpl.
  - reflexivity.
  - rewrite IHHperm.
    reflexivity.
  - destruct (Nat.eqb z x); destruct (Nat.eqb z y); reflexivity.
  - rewrite IHHperm1, IHHperm2.
    reflexivity.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[Closure of a term is monotone under one-step context extension. Once all variables of `t` are recognized in `ctx`, they remain recognized after adding any extra variable to the front.]]@*)

Lemma term_closedb_ctx_weaken :
  forall ctx t x,
    term_closedb_ctx ctx t = true ->
    term_closedb_ctx (x :: ctx) t = true.
Proof.
  induction t as [y| |u IHu|u IHu v IHv]; intros x Hclosed; simpl in *.
  - apply existsb_nat_eq_weaken.
    exact Hclosed.
  - reflexivity.
  - apply IHu.
    exact Hclosed.
  - apply andb_true_iff in Hclosed.
    destruct Hclosed as [Hu Hv].
    rewrite (IHu x Hu).
    rewrite (IHv x Hv).
    reflexivity.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[The term-closure test is likewise stable under extension by a whole prefix of extra context variables. This iterated weakening form is the convenient version for later bounded-quantifier arguments.]]@*)

Lemma term_closedb_ctx_weaken_many :
  forall extra ctx t,
    term_closedb_ctx ctx t = true ->
    term_closedb_ctx (extra ++ ctx) t = true.
Proof.
  induction extra as [|x extra IH]; intros ctx t Hclosed.
  - exact Hclosed.
  - simpl.
    apply term_closedb_ctx_weaken.
    apply IH.
    exact Hclosed.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[Duplicating the same variable in the context does not change the outcome of the term-closure checker. The boolean test depends only on presence of admissible variables, not on repeated occurrences.]]@*)

Lemma term_closedb_ctx_dup :
  forall ctx x t,
    term_closedb_ctx (x :: x :: ctx) t = term_closedb_ctx (x :: ctx) t.
Proof.
  induction t as [y| |u IHu|u IHu v IHv]; intros; simpl.
  - apply existsb_nat_eq_dup.
  - reflexivity.
  - apply IHu.
  - rewrite IHu, IHv.
    reflexivity.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[Term closure is invariant under permutation of the context list. This makes explicit that the checker treats the context extensionally as a finite set of available variables.]]@*)

Lemma term_closedb_ctx_perm :
  forall ctx1 ctx2 t,
    Permutation ctx1 ctx2 ->
    term_closedb_ctx ctx1 t = term_closedb_ctx ctx2 t.
Proof.
  intros ctx1 ctx2 t Hperm.
  induction t as [x| |u IHu|u IHu v IHv]; simpl.
  - apply existsb_nat_eq_perm.
    exact Hperm.
  - reflexivity.
  - exact IHu.
  - rewrite IHu, IHv.
    reflexivity.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[The same extensionality principle lifts from terms to formulas. Reordering the ambient context leaves formula closure unchanged because each recursive clause only queries variable membership.]]@*)

Lemma formula_closedb_ctx_perm :
  forall ctx1 ctx2 phi,
    Permutation ctx1 ctx2 ->
    formula_closedb_ctx ctx1 phi = formula_closedb_ctx ctx2 phi.
Proof.
  intros ctx1 ctx2 phi.
  revert ctx1 ctx2.
  induction phi as
      [s t| |a IHa b IHb|a IHa b IHb|a IHa b IHb
      |x psi IHpsi|x psi IHpsi];
    intros ctx1 ctx2 Hperm; simpl.
  - rewrite (term_closedb_ctx_perm _ _ s Hperm).
    rewrite (term_closedb_ctx_perm _ _ t Hperm).
    reflexivity.
  - reflexivity.
  - rewrite (IHa _ _ Hperm), (IHb _ _ Hperm).
    reflexivity.
  - rewrite (IHa _ _ Hperm), (IHb _ _ Hperm).
    reflexivity.
  - rewrite (IHa _ _ Hperm), (IHb _ _ Hperm).
    reflexivity.
  - apply IHpsi.
    now apply perm_skip.
  - apply IHpsi.
    now apply perm_skip.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[Formula closure is monotone under one-step context extension. A formula already closed relative to `ctx` stays closed after adding any further available variable to that context.]]@*)

Lemma formula_closedb_ctx_weaken :
  forall ctx phi x,
    formula_closedb_ctx ctx phi = true ->
    formula_closedb_ctx (x :: ctx) phi = true.
Proof.
  intros ctx phi.
  revert ctx.
  induction phi as
      [s t| |a IHa b IHb|a IHa b IHb|a IHa b IHb
      |y psi IHpsi|y psi IHpsi];
    intros ctx x Hclosed; simpl in *.
  - apply andb_true_iff in Hclosed.
    destruct Hclosed as [Hs Ht].
    rewrite (term_closedb_ctx_weaken _ _ x Hs).
    rewrite (term_closedb_ctx_weaken _ _ x Ht).
    reflexivity.
  - reflexivity.
  - apply andb_true_iff in Hclosed.
    destruct Hclosed as [Ha Hb].
    rewrite (IHa ctx x Ha), (IHb ctx x Hb).
    reflexivity.
  - apply andb_true_iff in Hclosed.
    destruct Hclosed as [Ha Hb].
    rewrite (IHa ctx x Ha), (IHb ctx x Hb).
    reflexivity.
  - apply andb_true_iff in Hclosed.
    destruct Hclosed as [Ha Hb].
    rewrite (IHa ctx x Ha), (IHb ctx x Hb).
    reflexivity.
  - pose proof (IHpsi (y :: ctx) x Hclosed) as Htmp.
    pose proof
      (formula_closedb_ctx_perm
        (x :: y :: ctx) (y :: x :: ctx) psi (perm_swap y x ctx))
      as Hperm.
    rewrite <- Hperm.
    exact Htmp.
  - pose proof (IHpsi (y :: ctx) x Hclosed) as Htmp.
    pose proof
      (formula_closedb_ctx_perm
        (x :: y :: ctx) (y :: x :: ctx) psi (perm_swap y x ctx))
      as Hperm.
    rewrite <- Hperm.
    exact Htmp.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[This is the iterated weakening form for formulas. It lets later proofs add an arbitrary prefix of helper variables to the context without reopening the structural closure recursion.]]@*)

Lemma formula_closedb_ctx_weaken_many :
  forall extra ctx phi,
    formula_closedb_ctx ctx phi = true ->
    formula_closedb_ctx (extra ++ ctx) phi = true.
Proof.
  induction extra as [|x extra IH]; intros ctx phi Hclosed.
  - exact Hclosed.
  - simpl.
    apply formula_closedb_ctx_weaken.
    apply IH.
    exact Hclosed.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[Duplicate entries in the context are semantically irrelevant for formula closure. This lemma is used when bounded quantifier bookkeeping introduces the same variable more than once during context rearrangements.]]@*)

Lemma formula_closedb_ctx_dup :
  forall ctx x phi,
    formula_closedb_ctx (x :: x :: ctx) phi = formula_closedb_ctx (x :: ctx) phi.
Proof.
  intros ctx x phi.
  revert ctx x.
  induction phi as
      [s t| |a IHa b IHb|a IHa b IHb|a IHa b IHb
      |y psi IHpsi|y psi IHpsi];
    intros ctx x; simpl.
  - rewrite (term_closedb_ctx_dup ctx x s), (term_closedb_ctx_dup ctx x t).
    reflexivity.
  - reflexivity.
  - rewrite IHa, IHb.
    reflexivity.
  - rewrite IHa, IHb.
    reflexivity.
  - rewrite IHa, IHb.
    reflexivity.
  - pose proof
      (formula_closedb_ctx_perm
        (y :: x :: x :: ctx) (x :: x :: y :: ctx) psi
        (Permutation_trans
          (perm_swap x y (x :: ctx))
          (perm_skip x (perm_swap x y ctx))))
      as Hperm_l.
    pose proof
      (formula_closedb_ctx_perm
        (y :: x :: ctx) (x :: y :: ctx) psi
        (perm_swap x y ctx))
      as Hperm_r.
    rewrite Hperm_l, Hperm_r.
    rewrite (IHpsi (y :: ctx) x).
    reflexivity.
  - pose proof
      (formula_closedb_ctx_perm
        (y :: x :: x :: ctx) (x :: x :: y :: ctx) psi
        (Permutation_trans
          (perm_swap x y (x :: ctx))
          (perm_skip x (perm_swap x y ctx))))
      as Hperm_l.
    pose proof
      (formula_closedb_ctx_perm
        (y :: x :: ctx) (x :: y :: ctx) psi
        (perm_swap x y ctx))
      as Hperm_r.
    rewrite Hperm_l, Hperm_r.
    rewrite (IHpsi (y :: ctx) x).
    reflexivity.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[`BoundedGuardOn ctx x guard` is the semantic shape predicate for admissible bounded guards. It says that `guard` is exactly an additive bound witness `x <= bound`, with a fresh witness variable not already present in `ctx` and with the bounding term closed over `ctx`.]]@*)

Definition BoundedGuardOn (ctx : list nat) (x : nat) (guard : RAFormula) : Prop :=
  exists witness bound,
    guard = leq_atom x witness bound /\
    witness <> x /\
    existsb (Nat.eqb witness) ctx = false /\
    term_closedb_ctx ctx bound = true.

(*@c.inline@[[`leq_guardb` is the executable recognizer for the raw syntactic pattern `leq_atom x witness bound` when the bound term is fixed externally. It checks only the constructor shape and the required equalities of the exposed variables and bound term.]]@*)

Definition leq_guardb (x : nat) (bound : RATerm) (phi : RAFormula) : bool :=
  match phi with
  | RA_Exists witness (RA_Eq rhs (RA_Add (RA_Var x') (RA_Var witness'))) =>
      Nat.eqb x x' &&
      Nat.eqb witness witness' &&
      if RATerm_eq_dec rhs bound then true else false
  | _ => false
  end.

(*@c.inline@[[`bounded_guard_onb` is the fully internal boolean recognizer for admissible bounded guards. In addition to the raw `leq_atom` shape, it checks freshness of the witness relative to `x` and `ctx`, and it verifies that the bound term is closed over the ambient context.]]@*)

Definition bounded_guard_onb (ctx : list nat) (x : nat) (phi : RAFormula) : bool :=
  match phi with
  | RA_Exists witness (RA_Eq bound (RA_Add (RA_Var x') (RA_Var witness'))) =>
      Nat.eqb x x' &&
      Nat.eqb witness witness' &&
      negb (Nat.eqb witness x) &&
      negb (existsb (Nat.eqb witness) ctx) &&
      term_closedb_ctx ctx bound
  | _ => false
  end.

(*@c.inline@[[The first correctness lemma for guards isolates the bare syntactic pattern recognized by `leq_guardb`. A successful boolean test is equivalent to saying that the formula is some `leq_atom x witness bound`.]]@*)

Lemma leq_guardb_correct :
  forall x bound guard,
    leq_guardb x bound guard = true <->
    exists witness, guard = leq_atom x witness bound.
Proof.
  intros x bound guard.
  split.
  - destruct guard as [s t| |a b|a b|a b|w psi|w psi];
      simpl; try discriminate.
    destruct psi as [lhs rhs| |a b|a b|a b|q psi|q psi];
      simpl; try discriminate.
    destruct rhs as [| |rhs|rhs_l rhs_r];
      simpl; try discriminate.
    destruct rhs_l as [x'| |u|u v];
      simpl; try discriminate.
    destruct rhs_r as [w'| |u|u v];
      simpl; try discriminate.
    intro Hguard.
    repeat rewrite andb_true_iff in Hguard.
    destruct Hguard as [[Hx Hw] Hbound].
    apply Nat.eqb_eq in Hx.
    apply Nat.eqb_eq in Hw.
    destruct (RATerm_eq_dec lhs bound) as [Heq | Hneq].
    + subst x' w' lhs.
      exists w.
      reflexivity.
    + discriminate Hbound.
  - intros [witness Hguard].
    subst guard.
    unfold leq_guardb, leq_atom, mk_exists, mk_eq.
    simpl.
    repeat rewrite Nat.eqb_refl.
    destruct (RATerm_eq_dec bound bound) as [_ | Hneq].
    + reflexivity.
    + exfalso.
      apply Hneq.
      reflexivity.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[`bounded_guard_onb_correct` upgrades the raw shape test to the full semantic bounded-guard predicate. It shows that the executable checker captures exactly the intended freshness and closedness side conditions bundled in `BoundedGuardOn`.]]@*)
(*@c.unicode@[[Guard checker adequacy: bounded_guard_onb(ctx,x,g)=true ↔ BoundedGuardOn(ctx,x,g).]]@*)

Lemma bounded_guard_onb_correct :
  forall ctx x guard,
    bounded_guard_onb ctx x guard = true <->
    BoundedGuardOn ctx x guard.
Proof.
  intros ctx x guard.
  split.
  - destruct guard as [s t| |a b|a b|a b|w psi|w psi];
      simpl; try discriminate.
    destruct psi as [lhs rhs| |a b|a b|a b|q psi|q psi];
      simpl; try discriminate.
    destruct rhs as [| |rhs|rhs_l rhs_r];
      simpl; try discriminate.
    destruct rhs_l as [x'| |u|u v];
      simpl; try discriminate.
    destruct rhs_r as [w'| |u|u v];
      simpl; try discriminate.
    intro Hguard.
    repeat rewrite andb_true_iff in Hguard.
    destruct Hguard as [[[[Hx Hw] Hfresh_x] Hfresh_ctx] Hbound].
    apply Nat.eqb_eq in Hx.
    apply Nat.eqb_eq in Hw.
    apply negb_true_iff in Hfresh_x.
    apply negb_true_iff in Hfresh_ctx.
    apply Nat.eqb_neq in Hfresh_x.
    subst x' w'.
    exists w, lhs.
    split.
    + reflexivity.
    + split.
      * exact Hfresh_x.
      * split.
        { exact Hfresh_ctx. }
        { exact Hbound. }
  - intros [witness [bound [Hguard [Hneq [Hfresh Hbound]]]]].
    subst guard.
    unfold bounded_guard_onb, leq_atom, mk_exists, mk_eq.
    simpl.
    repeat rewrite Nat.eqb_refl.
    apply Nat.eqb_neq in Hneq.
    rewrite Hneq.
    rewrite Hfresh.
    exact Hbound.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[Every semantic bounded guard is closed once the bounded variable itself is added to the context. This is the closure fact needed when bounded quantifiers are admitted into the `Delta_0` class.]]@*)

Lemma BoundedGuardOn_closedb :
  forall ctx x guard,
    BoundedGuardOn ctx x guard ->
    formula_closedb_ctx (x :: ctx) guard = true.
Proof.
  intros ctx x guard [witness [bound [Hguard [_ [_ Hbound]]]]].
  subst guard.
  unfold leq_atom, mk_exists, mk_eq.
  simpl.
  pose proof
    (term_closedb_ctx_weaken_many [witness; x] ctx bound Hbound)
    as Hbound'.
  simpl in Hbound'.
  rewrite Hbound'.
  simpl.
  rewrite Nat.eqb_refl.
  destruct (Nat.eqb x witness); simpl; rewrite Nat.eqb_refl; reflexivity.
Qed.

(*@box.section@[[DELTA-0 ON CONTEXT]]@*)

(*@c.inline@[[`Delta_0` is first defined relative to a context of allowed free variables. Atomic equalities must be well formed in that context, and quantifiers must appear in the bounded shapes generated from `leq_atom`.]]@*)

Fixpoint Delta0Onb (ctx : list nat) (phi : RAFormula) : bool :=
  match phi with
  | RA_Eq s t => term_closedb_ctx ctx s && term_closedb_ctx ctx t
  | RA_Bot => true
  | RA_Imp a b => Delta0Onb ctx a && Delta0Onb ctx b
  | RA_And a b => Delta0Onb ctx a && Delta0Onb ctx b
  | RA_Or a b => Delta0Onb ctx a && Delta0Onb ctx b
  | RA_Forall x psi =>
      match psi with
      | RA_Imp guard body =>
          bounded_guard_onb ctx x guard && Delta0Onb (x :: ctx) body
      | _ => false
      end
  | RA_Exists x psi =>
      match psi with
      | RA_And guard body =>
          bounded_guard_onb ctx x guard && Delta0Onb (x :: ctx) body
      | _ => false
      end
  end.

(*@c.inline@[[`Delta0On` is the semantic inductive presentation of bounded formulas over a variable context. Atomic equalities must be closed in context, and quantifiers are allowed only in the bounded forms controlled by `BoundedGuardOn`.]]@*)

Inductive Delta0On : list nat -> RAFormula -> Prop :=
| Delta0On_eq :
    forall ctx s t,
      term_closedb_ctx ctx s = true ->
      term_closedb_ctx ctx t = true ->
      Delta0On ctx (RA_Eq s t)
| Delta0On_bot :
    forall ctx,
      Delta0On ctx RA_Bot
| Delta0On_imp :
    forall ctx a b,
      Delta0On ctx a ->
      Delta0On ctx b ->
      Delta0On ctx (RA_Imp a b)
| Delta0On_and :
    forall ctx a b,
      Delta0On ctx a ->
      Delta0On ctx b ->
      Delta0On ctx (RA_And a b)
| Delta0On_or :
    forall ctx a b,
      Delta0On ctx a ->
      Delta0On ctx b ->
      Delta0On ctx (RA_Or a b)
| Delta0On_bounded_forall :
    forall ctx x guard body,
      BoundedGuardOn ctx x guard ->
      Delta0On (x :: ctx) body ->
      Delta0On ctx (RA_Forall x (RA_Imp guard body))
| Delta0On_bounded_exists :
    forall ctx x guard body,
      BoundedGuardOn ctx x guard ->
      Delta0On (x :: ctx) body ->
      Delta0On ctx (RA_Exists x (RA_And guard body)).

(*@c.inline@[[Semantic `Delta_0` formulas are automatically well formed in their ambient context. This lemma extracts the general closure consequence of the inductive class definition.]]@*)

Lemma Delta0On_closedb :
  forall ctx phi,
    Delta0On ctx phi ->
    formula_closedb_ctx ctx phi = true.
Proof.
  intros ctx phi Hdelta.
  induction Hdelta.
  - simpl.
    rewrite H, H0.
    reflexivity.
  - reflexivity.
  - simpl.
    rewrite IHHdelta1, IHHdelta2.
    reflexivity.
  - simpl.
    rewrite IHHdelta1, IHHdelta2.
    reflexivity.
  - simpl.
    rewrite IHHdelta1, IHHdelta2.
    reflexivity.
  - simpl.
    rewrite (BoundedGuardOn_closedb _ _ _ H).
    rewrite IHHdelta.
    reflexivity.
  - simpl.
    rewrite (BoundedGuardOn_closedb _ _ _ H).
    rewrite IHHdelta.
    reflexivity.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[`Delta0Onb_sound` is the forward direction of adequacy for the executable bounded-class recognizer. Whenever the boolean test accepts a formula, the corresponding semantic `Delta0On` judgment can be reconstructed.]]@*)

(*@c.unicode@[[Forward adequacy for `Δ₀`: `Delta0Onb ctx φ = true ⟹ Delta0On ctx φ`.]]@*)

Lemma Delta0Onb_sound_bounded_forall :
  forall ctx x guard body,
    bounded_guard_onb ctx x guard = true ->
    Delta0On (x :: ctx) body ->
    Delta0On ctx (RA_Forall x (RA_Imp guard body)).
Proof.
  intros ctx x guard body Hguard Hbody.
  apply Delta0On_bounded_forall.
  - apply bounded_guard_onb_correct.
    exact Hguard.
  - exact Hbody.
Qed.

Lemma Delta0Onb_sound_bounded_exists :
  forall ctx x guard body,
    bounded_guard_onb ctx x guard = true ->
    Delta0On (x :: ctx) body ->
    Delta0On ctx (RA_Exists x (RA_And guard body)).
Proof.
  intros ctx x guard body Hguard Hbody.
  apply Delta0On_bounded_exists.
  - apply bounded_guard_onb_correct.
    exact Hguard.
  - exact Hbody.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[`Delta0Onb_sound` reconstructs semantic `Delta_0` membership from the boolean recognizer. The bounded-quantifier boolean-to-semantic recovery is factored into dedicated helper lemmas (`Delta0Onb_sound_bounded_forall` / `Delta0Onb_sound_bounded_exists`), so extending side conditions no longer duplicates constructor reconstruction logic.]]@*)

Lemma Delta0Onb_sound :
  forall ctx phi,
    Delta0Onb ctx phi = true ->
    Delta0On ctx phi.
Proof.
  fix IH 2.
  intros ctx phi Hdelta.
  destruct phi as
      [s t| |a b|a b|a b|x psi|x psi];
    simpl in Hdelta.
  - apply andb_true_iff in Hdelta.
    destruct Hdelta as [Hs Ht].
    apply Delta0On_eq; assumption.
  - apply Delta0On_bot.
  - apply andb_true_iff in Hdelta.
    destruct Hdelta as [Ha Hb].
    apply Delta0On_imp.
    + apply IH. exact Ha.
    + apply IH. exact Hb.
  - apply andb_true_iff in Hdelta.
    destruct Hdelta as [Ha Hb].
    apply Delta0On_and.
    + apply IH. exact Ha.
    + apply IH. exact Hb.
  - apply andb_true_iff in Hdelta.
    destruct Hdelta as [Ha Hb].
    apply Delta0On_or.
    + apply IH. exact Ha.
    + apply IH. exact Hb.
  - destruct psi as [s t| |guard body|a b|a b|q body|q body];
      simpl in Hdelta; try discriminate.
    apply andb_true_iff in Hdelta.
    destruct Hdelta as [Hguard Hbody].
    apply Delta0Onb_sound_bounded_forall.
    + exact Hguard.
    + apply IH.
      exact Hbody.
  - destruct psi as [s t| |a b|guard body|a b|q body|q body];
      simpl in Hdelta; try discriminate.
    apply andb_true_iff in Hdelta.
    destruct Hdelta as [Hguard Hbody].
    apply Delta0Onb_sound_bounded_exists.
    + exact Hguard.
    + apply IH.
      exact Hbody.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[`Delta0Onb_complete` is the converse adequacy direction. Every semantically bounded formula is accepted by the recursive boolean recognizer, so no intended `Delta_0` case is missed.]]@*)

Lemma Delta0Onb_complete :
  forall ctx phi,
    Delta0On ctx phi ->
    Delta0Onb ctx phi = true.
Proof.
  intros ctx phi Hdelta.
  induction Hdelta.
  - simpl.
    rewrite H, H0.
    reflexivity.
  - reflexivity.
  - simpl.
    rewrite IHHdelta1, IHHdelta2.
    reflexivity.
  - simpl.
    rewrite IHHdelta1, IHHdelta2.
    reflexivity.
  - simpl.
    rewrite IHHdelta1, IHHdelta2.
    reflexivity.
  - simpl.
    pose proof (proj2 (bounded_guard_onb_correct ctx x guard) H) as Hguardb.
    rewrite Hguardb.
    rewrite IHHdelta.
    reflexivity.
  - simpl.
    pose proof (proj2 (bounded_guard_onb_correct ctx x guard) H) as Hguardb.
    rewrite Hguardb.
    rewrite IHHdelta.
    reflexivity.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[Combining soundness and completeness yields the exact boolean-versus-semantic characterization of `Delta_0` over an arbitrary context. This theorem is the main local interface consumed by later routes.]]@*)
(*@c.unicode@[[Δ₀ recognizer correctness on context: Delta0Onb(ctx,φ)=true ↔ Delta0On(ctx,φ).]]@*)

Theorem Delta0Onb_correct :
  forall ctx phi,
    Delta0Onb ctx phi = true <-> Delta0On ctx phi.
Proof.
  intros ctx phi.
  split.
  - apply Delta0Onb_sound.
  - apply Delta0Onb_complete.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[The public closed `Delta_0` class is obtained by instantiating the contextual definitions at the empty context. `Delta0Formula` is the semantic predicate, while `Delta0Formulab` is its executable recognizer.]]@*)

Definition Delta0Formula (phi : RAFormula) : Prop :=
  Delta0On [] phi.

(*@c.unicode@[[Closed boolean class test: `Delta0Formulab φ ≝ Delta0Onb [] φ`.]]@*)

Definition Delta0Formulab (phi : RAFormula) : bool :=
  Delta0Onb [] phi.

(*@c.inline@[[At the closed level, the boolean recognizer and semantic predicate for `Delta_0` coincide exactly. This is the public empty-context specialization of the contextual adequacy theorem.]]@*)

Theorem Delta0Formulab_correct :
  forall phi,
    Delta0Formulab phi = true <-> Delta0Formula phi.
Proof.
  intro phi.
  exact (Delta0Onb_correct [] phi).
Qed.

(*@box.section@[[SIGMA-1 ON CONTEXT]]@*)

(*@c.inline@[[`Sigma_1` is built as an existential prefix over a `Delta_0` matrix while preserving the same context discipline. The public closed aliases are recovered by instantiating the context with `[]`.]]@*)

Fixpoint Sigma1Onb (ctx : list nat) (phi : RAFormula) : bool :=
  match phi with
  | RA_Exists x psi => Sigma1Onb (x :: ctx) psi
  | _ => Delta0Onb ctx phi
  end.

(*@c.unicode@[[Semantic `Σ₁` predicate on a context: peel leading `∃`-binders, then require a `Δ₀` matrix (`Delta0On`) at the resulting context.]]@*)

Fixpoint Sigma1On (ctx : list nat) (phi : RAFormula) : Prop :=
  match phi with
  | RA_Exists x psi => Sigma1On (x :: ctx) psi
  | _ => Delta0On ctx phi
  end.

(*@c.unicode@[[Closure consequence for `Σ₁`: `Sigma1On ctx φ ⟹ formula_closedb_ctx ctx φ = true`.]]@*)

Lemma Sigma1On_closedb :
  forall ctx phi,
    Sigma1On ctx phi ->
    formula_closedb_ctx ctx phi = true.
Proof.
  intros ctx phi.
  revert ctx.
  induction phi as
      [s t| |a b|a b|a b|x psi IHpsi|x psi IHpsi];
    intros ctx Hsigma; simpl in Hsigma.
  - apply Delta0On_closedb.
    exact Hsigma.
  - apply Delta0On_closedb.
    exact Hsigma.
  - apply Delta0On_closedb.
    exact Hsigma.
  - apply Delta0On_closedb.
    exact Hsigma.
  - apply Delta0On_closedb.
    exact Hsigma.
  - apply Delta0On_closedb.
    exact Hsigma.
  - simpl.
    apply (IHpsi (x :: ctx)).
    exact Hsigma.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[`Sigma1Onb_sound` is the forward adequacy statement for the executable `Sigma_1` recognizer. If the boolean test accepts, the formula belongs to the semantic `Sigma1On` class over the same context.]]@*)

Lemma Sigma1Onb_sound :
  forall ctx phi,
    Sigma1Onb ctx phi = true ->
    Sigma1On ctx phi.
Proof.
  fix IH 2.
  intros ctx phi Hsigma.
  destruct phi as
      [s t| |a b|a b|a b|x psi|x psi];
    simpl in Hsigma.
  - apply Delta0Onb_sound.
    exact Hsigma.
  - apply Delta0Onb_sound.
    exact Hsigma.
  - apply Delta0Onb_sound.
    exact Hsigma.
  - apply Delta0Onb_sound.
    exact Hsigma.
  - apply Delta0Onb_sound.
    exact Hsigma.
  - apply Delta0Onb_sound.
    exact Hsigma.
  - apply (IH (x :: ctx) psi).
    exact Hsigma.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[`Sigma1Onb_complete` is the converse adequacy direction. Every semantically `Sigma_1` formula is accepted by the recursive boolean recognizer, including any initial block of existential quantifiers.]]@*)

Lemma Sigma1Onb_complete :
  forall ctx phi,
    Sigma1On ctx phi ->
    Sigma1Onb ctx phi = true.
Proof.
  fix IH 2.
  intros ctx phi Hsigma.
  destruct phi as
      [s t| |a b|a b|a b|x psi|x psi];
    simpl in Hsigma.
  - apply Delta0Onb_complete.
    exact Hsigma.
  - apply Delta0Onb_complete.
    exact Hsigma.
  - apply Delta0Onb_complete.
    exact Hsigma.
  - apply Delta0Onb_complete.
    exact Hsigma.
  - apply Delta0Onb_complete.
    exact Hsigma.
  - apply Delta0Onb_complete.
    exact Hsigma.
  - simpl.
    apply (IH (x :: ctx) psi).
    exact Hsigma.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[This theorem packages soundness and completeness into the exact boolean-semantic characterization of `Sigma_1` over an arbitrary context. It is the main local interface for the later schema layer.]]@*)
(*@c.unicode@[[Σ₁ recognizer correctness on context: Sigma1Onb(ctx,φ)=true ↔ Sigma1On(ctx,φ).]]@*)

Theorem Sigma1Onb_correct :
  forall ctx phi,
    Sigma1Onb ctx phi = true <-> Sigma1On ctx phi.
Proof.
  intros ctx phi.
  split.
  - apply Sigma1Onb_sound.
  - apply Sigma1Onb_complete.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[The public closed `Sigma_1` class is obtained, just as for `Delta_0`, by instantiating the contextual definitions at the empty context. The semantic and executable presentations are kept side by side.]]@*)

Definition Sigma1Formula (phi : RAFormula) : Prop :=
  Sigma1On [] phi.

(*@c.unicode@[[Closed boolean `Σ₁` recognizer alias: `Sigma1Formulab φ ≝ Sigma1Onb [] φ`.]]@*)

Definition Sigma1Formulab (phi : RAFormula) : bool :=
  Sigma1Onb [] phi.

(*@c.inline@[[The closed boolean recognizer `Sigma1Formulab` is exact for the public semantic predicate `Sigma1Formula`. This is the empty-context specialization of `Sigma1Onb_correct`.]]@*)

Theorem Sigma1Formulab_correct :
  forall phi,
    Sigma1Formulab phi = true <-> Sigma1Formula phi.
Proof.
  intro phi.
  exact (Sigma1Onb_correct [] phi).
Qed.


(* ---- theories/D001/D001_06__Schemas.v ---- *)

(*@file@*)
(*@H.1@[[Proofcase / D001 / Schemas]]@*)
(*@H.2@[[Overview]]@*)
(*@p.l@[[This construction is the internal schema layer for D001.]]@*)
(*@p.l@[[It assembles the fixed additive base together with parameterized bounded induction and parameterized bounded `Sigma_1` collection.]]@*)
(*@p.l@[[Its stabilized downstream exposure is provided through `D001_14__RA_Language`, while the final combined arithmetic-theory predicate is assembled one route later in `D001_07__RA_Theory`.]]@*)
(*@H.2@[[Contents]]@*)
(*@ptoclist.roman@[[PREDICATIVE FORM]][[Internal finite additive base builders, predicate, recognizer, and sentence facts.]]@*)
(*@ptoclist.roman@[[BOUNDED INDUCTION]][[Parameterized `I Delta_0` builders, predicate, recognizer, and sentence theorem.]]@*)
(*@ptoclist.roman@[[B SIGMA-1 COLLECTION]][[Parameterized bounded-collection builders, predicate, recognizer, and sentence theorem.]]@*)

From D001 Require Export D001_05__Classes.

Local Open Scope nat_scope.

(*@box.section@[[PREDICATIVE FORM]]@*)

(*@c.inline@[[`Sentenceb` re-exports the boolean closedness test from `D001_03__RA` under the local schema-layer vocabulary. This keeps the public sentence recognizer available without reopening the lower route.]]@*)

Definition Sentenceb : RAFormula -> bool := sentenceb.

(*@c.inline@[[`Sentence` is the corresponding propositional alias for closed arithmetic formulas. The schema layer uses the same notion of sentencehood as the foundational route, only under a local name.]]@*)

Definition Sentence : RAFormula -> Prop := D001_03__RA.Sentence.

(*@c.inline@[[The first fixed base axiom states that no successor is equal to zero. It is presented here in closed formula form as part of the finite additive base packaged by this route.]]@*)

Definition ax_s_ne_0 : RAFormula :=
  mk_forall 0 (mk_neq (RA_Succ (RA_Var 0)) RA_Zero).

(*@c.inline@[[The second fixed base axiom is successor injectivity. Written with two universally bound variables, it says that equal successors must have equal predecessors.]]@*)

Definition ax_s_inj : RAFormula :=
  mk_forall_chain [0; 1]
    (mk_imp
      (mk_eq (RA_Succ (RA_Var 0)) (RA_Succ (RA_Var 1)))
      (mk_eq (RA_Var 0) (RA_Var 1))).

(*@c.inline@[[The predecessor axiom states that every nonzero element is a successor. It is the existential part of the finite additive base, presented in closed form over the primitive language.]]@*)

Definition ax_pred : RAFormula :=
  mk_forall 0
    (mk_imp
      (mk_neq (RA_Var 0) RA_Zero)
      (mk_exists 1 (mk_eq (RA_Var 0) (RA_Succ (RA_Var 1))))).

(*@c.inline@[[The next base axiom fixes zero as a right additive identity. It is the first of the two primitive recursive clauses governing addition in the finite base.]]@*)

Definition ax_add_zero : RAFormula :=
  mk_forall 0 (mk_eq (RA_Add (RA_Var 0) RA_Zero) (RA_Var 0)).

(*@c.inline@[[The final base axiom is the recursive successor clause for addition. It says that adding a successor on the right is equivalent to taking the successor after addition.]]@*)

Definition ax_add_succ : RAFormula :=
  mk_forall_chain [0; 1]
    (mk_eq
      (RA_Add (RA_Var 0) (RA_Succ (RA_Var 1)))
      (RA_Succ (RA_Add (RA_Var 0) (RA_Var 1)))).

(*@c.inline@[[The fixed additive base is packaged as one finite list of closed formulas. This keeps the semantic predicate, executable recognizer, and later theory assembly aligned around a single source of axioms.]]@*)

Definition base_axiom_list : list RAFormula :=
  [ax_s_ne_0; ax_s_inj; ax_pred; ax_add_zero; ax_add_succ].

(*@c.inline@[[`BaseAxiom` is the semantic membership predicate for the finite additive base. A formula is a base axiom exactly when it occurs in `base_axiom_list`.]]@*)

Definition BaseAxiom (phi : RAFormula) : Prop :=
  In phi base_axiom_list.

(*@c.inline@[[`BaseAxiomb` is the executable recognizer for the same finite base. It scans the canonical list and compares formulas with the decidable equality coming from `D001_03__RA`.]]@*)

Definition BaseAxiomb (phi : RAFormula) : bool :=
  existsb
    (fun psi => if RAFormula_eq_dec phi psi then true else false)
    base_axiom_list.

(*@c.inline@[[The boolean base-axiom checker is exact for the semantic base predicate. This is the basic adequacy statement that lets later routes move freely between computation and propositional reasoning about the fixed base.]]@*)
(*@c.unicode@[[Finite-base adequacy: BaseAxiomb(φ)=true ↔ BaseAxiom(φ).]]@*)

Theorem BaseAxiomb_correct :
  forall phi,
    BaseAxiomb phi = true <-> BaseAxiom phi.
Proof.
  intro phi.
  unfold BaseAxiomb, BaseAxiom.
  rewrite existsb_exists.
  split.
  - intros [psi [Hin Hhit]].
    destruct (RAFormula_eq_dec phi psi) as [Heq | Hneq].
    + subst psi.
      exact Hin.
    + discriminate Hhit.
  - intro Hin.
    exists phi.
    split.
    + exact Hin.
    + destruct (RAFormula_eq_dec phi phi) as [_ | Hneq].
      * reflexivity.
      * exfalso.
        apply Hneq.
        reflexivity.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[Each concrete base axiom is a sentence, and we record those facts explicitly once so later sentence arguments over the finite base can be discharged by case analysis rather than recomputation.]]@*)

Lemma ax_s_ne_0_sentence : Sentence ax_s_ne_0.
Proof. vm_compute. reflexivity. Qed.

(*@c.unicode@[[Sentence certificate for successor injectivity axiom: `Sentence(ax_s_inj) = true` at the boolean closure layer.]]@*)

Lemma ax_s_inj_sentence : Sentence ax_s_inj.
Proof. vm_compute. reflexivity. Qed.

(*@c.unicode@[[Sentence certificate for predecessor axiom: `Sentence(ax_pred)` holds by computation.]]@*)

Lemma ax_pred_sentence : Sentence ax_pred.
Proof. vm_compute. reflexivity. Qed.

(*@c.unicode@[[Sentence certificate for additive-zero axiom: `Sentence(ax_add_zero)` holds at closed-form level.]]@*)

Lemma ax_add_zero_sentence : Sentence ax_add_zero.
Proof. vm_compute. reflexivity. Qed.

(*@c.unicode@[[Sentence certificate for additive-successor axiom: `Sentence(ax_add_succ)` is verified by computation.]]@*)

Lemma ax_add_succ_sentence : Sentence ax_add_succ.
Proof. vm_compute. reflexivity. Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[Every semantic base axiom is closed. Since the base is finite and explicit, the sentence theorem is proved by enumerating the five canonical axioms.]]@*)
(*@c.unicode@[[Sentence closure of finite base: BaseAxiom(φ) ⇒ Sentence(φ).]]@*)

Theorem BaseAxiom_sentence :
  forall phi,
    BaseAxiom phi ->
    Sentence phi.
Proof.
  intros phi Haxiom.
  unfold BaseAxiom, base_axiom_list in Haxiom.
  destruct Haxiom as [Haxiom | Haxiom].
  - subst phi.
    exact ax_s_ne_0_sentence.
  - destruct Haxiom as [Haxiom | Haxiom].
    + subst phi.
      exact ax_s_inj_sentence.
    + destruct Haxiom as [Haxiom | Haxiom].
      * subst phi.
        exact ax_pred_sentence.
      * destruct Haxiom as [Haxiom | Haxiom].
        { subst phi.
          exact ax_add_zero_sentence. }
        { destruct Haxiom as [Haxiom | Haxiom].
          - subst phi.
            exact ax_add_succ_sentence.
          - contradiction. }
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[The boolean base-axiom recognizer therefore also implies sentencehood. This corollary is the computational form of `BaseAxiom_sentence`.]]@*)

Corollary BaseAxiomb_sentence :
  forall phi,
    BaseAxiomb phi = true ->
    Sentence phi.
Proof.
  intros phi Haxiomb.
  apply BaseAxiom_sentence.
  apply BaseAxiomb_correct.
  exact Haxiomb.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[`Sentenceb_correct` simply restates that the boolean and propositional sentence predicates exported by this route are definitionally the same notion of closedness.]]@*)

Lemma Sentenceb_correct :
  forall phi,
    Sentenceb phi = true <-> Sentence phi.
Proof.
  intro phi.
  unfold Sentenceb, Sentence, sentenceb, D001_03__RA.Sentence.
  tauto.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[`formula_eqb` is the boolean packaging of formula equality. It exposes the decidable equality from `D001_03__RA` in the executable form needed by list recognizers and finite-search lemmas below.]]@*)

Definition formula_eqb (phi psi : RAFormula) : bool :=
  if RAFormula_eq_dec phi psi then true else false.

(*@c.unicode@[[Reflexivity of boolean formula equality: `formula_eqb φ φ = true` for all `φ`.]]@*)

Lemma formula_eqb_refl :
  forall phi,
    formula_eqb phi phi = true.
Proof.
  intro phi.
  unfold formula_eqb.
  destruct (RAFormula_eq_dec phi phi) as [_ | Hneq].
  - reflexivity.
  - exfalso.
    apply Hneq.
    reflexivity.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[The basic correctness fact for `formula_eqb` says that a successful boolean comparison is equivalent to literal formula equality. This is the key bridge from executable finite-list tests back to semantic membership arguments.]]@*)

Lemma formula_eqb_true_iff :
  forall phi psi,
    formula_eqb phi psi = true <-> phi = psi.
Proof.
  intros phi psi.
  unfold formula_eqb.
  destruct (RAFormula_eq_dec phi psi) as [Heq | Hneq].
  - split.
    + intro H.
      clear H.
      exact Heq.
    + intro H.
      clear H.
      reflexivity.
  - split.
    + intro H.
      discriminate H.
    + intro Heq.
      exfalso.
      apply Hneq.
      exact Heq.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[This lemma is the negative membership specification for boolean list search on naturals. It lets later no-duplication arguments turn a failed `existsb (Nat.eqb x)` test into the semantic statement that `x` does not occur in the list.]]@*)

Lemma existsb_nat_eq_false_iff :
  forall x xs,
    existsb (Nat.eqb x) xs = false <-> ~ In x xs.
Proof.
  intros x xs.
  induction xs as [|y xs IH]; simpl.
  - split.
    + intro H.
      clear H.
      intros [].
    + intro H.
      clear H.
      reflexivity.
  - destruct (Nat.eqb x y) eqn:Hxy.
    + apply Nat.eqb_eq in Hxy.
      subst y.
      split.
      * discriminate.
      * intro H.
        exfalso.
        apply H.
        left.
        reflexivity.
    + rewrite IH.
      split.
      * intro H.
        intros [Hin | Hin].
        { apply Nat.eqb_neq in Hxy.
          exfalso.
          apply Hxy.
          symmetry.
          exact Hin. }
        exact (H Hin).
      * intro H.
        intro Hin.
        apply H.
        right.
        exact Hin.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[`list_nodupb` is the executable duplicate check on lists of variables. It is used later when schema builders need a computable side condition expressing that parameter blocks contain no repeated binders.]]@*)

Fixpoint list_nodupb (xs : list nat) : bool :=
  match xs with
  | [] => true
  | x :: xs' => negb (existsb (Nat.eqb x) xs') && list_nodupb xs'
  end.

(*@c.unicode@[[Boolean/propositional equivalence for duplicate-freeness: `list_nodupb xs = true ↔ NoDup xs`.]]@*)

Lemma list_nodupb_correct :
  forall xs,
    list_nodupb xs = true <-> NoDup xs.
Proof.
  induction xs as [|x xs IH]; simpl.
  - split.
    + intro H.
      clear H.
      constructor.
    + intro H.
      clear H.
      reflexivity.
  - rewrite andb_true_iff.
    rewrite IH.
    rewrite negb_true_iff.
    rewrite existsb_nat_eq_false_iff.
    split.
    + intros [Hnotin Hnodup].
      constructor.
      * exact Hnotin.
      * exact Hnodup.
    + intros Hnodup.
      inversion Hnodup as [|y ys Hnotin Hrest]; subst.
      split.
      * exact Hnotin.
      * exact Hrest.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[`mk_forall_chain_app` records that universal-prefix construction respects concatenation of binder lists. This algebraic normalization is used when schema builders split or recombine parameter blocks.]]@*)

Lemma mk_forall_chain_app :
  forall xs ys phi,
    mk_forall_chain (xs ++ ys) phi =
    mk_forall_chain xs (mk_forall_chain ys phi).
Proof.
  induction xs as [|x xs IH]; intros ys phi; simpl.
  - reflexivity.
  - rewrite IH.
    reflexivity.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[Adding a universal prefix to a formula corresponds, at the closure-check level, to extending the ambient context by those binders in reverse accumulation order. This is the basic normalization lemma used when sentencehood is read off from peeled quantifier blocks.]]@*)

Lemma formula_closedb_ctx_mk_forall_chain :
  forall xs ctx phi,
    formula_closedb_ctx ctx (mk_forall_chain xs phi) =
    formula_closedb_ctx (rev xs ++ ctx) phi.
Proof.
  induction xs as [|x xs IH]; intros ctx phi; simpl.
  - reflexivity.
  - rewrite IH.
    rewrite <- app_assoc.
    reflexivity.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[Reversing a binder list does not change its variables up to permutation. This small combinatorial fact is used when relating syntactic quantifier prefixes to context-based closure checks that expose binders in reverse accumulation order.]]@*)

Lemma Permutation_rev_self :
  forall xs : list nat,
    Permutation xs (rev xs).
Proof.
  induction xs as [|x xs IH]; simpl.
  - constructor.
  - transitivity (x :: rev xs).
    + now apply perm_skip.
    + replace (x :: rev xs) with ((x :: rev xs) ++ []).
      2:{ rewrite app_nil_r. reflexivity. }
      replace (rev xs ++ [x]) with (rev xs ++ x :: []).
      2:{ reflexivity. }
      apply Permutation_middle.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[`peel_forall_prefix` separates an arithmetic formula into its maximal leading universal binder block and the remaining core. It is the structural inverse of `mk_forall_chain` for the schema analyses below.]]@*)

Fixpoint peel_forall_prefix (phi : RAFormula) : list nat * RAFormula :=
  match phi with
  | RA_Forall x psi =>
      let (xs, core) := peel_forall_prefix psi in
      (x :: xs, core)
  | _ => ([], phi)
  end.

(*@c.inline@[[The peeler specification says that every formula can be reconstructed exactly from its maximal leading universal block and the residual core returned by `peel_forall_prefix`. This turns the recursive decomposition into a reusable structural identity.]]@*)

Lemma peel_forall_prefix_spec :
  forall phi,
    let (xs, core) := peel_forall_prefix phi in
    phi = mk_forall_chain xs core.
Proof.
  fix IH 1.
  intro phi.
  destruct phi as
      [s t| |a b|a b|a b|x psi|x psi];
    simpl.
  - reflexivity.
  - reflexivity.
  - reflexivity.
  - reflexivity.
  - reflexivity.
  - destruct (peel_forall_prefix psi) as [xs core] eqn:Hpeel.
    specialize (IH psi).
    simpl.
    rewrite Hpeel in IH.
    simpl in IH.
    rewrite IH.
    reflexivity.
  - reflexivity.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[Peeling after building a universal chain simply appends the newly introduced binders to whatever universal prefix the core already carries. This is the normalization lemma connecting the builder and peeler directly.]]@*)

Lemma peel_forall_prefix_mk_forall_chain :
  forall xs core,
    peel_forall_prefix (mk_forall_chain xs core) =
    let (ys, inner) := peel_forall_prefix core in
    (xs ++ ys, inner).
Proof.
  induction xs as [|x xs IH]; intros core; simpl.
  - destruct (peel_forall_prefix core) as [ys inner].
    reflexivity.
  - rewrite IH.
    destruct (peel_forall_prefix core) as [ys inner].
    reflexivity.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[`split_last` is the auxiliary list routine that isolates the final binder of a nonempty parameter list. Later induction-schema builders use it to single out the induction variable from the preceding parameter block.]]@*)

Fixpoint split_last (xs : list nat) : option (list nat * nat) :=
  match xs with
  | [] => None
  | [x] => Some ([], x)
  | x :: xs' =>
      match split_last xs' with
      | Some (ys, y) => Some (x :: ys, y)
      | None => None
      end
  end.

(*@c.inline@[[Appending one element to a list is exactly the case that `split_last` is designed to recover. This computation lemma is the basic evaluator fact behind the more general specification theorem.]]@*)

Lemma split_last_app_singleton :
  forall xs x,
    split_last (xs ++ [x]) = Some (xs, x).
Proof.
  induction xs as [|y xs IH]; intro x; simpl.
  - reflexivity.
  - destruct xs as [|z xs']; simpl.
    + reflexivity.
    + change
        (match split_last ((z :: xs') ++ [x]) with
         | Some (ys, y') => Some (y :: ys, y')
         | None => None
         end = Some (y :: z :: xs', x)).
      rewrite IH.
      reflexivity.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[The specification of `split_last` says that a successful split reconstructs the original list as an initial segment followed by its distinguished last entry. This turns the auxiliary computation into a clean structural decomposition principle.]]@*)

Lemma split_last_spec :
  forall xs params u,
    split_last xs = Some (params, u) ->
    xs = params ++ [u].
Proof.
  intros xs.
  induction xs using rev_ind; intros params u Hsplit.
  - simpl in Hsplit.
    discriminate.
  - rewrite split_last_app_singleton in Hsplit.
    inversion Hsplit; subst.
    reflexivity.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[Applying `split_last` to a binder block lets us rewrite the corresponding universal chain as an outer parameter prefix followed by one final quantifier. This is the form needed for the induction-scheme factorization lemmas.]]@*)

Lemma split_last_mk_forall_chain :
  forall binders params u core,
    split_last binders = Some (params, u) ->
    mk_forall_chain binders core =
    mk_forall_chain params (mk_forall u core).
Proof.
  intros binders params u core Hsplit.
  apply split_last_spec in Hsplit.
  subst binders.
  rewrite mk_forall_chain_app.
  simpl.
  reflexivity.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[Substituting a term that is already closed over `ctx` into a term closed over `x :: ctx` preserves closure over `ctx`. This is the basic closure-preservation lemma for the substitution machinery used by the schema instances.]]@*)

Lemma subst_term_closedb_ctx :
  forall ctx x rep t,
    term_closedb_ctx ctx rep = true ->
    term_closedb_ctx (x :: ctx) t = true ->
    term_closedb_ctx ctx (subst_term x rep t) = true.
Proof.
  intros ctx x rep t Hrep.
  induction t as [y| |u IHu|u IHu v IHv]; intro Hclosed; simpl in *.
  - destruct (Nat.eqb x y) eqn:Hxy.
    + exact Hrep.
    + rewrite Nat.eqb_sym in Hxy.
      rewrite Hxy in Hclosed.
      exact Hclosed.
  - reflexivity.
  - apply IHu.
    exact Hclosed.
  - apply andb_true_iff in Hclosed.
    destruct Hclosed as [Hu Hv].
    rewrite (IHu Hu), (IHv Hv).
    reflexivity.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[Substitution also preserves closure at the formula level: plugging a term closed over `ctx` into a formula closed over `x :: ctx` yields a formula closed over `ctx`. This is the main closure lemma needed for the induction base and step instances.]]@*)

(*@todo@[[Temporary state: `subst_formula_closedb_ctx` is still a long structural closure transport over every formula constructor, with duplicated quantified-variable cases. If later schema builders need more substitution closure facts, extract the quantified branches into smaller local lemmas before adding more traffic here.]]@*)

(*@c.unicode@[[Formula-level substitution closure transport: if `rep` is closed on `ctx` and `φ` is closed on `(x :: ctx)`, then `subst_formula x rep φ` is closed on `ctx`.]]@*)

Lemma subst_formula_closedb_ctx :
  forall ctx x rep phi,
    term_closedb_ctx ctx rep = true ->
    formula_closedb_ctx (x :: ctx) phi = true ->
    formula_closedb_ctx ctx (subst_formula x rep phi) = true.
Proof.
  intros ctx x rep phi.
  revert ctx.
  induction phi as
      [s t| |a IHa b IHb|a IHa b IHb|a IHa b IHb
      |y psi IHpsi|y psi IHpsi];
    intros ctx Hrep Hclosed; simpl in *.
  - apply andb_true_iff in Hclosed.
    destruct Hclosed as [Hs Ht].
    rewrite (subst_term_closedb_ctx ctx x rep s Hrep Hs).
    rewrite (subst_term_closedb_ctx ctx x rep t Hrep Ht).
    reflexivity.
  - reflexivity.
  - apply andb_true_iff in Hclosed.
    destruct Hclosed as [Ha Hb].
    rewrite (IHa ctx Hrep Ha), (IHb ctx Hrep Hb).
    reflexivity.
  - apply andb_true_iff in Hclosed.
    destruct Hclosed as [Ha Hb].
    rewrite (IHa ctx Hrep Ha), (IHb ctx Hrep Hb).
    reflexivity.
  - apply andb_true_iff in Hclosed.
    destruct Hclosed as [Ha Hb].
    rewrite (IHa ctx Hrep Ha), (IHb ctx Hrep Hb).
    reflexivity.
  - destruct (Nat.eqb x y) eqn:Hxy.
    + apply Nat.eqb_eq in Hxy.
      subst y.
      simpl in Hclosed.
      rewrite formula_closedb_ctx_dup in Hclosed.
      exact Hclosed.
    + simpl in Hclosed |- *.
      apply (IHpsi (y :: ctx)).
      * apply term_closedb_ctx_weaken.
        exact Hrep.
      * pose proof
          (formula_closedb_ctx_perm
            (y :: x :: ctx) (x :: y :: ctx) psi (perm_swap x y ctx))
          as Hperm.
        rewrite Hperm in Hclosed.
        exact Hclosed.
  - destruct (Nat.eqb x y) eqn:Hxy.
    + apply Nat.eqb_eq in Hxy.
      subst y.
      simpl in Hclosed.
      rewrite formula_closedb_ctx_dup in Hclosed.
      exact Hclosed.
    + simpl in Hclosed |- *.
      apply (IHpsi (y :: ctx)).
      * apply term_closedb_ctx_weaken.
        exact Hrep.
      * pose proof
          (formula_closedb_ctx_perm
            (y :: x :: ctx) (x :: y :: ctx) psi (perm_swap x y ctx))
          as Hperm.
        rewrite Hperm in Hclosed.
        exact Hclosed.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[The raw guard recognizer accepts the canonical bounded guard `leq_atom x witness bound` definitionally. This computation fact is reused when building executable tests for induction and collection instances.]]@*)

Lemma leq_guardb_refl :
  forall x witness bound,
    leq_guardb x bound (leq_atom x witness bound) = true.
Proof.
  intros x witness bound.
  unfold leq_guardb, leq_atom, mk_exists, mk_eq.
  simpl.
  repeat rewrite Nat.eqb_refl.
  destruct (RATerm_eq_dec bound bound) as [_ | Hneq].
  - reflexivity.
  - exfalso.
    apply Hneq.
    reflexivity.
Qed.

(*@box.section@[[BOUNDED INDUCTION]]@*)

(*@c.inline@[[The induction base case is obtained by substituting `0` for the induction variable. It expresses the initial instance from which the induction implication chain begins.]]@*)

Definition induction_base_case (x : nat) (phi : RAFormula) : RAFormula :=
  subst_formula x RA_Zero phi.

(*@c.unicode@[[Induction step schema component: `∀x.(φ(x) → φ(Sx))`, encoded via capture-avoiding substitution into `RA_Succ (RA_Var x)`.]]@*)

Definition induction_step_case (x : nat) (phi : RAFormula) : RAFormula :=
  mk_forall x
    (mk_imp
      phi
      (subst_formula x (RA_Succ (RA_Var x)) phi)).

(*@c.inline@[[The induction conclusion is simply the universal closure of the matrix in the induction variable. It is the target statement delivered by the schema after base and step are assumed.]]@*)
