Definition induction_conclusion (x : nat) (phi : RAFormula) : RAFormula :=
  mk_forall x phi.

(*@c.inline@[[`induction_instance` packages the usual three-part induction schema: base case, step case, and universally quantified conclusion. This is the core formula shape that later receives optional outer parameter quantifiers.]]@*)

Definition induction_instance (x : nat) (phi : RAFormula) : RAFormula :=
  mk_imp
    (induction_base_case x phi)
    (mk_imp
      (induction_step_case x phi)
      (induction_conclusion x phi)).

(*@c.inline@[[The capitalized names re-export the three components of an induction instance under the stabilized schema-layer API. Downstream routes can refer to these builders without depending on the local internal naming choices.]]@*)

Definition InductionBaseCase : nat -> RAFormula -> RAFormula :=
  induction_base_case.

(*@c.unicode@[[API alias for the step component: `InductionStepCase x φ ≝ ∀x.(φ(x) → φ(Sx))`.]]@*)

Definition InductionStepCase : nat -> RAFormula -> RAFormula :=
  induction_step_case.

(*@c.unicode@[[API alias for the conclusion component: `InductionConclusion x φ ≝ ∀x.φ(x)`.]]@*)

Definition InductionConclusion : nat -> RAFormula -> RAFormula :=
  induction_conclusion.

(*@c.unicode@[[Parameterized induction instance builder: `InductionInstanceOn params x φ ≝ ∀ params, (Base(x,φ) → (Step(x,φ) → Conclusion(x,φ)))`.]]@*)

Definition InductionInstanceOn
    (params : list nat) (x : nat) (phi : RAFormula) : RAFormula :=
  mk_forall_chain params (induction_instance x phi).

(*@c.inline@[[`InductionInstance` is the no-parameter specialization of `InductionInstanceOn`. It is the closed-form builder used when no extra universally quantified parameters are carried outside the induction variable.]]@*)

Definition InductionInstance : nat -> RAFormula -> RAFormula :=
  fun x phi => InductionInstanceOn [] x phi.

(*@c.inline@[[If the induction matrix is closed relative to the induction variable and ambient parameters, then the whole three-part induction instance is closed over the ambient context alone. This is the main sentence-preparation lemma for induction schemas.]]@*)

Lemma induction_instance_closedb_ctx :
  forall ctx x body,
    formula_closedb_ctx (x :: ctx) body = true ->
    formula_closedb_ctx ctx (induction_instance x body) = true.
Proof.
  intros ctx x body Hbody.
  unfold induction_instance, induction_base_case,
    induction_step_case, induction_conclusion.
  simpl.
  pose proof
    (subst_formula_closedb_ctx ctx x RA_Zero body eq_refl Hbody)
    as Hbase.
  assert (Hsucc_rep : term_closedb_ctx (x :: ctx) (RA_Succ (RA_Var x)) = true).
  { simpl.
    rewrite Nat.eqb_refl.
    reflexivity. }
  assert (Hbody_dup : formula_closedb_ctx (x :: x :: ctx) body = true).
  { rewrite formula_closedb_ctx_dup.
    exact Hbody. }
  pose proof
    (subst_formula_closedb_ctx (x :: ctx) x (RA_Succ (RA_Var x)) body
      Hsucc_rep Hbody_dup)
    as Hstep_body.
  rewrite Hbase.
  rewrite Hstep_body.
  simpl.
  rewrite Hbody.
  reflexivity.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[`InductionAxiom` is the semantic predicate for admissible bounded induction instances. It records a parameter block, a distinguished induction variable, the exact schema shape, freshness via `NoDup`, and the requirement that the matrix be `Delta_0` in the extended context.]]@*)

Definition InductionAxiom (phi : RAFormula) : Prop :=
  exists params x body,
    NoDup (x :: params) /\
    phi = InductionInstanceOn params x body /\
    Delta0On (x :: params) body.

(*@c.unicode@[[Executable induction-axiom recognizer: checks prefix form `∀params.(base → step → ∀x body)`, freshness (`NoDup` via `list_nodupb`), component equalities, and `Δ₀`-matrix condition.]]@*)

Definition InductionAxiomb (phi : RAFormula) : bool :=
  match peel_forall_prefix phi with
  | (params, RA_Imp base (RA_Imp step (RA_Forall x body))) =>
      list_nodupb (x :: params) &&
      formula_eqb base (InductionBaseCase x body) &&
      formula_eqb step (InductionStepCase x body) &&
      Delta0Onb (x :: params) body
  | _ => false
  end.

(*@c.inline@[[`InductionAxiomb_correct` shows that the executable induction-schema recognizer is exact for the semantic predicate `InductionAxiom`. The proof extracts the peeled quantifier prefix, checks the three displayed schema components, and bridges the matrix test through `Delta0Onb_correct`.]]@*)

(*@todo@[[Temporary state: `InductionAxiomb_correct` still depends on a broad manual peel-and-rebuild script over the displayed schema shape. If the induction interface gains new guards or parameters, move the shape-recovery and boolean-block unpacking into dedicated helper lemmas rather than extending this adequacy proof inline.]]@*)

(*@c.unicode@[[Adequacy bridge for induction schemas: `InductionAxiomb φ = true ↔ InductionAxiom φ`.]]@*)

Theorem InductionAxiomb_correct :
  forall phi,
    InductionAxiomb phi = true <-> InductionAxiom phi.
Proof.
  intro phi.
  split.
  - unfold InductionAxiomb, InductionAxiom.
    destruct (peel_forall_prefix phi) as [params core] eqn:Hpeel.
    destruct core as [s t| |base rest|a b|a b|x body0|x body0];
      simpl; try discriminate.
    destruct rest as [s t| |step concl|a b|a b|x' body'|x' body'];
      simpl; try discriminate.
    destruct concl as [s t| |a b|a b|a b|x body|x body];
      simpl; try discriminate.
    intro Haxiom.
    repeat rewrite andb_true_iff in Haxiom.
    destruct Haxiom as [[[[Hnotin Hnodup_tail] Hbase] Hstep] Hdelta].
    apply negb_true_iff in Hnotin.
    apply existsb_nat_eq_false_iff in Hnotin.
    apply list_nodupb_correct in Hnodup_tail.
    apply formula_eqb_true_iff in Hbase.
    apply formula_eqb_true_iff in Hstep.
    apply Delta0Onb_correct in Hdelta.
    pose proof (peel_forall_prefix_spec phi) as Hspec.
    rewrite Hpeel in Hspec.
    simpl in Hspec.
    exists params, x, body.
    repeat split.
    + constructor.
      * exact Hnotin.
      * exact Hnodup_tail.
    + subst base step.
      exact Hspec.
    + exact Hdelta.
  - intros [params [x [body [Hnodup [Hphi Hdelta]]]]].
    subst phi.
    unfold InductionAxiomb, InductionInstanceOn.
    rewrite peel_forall_prefix_mk_forall_chain.
    simpl.
    repeat rewrite app_nil_r.
    inversion Hnodup as [|? ? Hnotin Hparams]; subst.
    pose proof (proj2 (existsb_nat_eq_false_iff x params) Hnotin) as Hnotinb.
    pose proof (proj2 (list_nodupb_correct params) Hparams) as Hparamsb.
    repeat rewrite andb_true_iff.
    repeat split.
    + apply negb_true_iff.
      exact Hnotinb.
    + exact Hparamsb.
    + apply formula_eqb_refl.
    + apply formula_eqb_refl.
    + apply Delta0Onb_correct.
      exact Hdelta.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[Every semantic induction axiom is a sentence. The proof reduces the quantified parameter prefix to the matrix closure guaranteed by the `Delta_0` side condition and the induction-instance closedness lemma.]]@*)

Corollary InductionAxiom_sentence :
  forall phi,
    InductionAxiom phi ->
    Sentence phi.
Proof.
  intros phi [params [x [body [_ [Hphi Hdelta]]]]].
  subst phi.
  unfold Sentence, D001_03__RA.Sentence, sentenceb, InductionInstanceOn.
  rewrite formula_closedb_ctx_mk_forall_chain.
  repeat rewrite app_nil_r.
  pose proof (Delta0On_closedb (x :: params) body Hdelta) as Hbody.
  pose proof
    (formula_closedb_ctx_perm
      (x :: params) (x :: rev params) body
      (perm_skip x (Permutation_rev_self params)))
    as Hperm.
  rewrite Hperm in Hbody.
  apply induction_instance_closedb_ctx in Hbody.
  exact Hbody.
Qed.

(*@box.section@[[B SIGMA-1 COLLECTION]]@*)

(*@c.inline@[[The bounded `Sigma_1` collection schema is organized around a premise and conclusion builder. The premise says that every `x <= u` admits some `y` satisfying `theta`, while the conclusion produces a bound `v` uniformly collecting such witnesses.]]@*)

Definition BSigma1Premise
    (u x xw y : nat) (theta : RAFormula) : RAFormula :=
  bounded_forall x xw (RA_Var u) (mk_exists y theta).

(*@c.unicode@[[Bounded-collection conclusion builder: `∃v ∀x≤u ∃y≤v θ(x,y,…)`, realized via nested bounded quantifier macros.]]@*)

Definition BSigma1Conclusion
    (u x xw y yv v : nat) (theta : RAFormula) : RAFormula :=
  mk_exists v
    (bounded_forall x xw (RA_Var u)
      (bounded_exists y yv (RA_Var v) theta)).

(*@c.unicode@[[Full `BΣ₁` instance with parameters: `∀params ∀u (Premise(u) → Conclusion(u))`.]]@*)

Definition BSigma1InstanceOn
    (params : list nat)
    (u x xw_prem xw_concl y yv v : nat)
    (theta : RAFormula) : RAFormula :=
  mk_forall_chain params
    (mk_forall u
      (mk_imp
        (BSigma1Premise u x xw_prem y theta)
        (BSigma1Conclusion u x xw_concl y yv v theta))).

(*@c.inline@[[A bounded universal quantifier preserves closure when its bound term is closed over the ambient context and its body is closed after adding the quantified variable. This is the basic closure lemma for the collection premise and conclusion builders.]]@*)

Lemma bounded_forall_closedb_ctx :
  forall ctx x witness bound body,
    term_closedb_ctx ctx bound = true ->
    formula_closedb_ctx (x :: ctx) body = true ->
    formula_closedb_ctx ctx (bounded_forall x witness bound body) = true.
Proof.
  intros ctx x witness bound body Hbound Hbody.
  unfold bounded_forall, leq_atom, mk_forall, mk_imp, mk_exists, mk_eq.
  cbn [formula_closedb_ctx term_closedb_ctx].
  assert (Hbound' : term_closedb_ctx (witness :: x :: ctx) bound = true).
  { change (term_closedb_ctx ([witness; x] ++ ctx) bound = true).
    apply term_closedb_ctx_weaken_many.
    exact Hbound. }
  rewrite Hbound'.
  rewrite Hbody.
  cbn [term_closedb_ctx existsb].
  rewrite Nat.eqb_refl.
  rewrite Nat.eqb_refl.
  destruct (Nat.eqb x witness); reflexivity.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[The existential bounded quantifier enjoys the analogous closure property. It is the companion lemma needed for the inner witness-bound component of the collection conclusion.]]@*)

Lemma bounded_exists_closedb_ctx :
  forall ctx x witness bound body,
    term_closedb_ctx ctx bound = true ->
    formula_closedb_ctx (x :: ctx) body = true ->
    formula_closedb_ctx ctx (bounded_exists x witness bound body) = true.
Proof.
  intros ctx x witness bound body Hbound Hbody.
  unfold bounded_exists, leq_atom, mk_exists, mk_eq.
  cbn [formula_closedb_ctx term_closedb_ctx].
  assert (Hbound' : term_closedb_ctx (witness :: x :: ctx) bound = true).
  { change (term_closedb_ctx ([witness; x] ++ ctx) bound = true).
    apply term_closedb_ctx_weaken_many.
    exact Hbound. }
  rewrite Hbound'.
  rewrite Hbody.
  cbn [term_closedb_ctx existsb].
  rewrite Nat.eqb_refl.
  rewrite Nat.eqb_refl.
  destruct (Nat.eqb x witness); reflexivity.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[If the matrix `theta` is closed relative to its free variables, then the whole collection premise is closed relative to the outer bound variable `u` and ambient parameters. This packages the necessary context permutations once and for all.]]@*)

Lemma BSigma1Premise_closedb_ctx :
  forall ctx u x xw y theta,
    formula_closedb_ctx (x :: y :: ctx) theta = true ->
    formula_closedb_ctx (u :: ctx) (BSigma1Premise u x xw y theta) = true.
Proof.
  intros ctx u x xw y theta Htheta.
  unfold BSigma1Premise.
  apply bounded_forall_closedb_ctx.
  - simpl.
    rewrite Nat.eqb_refl.
    reflexivity.
  - simpl.
    pose proof
      (formula_closedb_ctx_weaken_many [u] (x :: y :: ctx) theta Htheta)
      as Htheta'.
    simpl in Htheta'.
    pose proof
      (formula_closedb_ctx_perm
        (u :: x :: y :: ctx) (y :: x :: u :: ctx) theta
        (Permutation_trans
          (perm_swap x u (y :: ctx))
          (Permutation_trans
            (perm_skip x (perm_swap y u ctx))
            (perm_swap y x (u :: ctx)))))
      as Hperm.
    rewrite Hperm in Htheta'.
    exact Htheta'.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[The same closure bookkeeping is carried out for the collection conclusion. Here one must account both for the outer bounding witness `v` and for the inner bounded existential over `y`.]]@*)

Lemma BSigma1Conclusion_closedb_ctx :
  forall ctx u x xw y yv v theta,
    formula_closedb_ctx (x :: y :: ctx) theta = true ->
    formula_closedb_ctx (u :: ctx) (BSigma1Conclusion u x xw y yv v theta) = true.
Proof.
  intros ctx u x xw y yv v theta Htheta.
  unfold BSigma1Conclusion.
  simpl.
  change
    (formula_closedb_ctx (v :: u :: ctx)
      (bounded_forall x xw (RA_Var u)
        (bounded_exists y yv (RA_Var v) theta)) = true).
  apply bounded_forall_closedb_ctx.
  - simpl.
    rewrite Nat.eqb_refl.
    destruct (Nat.eqb u v); reflexivity.
  - apply bounded_exists_closedb_ctx.
    + simpl.
      rewrite Nat.eqb_refl.
      destruct (Nat.eqb v yv), (Nat.eqb v y), (Nat.eqb v x); reflexivity.
    + pose proof
        (formula_closedb_ctx_weaken_many [u; v] (x :: y :: ctx) theta Htheta)
        as Htheta'.
      simpl in Htheta'.
      pose proof
        (formula_closedb_ctx_perm
          (u :: v :: x :: y :: ctx) (y :: x :: v :: u :: ctx) theta
          (Permutation_trans
            (Permutation_sym (Permutation_middle [u; v; x] ctx y))
            (perm_skip y
              (Permutation_trans
                (perm_swap v u (x :: ctx))
                (Permutation_trans
                  (perm_skip v (perm_swap x u ctx))
                  (perm_swap x v (u :: ctx)))))))
        as Hperm.
      rewrite Hperm in Htheta'.
      exact Htheta'.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[The three guard checkers isolate the specific bounded-guard shapes that may appear in a `B Sigma_1` collection instance: the premise guard for `x <= u`, the conclusion copy of that `x`-guard, and the final `y <= v` guard inside the collected witness block.]]@*)

Definition premise_guard_ok (params : list nat) (u x : nat) (guard : RAFormula) : bool :=
  match guard with
  | RA_Exists _ (RA_Eq (RA_Var u') (RA_Add _ _)) =>
      Nat.eqb u u' && bounded_guard_onb (u :: params) x guard
  | _ => false
  end.

(*@c.unicode@[[Conclusion-side `x ≤ u` guard checker: validates shape `∃w. u = x + w` plus bounded-guard side conditions in context `(v :: u :: params)`.]]@*)

Definition conclusion_x_guard_ok
    (params : list nat) (u v x : nat) (guard : RAFormula) : bool :=
  match guard with
  | RA_Exists _ (RA_Eq (RA_Var u') (RA_Add _ _)) =>
      Nat.eqb u u' && bounded_guard_onb (v :: u :: params) x guard
  | _ => false
  end.

(*@c.unicode@[[Conclusion-side `y ≤ v` guard checker: validates shape `∃w. v = y + w` with bounded-guard constraints in context `(x :: v :: u :: params)`.]]@*)

Definition conclusion_y_guard_ok
    (params : list nat) (u v x y : nat) (guard : RAFormula) : bool :=
  match guard with
  | RA_Exists _ (RA_Eq (RA_Var v') (RA_Add _ _)) =>
      Nat.eqb v v' && bounded_guard_onb (x :: v :: u :: params) y guard
  | _ => false
  end.

(*@c.inline@[[`premise_guard_ok_correct` shows that the executable premise-guard test is exact for the intended semantic condition: the guard must be precisely a bounded `x <= u` atom and satisfy the full `BoundedGuardOn` side conditions in the correct context.]]@*)


Lemma premise_guard_ok_correct :
  forall params u x guard,
    premise_guard_ok params u x guard = true <->
    exists witness,
      guard = leq_atom x witness (RA_Var u) /\
      BoundedGuardOn (u :: params) x guard.
Proof.
  intros params u x guard.
  split.
  - destruct guard as [s t| |a b|a b|a b|w psi|w psi];
      cbn [premise_guard_ok]; try discriminate.
    destruct psi as [lhs rhs| |a b|a b|a b|q psi|q psi];
      cbn [premise_guard_ok]; try discriminate.
    destruct lhs as [u'| |u''|u_l u_r];
      cbn [premise_guard_ok]; try discriminate.
    destruct rhs as [x'| |rhs|rhs_l rhs_r];
      cbn [premise_guard_ok]; try discriminate.
    intro Hguard.
    apply andb_true_iff in Hguard.
    destruct Hguard as [Hu Hbounded].
    apply Nat.eqb_eq in Hu.
    subst u'.
    apply bounded_guard_onb_correct in Hbounded.
    destruct Hbounded as [witness [bound [Hshape [Hneq [Hfresh Hclosed]]]]].
    unfold leq_atom in Hshape.
    cbn in Hshape.
    inversion Hshape; subst.
    exists witness.
    split.
    + exact Hshape.
    + exists witness, (RA_Var u).
      split.
      * exact Hshape.
      * split; [exact Hneq| split; assumption].
  - intros [witness [Hguard Hbounded]].
    subst guard.
    unfold leq_atom, mk_exists, mk_eq.
    unfold premise_guard_ok.
    cbn [premise_guard_ok].
    rewrite Nat.eqb_refl.
    change
      (bounded_guard_onb (u :: params) x
        (RA_Exists witness (RA_Eq (RA_Var u) (RA_Add (RA_Var x) (RA_Var witness)))) = true).
    apply (proj2 (bounded_guard_onb_correct (u :: params) x (leq_atom x witness (RA_Var u)))).
    exact Hbounded.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[This is the corresponding adequacy statement for the copied `x <= u` guard appearing in the collection conclusion. The only change is the enlarged ambient context that already contains the collection bound `v`.]]@*)

Lemma conclusion_x_guard_ok_correct :
  forall params u v x guard,
    conclusion_x_guard_ok params u v x guard = true <->
    exists witness,
      guard = leq_atom x witness (RA_Var u) /\
      BoundedGuardOn (v :: u :: params) x guard.
Proof.
  intros params u v x guard.
  split.
  - destruct guard as [s t| |a b|a b|a b|w psi|w psi];
      cbn [conclusion_x_guard_ok]; try discriminate.
    destruct psi as [lhs rhs| |a b|a b|a b|q psi|q psi];
      cbn [conclusion_x_guard_ok]; try discriminate.
    destruct lhs as [u'| |u''|u_l u_r];
      cbn [conclusion_x_guard_ok]; try discriminate.
    destruct rhs as [x'| |rhs|rhs_l rhs_r];
      cbn [conclusion_x_guard_ok]; try discriminate.
    intro Hguard.
    apply andb_true_iff in Hguard.
    destruct Hguard as [Hu Hbounded].
    apply Nat.eqb_eq in Hu.
    subst u'.
    apply bounded_guard_onb_correct in Hbounded.
    destruct Hbounded as [witness [bound [Hshape [Hneq [Hfresh Hclosed]]]]].
    unfold leq_atom in Hshape.
    cbn in Hshape.
    inversion Hshape; subst.
    exists witness.
    split.
    + exact Hshape.
    + exists witness, (RA_Var u).
      split.
      * exact Hshape.
      * split; [exact Hneq| split; assumption].
  - intros [witness [Hguard Hbounded]].
    subst guard.
    unfold leq_atom, mk_exists, mk_eq.
    unfold conclusion_x_guard_ok.
    cbn [conclusion_x_guard_ok].
    rewrite Nat.eqb_refl.
    change
      (bounded_guard_onb (v :: u :: params) x
        (RA_Exists witness (RA_Eq (RA_Var u) (RA_Add (RA_Var x) (RA_Var witness)))) = true).
    apply (proj2 (bounded_guard_onb_correct (v :: u :: params) x (leq_atom x witness (RA_Var u)))).
    exact Hbounded.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[The final guard-correctness lemma handles the inner `y <= v` bound from the collection conclusion. It identifies exactly the executable condition under which the guard has the intended semantic shape in the full parameter context.]]@*)

Lemma conclusion_y_guard_ok_correct :
  forall params u v x y guard,
    conclusion_y_guard_ok params u v x y guard = true <->
    exists witness,
      guard = leq_atom y witness (RA_Var v) /\
      BoundedGuardOn (x :: v :: u :: params) y guard.
Proof.
  intros params u v x y guard.
  split.
  - destruct guard as [s t| |a b|a b|a b|w psi|w psi];
      cbn [conclusion_y_guard_ok]; try discriminate.
    destruct psi as [lhs rhs| |a b|a b|a b|q psi|q psi];
      cbn [conclusion_y_guard_ok]; try discriminate.
    destruct lhs as [v'| |u''|u_l u_r];
      cbn [conclusion_y_guard_ok]; try discriminate.
    destruct rhs as [y'| |rhs|rhs_l rhs_r];
      cbn [conclusion_y_guard_ok]; try discriminate.
    intro Hguard.
    apply andb_true_iff in Hguard.
    destruct Hguard as [Hv Hbounded].
    apply Nat.eqb_eq in Hv.
    subst v'.
    apply bounded_guard_onb_correct in Hbounded.
    destruct Hbounded as [witness [bound [Hshape [Hneq [Hfresh Hclosed]]]]].
    unfold leq_atom in Hshape.
    cbn in Hshape.
    inversion Hshape; subst.
    exists witness.
    split.
    + exact Hshape.
    + exists witness, (RA_Var v).
      split.
      * exact Hshape.
      * split; [exact Hneq| split; assumption].
  - intros [witness [Hguard Hbounded]].
    subst guard.
    unfold leq_atom, mk_exists, mk_eq.
    unfold conclusion_y_guard_ok.
    cbn [conclusion_y_guard_ok].
    rewrite Nat.eqb_refl.
    change
      (bounded_guard_onb (x :: v :: u :: params) y
        (RA_Exists witness (RA_Eq (RA_Var v) (RA_Add (RA_Var y) (RA_Var witness)))) = true).
    apply (proj2 (bounded_guard_onb_correct (x :: v :: u :: params) y (leq_atom y witness (RA_Var v)))).
    exact Hbounded.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[`BSigma1Axiom` is the semantic predicate for bounded `Sigma_1` collection instances. It records the full parameter block, freshness/no-duplication side conditions, the three bounded guards, the exact schema shape, and the requirement that the matrix `theta` be `Delta_0` in the intended context.]]@*)

Definition BSigma1Axiom (phi : RAFormula) : Prop :=
  exists params u x xw_prem xw_concl y yv v theta,
    NoDup (u :: x :: y :: v :: params) /\
    BoundedGuardOn (u :: params) x (leq_atom x xw_prem (RA_Var u)) /\
    BoundedGuardOn (v :: u :: params) x (leq_atom x xw_concl (RA_Var u)) /\
    BoundedGuardOn (x :: v :: u :: params) y (leq_atom y yv (RA_Var v)) /\
    phi = BSigma1InstanceOn params u x xw_prem xw_concl y yv v theta /\
    Delta0On (x :: y :: params) theta.

(*@c.inline@[[`BSigma1Axiomb` is the executable recognizer for bounded `Sigma_1` collection instances. It peels the outer parameter block, isolates the distinguished bound variable `u`, checks the displayed premise and conclusion guards, compares the two matrix occurrences, and finally verifies that the shared matrix is `Delta_0` in the intended context.]]@*)

Definition BSigma1Axiomb (phi : RAFormula) : bool :=
  match peel_forall_prefix phi with
  | (binders, RA_Imp premise conclusion) =>
      match split_last binders with
      | Some (params, u) =>
          match premise with
          | RA_Forall x (RA_Imp guard_x (RA_Exists y theta)) =>
              match conclusion with
              | RA_Exists v
                  (RA_Forall x'
                    (RA_Imp guard_x'
                      (RA_Exists y' (RA_And guard_y theta')))) =>
                  list_nodupb (u :: x :: y :: v :: params) &&
                  Nat.eqb x x' &&
                  Nat.eqb y y' &&
                  premise_guard_ok params u x guard_x &&
                  conclusion_x_guard_ok params u v x guard_x' &&
                  conclusion_y_guard_ok params u v x y guard_y &&
                  formula_eqb theta theta' &&
                  Delta0Onb (x :: y :: params) theta
              | _ => false
              end
          | _ => false
          end
      | None => false
      end
  | _ => false
  end.

(*@c.inline@[[`BSigma1Axiomb_correct` is the adequacy theorem for the executable bounded-collection recognizer. By peeling the outer quantifier block and checking the three displayed guards plus matrix agreement, the boolean test is shown to capture exactly the semantic `BSigma1Axiom` predicate.]]@*)

(*@todo@[[Temporary state: `BSigma1Axiomb_correct` is one of the tactic-heaviest proofs in Register Arithmetic. It currently interleaves deep syntax peeling, repeated boolean decomposition, and witness reconstruction in one script; if this schema evolves, split the guard recovery and quantifier-shape recovery into named local bridges before further changes land here.]]@*)

(*@c.unicode@[[Adequacy bridge for bounded `Σ₁` collection schemas: `BSigma1Axiomb φ = true ↔ BSigma1Axiom φ`.]]@*)

Theorem BSigma1Axiomb_correct :
  forall phi,
    BSigma1Axiomb phi = true <-> BSigma1Axiom phi.
Proof.
  intro phi.
  split.
  - unfold BSigma1Axiomb, BSigma1Axiom.
    intro Haxiom.
    destruct (peel_forall_prefix phi) as [binders core] eqn:Hpeel;
      destruct core as [s t| |premise conclusion|a b|a b|x psi|x psi];
      simpl in Haxiom; try discriminate Haxiom.
    destruct (split_last binders) as [[params u]|] eqn:Hsplit;
      simpl in Haxiom; try discriminate Haxiom.
    destruct premise as [s t| |a b|a b|a b|x premise_tail|x premise_tail];
      simpl in Haxiom; try discriminate Haxiom.
    destruct premise_tail as [s t| |guard_x premise_exists|a b|a b|x' psi'|x' psi'];
      simpl in Haxiom; try discriminate Haxiom.
    destruct premise_exists as [s t| |a b|a b|a b|x' psi'|y theta];
      simpl in Haxiom; try discriminate Haxiom.
    destruct conclusion as [s t| |a b|a b|a b|x' psi'|v concl_tail];
      simpl in Haxiom; try discriminate Haxiom.
    destruct concl_tail as [s t| |a b|a b|a b|x' concl_body|x' concl_body];
      simpl in Haxiom; try discriminate Haxiom.
    destruct concl_body as [s t| |guard_x' concl_exists|a b|a b|x'' psi''|x'' psi''];
      simpl in Haxiom; try discriminate Haxiom.
    destruct concl_exists as [s t| |a b|a b|a b|x'' psi''|y' concl_and];
      simpl in Haxiom; try discriminate Haxiom.
    destruct concl_and as [s t| |a b|guard_y theta'|a b|x'' psi''|x'' psi''];
      simpl in Haxiom; try discriminate Haxiom.
	    apply andb_true_iff in Haxiom.
	    destruct Haxiom as [Haxiom Hdelta].
	    apply andb_true_iff in Haxiom.
	    destruct Haxiom as [Haxiom Htheta].
	    apply andb_true_iff in Haxiom.
	    destruct Haxiom as [Haxiom Hguard_yb].
	    apply andb_true_iff in Haxiom.
	    destruct Haxiom as [Haxiom Hguard_conclb].
	    apply andb_true_iff in Haxiom.
	    destruct Haxiom as [Haxiom Hguard_premb].
	    apply andb_true_iff in Haxiom.
	    destruct Haxiom as [Haxiom Hyy].
	    apply andb_true_iff in Haxiom.
	    destruct Haxiom as [Hnodup Hxx].
	    change (list_nodupb (u :: x :: y :: v :: params) = true) in Hnodup.
	    apply list_nodupb_correct in Hnodup.
    apply Nat.eqb_eq in Hxx.
    apply Nat.eqb_eq in Hyy.
    apply premise_guard_ok_correct in Hguard_premb.
    apply conclusion_x_guard_ok_correct in Hguard_conclb.
    apply conclusion_y_guard_ok_correct in Hguard_yb.
    apply formula_eqb_true_iff in Htheta.
    apply Delta0Onb_correct in Hdelta.
    destruct Hguard_premb as [xw_prem [Hguard_prem_eq Hguard_prem]].
    destruct Hguard_conclb as [xw_concl [Hguard_concl_eq Hguard_concl]].
    destruct Hguard_yb as [yv [Hguard_y_eq Hguard_y_sem]].
    pose proof
      (split_last_mk_forall_chain
        binders params u
        (RA_Imp
          (RA_Forall x (RA_Imp guard_x (RA_Exists y theta)))
          (RA_Exists v
            (RA_Forall x'
              (RA_Imp guard_x' (RA_Exists y' (RA_And guard_y theta'))))))
        Hsplit)
      as Hchain.
    pose proof (peel_forall_prefix_spec phi) as Hspec.
    rewrite Hpeel in Hspec.
	    simpl in Hspec.
	    rewrite Hchain in Hspec.
	    subst x' y' theta'.
	    rewrite Hguard_prem_eq, Hguard_concl_eq, Hguard_y_eq in Hspec.
	    rewrite Hguard_prem_eq in Hguard_prem.
	    rewrite Hguard_concl_eq in Hguard_concl.
	    rewrite Hguard_y_eq in Hguard_y_sem.
	    exists params, u, x, xw_prem, xw_concl, y, yv, v, theta.
	    split.
	    { exact Hnodup. }
	    split.
	    { exact Hguard_prem. }
    split.
    { exact Hguard_concl. }
    split.
	    { exact Hguard_y_sem. }
    split.
    { exact Hspec. }
    { exact Hdelta. }
  - intros [params [u [x [xw_prem [xw_concl [y [yv [v [theta [Hnodup [Hguard_prem [Hguard_concl [Hguard_y [Hphi Hdelta]]]]]]]]]]]]]].
    subst phi.
    unfold BSigma1Axiomb, BSigma1InstanceOn.
    rewrite peel_forall_prefix_mk_forall_chain.
    simpl.
    rewrite split_last_app_singleton.
    simpl.
    pose proof
      (proj2 (list_nodupb_correct (u :: x :: y :: v :: params)) Hnodup)
      as Hnodupb.
    pose proof
      (proj2 (premise_guard_ok_correct params u x (leq_atom x xw_prem (RA_Var u)))
        (ex_intro _ xw_prem (conj eq_refl Hguard_prem)))
      as Hguard_premb.
    pose proof
      (proj2 (conclusion_x_guard_ok_correct params u v x (leq_atom x xw_concl (RA_Var u)))
        (ex_intro _ xw_concl (conj eq_refl Hguard_concl)))
      as Hguard_conclb.
	    pose proof
	      (proj2 (conclusion_y_guard_ok_correct params u v x y (leq_atom y yv (RA_Var v)))
	        (ex_intro _ yv (conj eq_refl Hguard_y)))
	      as Hguard_yb.
	    cbn [list_nodupb existsb] in Hnodupb.
	    cbn [premise_guard_ok conclusion_x_guard_ok conclusion_y_guard_ok
	      bounded_guard_onb leq_atom mk_exists mk_eq existsb term_closedb_ctx]
	      in Hguard_premb, Hguard_conclb, Hguard_yb.
	    repeat rewrite Nat.eqb_refl in Hguard_premb, Hguard_conclb, Hguard_yb.
	    cbn in Hguard_premb, Hguard_conclb, Hguard_yb.
	    repeat rewrite andb_true_r in Hguard_premb, Hguard_conclb, Hguard_yb.
	    repeat rewrite andb_true_l in Hguard_premb, Hguard_conclb, Hguard_yb.
	    apply andb_true_iff.
	    split.
	    + apply andb_true_iff.
	      split.
	      * apply andb_true_iff.
	        split.
	        { apply andb_true_iff.
	          split.
	          - apply andb_true_iff.
	            split.
	            + apply andb_true_iff.
	              split.
	              * apply andb_true_iff.
	                split.
	                { exact Hnodupb. }
	                { exact (Nat.eqb_refl x). }
	              * exact (Nat.eqb_refl y).
	            + repeat rewrite Nat.eqb_refl.
	              cbn.
	              exact Hguard_premb.
	          - repeat rewrite Nat.eqb_refl.
	            cbn.
	            exact Hguard_conclb. }
	        { repeat rewrite Nat.eqb_refl.
	          cbn.
	          exact Hguard_yb. }
	      * exact (formula_eqb_refl theta).
	    + exact (proj2 (Delta0Onb_correct (x :: y :: params) theta) Hdelta).
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[Every semantic bounded `Sigma_1` collection axiom is a sentence. The proof reduces sentencehood to the closedness of the `Delta_0` matrix together with the closure lemmas for the collection premise and conclusion builders.]]@*)
(*@c.unicode@[[Collection-schema closure: BSigma1Axiom(φ) ⇒ Sentence(φ).]]@*)

Corollary BSigma1Axiom_sentence :
  forall phi,
    BSigma1Axiom phi ->
    Sentence phi.
Proof.
  intros phi
    [params [u [x [xw_prem [xw_concl [y [yv [v [theta
      [_ [_ [_ [_ [Hphi Hdelta]]]]]]]]]]]]]].
  subst phi.
  unfold Sentence, D001_03__RA.Sentence, sentenceb, BSigma1InstanceOn.
  rewrite formula_closedb_ctx_mk_forall_chain.
  simpl.
  repeat rewrite app_nil_r.
  fold (BSigma1Premise u x xw_prem y theta).
  fold (BSigma1Conclusion u x xw_concl y yv v theta).
  pose proof (Delta0On_closedb (x :: y :: params) theta Hdelta) as Htheta.
  pose proof
    (formula_closedb_ctx_perm
      (x :: y :: params) (x :: y :: rev params) theta
      (perm_skip x (perm_skip y (Permutation_rev_self params))))
    as Hperm.
  rewrite Hperm in Htheta.
  change
    (formula_closedb_ctx (u :: rev params) (BSigma1Premise u x xw_prem y theta) &&
     formula_closedb_ctx (u :: rev params) (BSigma1Conclusion u x xw_concl y yv v theta) = true).
  rewrite (BSigma1Premise_closedb_ctx (rev params) u x xw_prem y theta Htheta).
  rewrite (BSigma1Conclusion_closedb_ctx (rev params) u x xw_concl y yv v theta Htheta).
  reflexivity.
Qed.


(* ---- theories/D001/D001_07__RA_Theory.v ---- *)

(*@file@*)
(*@H.1@[[Proofcase / D001 / RA Theory]]@*)
(*@H.2@[[Overview]]@*)
(*@p.l@[[This construction is the internal Register Arithmetic closure layer for D001.]]@*)
(*@p.l@[[It has two stabilized jobs inside the arithmetic folder. First, it combines the fixed additive base, bounded induction, and bounded `Sigma_1` collection surfaces from `D001_06__Schemas` into the final recursive Register Arithmetic theory predicate. Second, it packages the guarded pairing interface, constructor-code normal forms, pair lower bounds, and code-budget lemmas that downstream decoders and proof-theory routes need.]]@*)
(*@p.l@[[Its downstream exposure is provided by `D001_14__RA_Language`; this route remains internal, but it is where the recursive theory closure and the arithmetic coding-stabilization facts are assembled before that public re-export.]]@*)
(*@H.2@[[Contents]]@*)
(*@ptoclist.roman@[[RECURSIVE THEORY PREDICATE]][[Combined semantic predicate, executable recognizer, adequacy theorem, and sentence theorem for final Register Arithmetic.]]@*)
(*@ptoclist.roman@[[CODING STABILIZATION]][[Guarded pairing interface, constructor-code normal forms, pair lower bounds, and budget lemmas later re-exported through the public arithmetic boundary.]]@*)

From D001 Require Export D001_06__Schemas.

(*@box.section@[[RECURSIVE THEORY PREDICATE]]@*)

(*@c.inline@[[`RATheoryAxiom` is the final semantic Register Arithmetic theory predicate. A formula belongs to the theory exactly when it is either one of the fixed additive base axioms, a bounded-induction axiom, or a bounded `Sigma_1` collection axiom.]]@*)
(*@c.unicode@[[Combined semantic theory predicate: RATheoryAxiom(φ) :≡ BaseAxiom(φ) ∨ InductionAxiom(φ) ∨ BSigma1Axiom(φ).]]@*)

Definition RATheoryAxiom : RAFormula -> Prop :=
  fun phi => BaseAxiom phi \/ InductionAxiom phi \/ BSigma1Axiom phi.

(*@c.inline@[[`RATheoryAxiomb` is the executable recognizer for the same recursive theory surface. It accepts exactly when one of the three constituent public schema recognizers accepts.]]@*)

Definition RATheoryAxiomb : RAFormula -> bool :=
  fun phi => BaseAxiomb phi || InductionAxiomb phi || BSigma1Axiomb phi.

(*@c.inline@[[The correctness theorem states that the combined recognizer is exact for the combined semantic theory predicate. This is the final boolean-to-Prop bridge for arithmetic theory membership before the public API route re-exports it.]]@*)
(*@c.unicode@[[Recognizer adequacy at theory level: RATheoryAxiomb(φ)=true ↔ RATheoryAxiom(φ).]]@*)

Theorem RATheoryAxiomb_correct :
  forall phi,
    RATheoryAxiomb phi = true <-> RATheoryAxiom phi.
Proof.
  intro phi.
  unfold RATheoryAxiomb, RATheoryAxiom.
  split.
  - intro H.
    apply orb_true_iff in H.
    destruct H as [Hleft | Hcol].
    + apply orb_true_iff in Hleft.
      destruct Hleft as [Hbase | Hind].
      * left.
        apply BaseAxiomb_correct.
        exact Hbase.
      * right.
        left.
        apply InductionAxiomb_correct.
        exact Hind.
    + right.
      right.
      apply BSigma1Axiomb_correct.
      exact Hcol.
  - intros [Hbase | [Hind | Hcol]].
    + apply orb_true_iff.
      left.
      apply orb_true_iff.
      left.
      apply BaseAxiomb_correct.
      exact Hbase.
    + apply orb_true_iff.
      left.
      apply orb_true_iff.
      right.
      apply InductionAxiomb_correct.
      exact Hind.
    + apply orb_true_iff.
      right.
      apply BSigma1Axiomb_correct.
      exact Hcol.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[Every semantic Register Arithmetic axiom is a sentence, because each of the three constituent axiom families is already known to be closed.]]@*)
(*@c.unicode@[[Sentence closure of combined theory: RATheoryAxiom(φ) ⇒ Sentence(φ).]]@*)

Corollary RATheoryAxiom_sentence :
  forall phi,
    RATheoryAxiom phi ->
    Sentence phi.
Proof.
  intros phi [Hbase | [Hind | Hcol]].
  - apply BaseAxiom_sentence.
    exact Hbase.
  - apply InductionAxiom_sentence.
    exact Hind.
  - apply BSigma1Axiom_sentence.
    exact Hcol.
Qed.

(*@c.section@[[CODING STABILIZATION]]@*)

(*@c.inline@[[This construction also stabilizes the quantitative coding interface used downstream. The concrete Fibonacci engine remains in `D001_01__Carryless_Pairing` and `D001_02__Base_Fibonacci`, but its guarded pairing consequences, constructor-code normal forms, pair lower bounds, and code-budget lemmas are proved once here and then re-exported through the public arithmetic boundary.]]@*)

Definition checked_unpair (n : nat) : option (nat * nat) :=
  let '(a, b) := base_unpair n in
  if Nat.eqb (base_pair a b) n then Some (a, b) else None.

(*@c.unicode@[[Soundness of guarded decoding: `checked_unpair n = Some(a,b) ⟹ base_pair a b = n`.]]@*)

Lemma checked_unpair_Some :
  forall n a b,
    checked_unpair n = Some (a, b) ->
    base_pair a b = n.
Proof.
  intros n a b H.
  unfold checked_unpair in H.
  destruct (base_unpair n) as [a' b'] eqn:Hup.
  destruct (Nat.eqb (base_pair a' b') n) eqn:Heq; try discriminate.
  inversion H.
  subst.
  apply Nat.eqb_eq.
  exact Heq.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[Completeness on encoded pairs: `checked_unpair(base_pair a b) = Some(a,b)`.]]@*)

Lemma checked_unpair_pair :
  forall a b,
    checked_unpair (base_pair a b) = Some (a, b).
Proof.
  intros a b.
  unfold checked_unpair.
  rewrite base_unpair_pair_closed.
  rewrite Nat.eqb_refl.
  reflexivity.
Qed.

(*@c.unicode@[[Guarded decode uniqueness: if `checked_unpair n` yields both `(a,b)` and `(a',b')`, then `a=a' ∧ b=b'`.]]@*)

Lemma checked_unpair_unique :
  forall n a b a' b',
    checked_unpair n = Some (a, b) ->
    checked_unpair n = Some (a', b') ->
    a = a' /\ b = b'.
Proof.
  intros n a b a' b' H1 H2.
  rewrite H1 in H2.
  inversion H2; subst.
  split; reflexivity.
Qed.

(*@c.unicode@[[Tag-1 reconstruction from guarded decode: `checked_unpair k = Some(tag,payload) ⟹ code_tag1 tag payload = S k`.]]@*)

Lemma checked_unpair_code_tag1 :
  forall k tag payload,
    checked_unpair k = Some (tag, payload) ->
    code_tag1 tag payload = S k.
Proof.
  intros k tag payload Hpair.
  unfold code_tag1.
  f_equal.
  apply checked_unpair_Some in Hpair.
  exact Hpair.
Qed.

(*@c.unicode@[[Tag-2 reconstruction from guarded decode: `checked_unpair k = Some(tag,⟨a,b⟩) ⟹ code_tag2 tag a b = S k`.]]@*)

Lemma checked_unpair_code_tag2 :
  forall k tag a b,
    checked_unpair k = Some (tag, base_pair a b) ->
    code_tag2 tag a b = S k.
Proof.
  intros k tag a b Hpair.
  unfold code_tag2.
  f_equal.
  apply checked_unpair_Some in Hpair.
  exact Hpair.
Qed.

(*@c.unicode@[[Constructor normal form (variable case): `code_term(Var x) = code_tag1 0 x`.]]@*)

Lemma code_term_var_eq :
  forall x,
    code_term (RA_Var x) = code_tag1 0 x.
Proof. reflexivity. Qed.

(*@c.unicode@[[Constructor normal form (zero case): `code_term(0) = code_tag1 1 0`.]]@*)

Lemma code_term_zero_eq :
  code_term RA_Zero = code_tag1 1 0.
Proof. reflexivity. Qed.

(*@c.unicode@[[Constructor normal form (successor case): `code_term(S u) = code_tag1 2 (code_term u)`.]]@*)

Lemma code_term_succ_eq :
  forall u,
    code_term (RA_Succ u) = code_tag1 2 (code_term u).
Proof. reflexivity. Qed.

(*@c.unicode@[[Constructor normal form (addition case): `code_term(u + v) = code_tag2 3 (code_term u) (code_term v)`.]]@*)

Lemma code_term_add_eq :
  forall u v,
    code_term (RA_Add u v) = code_tag2 3 (code_term u) (code_term v).
Proof. reflexivity. Qed.

(*@c.unicode@[[Formula-coding normal form (equality): `code_formula(s = t) = code_tag2 0 (code_term s) (code_term t)`.]]@*)

Lemma code_formula_eq_eq :
  forall s t,
    code_formula (RA_Eq s t) = code_tag2 0 (code_term s) (code_term t).
Proof. reflexivity. Qed.

(*@c.unicode@[[Formula-coding normal form (bottom): `code_formula(⊥) = code_tag1 1 0`.]]@*)

Lemma code_formula_bot_eq :
  code_formula RA_Bot = code_tag1 1 0.
Proof. reflexivity. Qed.

(*@c.unicode@[[Formula-coding normal form (implication): `code_formula(a → b) = code_tag2 2 (code_formula a) (code_formula b)`.]]@*)

Lemma code_formula_imp_eq :
  forall a b,
    code_formula (RA_Imp a b) = code_tag2 2 (code_formula a) (code_formula b).
Proof. reflexivity. Qed.

(*@c.unicode@[[Formula-coding normal form (conjunction): `code_formula(a ∧ b) = code_tag2 3 (code_formula a) (code_formula b)`.]]@*)

Lemma code_formula_and_eq :
  forall a b,
    code_formula (RA_And a b) = code_tag2 3 (code_formula a) (code_formula b).
Proof. reflexivity. Qed.

(*@c.unicode@[[Formula-coding normal form (disjunction): `code_formula(a ∨ b) = code_tag2 4 (code_formula a) (code_formula b)`.]]@*)

Lemma code_formula_or_eq :
  forall a b,
    code_formula (RA_Or a b) = code_tag2 4 (code_formula a) (code_formula b).
Proof. reflexivity. Qed.

(*@c.unicode@[[Formula-coding normal form (universal quantifier): `code_formula(∀x.body) = code_tag2 5 x (code_formula body)`.]]@*)

Lemma code_formula_forall_eq :
  forall x body,
    code_formula (RA_Forall x body) = code_tag2 5 x (code_formula body).
Proof. reflexivity. Qed.

(*@c.unicode@[[Formula-coding normal form (existential quantifier): `code_formula(∃x.body) = code_tag2 6 x (code_formula body)`.]]@*)

Lemma code_formula_exists_eq :
  forall x body,
    code_formula (RA_Exists x body) = code_tag2 6 x (code_formula body).
Proof. reflexivity. Qed.

(*@c.unicode@[[Monotone doubling on support indices: `sum_fib(map two xs) ≥ sum_fib xs`.]]@*)

Lemma sum_fib_map_two_ge :
  forall xs,
    sum_fib (map two xs) >= sum_fib xs.
Proof.
  induction xs as [|a xs IH]; simpl.
  - lia.
  - assert (Hfib : fib a <= fib (two a)).
    {
      apply fib_le_of_le.
      unfold two.
      lia.
    }
    lia.
Qed.

(*@c.unicode@[[Odd-band monotonicity bound: for `all_ge_2 xs`, mapping `j ↦ Bx + (2j−1)` preserves a lower bound on evaluated sum (`≥`).]]@*)

Lemma sum_fib_map_odd_ge :
  forall Bx xs,
    all_ge_2 xs ->
    sum_fib (map (fun j => Bx + two_j_minus1 j) xs) >= sum_fib xs.
Proof.
  intros Bx xs.
  induction xs as [|j xs IH]; intro Hge; simpl.
  - lia.
  - destruct Hge as [Hj2 Hxs].
    assert (Hjle : j <= Bx + two_j_minus1 j).
    {
      assert (Hodd_ge : j <= two_j_minus1 j).
      {
        rewrite two_j_minus1_formula.
        lia.
      }
      lia.
    }
    assert (Hfib : fib j <= fib (Bx + two_j_minus1 j)).
    {
      apply fib_le_of_le.
      exact Hjle.
    }
    specialize (IH Hxs).
    lia.
Qed.

(*@c.unicode@[[Left-coordinate lower bound for pairing: `x ≤ base_pair x y`.]]@*)

Lemma base_pair_ge_l :
  forall x y,
    x <= base_pair x y.
Proof.
  intros x y.
  unfold base_pair.
  rewrite pair_base_as_odd_even_sum.
  rewrite sum_fib_app.
  assert (Hx : x <= sum_fib (even_band base_params x)).
  {
    rewrite <- Z0_sound at 1.
    unfold even_band, base_params.
    apply sum_fib_map_two_ge.
  }
  lia.
Qed.

(*@c.unicode@[[Right-coordinate lower bound for pairing: `y ≤ base_pair x y`.]]@*)

Lemma base_pair_ge_r :
  forall x y,
    y <= base_pair x y.
Proof.
  intros x y.
  unfold base_pair.
  rewrite pair_base_as_odd_even_sum.
  rewrite sum_fib_app.
  assert (Hy : y <= sum_fib (odd_band base_params x y)).
  {
    rewrite <- Z0_sound at 1.
    unfold odd_band, base_params.
    apply sum_fib_map_odd_ge.
    destruct (Z0_valid y) as [_ [_ Hge]].
    exact Hge.
  }
  lia.
Qed.

(*@c.unicode@[[Combined payload bound: `x + y ≤ base_pair x y`.]]@*)

Lemma base_pair_ge_sum :
  forall x y,
    x + y <= base_pair x y.
Proof.
  intros x y.
  unfold base_pair.
  rewrite pair_base_as_odd_even_sum.
  rewrite sum_fib_app.
  assert (Hx : x <= sum_fib (even_band base_params x)).
  {
    rewrite <- Z0_sound at 1.
    unfold even_band, base_params.
    apply sum_fib_map_two_ge.
  }
  assert (Hy : y <= sum_fib (odd_band base_params x y)).
  {
    rewrite <- Z0_sound at 1.
    unfold odd_band, base_params.
    apply sum_fib_map_odd_ge.
    destruct (Z0_valid y) as [_ [_ Hge]].
    exact Hge.
  }
  lia.
Qed.

(*@c.unicode@[[Nested-pair payload bound (left component): `a ≤ ⟨tag,⟨a,b⟩⟩`.]]@*)

Lemma base_pair_ge_payload_l :
  forall tag a b,
    a <= base_pair tag (base_pair a b).
Proof.
  intros tag a b.
  eapply Nat.le_trans.
  - apply base_pair_ge_l.
  - apply base_pair_ge_r.
Qed.

(*@c.unicode@[[Nested-pair payload bound (right component): `b ≤ ⟨tag,⟨a,b⟩⟩`.]]@*)

Lemma base_pair_ge_payload_r :
  forall tag a b,
    b <= base_pair tag (base_pair a b).
Proof.
  intros tag a b.
  eapply Nat.le_trans.
  - apply base_pair_ge_r.
  - apply base_pair_ge_r.
Qed.

(*@c.unicode@[[Quantifier-body budget lower bound: `body ≤ ⟨tag,⟨x,body⟩⟩`.]]@*)

Lemma base_pair_ge_quant_body :
  forall tag x body,
    body <= base_pair tag (base_pair x body).
Proof.
  intros tag x body.
  apply base_pair_ge_payload_r.
Qed.

(*@c.unicode@[[List-code head bound: `h ≤ ⟨h,t⟩`.]]@*)

Lemma base_pair_ge_list_head :
  forall h t,
    h <= base_pair h t.
Proof.
  intros h t.
  apply base_pair_ge_l.
Qed.

(*@c.unicode@[[List-code tail bound: `t ≤ ⟨h,t⟩`.]]@*)

Lemma base_pair_ge_list_tail :
  forall h t,
    t <= base_pair h t.
Proof.
  intros h t.
  apply base_pair_ge_r.
Qed.

(*@c.unicode@[[Generic list-code head budget: `S x ≤ code_nat_list (x :: xs)`.]]@*)

Lemma code_nat_list_head_budget :
  forall x xs,
    S x <= code_nat_list (x :: xs).
Proof.
  intros x xs.
  simpl.
  pose proof (base_pair_ge_list_head x (code_nat_list xs)) as H.
  lia.
Qed.

(*@c.unicode@[[Generic list-code tail budget: `S(code_nat_list xs) ≤ code_nat_list (x :: xs)`.]]@*)

Lemma code_nat_list_tail_budget :
  forall x xs,
    S (code_nat_list xs) <= code_nat_list (x :: xs).
Proof.
  intros x xs.
  simpl.
  pose proof (base_pair_ge_list_tail x (code_nat_list xs)) as H.
  lia.
Qed.

(*@c.unicode@[[Successor-code budget bound: `S(code_term u) ≤ code_term(S u)`.]]@*)

Lemma code_term_succ_budget :
  forall u,
    S (code_term u) <= code_term (RA_Succ u).
Proof.
  intro u.
  rewrite code_term_succ_eq.
  unfold code_tag1.
  pose proof (base_pair_ge_r 2 (code_term u)) as H.
  lia.
Qed.

(*@c.unicode@[[Inner successor payload bound: `S(code_term u) ≤ ⟨2, code_term u⟩`.]]@*)

Lemma code_term_succ_inner_budget :
  forall u,
    S (code_term u) <= base_pair 2 (code_term u).
Proof.
  intro u.
  pose proof (base_pair_ge_sum 2 (code_term u)) as H.
  lia.
Qed.

(*@c.unicode@[[Addition-code left budget: `S(code_term u) ≤ code_term(u + v)`.]]@*)

Lemma code_term_add_left_budget :
  forall u v,
    S (code_term u) <= code_term (RA_Add u v).
Proof.
  intros u v.
  rewrite code_term_add_eq.
  unfold code_tag2.
  pose proof (base_pair_ge_payload_l 3 (code_term u) (code_term v)) as H.
  lia.
Qed.

(*@c.unicode@[[Addition-code right budget: `S(code_term v) ≤ code_term(u + v)`.]]@*)

Lemma code_term_add_right_budget :
  forall u v,
    S (code_term v) <= code_term (RA_Add u v).
Proof.
  intros u v.
  rewrite code_term_add_eq.
  unfold code_tag2.
  pose proof (base_pair_ge_payload_r 3 (code_term u) (code_term v)) as H.
  lia.
Qed.

(*@c.unicode@[[Equality-code left payload budget: `S(code_term s) ≤ code_formula(s = t)`.]]@*)

Lemma code_formula_eq_left_budget :
  forall s t,
    S (code_term s) <= code_formula (RA_Eq s t).
Proof.
  intros s t.
  rewrite code_formula_eq_eq.
  unfold code_tag2.
  pose proof (base_pair_ge_payload_l 0 (code_term s) (code_term t)) as H.
  lia.
Qed.

(*@c.unicode@[[Equality-code right payload budget: `S(code_term t) ≤ code_formula(s = t)`.]]@*)

Lemma code_formula_eq_right_budget :
  forall s t,
    S (code_term t) <= code_formula (RA_Eq s t).
Proof.
  intros s t.
  rewrite code_formula_eq_eq.
  unfold code_tag2.
  pose proof (base_pair_ge_payload_r 0 (code_term s) (code_term t)) as H.
  lia.
Qed.

(*@c.unicode@[[Implication-code left budget: `S(code_formula a) ≤ code_formula(a → b)`.]]@*)

Lemma code_formula_imp_left_budget :
  forall a b,
    S (code_formula a) <= code_formula (RA_Imp a b).
Proof.
  intros a b.
  rewrite code_formula_imp_eq.
  unfold code_tag2.
  pose proof (base_pair_ge_payload_l 2 (code_formula a) (code_formula b)) as H.
  lia.
Qed.

(*@c.unicode@[[Implication-code right budget: `S(code_formula b) ≤ code_formula(a → b)`.]]@*)

Lemma code_formula_imp_right_budget :
  forall a b,
    S (code_formula b) <= code_formula (RA_Imp a b).
Proof.
  intros a b.
  rewrite code_formula_imp_eq.
  unfold code_tag2.
  pose proof (base_pair_ge_payload_r 2 (code_formula a) (code_formula b)) as H.
  lia.
Qed.

(*@c.unicode@[[Conjunction-code left budget: `S(code_formula a) ≤ code_formula(a ∧ b)`.]]@*)

Lemma code_formula_and_left_budget :
  forall a b,
    S (code_formula a) <= code_formula (RA_And a b).
Proof.
  intros a b.
  rewrite code_formula_and_eq.
  unfold code_tag2.
  pose proof (base_pair_ge_payload_l 3 (code_formula a) (code_formula b)) as H.
  lia.
Qed.

(*@c.unicode@[[Conjunction-code right budget: `S(code_formula b) ≤ code_formula(a ∧ b)`.]]@*)

Lemma code_formula_and_right_budget :
  forall a b,
    S (code_formula b) <= code_formula (RA_And a b).
Proof.
  intros a b.
  rewrite code_formula_and_eq.
  unfold code_tag2.
  pose proof (base_pair_ge_payload_r 3 (code_formula a) (code_formula b)) as H.
  lia.
Qed.

(*@c.unicode@[[Disjunction-code left budget: `S(code_formula a) ≤ code_formula(a ∨ b)`.]]@*)

Lemma code_formula_or_left_budget :
  forall a b,
    S (code_formula a) <= code_formula (RA_Or a b).
Proof.
  intros a b.
  rewrite code_formula_or_eq.
  unfold code_tag2.
  pose proof (base_pair_ge_payload_l 4 (code_formula a) (code_formula b)) as H.
  lia.
Qed.

(*@c.unicode@[[Disjunction-code right budget: `S(code_formula b) ≤ code_formula(a ∨ b)`.]]@*)

Lemma code_formula_or_right_budget :
  forall a b,
    S (code_formula b) <= code_formula (RA_Or a b).
Proof.
  intros a b.
  rewrite code_formula_or_eq.
  unfold code_tag2.
  pose proof (base_pair_ge_payload_r 4 (code_formula a) (code_formula b)) as H.
  lia.
Qed.

(*@c.unicode@[[Universal-body budget: `S(code_formula body) ≤ code_formula(∀x.body)`.]]@*)

Lemma code_formula_forall_body_budget :
  forall x body,
    S (code_formula body) <= code_formula (RA_Forall x body).
Proof.
  intros x body.
  rewrite code_formula_forall_eq.
  unfold code_tag2.
  pose proof (base_pair_ge_quant_body 5 x (code_formula body)) as H.
  lia.
Qed.

(*@c.unicode@[[Existential-body budget: `S(code_formula body) ≤ code_formula(∃x.body)`.]]@*)

Lemma code_formula_exists_body_budget :
  forall x body,
    S (code_formula body) <= code_formula (RA_Exists x body).
Proof.
  intros x body.
  rewrite code_formula_exists_eq.
  unfold code_tag2.
  pose proof (base_pair_ge_quant_body 6 x (code_formula body)) as H.
  lia.
Qed.

(*@c.unicode@[[Proof-code head budget: the encoded first formula contributes at least `S(code_formula φ)` to `code_nat_list(map code_formula (φ :: pf))`.]]@*)

Lemma code_proof_head_budget :
  forall phi pf,
    S (code_formula phi) <= code_nat_list (map code_formula (phi :: pf)).
Proof.
  intros phi pf.
  simpl.
  pose proof (base_pair_ge_list_head (code_formula phi) (code_nat_list (map code_formula pf))) as H.
  lia.
Qed.

(*@c.unicode@[[Proof-code tail budget: the encoded tail contributes at least `S(code_nat_list(map code_formula pf))` inside `code_nat_list(map code_formula (φ :: pf))`.]]@*)

Lemma code_proof_tail_budget :
  forall phi pf,
    S (code_nat_list (map code_formula pf)) <= code_nat_list (map code_formula (phi :: pf)).
Proof.
  intros phi pf.
  simpl.
  pose proof (base_pair_ge_list_tail (code_formula phi) (code_nat_list (map code_formula pf))) as H.
  lia.
Qed.


(* ---- theories/D001/D001_08__Proof_Theory_Input.v ---- *)

(*@file@*)
(*@H.1@[[Proofcase / D001 / Proof-Theory Input]]@*)
(*@H.2@[[Overview]]@*)
(*@p.l@[[This route fixes the local abstract input interface for the additive proof theory.]]@*)
(*@todo@[[Temporary state: the folder does not import Register Arithmetic directly. Instead it is parameterized over this stable object-theory surface, and the concrete Register Arithmetic instantiation is deferred to the internal realization route `T002/CIC__Provability_Realization/R01__Register_Arithmetic_Input`, which is then consumed by `T002/CIC`.]]@*)
(*@p.l@[[The interface includes only the syntax, coding, and recursive arithmetic-theory facts needed by the additive proof checker and its proof predicates.]]@*)
(*@H.2@[[Contents]]@*)
(*@ptoclist.roman@[[ABSTRACT OBJECT LANGUAGE]][[Formulas, stable implication/bottom builders, and the implication view used by the checker.]]@*)
(*@ptoclist.roman@[[ABSTRACT CODING SURFACE]][[Pairing, formula coding, list coding, and the injectivity/closure lemmas needed by the numbering layer.]]@*)
(*@ptoclist.roman@[[ABSTRACT THEORY SURFACE]][[The recursive arithmetic-theory predicate and recognizer used by the Hilbert evaluator.]]@*)

From D001 Require Export D001_07__RA_Theory.

(*@c.inline@[[The additive proof theory is parameterized by one abstract input signature. This module type isolates exactly the object-language, coding, decoding, and recursive-theory data that the later numbering, checker, proof-predicate, and theoremhood routes require.]]@*)
(*@c.unicode@[[Abstract proof-theory input signature]]@*)

Module Type INPUT.

  (*@c.inline@[[The abstract object language enters through a single formula type. Every later proof-theory notion in this folder is built over this type and does not assume any more concrete syntax.]]@*)
  (*@c.unicode@[[Object-language carrier type: RAFormula]]@*)

  Parameter RAFormula : Type.

  (*@c.inline@[[Only implication and bottom are primitive logical constructors at this interface. They are the minimal public syntax needed by the Hilbert checker and proof-predicate layers.]]@*)
  (*@c.unicode@[[Primitive logical constructors: Imp and Bot]]@*)

  Parameter Imp : RAFormula -> RAFormula -> RAFormula.
  
  Parameter Bot : RAFormula.

  (*@c.inline@[[The checker also needs an executable implication view. `as_imp` attempts to read a formula as an implication, while `as_imp_Some` states that every successful readout really came from the public implication constructor.]]@*)
  (*@c.unicode@[[Implication recognizer: as_imp φ = Some(a,b) ⇒ φ = Imp a b]]@*)

  Parameter as_imp : RAFormula -> option (RAFormula * RAFormula).

  Parameter as_imp_Some :
    forall phi a b,
      as_imp phi = Some (a, b) ->
      phi = Imp a b.

  (*@c.inline@[[Conversely, the public implication constructor must decode correctly through the view. `as_imp_Imp` is the direct completeness law for the implication recognizer.]]@*)  
  (*@c.unicode@[[Recognizer completeness: as_imp (Imp a b) = Some(a,b)]]@*)

  Parameter as_imp_Imp :
    forall a b,
      as_imp (Imp a b) = Some (a, b).

  (*@c.inline@[[Decidable equality on formulas is part of the abstract contract because the executable checker performs finite searches and equality tests over formulas.]]@*)
  (*@c.unicode@[[Decidable formula equality service: RAFormula_eq_dec]]@*)

  Parameter RAFormula_eq_dec :
    forall (u v : RAFormula), {u = v} + {u <> v}.

  (*@c.inline@[[The numbering surface begins with the distinguished pairing interface and the formula-coding function. The roundtrip and injectivity laws here are the basic recovery facts used throughout the carryless Godel-numbering layer.]]@*)
  (*@c.unicode@[[Pairing/coding surface: base_pair, base_unpair, code_formula, injectivity]]@*)

  Parameter base_pair : nat -> nat -> nat.
  Parameter base_unpair : nat -> nat * nat.

  (*@c.unicode@[[Pair round-trip axiom in the abstract signature: base_unpair(base_pair x y) = (x,y).]]@*)
  Parameter base_unpair_pair_closed :
    forall x y,
      base_unpair (base_pair x y) = (x, y).

  Parameter code_formula : RAFormula -> nat.

  Parameter code_formula_injective :
    forall phi psi,
      code_formula phi = code_formula psi ->
      phi = psi.

  (*@c.inline@[[Single-formula decoding is also abstract. `formula_of_code_correct` requires exact adequacy with the chosen formula coding rather than only a one-sided soundness statement.]]@*)
  (*@c.unicode@[[Decoding adequacy: formula_of_code u = Some φ ↔ code_formula φ = u]]@*)

  Parameter formula_of_code : nat -> option RAFormula.

  Parameter formula_of_code_correct :
    forall u phi,
      formula_of_code u = Some phi <-> code_formula phi = u.

  (*@c.inline@[[Finite proof traces are coded as natural lists over formula codes. The list-coding equations and `code_tag2` injectivity are the structural arithmetic facts used when proof traces and packaged proof codes are unpacked.]]@*)
  (*@c.unicode@[[Trace coding surface: code_nat_list, cons/nil equations, code_tag2 injectivity]]@*)

  Parameter code_nat_list : list nat -> nat.

  Parameter code_nat_list_nil :
    code_nat_list [] = 0.

  Parameter code_nat_list_cons :
    forall x xs,
      code_nat_list (x :: xs) = S (base_pair x (code_nat_list xs)).

  Parameter code_tag2 : nat -> nat -> nat -> nat.

  Parameter code_tag2_injective :
    forall tag a b tag' a' b',
      code_tag2 tag a b = code_tag2 tag' a' b' ->
      tag = tag' /\ a = a' /\ b = b'.

  (*@c.inline@[[The proof decoder recovers full finite proof traces from their numerical codes. It is required both to be sound for the list coding and complete on every actually encoded proof trace.]]@*)
  (*@c.unicode@[[Proof decoder interface: proof_of_code soundness and completeness]]@*)

  Parameter proof_of_code : nat -> option (list RAFormula).

  (*@c.unicode@[[Decoder soundness contract: proof_of_code p = Some pf ⇒ code_nat_list(map code_formula pf) = p.]]@*)
  Parameter proof_of_code_sound :
    forall p pf,
      proof_of_code p = Some pf ->
      code_nat_list (map code_formula pf) = p.

  (*@c.unicode@[[Decoder completeness contract: decoding re-encodes canonically, proof_of_code(code_nat_list(map code_formula pf)) = Some pf.]]@*)
  Parameter proof_of_code_complete :
    forall pf,
      proof_of_code (code_nat_list (map code_formula pf)) = Some pf.

  (*@c.inline@[[Finally, the input interface exposes the recursive arithmetic-theory predicate together with its boolean recognizer. The checker uses this pair to treat the object-theory axioms as one branch of its accepted axiom-line surface.]]@*)
  (*@c.unicode@[[Theory-axiom interface: RATheoryAxiom with boolean recognizer RATheoryAxiomb]]@*)

  Parameter RATheoryAxiom : RAFormula -> Prop.

  Parameter RATheoryAxiomb : RAFormula -> bool.

  Parameter RATheoryAxiomb_correct :
    forall phi,
      RATheoryAxiomb phi = true <-> RATheoryAxiom phi.

End INPUT.


(* ---- theories/D001/D001_09__Carryless_Numbering.v ---- *)

(*@file@*)
(*@H.1@[[Proofcase / D001 / Carryless Godel Numbering]]@*)
(*@H.2@[[Overview]]@*)
(*@p.l@[[This route is the numbering layer of the additive proof theory.]]@*)
(*@p.l@[[It is abstract over the local proof-theory input interface and packages exactly the numerical surface needed by later routes: proof traces, concrete proof objects, coded sequence accessors, and formula-code comparison lemmas.]]@*)
(*@p.l@[[No axiom recognition, checker logic, or theoremhood appears here.]]@*)
(*@H.2@[[Contents]]@*)
(*@ptoclist.roman@[[LOCAL PROOF-THEORY SYNTAX]][[Short local aliases for formulas, proofs, implication, and bottom, together with executable formula equality.]]@*)
(*@ptoclist.roman@[[CARRYLESS NUMBERING]][[Formula codes, proof-trace codes, and the packaged code of a concrete proof object.]]@*)
(*@ptoclist.roman@[[CODED SEQUENCES]][[Head, tail, indexed access, and the immediate inversion lemmas for coded finite sequences.]]@*)
(*@ptoclist.roman@[[TARGET RECOVERY]][[The code-level fact that equality of packaged concrete-proof codes forces equality of their target formulas.]]@*)

From D001 Require Export D001_08__Proof_Theory_Input.

Local Open Scope nat_scope.

Module Make (A : D001_08__Proof_Theory_Input.INPUT).
Import A.

(*@box.section@[[LOCAL PROOF-THEORY SYNTAX]]@*)

(*@c.inline@[[The additive proof theory does not introduce a second object-language syntax. It reuses arithmetic formulas directly and packages proof traces as finite lists of such formulas.]]@*)
(*@c.unicode@[[Local aliases: Form = RAFormula, Proof = list Form]]@*)

Definition Form : Type := RAFormula.

(*@c.unicode@[[Local proof-trace alias: `Proof ≔ list Form`, i.e. finite derivation traces are represented as formula lists.]]@*)

Definition Proof : Type := list Form.

(*@c.unicode@[[Local connective alias: `Imp` reuses the imported implication constructor on `Form` (`A → B`).]]@*)

Definition Imp : Form -> Form -> Form := A.Imp.

(*@c.unicode@[[Local constant alias: `Bot` reuses imported falsity `⊥` on the shared formula type `Form`.]]@*)

Definition Bot : Form := A.Bot.

(*@c.unicode@[[Local decomposition alias: `as_imp` exposes implication shape analysis `φ ↦ option (A,B)` for checker-side MP steps.]]@*)

Definition as_imp := A.as_imp.

(*@c.inline@[[Boolean formula equality is kept local to the proof-theory folder so the checker can compare candidate lines inside finite traces without unfolding full propositional proofs each time.]]@*)
(*@c.unicode@[[Boolean equality helper on formulas: form_eqb]]@*)

Definition form_eqb (u v : Form) : bool :=
  if RAFormula_eq_dec u v then true else false.

(*@c.unicode@[[Reflexivity law for boolean formula equality: every formula is self-equal (`form_eqb u u = true`).]]@*)

Lemma form_eqb_refl :
  forall u,
    form_eqb u u = true.
Proof.
  (*@c.unicode@[[Boolean reflexivity contract: ∀u, form_eqb(u,u)=true.]]@*)
  intro u.
  unfold form_eqb.
  destruct (RAFormula_eq_dec u u) as [_ | Hneq].
  - reflexivity.
  - exfalso.
    apply Hneq.
    reflexivity.
Qed.

(*@c.unicode@[[Correctness bridge for boolean equality: `form_eqb u v = true` iff syntactic equality `u = v`.]]@*)

Lemma form_eqb_true_iff :
  forall u v,
    form_eqb u v = true <-> u = v.
Proof.
  intros u v.
  unfold form_eqb.
  destruct (RAFormula_eq_dec u v) as [Heq | Hneq].
  - split.
    + intro H.
      clear H.
      exact Heq.
    + intro H.
      clear H.
      reflexivity.
  - split.
    + intro H.
      discriminate H.
    + intro Heq.
      exfalso.
      apply Hneq.
      exact Heq.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[The implication view is supplied by the abstract input interface rather than recovered by pattern-matching on a concrete arithmetic syntax.]]@*)
(*@c.unicode@[[Implication-view lemma alias: as_imp_Some]]@*)

Definition as_imp_Some := A.as_imp_Some.

(*@box.section@[[CARRYLESS NUMBERING]]@*)

(*@c.inline@[[The proof-theory numbering layer reuses arithmetic formula codes and adds only the proof-trace and concrete-proof-object packaging needed by the later checker and proof-predicate routes.]]@*)
(*@c.unicode@[[Numbering aliases: code_of_formula and concrete-code packaging layer]]@*)

Definition code_of_formula : Form -> nat := code_formula.

(*@c.unicode@[[Injectivity bridge for formula coding: `code_of_formula_injective` states that equal codes imply equal formulas.]]@*)

Definition code_of_formula_injective :=
  code_formula_injective.

(*@c.inline@[[`code_of_proof` compresses a finite proof trace by mapping each line to its formula code and then using the arithmetic list coding. This is the canonical numerical representation of a proof trace inside the proof-theory folder.]]@*)
(*@c.unicode@[[Proof-trace code: code_of_proof pf = code_nat_list (map code_of_formula pf)]]@*)

Definition code_of_proof (pf : Proof) : nat :=
  code_nat_list (map code_of_formula pf).

(*@c.inline@[[`code_of_concrete` packages a proof trace together with its asserted target formula into one tagged binary code. The distinguished outer tag separates full concrete proof objects from the plain list codes used for traces alone.]]@*)
(*@c.unicode@[[Concrete proof-object code: code_tag2 8 (code_of_proof pf) (code_of_formula target)]]@*)

Definition code_of_concrete (pf : Proof) (target : Form) : nat :=
  code_tag2 8 (code_of_proof pf) (code_of_formula target).

(*@box.section@[[CODED SEQUENCES]]@*)

(*@c.inline@[[The proof theory keeps its coded finite-sequence accessors local. They are simple consequences of the arithmetic code-level pairing surface already available through the public API, so no direct dependency on the arithmetic mapping route is needed here.]]@*)
(*@c.unicode@[[Coded-sequence destructors: coded_list_head / coded_list_tail / coded_nth]]@*)

Definition coded_list_head : nat -> nat :=
  fun n =>
    match n with
    | 0 => 0
    | S k => fst (base_unpair k)
    end.

(*@c.inline@[[`coded_list_tail` is the companion destructor for coded traces. On a nonempty list code it removes the outer cons cell and returns the code of the remaining suffix, while `0` stays the null code.]]@*)
(*@c.unicode@[[Tail destructor: coded_list_tail 0 = 0; coded_list_tail (S k) = snd(base_unpair k)]]@*)

Definition coded_list_tail : nat -> nat :=
  fun n =>
    match n with
    | 0 => 0
    | S k => snd (base_unpair k)
    end.

(*@c.inline@[[`coded_nth` iterates the head-tail destructors over a coded trace. It is the local code-level readout operation for the `i`-th stored line of a proof trace.]]@*)
(*@c.unicode@[[Indexed coded-trace readout: coded_nth i n]]@*)
(*@c.unicode@[[Recursive definition clause: coded_nth(S i, n) = coded_nth(i, coded_list_tail n), with base coded_nth(0,n)=coded_list_head n.]]@*)

Fixpoint coded_nth (i n : nat) : nat :=
  match i with
  | 0 => coded_list_head n
  | S i' => coded_nth i' (coded_list_tail n)
  end.

(*@c.unicode@[[Iterated tail extractor on codes: `coded_tailn i n` applies `coded_list_tail` exactly `i` times.]]@*)

Fixpoint coded_tailn (i n : nat) : nat :=
  match i with
  | 0 => n
  | S i' => coded_tailn i' (coded_list_tail n)
  end.

(*@c.unicode@[[Bounded indexed extraction on codes: `coded_nth_bounded fuel i n` returns `Some` line code only when the extraction finishes within `fuel` steps and before coded-list exhaustion.]]@*)

Fixpoint coded_nth_bounded (fuel i n : nat) : option nat :=
  match fuel with
  | 0 => None
  | S fuel' =>
      match i with
      | 0 =>
          match n with
          | 0 => None
          | S _ => Some (coded_list_head n)
          end
      | S i' => coded_nth_bounded fuel' i' (coded_list_tail n)
      end
  end.

(*@c.unicode@[[Bounded prefix extraction on codes: `coded_prefix fuel n` re-encodes at most the first `fuel` entries of coded list `n`.]]@*)

Fixpoint coded_prefix (fuel n : nat) : nat :=
  match fuel with
  | 0 => 0
  | S fuel' =>
      match n with
      | 0 => 0
      | S _ =>
          S (base_pair (coded_list_head n) (coded_prefix fuel' (coded_list_tail n)))
      end
  end.

(*@c.unicode@[[Bounded last extraction on codes: `coded_last_bounded fuel n` returns the final inspected entry if the end is reached within `fuel` tail-steps.]]@*)

Fixpoint coded_last_bounded (fuel n : nat) : option nat :=
  match fuel with
  | 0 => None
  | S fuel' =>
      match n with
      | 0 => None
      | S _ =>
          match coded_list_tail n with
          | 0 => Some (coded_list_head n)
          | t => coded_last_bounded fuel' t
          end
      end
  end.

(*@c.unicode@[[Bounded equality search over codes with explicit start index: `coded_find_eq_from fuel idx n target`.]]@*)

Fixpoint coded_find_eq_from (fuel idx n target : nat) : option nat :=
  match fuel with
  | 0 => None
  | S fuel' =>
      match n with
      | 0 => None
      | S _ =>
          if Nat.eqb (coded_list_head n) target
          then Some idx
          else coded_find_eq_from fuel' (S idx) (coded_list_tail n) target
      end
  end.

(*@c.unicode@[[Bounded equality search from head position (`idx = 0`).]]@*)

Definition coded_find_eq_bounded (fuel n target : nat) : option nat :=
  coded_find_eq_from fuel 0 n target.

(*@c.unicode@[[Bounded MP-witness shape search on coded traces: find indices for `u` and for implication-code `u→v` (`code_tag2 2 u v`) within one fuel bound.]]@*)

Definition coded_mp_witness_bounded (fuel n u v : nat) : option (nat * nat) :=
  match coded_find_eq_bounded fuel n u,
        coded_find_eq_bounded fuel n (code_tag2 2 u v) with
  | Some j, Some k => Some (j, k)
  | _, _ => None
  end.

(*@c.inline@[[The list-head and list-tail projections are correct on every nonempty coded trace. This first lemma says that decoding the head of an encoded cons cell immediately recovers the original first entry.]]@*)
(*@c.unicode@[[Head recovery law: coded_list_head (code_nat_list (x :: xs)) = x]]@*)

Lemma coded_list_cons_head :
  forall x xs,
    coded_list_head (code_nat_list (x :: xs)) = x.
Proof.
  intros x xs.
  rewrite code_nat_list_cons.
  change
    (fst
      (base_unpair
        (base_pair x (code_nat_list xs))) = x).
  rewrite (base_unpair_pair_closed x (code_nat_list xs)).
  reflexivity.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[The companion lemma identifies the tail projection of an encoded cons cell with the code of the remaining list. Together these two equations are the basic destructor facts for coded proof traces.]]@*)
(*@c.unicode@[[Tail recovery law: coded_list_tail (code_nat_list (x :: xs)) = code_nat_list xs]]@*)

Lemma coded_list_cons_tail :
  forall x xs,
    coded_list_tail (code_nat_list (x :: xs)) = code_nat_list xs.
Proof.
  intros x xs.
  rewrite code_nat_list_cons.
  change
    (snd
      (base_unpair
        (base_pair x (code_nat_list xs))) =
     code_nat_list xs).
  rewrite (base_unpair_pair_closed x (code_nat_list xs)).
  reflexivity.
Qed.

(*@c.unicode@[[Tail-iteration on zero code is stable: `coded_tailn i 0 = 0`.]]@*)

Lemma coded_tailn_zero :
  forall i,
    coded_tailn i 0 = 0.
Proof.
  induction i as [|i IH].
  - reflexivity.
  - simpl.
    exact IH.
Qed.

(*@c.unicode@[[Semantic tail correctness on encoded lists: `coded_tailn i (code_nat_list xs) = code_nat_list (skipn i xs)`.]]@*)

Lemma coded_tailn_code_nat_list :
  forall i xs,
    coded_tailn i (code_nat_list xs) = code_nat_list (skipn i xs).
Proof.
  induction i as [|i IH]; intro xs.
  - simpl.
    reflexivity.
  - destruct xs as [|x xs'].
    + rewrite code_nat_list_nil.
      simpl.
      rewrite coded_tailn_zero.
      exact (eq_sym code_nat_list_nil).
    + simpl.
      rewrite coded_list_cons_tail.
      exact (IH xs').
Qed.

(*@c.unicode@[[Semantic indexed-read correctness on encoded lists: `coded_nth i (code_nat_list xs) = nth i xs 0`.]]@*)

(*@c.unicode@[[Indexed-read on null code is zero: `coded_nth i 0 = 0`.]]@*)

Lemma coded_nth_zero :
  forall i,
    coded_nth i 0 = 0.
Proof.
  induction i as [|i IH].
  - reflexivity.
  - simpl.
    exact IH.
Qed.

Lemma coded_nth_code_nat_list :
  forall i xs,
    coded_nth i (code_nat_list xs) = nth i xs 0.
Proof.
  induction i as [|i IH]; intro xs.
  - destruct xs as [|x xs'].
    + rewrite code_nat_list_nil.
      reflexivity.
    + rewrite coded_list_cons_head.
      reflexivity.
  - destruct xs as [|x xs'].
    + rewrite code_nat_list_nil.
      rewrite coded_nth_zero.
      reflexivity.
    + simpl.
      rewrite coded_list_cons_tail.
      exact (IH xs').
Qed.

(*@c.unicode@[[Fuel upper-bound for bounded indexed extraction: if `fuel ≤ i`, then `coded_nth_bounded fuel i n = None`.]]@*)

Lemma coded_nth_bounded_too_small :
  forall fuel i n,
    fuel <= i ->
    coded_nth_bounded fuel i n = None.
Proof.
  induction fuel as [|fuel IH]; intros i n Hle.
  - reflexivity.
  - destruct i as [|i'].
    + inversion Hle.
    + simpl.
      apply IH.
      apply Nat.succ_le_mono in Hle.
      exact Hle.
Qed.

(*@c.unicode@[[Bounded indexed extraction on null code is always `None`: `coded_nth_bounded fuel i 0 = None`.]]@*)

Lemma coded_nth_bounded_nil :
  forall fuel i,
    coded_nth_bounded fuel i 0 = None.
Proof.
  induction fuel as [|fuel IH]; intro i.
  - reflexivity.
  - destruct i as [|i'].
    + reflexivity.
    + simpl.
      apply IH.
Qed.

(*@c.unicode@[[Bounded indexed extraction correctness on encoded lists: for `i < fuel`, bounded extraction matches `nth_error`.]]@*)

Lemma coded_nth_bounded_code_nat_list :
  forall fuel i xs,
    i < fuel ->
    coded_nth_bounded fuel i (code_nat_list xs) = nth_error xs i.
Proof.
  intros fuel i.
  revert fuel.
  induction i as [|i IH]; intros fuel xs Hlt.
  - destruct fuel as [|fuel'].
    + lia.
    + simpl.
      destruct xs as [|x xs'].
      * rewrite code_nat_list_nil.
        reflexivity.
      * rewrite code_nat_list_cons.
        simpl.
        rewrite (base_unpair_pair_closed x (code_nat_list xs')).
        reflexivity.
  - destruct fuel as [|fuel'].
    + lia.
    + simpl.
      destruct xs as [|x xs'].
      * rewrite code_nat_list_nil.
        apply coded_nth_bounded_nil.
      * rewrite coded_list_cons_tail.
        simpl.
        apply IH.
        lia.
Qed.

(*@c.unicode@[[Semantic bounded-prefix correctness: `coded_prefix fuel (code_nat_list xs) = code_nat_list (firstn fuel xs)`.]]@*)

Lemma coded_prefix_code_nat_list :
  forall fuel xs,
    coded_prefix fuel (code_nat_list xs) = code_nat_list (firstn fuel xs).
Proof.
  induction fuel as [|fuel IH]; intro xs.
  - simpl.
    exact (eq_sym code_nat_list_nil).
  - destruct xs as [|x xs'].
    + rewrite code_nat_list_nil.
      simpl.
      exact (eq_sym code_nat_list_nil).
    + rewrite code_nat_list_cons.
      simpl.
      repeat rewrite (base_unpair_pair_closed x (code_nat_list xs')).
      simpl.
      rewrite IH.
      rewrite code_nat_list_cons.
      reflexivity.
Qed.

(*@c.unicode@[[Semantic last-entry operator on plain lists of naturals.]]@*)

Fixpoint list_last_opt_nat (xs : list nat) : option nat :=
  match xs with
  | [] => None
  | [x] => Some x
  | _ :: xs' => list_last_opt_nat xs'
  end.

(*@c.unicode@[[Bounded last extraction correctness on encoded lists: if `length xs ≤ fuel`, then `coded_last_bounded fuel (code_nat_list xs) = list_last_opt_nat xs`.]]@*)

Lemma coded_last_bounded_code_nat_list :
  forall fuel xs,
    length xs <= fuel ->
    coded_last_bounded fuel (code_nat_list xs) = list_last_opt_nat xs.
Proof.
  intros fuel xs.
  revert fuel.
  induction xs as [|x xs IH]; intros fuel Hlen.
  - rewrite code_nat_list_nil.
    destruct fuel as [|fuel']; simpl; reflexivity.
  - destruct fuel as [|fuel'].
    + simpl in Hlen.
      lia.
    + destruct xs as [|y ys].
      * rewrite code_nat_list_cons.
        simpl.
        rewrite (base_unpair_pair_closed x (code_nat_list [])).
        rewrite code_nat_list_nil.
        simpl.
        reflexivity.
      * rewrite code_nat_list_cons.
        simpl.
        rewrite (base_unpair_pair_closed x (code_nat_list (y :: ys))).
        simpl.
        rewrite code_nat_list_cons.
        simpl.
        replace (S (base_pair y (code_nat_list ys)))
          with (code_nat_list (y :: ys)).
        2:{ apply code_nat_list_cons. }
        change (match ys with
                | [] => Some y
                | _ :: _ => list_last_opt_nat ys
                end) with (list_last_opt_nat (y :: ys)).
        apply (IH fuel').
        simpl in Hlen.
        apply le_S_n.
        exact Hlen.
Qed.

(*@c.unicode@[[Bounded search soundness with explicit start index: success yields an in-range witness position where the searched target occurs.]]@*)

Lemma coded_find_eq_from_sound :
  forall fuel idx xs target j,
    coded_find_eq_from fuel idx (code_nat_list xs) target = Some j ->
    exists off,
      j = idx + off /\
      off < fuel /\
      off < length xs /\
      nth off xs 0 = target.
Proof.
  induction fuel as [|fuel IH]; intros idx xs target j Hfind.
  - simpl in Hfind.
    discriminate Hfind.
  - destruct xs as [|x xs'].
    + rewrite code_nat_list_nil in Hfind.
      simpl in Hfind.
      discriminate Hfind.
    + rewrite code_nat_list_cons in Hfind.
      simpl in Hfind.
      rewrite (base_unpair_pair_closed x (code_nat_list xs')) in Hfind.
      simpl in Hfind.
      destruct (Nat.eqb x target) eqn:Heq.
      * inversion Hfind; subst j.
        exists 0.
        split.
        -- lia.
        -- split.
           ++ lia.
           ++ split.
              ** simpl. lia.
              ** apply Nat.eqb_eq in Heq.
                 subst.
                 reflexivity.
      * specialize (IH (S idx) xs' target j Hfind).
        destruct IH as [off [Hj [Hoff_fuel [Hoff_len Hnth]]]].
        exists (S off).
        split.
        -- lia.
        -- split.
           ++ lia.
           ++ split.
              ** simpl. lia.
              ** simpl. exact Hnth.
Qed.

(*@c.unicode@[[Bounded search soundness from head: success returns an index `< fuel` and `< length xs` whose entry equals the target.]]@*)

Lemma coded_find_eq_bounded_sound :
  forall fuel xs target j,
    coded_find_eq_bounded fuel (code_nat_list xs) target = Some j ->
    j < fuel /\ j < length xs /\ nth j xs 0 = target.
Proof.
  intros fuel xs target j Hfind.
  unfold coded_find_eq_bounded in Hfind.
  pose proof (coded_find_eq_from_sound fuel 0 xs target j Hfind)
    as [off [Hj [Hoff_fuel [Hoff_len Hnth]]]].
  subst j.
  split.
  - exact Hoff_fuel.
  - split.
    + exact Hoff_len.
    + exact Hnth.
Qed.

(*@c.unicode@[[Bounded search completeness (existential form): any occurrence within the inspected prefix guarantees some successful bounded-search witness.]]@*)

Lemma coded_find_eq_from_complete_off :
  forall fuel idx xs target off,
    off < fuel ->
    off < length xs ->
    nth off xs 0 = target ->
    exists k,
      coded_find_eq_from fuel idx (code_nat_list xs) target = Some k.
Proof.
  induction fuel as [|fuel IH]; intros idx xs target off Hoff_fuel Hoff_len Hnth.
  - lia.
  - destruct xs as [|x xs'].
    + simpl in Hoff_len.
      lia.
    + rewrite code_nat_list_cons.
      destruct off as [|off'].
      * simpl in Hnth.
        simpl.
        rewrite (base_unpair_pair_closed x (code_nat_list xs')).
        simpl.
        rewrite Hnth.
        rewrite Nat.eqb_refl.
        eexists.
        reflexivity.
      * simpl in Hoff_len.
        simpl in Hnth.
        simpl.
        rewrite (base_unpair_pair_closed x (code_nat_list xs')).
        simpl.
        destruct (Nat.eqb x target) eqn:Heq.
        -- eexists.
           reflexivity.
        -- assert (Hoff_fuel' : off' < fuel) by lia.
           assert (Hoff_len' : off' < length xs') by lia.
           specialize (IH (S idx) xs' target off' Hoff_fuel' Hoff_len' Hnth).
           destruct IH as [k Hk].
           exists k.
           exact Hk.
Qed.

Lemma coded_find_eq_bounded_complete :
  forall fuel xs target j,
    j < fuel ->
    j < length xs ->
    nth j xs 0 = target ->
    exists k,
      coded_find_eq_bounded fuel (code_nat_list xs) target = Some k.
Proof.
  intros fuel xs target j Hjfuel Hjlen Hnth.
  unfold coded_find_eq_bounded.
  exact
    (coded_find_eq_from_complete_off
      fuel 0 xs target j Hjfuel Hjlen Hnth).
Qed.

(*@c.unicode@[[Bounded MP-shape witness search soundness on encoded lists: if both indices are returned, they are in-range and point to `u` and `u→v` codes respectively.]]@*)

Lemma coded_mp_witness_bounded_sound :
  forall fuel xs u v j k,
    coded_mp_witness_bounded fuel (code_nat_list xs) u v = Some (j, k) ->
    j < fuel /\ k < fuel /\
    j < length xs /\ k < length xs /\
    nth j xs 0 = u /\
    nth k xs 0 = code_tag2 2 u v.
Proof.
  intros fuel xs u v j k Hmp.
  unfold coded_mp_witness_bounded in Hmp.
  destruct (coded_find_eq_bounded fuel (code_nat_list xs) u) as [j'|] eqn:Hj; try discriminate.
  destruct (coded_find_eq_bounded fuel (code_nat_list xs) (code_tag2 2 u v)) as [k'|] eqn:Hk; try discriminate.
  inversion Hmp; subst j' k'.
  pose proof (coded_find_eq_bounded_sound fuel xs u j Hj) as [Hjf [Hjl Hju]].
  pose proof (coded_find_eq_bounded_sound fuel xs (code_tag2 2 u v) k Hk) as [Hkf [Hkl Hkv]].
  split.
  - exact Hjf.
  - split.
    + exact Hkf.
    + split.
      * exact Hjl.
      * split.
        { exact Hkl. }
        { split.
          - exact Hju.
          - exact Hkv. }
Qed.

(*@box.section@[[TARGET RECOVERY]]@*)

(*@c.inline@[[Equality of packaged concrete-proof codes forces equality of the target-formula code, hence of the target formula itself. This is the key recovery fact used later by theoremhood/provability bridges.]]@*)
(*@c.unicode@[[Target recovery: equal concrete-proof codes imply equal target formulas]]@*)

Lemma code_of_concrete_target_eq :
  forall pf1 pf2 target1 target2,
    code_of_concrete pf1 target1 = code_of_concrete pf2 target2 ->
    target1 = target2.
Proof.
  (*@c.unicode@[[Target injectivity principle: code_of_concrete(pf₁,t₁)=code_of_concrete(pf₂,t₂) ⇒ t₁=t₂.]]@*)
  intros pf1 pf2 target1 target2 Hcode.
  unfold code_of_concrete in Hcode.
  apply code_tag2_injective in Hcode.
  destruct Hcode as [_ [_ Htarget_code]].
  apply code_formula_injective in Htarget_code.
  exact Htarget_code.
Qed.

End Make.

Module Type SIG (A : D001_08__Proof_Theory_Input.INPUT).
  Include Make A.
End SIG.


(* ---- theories/D001/D001_10__Evaluation.v ---- *)

(*@file@*)
(*@H.1@[[Proofcase / D001 / Evaluation]]@*)
(*@H.2@[[Overview]]@*)
(*@p.l@[[This route is the executable Hilbert-style evaluation layer of the additive proof theory.]]@*)
(*@p.l@[[It is abstract over the local proof-theory input interface. It owns logical axiom recognition, arithmetic-theory axiom entry through that abstract surface, modus-ponens witness search, trace validation, and the structural checker lemmas used later by the proof predicate and theoremhood layers.]]@*)
(*@p.l@[[It does not define the proof predicate or theoremhood.]]@*)
(*@H.2@[[Contents]]@*)
