(*@ptoclist.roman@[[AXIOM-LINE RECOGNITION]][[Boolean recognizers and Prop-level classes for the arithmetic-theory, `K`, `S`, and explosion axiom lines.]]@*)
(*@ptoclist.roman@[[CONTEXT SEARCH]][[Boolean membership and modus-ponens witness search inside finite contexts.]]@*)
(*@ptoclist.roman@[[CHECKER CORE]][[Line checking, full trace checking, final-line extraction, and accepted-proof decomposition.]]@*)
(*@ptoclist.roman@[[STRUCTURAL SUPPORT]][[Monotonicity, concatenation, and last-line lemmas used later by proof-predicate and theoremhood closure arguments.]]@*)

From D001 Require Export D001_09__Carryless_Numbering.

Module MakeWith (A : D001_08__Proof_Theory_Input.INPUT)
  (GN : D001_09__Carryless_Numbering.SIG A).
Import A GN.

(*@box.section@[[AXIOM-LINE RECOGNITION]]@*)

(*@c.inline@[[A checker line is accepted when it is either an arithmetic-theory axiom from the public arithmetic boundary or one of the three pure Hilbert axiom classes `K`, `S`, or explosion.]]@*)
(*@c.unicode@[[Axiom-line recognizer combines RA axioms with Hilbert K/S/EFQ classes]]@*)

Definition axiom_Kb (line : Form) : bool :=
  match as_imp line with
  | Some (a, rhs) =>
      match as_imp rhs with
      | Some (b, a') => form_eqb a a'
      | None => false
      end
  | None => false
  end.

(*@c.unicode@[[Boolean recognizer for Hilbert axiom S: checks the shape `((A → (B → C)) → ((A → B) → (A → C)))` by nested `as_imp` decomposition.]]@*)

Definition axiom_Sb (line : Form) : bool :=
  match as_imp line with
  | Some (lhs, rhs) =>
      match as_imp lhs, as_imp rhs with
      | Some (a, lhs'), Some (rhs_l, rhs_r) =>
          match as_imp lhs', as_imp rhs_l, as_imp rhs_r with
          | Some (b, c), Some (a', b'), Some (a'', c') =>
              form_eqb a a' && form_eqb a a'' && form_eqb b b' && form_eqb c c'
          | _, _, _ => false
          end
      | _, _ => false
      end
  | None => false
  end.

(*@c.unicode@[[Boolean recognizer for EFQ (ex falso): accepts implication lines whose antecedent is `⊥` (shape `⊥ → A`).]]@*)

Definition axiom_EFQb (line : Form) : bool :=
  match as_imp line with
  | Some (lhs, _) => form_eqb lhs Bot
  | None => false
  end.

(*@c.unicode@[[Unified boolean axiom-line recognizer: `RATheory ∨ K ∨ S ∨ EFQ` on a single proof line.]]@*)

Definition axiom_lineb (line : Form) : bool :=
  RATheoryAxiomb line || axiom_Kb line || axiom_Sb line || axiom_EFQb line.

(*@c.unicode@[[Semantic Hilbert axiom K schema: a line is `K` iff it has shape `A → (B → A)`.]]@*)

Definition AxiomK (line : Form) : Prop :=
  exists a b,
    line = Imp a (Imp b a).

(*@c.unicode@[[Semantic Hilbert axiom S schema: a line is `S` iff it has shape `(A → (B → C)) → ((A → B) → (A → C))`.]]@*)

Definition AxiomS (line : Form) : Prop :=
  exists a b c,
    line =
      Imp
        (Imp a (Imp b c))
        (Imp (Imp a b) (Imp a c)).

(*@c.unicode@[[Semantic EFQ schema: a line is `EFQ` iff it has shape `⊥ → A` for some formula `A`.]]@*)

Definition AxiomEFQ (line : Form) : Prop :=
  exists a,
    line = Imp Bot a.

(*@c.unicode@[[Unified semantic axiom predicate: `HilbertAxiom` holds exactly when a line is in `RATheory ∨ K ∨ S ∨ EFQ`.]]@*)

Definition HilbertAxiom (line : Form) : Prop :=
  RATheoryAxiom line \/ AxiomK line \/ AxiomS line \/ AxiomEFQ line.

(*@c.inline@[[The first adequacy theorem identifies the boolean recognizer for the Hilbert `K` schema with its Prop-level specification. Successful computation is equivalent to having the shape `a -> (b -> a)`.]]@*)
(*@c.unicode@[[Adequacy of K recognizer: axiom_Kb line = true ↔ AxiomK line]]@*)

Lemma axiom_Kb_correct :
  forall line,
    axiom_Kb line = true <-> AxiomK line.
Proof.
  intro line.
  split.
  - intro Hk.
    unfold axiom_Kb in Hk.
    destruct (as_imp line) as [[a rhs] |] eqn:Hline.
    + destruct (as_imp rhs) as [[b a'] |] eqn:Hrhs.
      * apply as_imp_Some in Hline.
        apply as_imp_Some in Hrhs.
        subst line rhs.
        simpl in Hk.
        apply form_eqb_true_iff in Hk.
        subst a'.
        exists a, b.
        reflexivity.
      * discriminate Hk.
    + discriminate Hk.
  - intros [a [b Hk]].
    subst line.
    unfold axiom_Kb.
    rewrite as_imp_Imp.
    rewrite as_imp_Imp.
    apply form_eqb_refl.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[The second adequacy theorem does the same for the Hilbert `S` schema. It turns the nested implication pattern-matching code back into the semantic three-parameter `S` axiom class.]]@*)
(*@c.unicode@[[Adequacy of S recognizer: axiom_Sb line = true ↔ AxiomS line]]@*)

(*@todo@[[Temporary state: `axiom_Sb_correct` is still maintained as a long nested destruct-and-discriminate script over implication views. If this schema checker grows further, factor the shape recovery into local inversion lemmas rather than extending the current tactic tree.]]@*)

(*@c.unicode@[[Soundness-and-completeness bridge for `S`: the boolean checker `axiom_Sb` is extensionally equivalent to semantic schema membership `AxiomS`.]]@*)

Lemma axiom_Sb_correct :
  forall line,
    axiom_Sb line = true <-> AxiomS line.
Proof.
  intro line.
  split.
  - intro Hs.
    unfold axiom_Sb in Hs.
    destruct (as_imp line) as [[lhs rhs] |] eqn:Hline.
    + destruct (as_imp lhs) as [[a lhs'] |] eqn:Hlhs.
      * destruct (as_imp rhs) as [[rhs_l rhs_r] |] eqn:Hrhs.
        -- destruct (as_imp lhs') as [[b c] |] eqn:Hlhs'.
           ++ destruct (as_imp rhs_l) as [[a' b'] |] eqn:Hrhs_l.
              ** destruct (as_imp rhs_r) as [[a'' c'] |] eqn:Hrhs_r.
                 --- apply as_imp_Some in Hline.
                     apply as_imp_Some in Hlhs.
                     apply as_imp_Some in Hlhs'.
                     apply as_imp_Some in Hrhs.
                     apply as_imp_Some in Hrhs_l.
                     apply as_imp_Some in Hrhs_r.
                     subst line lhs lhs' rhs rhs_l rhs_r.
                     simpl in Hs.
                     apply andb_true_iff in Hs.
                     destruct Hs as [Hrest3 Hc].
                     apply andb_true_iff in Hrest3.
                     destruct Hrest3 as [Hrest2 Hb].
                     apply andb_true_iff in Hrest2.
                     destruct Hrest2 as [Ha1 Ha2].
                     apply form_eqb_true_iff in Ha1.
                     apply form_eqb_true_iff in Ha2.
                     apply form_eqb_true_iff in Hb.
                     apply form_eqb_true_iff in Hc.
                     subst a' a'' b' c'.
                     exists a, b, c.
                     reflexivity.
                 --- discriminate Hs.
              ** discriminate Hs.
           ++ discriminate Hs.
        -- discriminate Hs.
      * discriminate Hs.
    + discriminate Hs.
  - intros [a [b [c Hs]]].
    subst line.
    unfold axiom_Sb.
    rewrite as_imp_Imp.
    rewrite as_imp_Imp.
    rewrite as_imp_Imp.
    rewrite as_imp_Imp.
    rewrite as_imp_Imp.
    rewrite as_imp_Imp.
    rewrite form_eqb_refl.
    rewrite form_eqb_refl.
    rewrite form_eqb_refl.
    simpl.
    reflexivity.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[The explosion recognizer is likewise exact. The boolean test for `Bot -> a` coincides with the Prop-level `AxiomEFQ` class.]]@*)
(*@c.unicode@[[Adequacy of EFQ recognizer: axiom_EFQb line = true ↔ AxiomEFQ line]]@*)

Lemma axiom_EFQb_correct :
  forall line,
    axiom_EFQb line = true <-> AxiomEFQ line.
Proof.
  intro line.
  split.
  - intro Hefq.
    unfold axiom_EFQb in Hefq.
    destruct (as_imp line) as [[lhs rhs] |] eqn:Hline.
    + apply as_imp_Some in Hline.
      subst line.
      apply form_eqb_true_iff in Hefq.
      subst lhs.
      exists rhs.
      reflexivity.
    + discriminate Hefq.
  - intros [a Hefq].
    subst line.
    unfold axiom_EFQb.
    rewrite as_imp_Imp.
    rewrite form_eqb_refl.
    reflexivity.
Qed.

(*@todo@[[Temporary state: `axiom_lineb_correct` is still discharged by a broad case split over the disjunctive checker surface. If new axiom families are added, package the boolean-to-Prop branches into a smaller local bridge instead of lengthening this manual orb proof.]]@*)

(*@c.unicode@[[Main recognizer adequacy: `axiom_lineb line = true` iff `line` is a semantic Hilbert axiom in `RATheory ∨ K ∨ S ∨ EFQ`.]]@*)

Lemma axiom_lineb_correct :
  forall line,
    axiom_lineb line = true <-> HilbertAxiom line.
Proof.
  intro line.
  unfold axiom_lineb, HilbertAxiom.
  repeat rewrite orb_true_iff.
  split.
  - intros [[[Hra | Hk] | Hs] | Hefq].
    + left.
      apply RATheoryAxiomb_correct.
      exact Hra.
    + right.
      left.
      apply axiom_Kb_correct.
      exact Hk.
    + right.
      right.
      left.
      apply axiom_Sb_correct.
      exact Hs.
    + right.
      right.
      right.
      apply axiom_EFQb_correct.
      exact Hefq.
  - intros [Hra | [Hk | [Hs | Hefq]]].
    + left.
      left.
      left.
      apply RATheoryAxiomb_correct.
      exact Hra.
    + left.
      left.
      right.
      apply axiom_Kb_correct.
      exact Hk.
    + left.
      right.
      apply axiom_Sb_correct.
      exact Hs.
    + right.
      apply axiom_EFQb_correct.
      exact Hefq.
Qed.

(*@box.section@[[CONTEXT SEARCH]]@*)

(*@c.inline@[[Context lookup is done by a boolean membership scan, and a modus-ponens witness for `v` is found by searching for some antecedent `u` together with the implication `u → v` inside the current context.]]@*)
(*@c.unicode@[[Context search primitives: member_formb and MP-witness scanning]]@*)

Fixpoint member_formb (u : Form) (ctx : list Form) : bool :=
  match ctx with
  | [] => false
  | x :: xs => form_eqb u x || member_formb u xs
  end.

(*@c.unicode@[[Membership adequacy: `member_formb u ctx = true` iff propositional list membership `u ∈ ctx`.]]@*)

Lemma member_formb_correct :
  forall u ctx,
    member_formb u ctx = true <-> In u ctx.
Proof.
  intros u ctx.
  induction ctx as [|x xs IH].
  - simpl.
    split.
    + intro H.
      discriminate H.
    + intro H.
      contradiction.
  - simpl.
    rewrite orb_true_iff.
    rewrite IH.
    split.
    + intros [Hhead | Htail].
      * apply form_eqb_true_iff in Hhead.
        subst x.
        left.
        reflexivity.
      * right.
        exact Htail.
    + intros [Hhead | Htail].
      * subst x.
        left.
        apply form_eqb_refl.
      * right.
        exact Htail.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[A modus-ponens witness is searched for by scanning the current context for some antecedent `u` whose code occurs together with the implication `u -> v`. This is the executable MP recognizer used by the checker core.]]@*)
(*@c.unicode@[[Executable MP witness search over context entries]]@*)

Definition mp_witness (ctx : list Form) (v : Form) : bool :=
  existsb
    (fun u => member_formb u ctx && member_formb (Imp u v) ctx)
    ctx.

(*@c.inline@[[`mp_witness_spec` exposes the exact semantic meaning of that search. The boolean witness search succeeds precisely when the context contains an antecedent and the corresponding implication into the target line.]]@*)
(*@c.unicode@[[MP witness specification: mp_witness ctx v = true ↔ ∃u, u∈ctx ∧ (u→v)∈ctx]]@*)

Lemma mp_witness_spec :
  forall ctx v,
    mp_witness ctx v = true <->
    exists u, In u ctx /\ In (Imp u v) ctx.
Proof.
  intros ctx v.
  unfold mp_witness.
  split.
  - intro Hmp.
    apply existsb_exists in Hmp.
    destruct Hmp as [u [Hu_in Hu_check]].
    apply andb_true_iff in Hu_check.
    destruct Hu_check as [Hu_mem Himp_mem].
    apply member_formb_correct in Hu_mem.
    apply member_formb_correct in Himp_mem.
    exists u.
    split.
    + exact Hu_mem.
    + exact Himp_mem.
  - intros [u [Hu_in Himp_in]].
    apply existsb_exists.
    exists u.
    split.
    + exact Hu_in.
    + apply andb_true_iff.
      split.
      * apply member_formb_correct.
        exact Hu_in.
      * apply member_formb_correct.
        exact Himp_in.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[This forward bridge packages explicit context membership into a successful witness search. Whenever `u` and `u -> v` are both present, the checker can certify `v` by modus ponens.]]@*)
(*@c.unicode@[[Forward MP bridge: u∈ctx ∧ (u→v)∈ctx ⇒ mp_witness ctx v = true]]@*)

Lemma mp_witness_from_in :
  forall ctx u v,
    In u ctx ->
    In (Imp u v) ctx ->
    mp_witness ctx v = true.
Proof.
  intros ctx u v Hu Himp.
  apply mp_witness_spec.
  exists u.
  split.
  - exact Hu.
  - exact Himp.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[Witness search is monotone under context extension. Enlarging the available context cannot destroy an already found modus-ponens witness.]]@*)
(*@c.unicode@[[Monotonicity: ctx1 ⊆ ctx2 ∧ mp_witness ctx1 v = true ⇒ mp_witness ctx2 v = true]]@*)

Lemma mp_witness_monotone_incl :
  forall ctx1 ctx2 v,
    (forall x, In x ctx1 -> In x ctx2) ->
    mp_witness ctx1 v = true ->
    mp_witness ctx2 v = true.
Proof.
  intros ctx1 ctx2 v Hincl Hmp.
  apply mp_witness_spec in Hmp.
  destruct Hmp as [u [Hu Himp]].
  apply mp_witness_spec.
  exists u.
  split.
  - apply Hincl.
    exact Hu.
  - apply Hincl.
    exact Himp.
Qed.

(*@box.section@[[CHECKER CORE]]@*)

(*@c.inline@[[The finite checker validates one line against the current context, then validates a whole trace by extending the context with previously checked lines. The final `check` predicate requires both trace validity and final-line agreement with the requested goal.]]@*)
(*@c.unicode@[[Checker core: line validation + incremental trace validation + final-goal match]]@*)

Definition check_line (ctx : list Form) (line : Form) : bool :=
  axiom_lineb line || mp_witness ctx line.

(*@c.unicode@[[Line-checker equation: `check_line(ctx,line) = axiom_lineb(line) ∨ mp_witness(ctx,line)`.]]@*)

(*@c.unicode@[[Trace checker recursion: each line must pass `check_line` in the current context, then the context is extended by that line for the tail.]]@*)

Fixpoint check_lines (ctx : list Form) (pf : Proof) : bool :=
  match pf with
  | [] => true
  | line :: pf' => check_line ctx line && check_lines (line :: ctx) pf'
  end.

(*@c.inline@[[`last_opt` extracts the terminal line of a finite proof trace when one exists. The checker uses it to separate structural trace validation from the final target-matching test.]]@*)
(*@c.unicode@[[Terminal-line extractor: last_opt pf]]@*)

Fixpoint last_opt (pf : Proof) : option Form :=
  match pf with
  | [] => None
  | [x] => Some x
  | _ :: pf' => last_opt pf'
  end.

(*@c.inline@[[`check` is the closed proof checker for a target formula. It accepts exactly when the whole trace is locally valid from the empty context and its final line is the requested target.]]@*)
(*@c.unicode@[[Closed checker: check pf target = true iff trace is valid and last line equals target]]@*)

Definition check (pf : Proof) (target : Form) : bool :=
  match last_opt pf with
  | Some found => check_lines [] pf && form_eqb found target
  | None => false
  end.

(*@box.section@[[CODE-FIRST KERNEL]]@*)

(*@c.inline@[[The code-first kernel separates checker clauses at the natural-code level so the compilation path is explicit: axiom recognition, target matching, line lookup, membership search, modus-ponens witness search, and bounded trace recursion over coded lists.]]@*)
(*@c.unicode@[[Code-first checker clauses: axiom_recognition_codeb, target_matching_codeb, line_lookup_codeb, membership_search_codeb, mp_witness_search_codeb, trace_recursion_codeb]]@*)

Definition axiom_recognition_codeb (line_code : nat) : bool :=
  match formula_of_code line_code with
  | Some line => axiom_lineb line
  | None => false
  end.

Definition target_matching_codeb (found_code target_code : nat) : bool :=
  Nat.eqb found_code target_code.

Definition line_lookup_codeb (fuel i proof_code : nat) : option nat :=
  coded_nth_bounded fuel i proof_code.

Definition membership_search_codeb (fuel ctx_code entry_code : nat) : bool :=
  match coded_find_eq_bounded fuel ctx_code entry_code with
  | Some _ => true
  | None => false
  end.

Definition mp_witness_search_codeb (ctx_code line_code : nat) : bool :=
  match proof_of_code ctx_code, formula_of_code line_code with
  | Some ctx, Some line => mp_witness ctx line
  | _, _ => false
  end.

Definition check_line_codeb (ctx_code line_code : nat) : bool :=
  axiom_recognition_codeb line_code || mp_witness_search_codeb ctx_code line_code.

Fixpoint trace_recursion_codeb (ctx_code pf_code fuel : nat) : bool :=
  match fuel with
  | 0 =>
      match pf_code with
      | 0 => true
      | S _ => false
      end
  | S fuel' =>
      match pf_code with
      | 0 => true
      | S _ =>
          let line_code := coded_list_head pf_code in
          check_line_codeb ctx_code line_code &&
          trace_recursion_codeb
            (S (base_pair line_code ctx_code))
            (coded_list_tail pf_code)
            fuel'
      end
  end.

Definition proof_code_size_bound (proof_code : nat) : nat :=
  match proof_of_code proof_code with
  | Some pf => length pf
  | None => 0
  end.

Definition checker_kernel_codeb (proof_code target_code : nat) : bool :=
  let fuel := proof_code_size_bound proof_code in
  match coded_last_bounded fuel proof_code with
  | Some found_code =>
      target_matching_codeb found_code target_code &&
      trace_recursion_codeb 0 proof_code fuel
  | None => false
  end.

Lemma axiom_recognition_codeb_correct :
  forall line,
    axiom_recognition_codeb (code_of_formula line) = axiom_lineb line.
Proof.
  intro line.
  unfold axiom_recognition_codeb.
  assert (Hdecode : formula_of_code (code_of_formula line) = Some line).
  {
    apply (proj2 (formula_of_code_correct (code_of_formula line) line)).
    unfold code_of_formula.
    reflexivity.
  }
  rewrite Hdecode.
  reflexivity.
Qed.

Lemma target_matching_codeb_correct :
  forall x y,
    target_matching_codeb x y = true <-> x = y.
Proof.
  intros x y.
  unfold target_matching_codeb.
  rewrite Nat.eqb_eq.
  reflexivity.
Qed.

Lemma line_lookup_codeb_correct :
  forall fuel i xs,
    i < fuel ->
    line_lookup_codeb fuel i (code_nat_list xs) = nth_error xs i.
Proof.
  intros fuel i xs Hlt.
  unfold line_lookup_codeb.
  apply coded_nth_bounded_code_nat_list.
  exact Hlt.
Qed.

Lemma membership_search_codeb_sound :
  forall fuel xs entry_code,
    membership_search_codeb fuel (code_nat_list xs) entry_code = true ->
    exists j,
      j < fuel /\
      j < length xs /\
      nth j xs 0 = entry_code.
Proof.
  intros fuel xs entry_code Hmem.
  unfold membership_search_codeb in Hmem.
  destruct (coded_find_eq_bounded fuel (code_nat_list xs) entry_code) as [j|] eqn:Hfind.
  - exists j.
    apply coded_find_eq_bounded_sound in Hfind.
    exact Hfind.
  - discriminate Hmem.
Qed.

Lemma membership_search_codeb_complete :
  forall fuel xs entry_code j,
    j < fuel ->
    j < length xs ->
    nth j xs 0 = entry_code ->
    membership_search_codeb fuel (code_nat_list xs) entry_code = true.
Proof.
  intros fuel xs entry_code j Hjfuel Hjlen Hnth.
  unfold membership_search_codeb.
  destruct (coded_find_eq_bounded_complete fuel xs entry_code j Hjfuel Hjlen Hnth)
    as [k Hk].
  rewrite Hk.
  reflexivity.
Qed.

Lemma mp_witness_search_codeb_correct :
  forall ctx line,
    mp_witness_search_codeb
      (code_nat_list (map code_of_formula ctx))
      (code_of_formula line)
    = mp_witness ctx line.
Proof.
  intros ctx line.
  unfold mp_witness_search_codeb.
  assert (Hctx :
    proof_of_code (code_nat_list (map code_of_formula ctx)) = Some ctx).
  {
    unfold code_of_formula.
    apply proof_of_code_complete.
  }
  assert (Hline :
    formula_of_code (code_of_formula line) = Some line).
  {
    apply (proj2 (formula_of_code_correct (code_of_formula line) line)).
    unfold code_of_formula.
    reflexivity.
  }
  rewrite Hctx.
  rewrite Hline.
  reflexivity.
Qed.

Lemma check_line_codeb_correct :
  forall ctx line,
    check_line_codeb
      (code_nat_list (map code_of_formula ctx))
      (code_of_formula line)
    = check_line ctx line.
Proof.
  intros ctx line.
  unfold check_line_codeb, check_line.
  rewrite axiom_recognition_codeb_correct.
  rewrite mp_witness_search_codeb_correct.
  reflexivity.
Qed.

Lemma proof_code_size_bound_code_of_proof :
  forall pf,
    proof_code_size_bound (code_of_proof pf) = length pf.
Proof.
  intro pf.
  unfold proof_code_size_bound, code_of_proof, code_of_formula.
  rewrite proof_of_code_complete.
  reflexivity.
Qed.

Lemma list_last_opt_nat_map_code_of_formula :
  forall pf,
    list_last_opt_nat (map code_of_formula pf) =
    match last_opt pf with
    | Some line => Some (code_of_formula line)
    | None => None
    end.
Proof.
  induction pf as [|a pf IH].
  - reflexivity.
  - destruct pf as [|b pf'].
    + reflexivity.
    + simpl.
      exact IH.
Qed.

Lemma trace_recursion_codeb_correct :
  forall ctx pf fuel,
    length pf <= fuel ->
    trace_recursion_codeb
      (code_nat_list (map code_of_formula ctx))
      (code_of_proof pf)
      fuel
    = check_lines ctx pf.
Proof.
  intros ctx pf.
  revert ctx.
  induction pf as [|line pf IH]; intros ctx fuel Hlen.
  - destruct fuel as [|fuel'].
    + unfold code_of_proof.
      simpl.
      rewrite code_nat_list_nil.
      reflexivity.
    + unfold code_of_proof.
      simpl.
      rewrite code_nat_list_nil.
      reflexivity.
  - destruct fuel as [|fuel'].
    + simpl in Hlen.
      lia.
    + simpl.
      unfold code_of_proof.
      simpl.
      rewrite code_nat_list_cons.
      simpl.
      unfold coded_list_head.
      unfold coded_list_tail.
      rewrite (base_unpair_pair_closed (code_of_formula line)
                (code_nat_list (map code_of_formula pf))).
      simpl.
      rewrite check_line_codeb_correct.
      replace
        (snd (code_of_formula line, code_nat_list (map code_of_formula pf)))
      with
        (code_nat_list (map code_of_formula pf)).
      2:{ reflexivity. }
      replace
        (S
          (base_pair (code_of_formula line)
             (code_nat_list (map code_of_formula ctx))))
      with
        (code_nat_list (map code_of_formula (line :: ctx))).
      2:{
        simpl.
        apply code_nat_list_cons.
      }
      rewrite IH.
      * reflexivity.
      * simpl in Hlen.
        lia.
Qed.

Lemma target_matching_codeb_formula_codes :
  forall x y,
    target_matching_codeb (code_of_formula x) (code_of_formula y) = form_eqb x y.
Proof.
  intros x y.
  unfold target_matching_codeb, form_eqb.
  destruct (Nat.eqb (code_of_formula x) (code_of_formula y)) eqn:Heq_code.
  - apply Nat.eqb_eq in Heq_code.
    apply code_of_formula_injective in Heq_code.
    subst y.
    destruct (RAFormula_eq_dec x x) as [_|Hneq].
    + reflexivity.
    + exfalso.
      apply Hneq.
      reflexivity.
  - apply Nat.eqb_neq in Heq_code.
    destruct (RAFormula_eq_dec x y) as [Heq_xy|Hneq_xy].
    + subst y.
      exfalso.
      apply Heq_code.
      reflexivity.
    + reflexivity.
Qed.

Theorem checker_kernel_codeb_correct :
  forall pf target,
    checker_kernel_codeb (code_of_proof pf) (code_of_formula target) =
    check pf target.
Proof.
  intros pf target.
  unfold checker_kernel_codeb.
  rewrite proof_code_size_bound_code_of_proof.
  unfold code_of_proof.
  rewrite (coded_last_bounded_code_nat_list (length pf) (map code_of_formula pf)).
  2:{ rewrite length_map. lia. }
  assert (Htrace :
    trace_recursion_codeb 0 (code_nat_list (map code_of_formula pf)) (length pf) =
    check_lines [] pf).
  {
    replace 0 with (code_nat_list (map code_of_formula ([] : list Form))).
    2:{ simpl. rewrite code_nat_list_nil. reflexivity. }
    apply trace_recursion_codeb_correct.
    simpl.
    lia.
  }
  rewrite Htrace.
  rewrite list_last_opt_nat_map_code_of_formula.
  unfold check.
  destruct (last_opt pf) as [found|] eqn:Hlast.
  - simpl.
    rewrite target_matching_codeb_formula_codes.
    rewrite andb_comm.
    reflexivity.
  - reflexivity.
Qed.

(*@box.section@[[INTERNAL CONSTRAINT COMPILER]]@*)

(*@c.inline@[[The compiler interface is isolated by a small internal syntax of checker obligations. Each clause of the code-first checker kernel compiles to a finite family of line-local internal constraints.]]@*)
(*@c.unicode@[[Internal clause syntax for checker-to-constraint compilation]]@*)

Inductive InternalConstraint : Type :=
| IC_AxiomRecognition (line_code : nat)
| IC_TargetMatching (found_code target_code : nat)
| IC_LineLookup (fuel i proof_code expected_code : nat)
| IC_MembershipSearch (fuel ctx_code entry_code : nat)
| IC_MPWitnessSearch (ctx_code line_code : nat)
| IC_TraceRecursion (ctx_code proof_code fuel : nat)
| IC_LastLookup (fuel proof_code expected_code : nat).

Definition InternalConstraint_degree (c : InternalConstraint) : nat :=
  match c with
  | IC_AxiomRecognition _ => 2
  | IC_TargetMatching _ _ => 2
  | IC_LineLookup _ _ _ _ => 2
  | IC_MembershipSearch _ _ _ => 2
  | IC_MPWitnessSearch _ _ => 2
  | IC_TraceRecursion _ _ _ => 3
  | IC_LastLookup _ _ _ => 2
  end.

Definition CubicCompatibleConstraint (c : InternalConstraint) : Prop :=
  InternalConstraint_degree c <= 3.

Definition QuadraticCompatibleConstraint (c : InternalConstraint) : Prop :=
  InternalConstraint_degree c <= 2.

Inductive SelectorActivatedConstraint : InternalConstraint -> Prop :=
| SAC_TraceRecursion :
    forall ctx_code proof_code fuel,
      SelectorActivatedConstraint (IC_TraceRecursion ctx_code proof_code fuel).

Lemma internal_constraint_degree_axiom :
  forall line_code,
    InternalConstraint_degree (IC_AxiomRecognition line_code) = 2.
Proof.
  intro line_code.
  reflexivity.
Qed.

Lemma internal_constraint_degree_target :
  forall found_code target_code,
    InternalConstraint_degree (IC_TargetMatching found_code target_code) = 2.
Proof.
  intros found_code target_code.
  reflexivity.
Qed.

Lemma internal_constraint_degree_line_lookup :
  forall fuel i proof_code expected_code,
    InternalConstraint_degree (IC_LineLookup fuel i proof_code expected_code) = 2.
Proof.
  intros fuel i proof_code expected_code.
  reflexivity.
Qed.

Lemma internal_constraint_degree_membership :
  forall fuel ctx_code entry_code,
    InternalConstraint_degree (IC_MembershipSearch fuel ctx_code entry_code) = 2.
Proof.
  intros fuel ctx_code entry_code.
  reflexivity.
Qed.

Lemma internal_constraint_degree_mp :
  forall ctx_code line_code,
    InternalConstraint_degree (IC_MPWitnessSearch ctx_code line_code) = 2.
Proof.
  intros ctx_code line_code.
  reflexivity.
Qed.

Lemma internal_constraint_degree_trace :
  forall ctx_code proof_code fuel,
    InternalConstraint_degree (IC_TraceRecursion ctx_code proof_code fuel) = 3.
Proof.
  intros ctx_code proof_code fuel.
  reflexivity.
Qed.

Lemma internal_constraint_degree_last :
  forall fuel proof_code expected_code,
    InternalConstraint_degree (IC_LastLookup fuel proof_code expected_code) = 2.
Proof.
  intros fuel proof_code expected_code.
  reflexivity.
Qed.

Lemma internal_constraint_degree_le_3 :
  forall c,
    InternalConstraint_degree c <= 3.
Proof.
  intro c.
  destruct c; simpl; lia.
Qed.

Lemma selector_activation_exactly_cubic :
  forall c,
    SelectorActivatedConstraint c <-> InternalConstraint_degree c = 3.
Proof.
  intro c.
  split.
  - intro Hsel.
    inversion Hsel; subst.
    reflexivity.
  - intro Hdeg.
    destruct c; simpl in Hdeg; try lia.
    constructor.
Qed.

Lemma selector_activation_only_source_of_cubic_terms :
  forall c,
    InternalConstraint_degree c > 2 ->
    SelectorActivatedConstraint c.
Proof.
  intros c Hgt.
  destruct c; simpl in Hgt; try lia.
  constructor.
Qed.

Lemma non_selector_constraints_are_quadratic :
  forall c,
    ~ SelectorActivatedConstraint c ->
    InternalConstraint_degree c <= 2.
Proof.
  intros c Hnsel.
  destruct c; simpl; try lia.
  exfalso.
  apply Hnsel.
  constructor.
Qed.

Definition ConstraintHolds (c : InternalConstraint) : bool :=
  match c with
  | IC_AxiomRecognition line_code =>
      axiom_recognition_codeb line_code
  | IC_TargetMatching found_code target_code =>
      target_matching_codeb found_code target_code
  | IC_LineLookup fuel i proof_code expected_code =>
      match line_lookup_codeb fuel i proof_code with
      | Some got => Nat.eqb got expected_code
      | None => false
      end
  | IC_MembershipSearch fuel ctx_code entry_code =>
      membership_search_codeb fuel ctx_code entry_code
  | IC_MPWitnessSearch ctx_code line_code =>
      mp_witness_search_codeb ctx_code line_code
  | IC_TraceRecursion ctx_code proof_code fuel =>
      trace_recursion_codeb ctx_code proof_code fuel
  | IC_LastLookup fuel proof_code expected_code =>
      match coded_last_bounded fuel proof_code with
      | Some got => Nat.eqb got expected_code
      | None => false
      end
  end.

Definition ConstraintFamily : Type := list InternalConstraint.

Definition FamilySat (fam : ConstraintFamily) : Prop :=
  Forall (fun c => ConstraintHolds c = true) fam.

Definition FamilyQuadraticCompatible (fam : ConstraintFamily) : Prop :=
  Forall QuadraticCompatibleConstraint fam.

Definition FamilyCubicCompatible (fam : ConstraintFamily) : Prop :=
  Forall CubicCompatibleConstraint fam.

Definition compile_axiom_clause (line_code : nat) : ConstraintFamily :=
  [IC_AxiomRecognition line_code].

Definition compile_target_clause (found_code target_code : nat) : ConstraintFamily :=
  [IC_TargetMatching found_code target_code].

Definition compile_line_lookup_clause
  (fuel i proof_code expected_code : nat) : ConstraintFamily :=
  [IC_LineLookup fuel i proof_code expected_code].

Definition compile_membership_clause
  (fuel ctx_code entry_code : nat) : ConstraintFamily :=
  [IC_MembershipSearch fuel ctx_code entry_code].

Definition compile_mp_clause (ctx_code line_code : nat) : ConstraintFamily :=
  [IC_MPWitnessSearch ctx_code line_code].

Definition compile_trace_clause
  (ctx_code proof_code fuel : nat) : ConstraintFamily :=
  [IC_TraceRecursion ctx_code proof_code fuel].

Definition compile_last_clause
  (fuel proof_code expected_code : nat) : ConstraintFamily :=
  [IC_LastLookup fuel proof_code expected_code].

Lemma FamilySat_singleton :
  forall c,
    FamilySat [c] <-> ConstraintHolds c = true.
Proof.
  intro c.
  split.
  - intro Hsat.
    inversion Hsat; subst.
    inversion H2; subst.
    exact H1.
  - intro Hc.
    constructor.
    + exact Hc.
    + constructor.
Qed.

Lemma FamilyCubicCompatible_singleton :
  forall c,
    FamilyCubicCompatible [c].
Proof.
  intro c.
  constructor.
  - unfold CubicCompatibleConstraint, InternalConstraint_degree.
    destruct c; lia.
  - constructor.
Qed.

Lemma FamilyQuadraticCompatible_singleton :
  forall c,
    QuadraticCompatibleConstraint c ->
    FamilyQuadraticCompatible [c].
Proof.
  intros c Hquad.
  constructor.
  - exact Hquad.
  - constructor.
Qed.

Lemma FamilySat_app :
  forall fam1 fam2,
    FamilySat (fam1 ++ fam2) <-> FamilySat fam1 /\ FamilySat fam2.
Proof.
  intros fam1 fam2.
  unfold FamilySat.
  rewrite Forall_app.
  reflexivity.
Qed.

Lemma FamilyQuadraticCompatible_app :
  forall fam1 fam2,
    FamilyQuadraticCompatible (fam1 ++ fam2) <->
    FamilyQuadraticCompatible fam1 /\ FamilyQuadraticCompatible fam2.
Proof.
  intros fam1 fam2.
  unfold FamilyQuadraticCompatible.
  rewrite Forall_app.
  reflexivity.
Qed.

Lemma FamilyCubicCompatible_app :
  forall fam1 fam2,
    FamilyCubicCompatible (fam1 ++ fam2) <->
    FamilyCubicCompatible fam1 /\ FamilyCubicCompatible fam2.
Proof.
  intros fam1 fam2.
  unfold FamilyCubicCompatible.
  rewrite Forall_app.
  reflexivity.
Qed.

Lemma compile_axiom_clause_correct :
  forall line_code,
    FamilySat (compile_axiom_clause line_code) <->
    axiom_recognition_codeb line_code = true.
Proof.
  intro line_code.
  unfold compile_axiom_clause.
  rewrite FamilySat_singleton.
  reflexivity.
Qed.

Lemma compile_target_clause_correct :
  forall found_code target_code,
    FamilySat (compile_target_clause found_code target_code) <->
    target_matching_codeb found_code target_code = true.
Proof.
  intros found_code target_code.
  unfold compile_target_clause.
  rewrite FamilySat_singleton.
  reflexivity.
Qed.

Lemma compile_line_lookup_clause_correct :
  forall fuel i proof_code expected_code,
    FamilySat (compile_line_lookup_clause fuel i proof_code expected_code) <->
    line_lookup_codeb fuel i proof_code = Some expected_code.
Proof.
  intros fuel i proof_code expected_code.
  unfold compile_line_lookup_clause.
  rewrite FamilySat_singleton.
  unfold ConstraintHolds.
  simpl.
  unfold line_lookup_codeb.
  destruct (coded_nth_bounded fuel i proof_code) as [got|] eqn:Hlookup.
  - split.
    + intro Heq.
      apply Nat.eqb_eq in Heq.
      subst got.
      reflexivity.
    + intro Hsome.
      inversion Hsome.
      subst got.
      apply Nat.eqb_eq.
      reflexivity.
  - split.
    + intro Hfalse.
      discriminate Hfalse.
    + intro Hsome.
      discriminate Hsome.
Qed.

Lemma compile_membership_clause_correct :
  forall fuel ctx_code entry_code,
    FamilySat (compile_membership_clause fuel ctx_code entry_code) <->
    membership_search_codeb fuel ctx_code entry_code = true.
Proof.
  intros fuel ctx_code entry_code.
  unfold compile_membership_clause.
  rewrite FamilySat_singleton.
  reflexivity.
Qed.

Lemma compile_mp_clause_correct :
  forall ctx_code line_code,
    FamilySat (compile_mp_clause ctx_code line_code) <->
    mp_witness_search_codeb ctx_code line_code = true.
Proof.
  intros ctx_code line_code.
  unfold compile_mp_clause.
  rewrite FamilySat_singleton.
  reflexivity.
Qed.

Lemma compile_trace_clause_correct :
  forall ctx_code proof_code fuel,
    FamilySat (compile_trace_clause ctx_code proof_code fuel) <->
    trace_recursion_codeb ctx_code proof_code fuel = true.
Proof.
  intros ctx_code proof_code fuel.
  unfold compile_trace_clause.
  rewrite FamilySat_singleton.
  reflexivity.
Qed.

Lemma compile_last_clause_correct :
  forall fuel proof_code expected_code,
    FamilySat (compile_last_clause fuel proof_code expected_code) <->
    coded_last_bounded fuel proof_code = Some expected_code.
Proof.
  intros fuel proof_code expected_code.
  unfold compile_last_clause.
  rewrite FamilySat_singleton.
  unfold ConstraintHolds.
  simpl.
  destruct (coded_last_bounded fuel proof_code) as [got|] eqn:Hlast.
  - split.
    + intro Heq.
      apply Nat.eqb_eq in Heq.
      subst got.
      reflexivity.
    + intro Hsome.
      inversion Hsome.
      subst got.
      apply Nat.eqb_eq.
      reflexivity.
  - split.
    + intro Hfalse.
      discriminate Hfalse.
    + intro Hsome.
      discriminate Hsome.
Qed.

Lemma compile_axiom_clause_quadratic :
  forall line_code,
    FamilyQuadraticCompatible (compile_axiom_clause line_code).
Proof.
  intro line_code.
  unfold compile_axiom_clause.
  apply FamilyQuadraticCompatible_singleton.
  unfold QuadraticCompatibleConstraint.
  rewrite internal_constraint_degree_axiom.
  lia.
Qed.

Lemma compile_target_clause_quadratic :
  forall found_code target_code,
    FamilyQuadraticCompatible (compile_target_clause found_code target_code).
Proof.
  intros found_code target_code.
  unfold compile_target_clause.
  apply FamilyQuadraticCompatible_singleton.
  unfold QuadraticCompatibleConstraint.
  rewrite internal_constraint_degree_target.
  lia.
Qed.

Lemma compile_line_lookup_clause_quadratic :
  forall fuel i proof_code expected_code,
    FamilyQuadraticCompatible
      (compile_line_lookup_clause fuel i proof_code expected_code).
Proof.
  intros fuel i proof_code expected_code.
  unfold compile_line_lookup_clause.
  apply FamilyQuadraticCompatible_singleton.
  unfold QuadraticCompatibleConstraint.
  rewrite internal_constraint_degree_line_lookup.
  lia.
Qed.

Lemma compile_membership_clause_quadratic :
  forall fuel ctx_code entry_code,
    FamilyQuadraticCompatible (compile_membership_clause fuel ctx_code entry_code).
Proof.
  intros fuel ctx_code entry_code.
  unfold compile_membership_clause.
  apply FamilyQuadraticCompatible_singleton.
  unfold QuadraticCompatibleConstraint.
  rewrite internal_constraint_degree_membership.
  lia.
Qed.

Lemma compile_mp_clause_quadratic :
  forall ctx_code line_code,
    FamilyQuadraticCompatible (compile_mp_clause ctx_code line_code).
Proof.
  intros ctx_code line_code.
  unfold compile_mp_clause.
  apply FamilyQuadraticCompatible_singleton.
  unfold QuadraticCompatibleConstraint.
  rewrite internal_constraint_degree_mp.
  lia.
Qed.

Lemma compile_last_clause_quadratic :
  forall fuel proof_code expected_code,
    FamilyQuadraticCompatible (compile_last_clause fuel proof_code expected_code).
Proof.
  intros fuel proof_code expected_code.
  unfold compile_last_clause.
  apply FamilyQuadraticCompatible_singleton.
  unfold QuadraticCompatibleConstraint.
  rewrite internal_constraint_degree_last.
  lia.
Qed.

Theorem axiom_clause_degree_budget :
  forall line_code,
    FamilyQuadraticCompatible (compile_axiom_clause line_code).
Proof.
  exact compile_axiom_clause_quadratic.
Qed.

Theorem extraction_clauses_degree_budget :
  forall fuel i proof_code expected_code
         fuel_m ctx_code entry_code
         fuel_last proof_code_last expected_last,
    FamilyQuadraticCompatible
      (compile_line_lookup_clause fuel i proof_code expected_code) /\
    FamilyQuadraticCompatible
      (compile_membership_clause fuel_m ctx_code entry_code) /\
    FamilyQuadraticCompatible
      (compile_last_clause fuel_last proof_code_last expected_last).
Proof.
  intros fuel i proof_code expected_code
         fuel_m ctx_code entry_code
         fuel_last proof_code_last expected_last.
  split.
  - apply compile_line_lookup_clause_quadratic.
  - split.
    + apply compile_membership_clause_quadratic.
    + apply compile_last_clause_quadratic.
Qed.

Theorem mp_clause_degree_budget :
  forall ctx_code line_code,
    FamilyQuadraticCompatible (compile_mp_clause ctx_code line_code).
Proof.
  exact compile_mp_clause_quadratic.
Qed.

Theorem selector_activation_is_only_cubic_source :
  forall c,
    InternalConstraint_degree c = 3 ->
    SelectorActivatedConstraint c.
Proof.
  intros c Hdeg.
  apply (proj2 (selector_activation_exactly_cubic c)).
  exact Hdeg.
Qed.

Theorem trace_clause_cubic_from_selector_activation :
  forall ctx_code proof_code fuel,
    SelectorActivatedConstraint (IC_TraceRecursion ctx_code proof_code fuel) /\
    InternalConstraint_degree (IC_TraceRecursion ctx_code proof_code fuel) = 3.
Proof.
  intros ctx_code proof_code fuel.
  split.
  - constructor.
  - rewrite internal_constraint_degree_trace.
    reflexivity.
Qed.

Lemma compile_axiom_clause_cubic :
  forall line_code,
    FamilyCubicCompatible (compile_axiom_clause line_code).
Proof.
  intro line_code.
  unfold compile_axiom_clause.
  apply FamilyCubicCompatible_singleton.
Qed.

Lemma compile_target_clause_cubic :
  forall found_code target_code,
    FamilyCubicCompatible (compile_target_clause found_code target_code).
Proof.
  intros found_code target_code.
  unfold compile_target_clause.
  apply FamilyCubicCompatible_singleton.
Qed.

Lemma compile_line_lookup_clause_cubic :
  forall fuel i proof_code expected_code,
    FamilyCubicCompatible
      (compile_line_lookup_clause fuel i proof_code expected_code).
Proof.
  intros fuel i proof_code expected_code.
  unfold compile_line_lookup_clause.
  apply FamilyCubicCompatible_singleton.
Qed.

Lemma compile_membership_clause_cubic :
  forall fuel ctx_code entry_code,
    FamilyCubicCompatible (compile_membership_clause fuel ctx_code entry_code).
Proof.
  intros fuel ctx_code entry_code.
  unfold compile_membership_clause.
  apply FamilyCubicCompatible_singleton.
Qed.

Lemma compile_mp_clause_cubic :
  forall ctx_code line_code,
    FamilyCubicCompatible (compile_mp_clause ctx_code line_code).
Proof.
  intros ctx_code line_code.
  unfold compile_mp_clause.
  apply FamilyCubicCompatible_singleton.
Qed.

Lemma compile_trace_clause_cubic :
  forall ctx_code proof_code fuel,
    FamilyCubicCompatible (compile_trace_clause ctx_code proof_code fuel).
Proof.
  intros ctx_code proof_code fuel.
  unfold compile_trace_clause.
  apply FamilyCubicCompatible_singleton.
Qed.

Lemma compile_last_clause_cubic :
  forall fuel proof_code expected_code,
    FamilyCubicCompatible (compile_last_clause fuel proof_code expected_code).
Proof.
  intros fuel proof_code expected_code.
  unfold compile_last_clause.
  apply FamilyCubicCompatible_singleton.
Qed.

Record CheckerCode : Type := {
  checker_code_proof : nat;
  checker_code_target : nat;
}.

Definition checker_accepts_code (code : CheckerCode) : Prop :=
  checker_kernel_codeb (checker_code_proof code) (checker_code_target code) = true.

Definition compile_checker_family (code : CheckerCode) : ConstraintFamily :=
  let proof_code := checker_code_proof code in
  let target_code := checker_code_target code in
  let fuel := proof_code_size_bound proof_code in
  match coded_last_bounded fuel proof_code with
  | Some found_code =>
      [ IC_LastLookup fuel proof_code found_code;
        IC_TargetMatching found_code target_code;
        IC_TraceRecursion 0 proof_code fuel ]
  | None =>
      [IC_LastLookup fuel proof_code 0]
  end.

Definition FamilyCompiled (code : CheckerCode) (fam : ConstraintFamily) : Prop :=
  fam = compile_checker_family code.

Lemma compile_checker_family_cubic :
  forall code,
    FamilyCubicCompatible (compile_checker_family code).
Proof.
  intros [proof_code target_code].
  unfold compile_checker_family.
  cbn.
  remember (coded_last_bounded (proof_code_size_bound proof_code) proof_code)
    as last eqn:Hlast.
  destruct last as [found_code|].
  - simpl.
    constructor.
    + unfold CubicCompatibleConstraint, InternalConstraint_degree.
      simpl.
      lia.
    + constructor.
      * unfold CubicCompatibleConstraint, InternalConstraint_degree.
        simpl.
        lia.
      * constructor.
        -- unfold CubicCompatibleConstraint, InternalConstraint_degree.
           simpl.
          lia.
        -- constructor.
  - simpl.
    constructor.
    + unfold CubicCompatibleConstraint, InternalConstraint_degree.
      simpl.
      lia.
    + constructor.
Qed.

(*@box.section@[[SEMANTIC FINITE-FAMILY LAYER]]@*)

(*@c.inline@[[We now expose finite internal families as first-class semantic objects. This layer is independent of checker recursion: it defines assignments, well-formedness, and satisfaction directly on families, and serves as the canonical intermediate object for downstream aggregation.]]@*)
(*@c.unicode@[[Canonical finite-family semantics: assignment ρ, well-formedness WF(F), satisfaction Sat(F,ρ), and satisfiability ∃ρ Sat(F,ρ)]]@*)

Definition FamilyAssignment : Type := nat -> nat.

Definition ConstraintSatisfiedUnder
  (rho : FamilyAssignment) (c : InternalConstraint) : Prop :=
  ConstraintHolds c = true.

Definition FamilyWellFormed (fam : ConstraintFamily) : Prop :=
  Forall (fun c => InternalConstraint_degree c <= 3) fam.

Definition FamilySatisfiedUnder
  (rho : FamilyAssignment) (fam : ConstraintFamily) : Prop :=
  Forall (fun c => ConstraintSatisfiedUnder rho c) fam.

Definition FamilySatisfiable (fam : ConstraintFamily) : Prop :=
  exists rho,
    FamilyWellFormed fam /\
    FamilySatisfiedUnder rho fam.

Record SemanticFamily : Type := {
  semantic_family_constraints : ConstraintFamily;
  semantic_family_wf : FamilyWellFormed semantic_family_constraints;
}.

Definition SemanticFamilySatUnder
  (rho : FamilyAssignment) (sf : SemanticFamily) : Prop :=
  FamilySatisfiedUnder rho (semantic_family_constraints sf).

Definition SemanticFamilySatisfiable (sf : SemanticFamily) : Prop :=
  exists rho, SemanticFamilySatUnder rho sf.

Lemma family_well_formed_iff_cubic_compatible :
  forall fam,
    FamilyWellFormed fam <-> FamilyCubicCompatible fam.
Proof.
  intro fam.
  unfold FamilyWellFormed, FamilyCubicCompatible, CubicCompatibleConstraint.
  reflexivity.
Qed.

Lemma family_satisfied_under_iff_family_sat :
  forall rho fam,
    FamilySatisfiedUnder rho fam <-> FamilySat fam.
Proof.
  intros rho fam.
  unfold FamilySatisfiedUnder, ConstraintSatisfiedUnder, FamilySat.
  reflexivity.
Qed.

Lemma compile_checker_family_well_formed :
  forall code,
    FamilyWellFormed (compile_checker_family code).
Proof.
  intro code.
  apply (proj2 (family_well_formed_iff_cubic_compatible _)).
  apply compile_checker_family_cubic.
Qed.

Definition CheckerGeneratedSemanticFamily (code : CheckerCode) : SemanticFamily.
Proof.
  refine
    {| semantic_family_constraints := compile_checker_family code;
       semantic_family_wf := compile_checker_family_well_formed code |}.
Defined.

Lemma checker_generated_semantic_family_constraints :
  forall code,
    semantic_family_constraints (CheckerGeneratedSemanticFamily code) =
    compile_checker_family code.
Proof.
  intro code.
  reflexivity.
Qed.

Theorem every_emitted_internal_constraint_degree_le_3 :
  forall code c,
    In c (compile_checker_family code) ->
    InternalConstraint_degree c <= 3.
Proof.
  intros code c Hin.
  pose proof (compile_checker_family_cubic code) as Hfam.
  unfold FamilyCubicCompatible in Hfam.
  apply Forall_forall with (x := c) in Hfam.
  - unfold CubicCompatibleConstraint in Hfam.
    exact Hfam.
  - exact Hin.
Qed.

Theorem emitted_cubic_terms_are_selector_activated :
  forall code c,
    In c (compile_checker_family code) ->
    InternalConstraint_degree c = 3 ->
    SelectorActivatedConstraint c.
Proof.
  intros code c Hin Hdeg.
  apply selector_activation_is_only_cubic_source.
  exact Hdeg.
Qed.

Lemma checker_accepts_implies_compiled_family_sat :
  forall code,
    checker_accepts_code code ->
    FamilySat (compile_checker_family code).
Proof.
  intros [proof_code target_code] Haccept.
  unfold checker_accepts_code in Haccept.
  unfold checker_kernel_codeb in Haccept.
  cbn in Haccept.
  destruct (coded_last_bounded (proof_code_size_bound proof_code) proof_code)
    as [found_code|] eqn:Hlast in Haccept.
  - simpl in Haccept.
    apply andb_true_iff in Haccept.
    destruct Haccept as [Htarget Htrace].
    unfold compile_checker_family.
    cbn.
    rewrite Hlast.
    simpl.
    constructor.
    + unfold ConstraintHolds.
      simpl.
      rewrite Hlast.
      apply Nat.eqb_refl.
    + constructor.
      * exact Htarget.
      * constructor.
        -- exact Htrace.
        -- constructor.
  - simpl in Haccept.
    discriminate Haccept.
Qed.

Lemma checker_accepts_implies_compiled_family_sat_with_degree :
  forall code,
    checker_accepts_code code ->
    FamilySat (compile_checker_family code) /\
    FamilyCubicCompatible (compile_checker_family code).
Proof.
  intros code Hacc.
  split.
  - apply checker_accepts_implies_compiled_family_sat.
    exact Hacc.
  - apply compile_checker_family_cubic.
Qed.

Lemma compiled_family_sat_implies_checker_accepts :
  forall code fam,
    FamilyCompiled code fam ->
    FamilySat fam ->
    checker_accepts_code code.
Proof.
  intros [proof_code target_code] fam Hcompiled Hsat.
  unfold FamilyCompiled in Hcompiled.
  subst fam.
  unfold checker_accepts_code.
  unfold FamilySat in Hsat.
  unfold compile_checker_family in Hsat.
  cbn in Hsat.
  destruct (coded_last_bounded (proof_code_size_bound proof_code) proof_code)
    as [found_last|] eqn:Hlast in Hsat.
  - simpl in Hsat.
    repeat match goal with
    | H : Forall _ (_ :: _) |- _ =>
        inversion H; subst; clear H
    | H : Forall _ [] |- _ =>
        inversion H; subst; clear H
    end.
    unfold ConstraintHolds in *.
    simpl in *.
    match goal with
    | H : target_matching_codeb _ _ = true |- _ =>
        pose proof H as Htarget_sat
    end.
    match goal with
    | H : trace_recursion_codeb _ _ _ = true |- _ =>
        pose proof H as Htrace_sat
    end.
    unfold checker_kernel_codeb.
    destruct (coded_last_bounded (proof_code_size_bound proof_code) proof_code)
      as [found_goal|] eqn:Hgoal.
    + rewrite Hlast in Hgoal.
      inversion Hgoal; subst.
      inversion Hlast; subst.
      simpl.
      apply andb_true_iff.
      split.
      * exact Htarget_sat.
      * exact Htrace_sat.
    + exfalso.
      congruence.
  - simpl in Hsat.
    inversion Hsat as [|c1 l1 Hlast_sat Hnil]; subst.
    inversion Hnil; subst.
    unfold ConstraintHolds in Hlast_sat.
    simpl in Hlast_sat.
    rewrite Hlast in Hlast_sat.
    simpl in Hlast_sat.
    discriminate Hlast_sat.
Qed.

Theorem checker_clause_compilation_master_with_degree_invariant :
  forall code,
    checker_accepts_code code <->
    exists fam,
      FamilyCompiled code fam /\
      FamilySat fam /\
      FamilyCubicCompatible fam.
Proof.
  intro code.
  split.
  - intro Hacc.
    exists (compile_checker_family code).
    split.
    + reflexivity.
    + split.
      * apply checker_accepts_implies_compiled_family_sat.
        exact Hacc.
      * apply compile_checker_family_cubic.
  - intros [fam [Hcompiled [Hsat _]]].
    eapply compiled_family_sat_implies_checker_accepts.
    + exact Hcompiled.
    + exact Hsat.
Qed.

Theorem checker_clause_compilation_master :
  forall code,
    checker_accepts_code code <->
    exists fam, FamilyCompiled code fam /\ FamilySat fam.
Proof.
  intro code.
  split.
  - intro Hacc.
    destruct (proj1 (checker_clause_compilation_master_with_degree_invariant code) Hacc)
      as [fam [Hcompiled [Hsat _]]].
    exists fam.
    split.
    + exact Hcompiled.
    + exact Hsat.
  - intros [fam [Hcompiled Hsat]].
    apply (proj2 (checker_clause_compilation_master_with_degree_invariant code)).
    exists fam.
    split.
    + exact Hcompiled.
    + split.
      * exact Hsat.
      * rewrite Hcompiled.
        apply compile_checker_family_cubic.
Qed.

Theorem checker_generated_family_semantic_equivalence :
  forall code,
    checker_accepts_code code <->
    SemanticFamilySatisfiable (CheckerGeneratedSemanticFamily code).
Proof.
  intro code.
  split.
  - intro Hacc.
    exists (fun _ => 0).
    unfold SemanticFamilySatUnder.
    apply (proj2 (family_satisfied_under_iff_family_sat _ _)).
    apply checker_accepts_implies_compiled_family_sat.
    exact Hacc.
  - intros [rho Hsat_under].
    apply (compiled_family_sat_implies_checker_accepts
             code
             (compile_checker_family code)).
    + reflexivity.
    + apply (proj1 (family_satisfied_under_iff_family_sat rho _)).
      exact Hsat_under.
Qed.

Theorem checker_generated_family_semantic_satisfiable_equivalence :
  forall code,
    checker_accepts_code code <->
    exists fam,
      FamilyCompiled code fam /\
      FamilySatisfiable fam.
Proof.
  intro code.
  split.
  - intro Hacc.
    exists (compile_checker_family code).
    split.
    + reflexivity.
    + exists (fun _ => 0).
      split.
      * apply compile_checker_family_well_formed.
      * apply (proj2 (family_satisfied_under_iff_family_sat _ _)).
        apply checker_accepts_implies_compiled_family_sat.
        exact Hacc.
  - intros [fam [Hcompiled [rho [_ Hsat_under]]]].
    apply (compiled_family_sat_implies_checker_accepts code fam).
    + exact Hcompiled.
    + apply (proj1 (family_satisfied_under_iff_family_sat rho fam)).
      exact Hsat_under.
Qed.

(*@box.section@[[STRUCTURAL SUPPORT]]@*)

(*@c.inline@[[The later proof-predicate and theoremhood routes use the standard structural facts of the checker: monotonicity under context inclusion, concatenation of checked prefixes and suffixes, and extraction of the accepted terminal line.]]@*)
(*@c.unicode@[[Structural support lemmas: monotonicity, concatenation, and terminal-line extraction]]@*)

Lemma check_line_monotone_incl :
  forall ctx1 ctx2 line,
    (forall x, In x ctx1 -> In x ctx2) ->
    check_line ctx1 line = true ->
    check_line ctx2 line = true.
Proof.
  (*@c.unicode@[[Monotonicity at line level: context inclusion preserves successful line checking.]]@*)
  intros ctx1 ctx2 line Hincl Hline.
  unfold check_line in *.
  apply orb_true_iff in Hline.
  apply orb_true_iff.
  destruct Hline as [Haxiom | Hmp].
  - left.
    exact Haxiom.
  - right.
    apply (mp_witness_monotone_incl ctx1 ctx2 line Hincl Hmp).
Qed.

(*@c.unicode@[[Checker monotonicity under context inclusion: enlarging the available context cannot invalidate a previously successful `check_lines`.]]@*)

Lemma check_lines_monotone_incl :
  forall ctx1 ctx2 pf,
    (forall x, In x ctx1 -> In x ctx2) ->
    check_lines ctx1 pf = true ->
    check_lines ctx2 pf = true.
Proof.
  intros ctx1 ctx2 pf.
  revert ctx1 ctx2.
  induction pf as [|line pf IH]; intros ctx1 ctx2 Hincl Hcheck.
  - simpl in *.
    reflexivity.
  - simpl in Hcheck.
    apply andb_true_iff in Hcheck.
    destruct Hcheck as [Hline Hrest].
    simpl.
    apply andb_true_iff.
    split.
    + apply (check_line_monotone_incl ctx1 ctx2 line Hincl Hline).
    + apply IH with (ctx1 := line :: ctx1) (ctx2 := line :: ctx2).
      * intros x Hinx.
        simpl in Hinx.
        destruct Hinx as [Hx | Hx].
        -- subst x.
           left.
           reflexivity.
        -- right.
           apply Hincl.
           exact Hx.
      * exact Hrest.
Qed.

(*@c.unicode@[[Checker concatenation bridge: if `pf1` checks from `ctx` and `pf2` checks from `rev pf1 ++ ctx`, then the concatenated proof `pf1 ++ pf2` checks from `ctx`.]]@*)

Lemma check_lines_cat :
  forall ctx pf1 pf2,
    check_lines ctx pf1 = true ->
    check_lines (rev pf1 ++ ctx) pf2 = true ->
    check_lines ctx (pf1 ++ pf2) = true.
Proof.
  intros ctx pf1.
  revert ctx.
  induction pf1 as [|line pf1 IH]; intros ctx pf2 Hpref Hsuf.
  - simpl in *.
    exact Hsuf.
  - simpl in Hpref.
    apply andb_true_iff in Hpref.
    destruct Hpref as [Hline Hrest].
    simpl.
    apply andb_true_iff.
    split.
    + exact Hline.
    + apply (IH (line :: ctx) pf2 Hrest).
      replace (rev (line :: pf1) ++ ctx) with ((rev pf1 ++ [line]) ++ ctx) in Hsuf.
      2:{ reflexivity. }
      replace ((rev pf1 ++ [line]) ++ ctx) with (rev pf1 ++ (line :: ctx)) in Hsuf.
      * exact Hsuf.
      * rewrite <- app_assoc.
        reflexivity.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[This is the empty-context specialization of the general concatenation lemma. It is the form used most often when separately checked proof fragments are concatenated into one closed proof trace.]]@*)
(*@c.unicode@[[Empty-context concatenation lemma for checked proof fragments]]@*)

Lemma check_lines_cat_nil :
  forall pf1 pf2,
    check_lines [] pf1 = true ->
    check_lines (rev pf1) pf2 = true ->
    check_lines [] (pf1 ++ pf2) = true.
Proof.
  intros pf1 pf2 Hpf1 Hpf2.
  apply (check_lines_cat [] pf1 pf2).
  - exact Hpf1.
  - rewrite app_nil_r.
    exact Hpf2.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[The next three lemmas isolate the elementary behavior of the terminal-line extractor. They say that a recovered last line belongs to the trace, still belongs after reversal, and is exactly the last line of an appended singleton.]]@*)
(*@c.unicode@[[Terminal-line extractor support lemmas: membership, reverse-membership, singleton-append behavior]]@*)

Lemma last_opt_in :
  forall pf x,
    last_opt pf = Some x ->
    In x pf.
Proof.
  (*@c.unicode@[[Terminal inclusion law: `last_opt pf = Some x` implies membership `x ∈ pf`.]]@*)
  induction pf as [|a pf IH]; intros x Hlast.
  - inversion Hlast.
  - simpl in Hlast.
    destruct pf as [|b pf'].
    + inversion Hlast.
      subst x.
      simpl.
      left.
      reflexivity.
    + simpl.
      right.
      apply IH.
      exact Hlast.
Qed.

(*@c.unicode@[[Reverse-membership lemma for terminal extraction: if `last_opt pf = Some x`, then `x` occurs in `rev pf`.]]@*)

Lemma last_opt_in_rev :
  forall pf x,
    last_opt pf = Some x ->
    In x (rev pf).
Proof.
  intros pf x Hlast.
  apply (proj1 (@in_rev Form pf x)).
  apply (last_opt_in pf x Hlast).
Qed.

(*@c.unicode@[[Singleton-append terminal law: appending `[x]` forces `x` to be the extracted last element (`last_opt (pf ++ [x]) = Some x`).]]@*)

Lemma last_opt_app_singleton :
  forall pf x,
    last_opt (pf ++ [x]) = Some x.
Proof.
  induction pf as [|a pf IH]; intro x.
  - reflexivity.
  - simpl.
    destruct pf as [|b pf'].
    + reflexivity.
    + simpl.
      apply IH.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[`check_true_components` is the main decomposition lemma for successful checking. It separates acceptance into trace validity from the empty context together with exact recovery of the checked target as the terminal line.]]@*)
(*@c.unicode@[[Checker decomposition: check pf target = true ⇒ check_lines [] pf = true ∧ last_opt pf = Some target]]@*)

Lemma check_true_components :
  forall pf target,
    check pf target = true ->
    check_lines [] pf = true /\ last_opt pf = Some target.
Proof.
  intros pf target Hcheck.
  unfold check in Hcheck.
  destruct (last_opt pf) as [found |] eqn:Hlast.
  - apply andb_true_iff in Hcheck.
    destruct Hcheck as [Hlines Heq].
    apply form_eqb_true_iff in Heq.
    subst found.
    split.
    + exact Hlines.
    + reflexivity.
  - discriminate Hcheck.
Qed.

End MakeWith.

Module Make (A : D001_08__Proof_Theory_Input.INPUT).
  Module GN := D001_09__Carryless_Numbering.Make A.
  Include MakeWith A GN.
End Make.

Module Type SIG (A : D001_08__Proof_Theory_Input.INPUT)
  (GN : D001_09__Carryless_Numbering.SIG A).
  Include MakeWith A GN.
End SIG.


(* ---- theories/D001/D001_11__Provability.v ---- *)

(*@file@*)
(*@H.1@[[Proofcase / D001 / Provability]]@*)
(*@H.2@[[Overview]]@*)
(*@p.l@[[This route packages the concrete proof predicate and the coded provability predicates of the additive proof theory.]]@*)
(*@p.l@[[It sits directly above the evaluator inside the abstract proof-theory functor: it turns accepted finite traces into numerical proof objects, defines `ProofOf` and `Prov_RA`, and proves the immediate bridges from checker acceptance to those predicates.]]@*)
(*@p.l@[[It does not yet package abstract theoremhood.]]@*)
(*@H.2@[[Contents]]@*)
(*@ptoclist.roman@[[CONCRETE PROOF OBJECTS]][[Packaged concrete proof objects coded by their trace and target together.]]@*)
(*@ptoclist.roman@[[CODED PROOF PREDICATES]][[Formula-code proof predicate `ProofOf`, derived provability `Prov_RA`, and concrete packaged-code provability.]]@*)
(*@ptoclist.roman@[[CHECKER BRIDGES]][[Immediate bridges from checker acceptance into the concrete and abstract coded proof predicates.]]@*)
(*@ptoclist.roman@[[CHECKER-LEVEL MP]][[Explicit modus-ponens composition at the checker level, now landing in coded provability.]]@*)

From D001 Require Export D001_10__Evaluation.

Module MakeWith (A : D001_08__Proof_Theory_Input.INPUT)
  (GN : D001_09__Carryless_Numbering.SIG A)
  (Eval : D001_10__Evaluation.SIG A GN).
Import A GN Eval.

(*@box.section@[[CONCRETE PROOF OBJECTS]]@*)

(*@c.inline@[[A concrete proof object remembers both the proof-trace code and the packaged concrete code carrying the target formula. This is the direct numerical shadow of a checked finite proof.]]@*)
(*@c.unicode@[[Concrete_Proof(p,u): witness trace/target with checker acceptance and matching codes]]@*)

Definition Concrete_Proof (p u : nat) : Prop :=
  exists pf target,
    p = code_of_proof pf /\
    u = code_of_concrete pf target /\
    check pf target = true.

(*@c.inline@[[`ProvCodeConcrete` forgets only the separate trace code and keeps the packaged concrete-proof code as the witness-bearing notion of concrete provability. It is the existential closure of `Concrete_Proof` over the auxiliary trace-code component.]]@*)
(*@c.unicode@[[Concrete-code provability: ProvCodeConcrete(u) ≔ ∃p, Concrete_Proof(p,u)]]@*)

Definition ProvCodeConcrete (u : nat) : Prop :=
  exists p, Concrete_Proof p u.

(*@box.section@[[CODED PROOF PREDICATES]]@*)

(*@c.inline@[[`ProofOf(p,u)` is the Prop-level coded proof predicate of the proof-theory nucleus. Here `u` is a formula code rather than a packaged concrete-proof code, so `Prov_RA(u)` reads as genuine coded provability of the formula coded by `u`.]]@*)
(*@c.unicode@[[Coded proof predicate over formula codes: ProofOf(p,u)]]@*)

Definition ProofOf (p u : nat) : Prop :=
  exists pf target,
    p = code_of_proof pf /\
    u = code_of_formula target /\
    check pf target = true.

(*@c.inline@[[`Prov_RA` is the witness-forgetting version of `ProofOf`: some coded proof trace establishes the formula whose code is `u`. This is the folder's canonical coded provability predicate over formula codes.]]@*)
(*@c.unicode@[[Coded provability predicate: Prov_RA(u) ≔ ∃p, ProofOf(p,u)]]@*)

Definition Prov_RA (u : nat) : Prop :=
  exists p, ProofOf p u.

(*@c.inline@[[`ProofOfbool` is the executable coded proof predicate. It decodes the candidate proof trace and the target formula through the abstract input interface and then runs the local checker on the recovered objects.]]@*)
(*@c.unicode@[[Executable coded proof predicate: ProofOfbool(p,u)]]@*)

Definition ProofOfbool (p u : nat) : bool :=
  match proof_of_code p, formula_of_code u with
  | Some pf, Some target => check pf target
  | _, _ => false
  end.

(*@box.section@[[CHECKER BRIDGES]]@*)

(*@c.inline@[[These unfold lemmas expose the concrete existential content of the proof predicates and then lift checker acceptance into the coded proof and coded provability surfaces.]]@*)
(*@c.unicode@[[Bridge unfolds expose existential witness content of coded proof predicates]]@*)

Theorem Concrete_Proof_unfold :
  forall p u,
    Concrete_Proof p u <->
    exists pf target,
      p = code_of_proof pf /\
      u = code_of_concrete pf target /\
      check pf target = true.
Proof.
  (*@c.unicode@[[Unfold equivalence: Concrete_Proof(p,u) ↔ ∃pf,target.(p=code_of_proof pf ∧ u=code_of_concrete pf target ∧ check pf target=true).]]@*)
  intros p u.
  unfold Concrete_Proof.
  tauto.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[`ProofOf_unfold` is the direct existential reading of the coded proof predicate. It exposes the exact proof trace, target formula, code equalities, and checker acceptance data hidden by the outer predicate name.]]@*)
(*@c.unicode@[[Unfold law for ProofOf(p,u) into explicit trace/target witnesses]]@*)

Theorem ProofOf_unfold :
  forall p u,
    ProofOf p u <->
    exists pf target,
      p = code_of_proof pf /\
      u = code_of_formula target /\
      check pf target = true.
Proof.
  intros p u.
  unfold ProofOf.
  tauto.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[`ProofOfbool_correct` is the main adequacy bridge for the executable coded proof predicate. Successful boolean recognition is equivalent to membership in the semantic coded proof predicate `ProofOf`.]]@*)
(*@c.unicode@[[Adequacy bridge: ProofOfbool p u = true ↔ ProofOf p u]]@*)

(*@todo@[[Temporary state: `ProofOfbool_correct` still carries duplicated decoder-completeness bookkeeping on both directions. If the coding layer changes again, extract the successful-code recovery steps into reusable lemmas before this bridge becomes harder to audit.]]@*)

(*@c.unicode@[[Main coded-proof adequacy theorem: boolean recognition and semantic coded provability coincide pointwise for every `(p,u)`.]]@*)

Theorem ProofOfbool_correct :
  forall p u,
    ProofOfbool p u = true <-> ProofOf p u.
Proof.
  intros p u.
  unfold ProofOfbool, ProofOf.
  split.
  - destruct (proof_of_code p) as [pf |] eqn:Hpf;
      destruct (formula_of_code u) as [target |] eqn:Htarget;
      simpl; try discriminate.
    intro Hcheck.
    exists pf, target.
    repeat split.
    + symmetry.
      unfold code_of_proof.
      apply proof_of_code_sound.
      exact Hpf.
    + symmetry.
      unfold code_of_formula.
      apply (proj1 (formula_of_code_correct u target)).
      exact Htarget.
    + exact Hcheck.
  - intros [pf [target [Hp [Hu Hcheck]]]].
    assert (Hpf_complete : proof_of_code p = Some pf).
    {
      rewrite Hp.
      unfold code_of_proof.
      apply proof_of_code_complete.
    }
    assert (Htarget_complete : formula_of_code u = Some target).
    {
      apply (proj2 (formula_of_code_correct u target)).
      symmetry.
      unfold code_of_formula.
      exact Hu.
    }
    rewrite Hpf_complete.
    rewrite Htarget_complete.
    exact Hcheck.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[The next bridge packages a successful checker run as a concrete proof object. It records both the proof-trace code and the packaged concrete code containing the proved target.]]@*)
(*@c.unicode@[[Checker acceptance yields Concrete_Proof at corresponding codes]]@*)

Lemma checker_accepts_implies_Concrete_Proof :
  forall pf target,
    check pf target = true ->
    Concrete_Proof (code_of_proof pf) (code_of_concrete pf target).
Proof.
  intros pf target Hcheck.
  unfold Concrete_Proof.
  exists pf, target.
  split.
  - reflexivity.
  - split.
    + reflexivity.
    + exact Hcheck.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[From a checked proof trace we immediately obtain the existentially packaged concrete-proof-code predicate. This is the one-step lift from checker acceptance into numerical concrete provability.]]@*)
(*@c.unicode@[[Checker acceptance lifts to ProvCodeConcrete for packaged concrete code]]@*)

Lemma checker_accepts_implies_ProvCodeConcrete :
  forall pf target,
    check pf target = true ->
    ProvCodeConcrete (code_of_concrete pf target).
Proof.
  intros pf target Hcheck.
  unfold ProvCodeConcrete.
  exists (code_of_proof pf).
  apply checker_accepts_implies_Concrete_Proof.
  exact Hcheck.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[A checked finite proof also yields the semantic coded proof predicate `ProofOf`: the proof-trace code and the target-formula code are simply the local codes of the accepted witness.]]@*)
(*@c.unicode@[[Checker acceptance lifts to ProofOf at formula-code level]]@*)

Lemma checker_accepts_implies_ProofOf :
  forall pf target,
    check pf target = true ->
    ProofOf (code_of_proof pf) (code_of_formula target).
Proof.
  (*@c.unicode@[[Checker-to-proof bridge: check(pf,target)=true ⇒ ProofOf(code_of_proof pf, code_of_formula target).]]@*)
  intros pf target Hcheck.
  unfold ProofOf.
  exists pf, target.
  split.
  - reflexivity.
  - split.
    + reflexivity.
    + exact Hcheck.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[Finally, checker acceptance lifts into coded provability itself by existentially hiding the concrete proof-trace code from `ProofOf`.]]@*)
(*@c.unicode@[[Checker acceptance lifts to coded provability predicate Prov_RA]]@*)

Lemma checker_accepts_implies_Prov_RA :
  forall pf target,
    check pf target = true ->
    Prov_RA (code_of_formula target).
Proof.
  intros pf target Hcheck.
  unfold Prov_RA.
  exists (code_of_proof pf).
  apply checker_accepts_implies_ProofOf.
  exact Hcheck.
Qed.

(*@box.section@[[CHECKER-LEVEL MP]]@*)

(*@c.inline@[[The explicit checker-level modus-ponens constructor is the first place where proof objects are genuinely composed. Starting from checked witnesses for `u` and `u → v`, it builds a checked witness for `v` and immediately records the resulting coded provability fact.]]@*)
(*@c.unicode@[[Checker-level MP constructor composes proofs and yields coded provability consequences]]@*)

(*@todo@[[Temporary state: `Prov_MP_checker` is one of the tactic-heaviest objects in this folder. The proof currently threads context-membership, concatenation, and witness-search facts inline; if this route is extended, split those transport steps into named helper lemmas before the constructor script grows further.]]@*)

(*@c.unicode@[[Checker-level modus ponens theorem: from checked witnesses of `u` and `u → v`, construct a checked witness of `v` together with its coded provability facts.]]@*)

Theorem Prov_MP_checker :
  forall (u v : Form) (pf_u pf_imp : Proof),
    check pf_u u = true ->
    check pf_imp (Imp u v) = true ->
    exists pf_v,
      check pf_v v = true /\
      Prov_RA (code_of_formula v) /\
      ProvCodeConcrete (code_of_concrete pf_v v).
Proof.
  (*@c.unicode@[[MP composition contract: accepted proofs of `u` and `u→v` yield an accepted proof of `v` plus both coded-provability witnesses.]]@*)
  intros u v pf_u pf_imp Hu Himp.
  destruct (check_true_components pf_u u Hu) as [Hlines_u Hlast_u].
  destruct (check_true_components pf_imp (Imp u v) Himp)
    as [Hlines_imp Hlast_imp].

  (*@c.step@[[1]][[We first shift the accepted implication proof into the context created by the accepted proof of `u`, then concatenate the two checked traces. The combined checked context now contains both `u` and `u -> v`, so a final singleton line `v` is accepted by modus-ponens witness search.]]@*)


  assert (Hlines_imp_shift : check_lines (rev pf_u) pf_imp = true).
  {
    apply (check_lines_monotone_incl [] (rev pf_u) pf_imp).
    - intros x Hx.
      inversion Hx.
    - exact Hlines_imp.
  }

  assert (Hlines_uimp : check_lines [] (pf_u ++ pf_imp) = true).
  {
    apply check_lines_cat_nil.
    - exact Hlines_u.
    - exact Hlines_imp_shift.
  }

  assert (Hin_u_ctx : In u (rev pf_imp ++ rev pf_u)).
  {
    apply in_or_app.
    right.
    apply (last_opt_in_rev pf_u u Hlast_u).
  }

  assert (Hin_imp_ctx : In (Imp u v) (rev pf_imp ++ rev pf_u)).
  {
    apply in_or_app.
    left.
    apply (last_opt_in_rev pf_imp (Imp u v) Hlast_imp).
  }

  assert (Hlines_v : check_lines (rev (pf_u ++ pf_imp)) [v] = true).
  {
    simpl.
    rewrite andb_true_r.
    unfold check_line.
    apply orb_true_iff.
    right.
    rewrite rev_app_distr.
    apply (mp_witness_from_in (rev pf_imp ++ rev pf_u) u v).
    - exact Hin_u_ctx.
    - exact Hin_imp_ctx.
  }

  assert (Hlines_total : check_lines [] ((pf_u ++ pf_imp) ++ [v]) = true).
  {
    apply (check_lines_cat_nil (pf_u ++ pf_imp) [v]).
    - exact Hlines_uimp.
    - exact Hlines_v.
  }

  set (pf_v := (pf_u ++ pf_imp) ++ [v]).
  assert (Hcheck_v : check pf_v v = true).
  {
    subst pf_v.
    unfold check.
    rewrite last_opt_app_singleton.
    apply andb_true_iff.
    split.
    - exact Hlines_total.
    - apply form_eqb_refl.
  }

  exists pf_v.
  split.
  - exact Hcheck_v.
  - split.
    + exact (checker_accepts_implies_Prov_RA pf_v v Hcheck_v).
    + exact (checker_accepts_implies_ProvCodeConcrete pf_v v Hcheck_v).
Qed.



(*@c.inline@[[At theoremhood level we forget the particular proof code and remember only that some checked finite proof ends at the target formula.]]@*)
(*@c.unicode@[[Witness-free theoremhood: ProvFormula φ ≔ ∃pf, check pf φ = true]]@*)

Definition ProvFormula (phi : Form) : Prop :=
  exists pf, check pf phi = true.

(*@c.inline@[[`Thm_RA` is the stabilized theoremhood name for the same witness-free notion. It keeps the theoremhood surface aligned with the coded provability vocabulary used in the public API.]]@*)
(*@c.unicode@[[Stabilized theoremhood alias: Thm_RA ≡ ProvFormula]]@*)

Definition Thm_RA : Form -> Prop := ProvFormula.

(*@c.inline@[[`Thm` is the short legacy alias of `Thm_RA`. No new content is introduced here; this name only preserves the conventional theoremhood notation for downstream clients.]]@*)
(*@c.unicode@[[Legacy short alias: Thm ≡ Thm_RA]]@*)

Definition Thm : Form -> Prop := Thm_RA.

(*@box.section@[[MP CLOSURE]]@*)

(*@c.inline@[[The checker-level modus-ponens constructor immediately yields abstract theoremhood closure.]]@*)
(*@c.unicode@[[Theoremhood MP closure via checker-level constructor]]@*)

Theorem Prov_MP :
  forall (u v : Form),
    ProvFormula (Imp u v) ->
    ProvFormula u ->
    ProvFormula v.
Proof.
  (*@c.unicode@[[Abstract MP rule: from ProvFormula(u→v) and ProvFormula(u), derive ProvFormula(v).]]@*)
  intros u v Himp Hu.
  destruct Himp as [pf_imp Hcheck_imp].
  destruct Hu as [pf_u Hcheck_u].
  destruct (Prov_MP_checker u v pf_u pf_imp Hcheck_u Hcheck_imp)
    as [pf_v [Hcheck_v _]].
  exists pf_v.
  exact Hcheck_v.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[`Thm_RA_MP` is the same modus-ponens closure fact restated under the stabilized theoremhood name. It ensures the public theoremhood surface inherits the checker-level MP constructor without reopening `ProvFormula`.]]@*)
(*@c.unicode@[[MP closure restated on stabilized theoremhood name Thm_RA]]@*)

Theorem Thm_RA_MP :
  forall (u v : Form),
    Thm_RA (Imp u v) ->
    Thm_RA u ->
    Thm_RA v.
Proof.
  (*@c.unicode@[[Same MP closure under theoremhood alias: Thm_RA(u→v) ∧ Thm_RA(u) ⇒ Thm_RA(v).]]@*)
  exact Prov_MP.
Qed.

(*@box.section@[[CODED BRIDGE]]@*)

(*@c.inline@[[The final proof-theoretic bridge identifies theoremhood of a concrete formula with coded provability of its formula code.]]@*)
(*@c.unicode@[[Main coded bridge: Prov_RA(code_of_formula φ) ↔ Thm_RA φ]]@*)

Theorem Prov_RA_iff_Thm_RA :
  forall phi,
    Prov_RA (code_of_formula phi) <-> Thm_RA phi.
Proof.
  (*@c.unicode@[[Code/Formula bridge contract: coded provability at `code_of_formula φ` is equivalent to theoremhood of `φ`.]]@*)
  intro phi.
  split.
  - intros [p Hproof].
    pose proof (proj2 (ProofOfbool_correct p (code_of_formula phi)) Hproof)
      as Hbool.
    unfold ProofOfbool in Hbool.
    destruct (proof_of_code p) as [pf |] eqn:Hpf;
      simpl in Hbool; try discriminate.
    destruct (formula_of_code (code_of_formula phi)) as [target |] eqn:Htarget;
      simpl in Hbool; try discriminate.
    assert (Hcode_target :
      code_of_formula target = code_of_formula phi).
    {
      apply (proj1 (formula_of_code_correct (code_of_formula phi) target)).
      exact Htarget.
    }
    apply code_of_formula_injective in Hcode_target.
    subst target.
    exists pf.
    exact Hbool.
  - intros [pf Hcheck].
    unfold Prov_RA.
    exists (code_of_proof pf).
    apply (proj1 (ProofOfbool_correct (code_of_proof pf) (code_of_formula phi))).
    unfold ProofOfbool.
    unfold code_of_proof, code_of_formula.
    rewrite proof_of_code_complete.
    assert (Hdecode :
      formula_of_code (code_formula phi) = Some phi).
    {
      apply (proj2 (formula_of_code_correct (code_formula phi) phi)).
      reflexivity.
    }
    rewrite Hdecode.
    exact Hcheck.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[`Prov_iff_Thm` is the short alias of the main coded-bridge theorem. It preserves the traditional provability/theoremhood naming while reusing the already established `Prov_RA_iff_Thm_RA` equivalence unchanged.]]@*)
(*@c.unicode@[[Short alias theorem for the coded bridge: Prov_iff_Thm]]@*)

Theorem Prov_iff_Thm :
  forall phi,
    Prov_RA (code_of_formula phi) <-> Thm_RA phi.
Proof.
  exact Prov_RA_iff_Thm_RA.
Qed.


End MakeWith.

Module Make (A : D001_08__Proof_Theory_Input.INPUT).
  Module GN := D001_09__Carryless_Numbering.Make A.
  Module Eval := D001_10__Evaluation.MakeWith A GN.
  Include MakeWith A GN Eval.
End Make.

Module Type SIG (A : D001_08__Proof_Theory_Input.INPUT)
  (GN : D001_09__Carryless_Numbering.SIG A)
  (Eval : D001_10__Evaluation.SIG A GN).
  Include MakeWith A GN Eval.
End SIG.

(*@file@*)
(*@H.1@[[Proofcase / D001 / Local Proof-Theory Chain]]@*)
(*@H.2@[[Overview]]@*)
(*@p.l@[[This file is the internal junction between the four abstract proof-theory stages and their downstream consumers.]]@*)
(*@p.l@[[It aliases each stage to a short canonical name so that the public prelude and terminal API can refer to `Input.INPUT`, `Numbering.SIG`, `Evaluation.SIG`, and `Provability.SIG` without long qualified paths.]]@*)
(*@H.2@[[Contents]]@*)
(*@ptoclist.roman@[[STAGE ALIASES]][[Short module names for the four abstract proof-theory stages exported to all downstream routes.]]@*)

From D001 Require Export D001_11__Provability.

(*@box.section@[[STAGE ALIASES]]@*)

(*@c.inline@[[`Input` exposes the abstract object-language and coding signature. Downstream functors declare their first argument as `(A : Input.INPUT)`.]]@*)

Module Input := D001_08__Proof_Theory_Input.

(*@c.inline@[[`Numbering` exposes the Gödel-numbering layer. Downstream functors declare `(GN : Numbering.SIG A)`.]]@*)

Module Numbering := D001_09__Carryless_Numbering.

(*@c.inline@[[`Evaluation` exposes the Hilbert-style checker layer. Downstream functors declare `(HEV : Evaluation.SIG A GN)`.]]@*)

Module Evaluation := D001_10__Evaluation.

(*@c.inline@[[`Provability` exposes the proof-predicate and coded-provability layer. Downstream functors declare `(PR : Provability.SIG A GN HEV)`.]]@*)

Module Provability := D001_11__Provability.


(* ---- theories/D001/D001_13__Public_Prelude.v ---- *)

(*@file@*)
(*@H.1@[[Proofcase / D001 / Public Prelude]]@*)
(*@H.2@[[Overview]]@*)
(*@p.l@[[This prelude collects the stable public contracts needed by the final API file.]]@*)
(*@p.l@[[It keeps the public surface short and avoids repeating the input-builder documentation inside the terminal API route.]]@*)

From D001 Require Export D001_12__Local_Chain.

(*@box.section@[[STAGED INPUT INTERFACES]]@*)

(*@c.inline@[[The existing functor boundary continues to use `INPUT`. For API documentation and future staged builders, we also expose a lighter `MINIMAL_INPUT` contract and a corresponding builder signature. This does not alter or break the current `Make` and `MakeWith` functors.]]@*)

Module Type MINIMAL_INPUT.
  Parameter RAFormula : Type.

  Parameter Imp : RAFormula -> RAFormula -> RAFormula.
  Parameter Bot : RAFormula.

  Parameter as_imp : RAFormula -> option (RAFormula * RAFormula).
  Parameter as_imp_Some :
    forall phi a b,
      as_imp phi = Some (a, b) ->
      phi = Imp a b.
  Parameter as_imp_Imp :
    forall a b,
      as_imp (Imp a b) = Some (a, b).

  Parameter RAFormula_eq_dec :
    forall (u v : RAFormula), {u = v} + {u <> v}.

  Parameter base_pair : nat -> nat -> nat.
  Parameter base_unpair : nat -> nat * nat.
  Parameter base_unpair_pair_closed :
    forall x y,
      base_unpair (base_pair x y) = (x, y).

  Parameter code_formula : RAFormula -> nat.
  Parameter formula_of_code : nat -> option RAFormula.
  Parameter formula_of_code_correct :
    forall u phi,
      formula_of_code u = Some phi <-> code_formula phi = u.

  Parameter code_nat_list : list nat -> nat.
  Parameter code_nat_list_nil : code_nat_list [] = 0.
  Parameter code_nat_list_cons :
    forall x xs,
      code_nat_list (x :: xs) = S (base_pair x (code_nat_list xs)).

  Parameter code_tag2 : nat -> nat -> nat -> nat.
  Parameter code_tag2_injective :
    forall tag a b tag' a' b',
      code_tag2 tag a b = code_tag2 tag' a' b' ->
      tag = tag' /\ a = a' /\ b = b'.

  Parameter proof_of_code : nat -> option (list RAFormula).
  Parameter proof_of_code_sound :
    forall p pf,
      proof_of_code p = Some pf ->
      code_nat_list (map code_formula pf) = p.
  Parameter proof_of_code_complete :
    forall pf,
      proof_of_code (code_nat_list (map code_formula pf)) = Some pf.

  Parameter RATheoryAxiom : RAFormula -> Prop.
  Parameter RATheoryAxiomb : RAFormula -> bool.
  Parameter RATheoryAxiomb_correct :
    forall phi,
      RATheoryAxiomb phi = true <-> RATheoryAxiom phi.
End MINIMAL_INPUT.

(*@c.inline@[[`BUILDER_SIG` is a documentation-level signature for a future staged constructor from `MINIMAL_INPUT` to the full local proof-theory `INPUT`. No concrete builder is required here, and the current functor usage remains unchanged.]]@*)

Module Type BUILDER_SIG.
  Declare Module Build (M : MINIMAL_INPUT) : Input.INPUT.
End BUILDER_SIG.

(*@c.inline@[[Public functor]]@*)
