(* ---- theories/D001/D001_15__Examples.v ---- *)

(*@file@*)
(*@H.1@[[Proofcase / D001 / Examples]]@*)
(*@H.2@[[Overview]]@*)
(*@p.l@[[This file exercises the public API of D001 through five tier-stratified example modules.]]@*)
(*@p.l@[[Each tier path imports the full API via `Make` and demonstrates one layer of the proof-theory surface: object-level theoremhood, coded provability, family-sat compilation, kernel-code bridges, and backward-compatible top-level aliases.]]@*)
(*@H.2@[[Contents]]@*)
(*@ptoclist.roman@[[TIER 1 PATH]][[Formula-level provability–theoremhood equivalence and modus ponens.]]@*)
(*@ptoclist.roman@[[TIER 2 PATH]][[Boolean semantic bridge and checker-to-coded-provability.]]@*)
(*@ptoclist.roman@[[TIER 3 PATH]][[Family-sat compilation master and generated semantic family equivalence.]]@*)
(*@ptoclist.roman@[[TIER 4 PATH]][[Kernel-code acceptance to theoremhood and cubic degree iff selector activation.]]@*)
(*@ptoclist.roman@[[TIER 5 PATH]][[Backward-compatible top-level checker and coded modus ponens.]]@*)

From D001 Require Export D001_14__RA_Language.

(*@box.section@[[USE CASES]]@*)

Module GenericExamples (A : D001_08__Proof_Theory_Input.INPUT).

  Module API := Make A.

  (*@c.inline@[[`Tier1_Path` covers the object-level surface: provability–theoremhood equivalence and modus ponens at the formula layer.]]@*)

  Module Tier1_Path.
    Example coded_provability_matches_theoremhood
      (phi : API.Tier1.Form) :
      API.Tier1.Prov_RA (API.Tier1.code_of_formula phi) <-> API.Tier1.Thm phi.
    Proof.
      exact (API.Tier1.Prov_iff_Thm phi).
    Qed.

    Example theoremhood_modus_ponens
      (u v : API.Tier1.Form) :
      API.Tier1.Thm_RA (API.Tier1.Imp u v) ->
      API.Tier1.Thm_RA u ->
      API.Tier1.Thm_RA v.
    Proof.
      exact (API.Tier1.Thm_RA_MP u v).
    Qed.

  End Tier1_Path.

  (*@c.inline@[[`Tier2_Path` covers the encoded operational surface: the boolean proof-of bridge and the checker-to-coded-provability bridge.]]@*)

  Module Tier2_Path.

    Example boolean_semantic_bridge
      (p u : nat) :
      API.Tier2.ProofOfbool p u = true <-> API.Tier2.ProofOf p u.
    Proof.
      exact (API.Tier2.ProofOfbool_correct p u).
    Qed.

    Example checked_trace_yields_coded_provability
      (pf : API.Tier2.Proof)
      (goal : API.Tier2.Form) :
      API.Tier2.check pf goal = true ->
      API.Tier2.Prov_RA (API.Tier2.code_of_formula goal).
    Proof.
      intro Hcheck.
      exact (API.Tier2.checker_accepts_implies_Prov_RA pf goal Hcheck).
    Qed.

  End Tier2_Path.

  (*@c.inline@[[`Tier3_Path` covers the in-tree clause compiler: the family-sat compilation master theorem and the generated semantic family satisfiability equivalence.]]@*)

  Module Tier3_Path.

    Example checker_code_compiles_to_family_sat
      (code : API.Tier3.CheckerCode) :
      API.Tier3.checker_accepts_code code <->
      exists fam,
        API.Tier3.FamilyCompiled code fam /\
        API.Tier3.FamilySat fam.
    Proof.
      exact (API.Tier3.checker_clause_compilation_master code).
    Qed.

    Example checker_code_matches_generated_semantic_family
      (code : API.Tier3.CheckerCode) :
      API.Tier3.checker_accepts_code code <->
      API.Tier3.SemanticFamilySatisfiable
        (API.Tier3.CheckerGeneratedSemanticFamily code).
    Proof.
      exact (API.Tier3.checker_generated_family_semantic_equivalence code).
    Qed.

  End Tier3_Path.

  (*@c.inline@[[`Tier4_Path` covers the low-level kernel-code internals: acceptance at the coded kernel implies theoremhood, and selector-activated constraints are exactly the cubic ones.]]@*)

  Module Tier4_Path.

    Example kernel_acceptance_yields_theoremhood
      (pf : API.Tier1.Proof)
      (goal : API.Tier1.Form) :
      API.Tier4.checker_kernel_codeb
        (API.Tier1.code_of_proof pf)
        (API.Tier1.code_of_formula goal) = true ->
      API.Tier1.Thm goal.
    Proof.

      intro Hkernel.
      assert (Hcheck : API.HEV.check pf goal = true).
      - pose proof (API.Tier4.checker_kernel_codeb_correct pf goal) as Hcorr.
        exact (eq_trans (eq_sym Hcorr) Hkernel).
      - pose proof (API.Tier1.checker_accepts_implies_Prov_RA pf goal Hcheck) as Hprov.
        exact (proj1 (API.Tier1.Prov_iff_Thm goal) Hprov).
    Qed.

    Example cubic_degree_iff_selector_activation
      (c : API.Tier3.InternalConstraint) :
      API.Tier3.SelectorActivatedConstraint c <->
      API.Tier3.InternalConstraint_degree c = 3.
    Proof.
      exact (API.Tier4.selector_activation_exactly_cubic c).
    Qed.

  End Tier4_Path.


  (*@c.inline@[[`Tier5_Path` covers the backward-compatible top-level conveniences: the flat `check`/`Thm` surface and coded modus ponens without tier qualification.]]@*)

  Module Conveniences.

    Example checked_trace_yields_theoremhood_compat
      (pf : API.Proof)
      (goal : API.Form) :
      API.check pf goal = true ->
      API.Thm goal.
    Proof.
      intro Hcheck.
      pose proof (API.Tier1.checker_accepts_implies_Prov_RA pf goal Hcheck) as Hprov.
      exact (proj1 (API.Prov_iff_Thm goal) Hprov).
    Qed.

    Example top_level_coded_modus_ponens
      (u v : API.Form) :
      API.Prov_RA (API.code_of_formula (API.Imp u v)) ->
      API.Prov_RA (API.code_of_formula u) ->
      API.Prov_RA (API.code_of_formula v).
    Proof.
      intros Himp Hu.
      apply (proj2 (API.Prov_iff_Thm v)).
      apply (API.Thm_RA_MP u v).
      - apply (proj1 (API.Prov_iff_Thm (API.Imp u v))).
        exact Himp.
      - apply (proj1 (API.Prov_iff_Thm u)).
        exact Hu.
    Qed.

  End Conveniences.

End GenericExamples.

(*@c.section@[[SANITY TEST]]@*)

(*@c.inline@[[...]]@*)


Module SanityTest.

  Module Generic (A : D001_08__Proof_Theory_Input.INPUT).

    Module API := Make A.

    Example checked_trace_yields_theoremhood
      (pf : API.Proof)
      (goal : API.Form) :
      API.check pf goal = true ->
      API.Thm goal.
    Proof.
      intro Hcheck.
      pose proof (API.Tier1.checker_accepts_implies_Prov_RA pf goal Hcheck) as Hprov.
      exact (proj1 (API.Prov_iff_Thm goal) Hprov).
    Qed.

    Example checker_code_compiles_to_family_sat
      (code : API.CheckerCode) :
      API.checker_accepts_code code <->
      exists fam,
        API.FamilyCompiled code fam /\
        API.FamilySat fam.
    Proof.
      exact (API.checker_clause_compilation_master code).
    Qed.

    Example kernel_acceptance_yields_theoremhood
      (pf : API.Proof)
      (goal : API.Form) :
      API.Tier4.checker_kernel_codeb
        (API.code_of_proof pf)
        (API.code_of_formula goal) = true ->
      API.Thm goal.
    Proof.
      intro Hkernel.
      pose proof (API.Tier4.checker_kernel_codeb_correct pf goal) as Hcorr.
      assert (Hcheck : API.check pf goal = true).
      { exact (eq_trans (eq_sym Hcorr) Hkernel). }
      exact (checked_trace_yields_theoremhood pf goal Hcheck).
    Qed.

  End Generic.

End SanityTest.


(* ---- theories/P002/P002_00_Premises.v ---- *)

(*@file@*)
(*@copyright@*)
(*@info.premise@*)

(*@H.1@[[Proofcase / P002_00_Premises : Inherited Arithmeticl Assumptions ]]@*)
(*@H.2@[[Overview]]@*)
(*@p.l@[[This file is the canonical semantic surface of the P002 problem statement. Its role is to fix, in an immutable form, (i) the syntax of cubic Diophantine equations over ℕ, (ii) their denotational semantics and the associated yes-predicate, (iii) the extensional and coded notions of decider against which all downstream claims are stated, and (iv) the three target statements that the rest of the development must establish or refute.]]@*)
(*@p.l@[[No later file in the development is permitted to redefine any of the notions introduced here; downstream layers may only build on top of them. The only non-logical assumption introduced by this file is the coding-surjectivity axiom in the Gödel-coding bridge, and it is disclosed explicitly at its point of use. Its concrete discharge is the responsibility of the D001 integration layer (P002_04_01_D001_API).]]@*)
(*@p.l@[[The file is organized in eight sections: proof environment, cubic polynomial syntax, well-formedness, semantics, the extensional solver notion, the coded solver notion, the coded-to-extensional communication lemmas, and the public target statements.]]@*)
(*@H.2@[[Contents]]@*)
(*@ptoclist.roman@[[PROOF ENVIRONMENT]][[Standard library imports; no non-logical axioms.]]@*)
(*@ptoclist.roman@[[CUBIC POLYNOMIALS]][[Monomial and term syntax, equation record, and valuation type.]]@*)
(*@ptoclist.roman@[[WELL-FORMEDNESS]][[Arity-bounded well-formedness predicate for monomials and equations.]]@*)
(*@ptoclist.roman@[[SEMANTICS]][[Denotational evaluation, solution relation, solvability, and the canonical yes-predicate.]]@*)
(*@ptoclist.roman@[[SOLVER OR METHOD DEFINITION]][[Extensional decider notion and its specialization to the P002 yes-language.]]@*)
(*@ptoclist.roman@[[CODED SOLVER NOTION]][[Abstract Gödel decoder, coverage axiom, coded yes-language, and transfer lemmas.]]@*)
(*@ptoclist.roman@[[COMMUNICATING]][[Coded-to-extensional projection and the correctness bridge lemmas.]]@*)
(*@ptoclist.roman@[[TARGET STATEMENTS]][[Three target predicates: H10 undecidability, quadratic decidability, and the principal P002 claim.]]@*)


(*@box.section@[[PROOF ENVIRONMENT]]@*)

(*@c.inline@[[We rely on Rocq's proof-checking kernel and on the imported standard library modules. Beyond the logic of Rocq itself, no axioms are introduced in this section; the single premise axiom of the file is the coding-surjectivity axiom stated in the Gödel-coding bridge below.]]@*)

From Stdlib Require Export Arith List.
Export ListNotations.

(*@box.section@[[CUBIC POLYNOMIALS]]@*)

(*@c.inline@[[Syntactic monomials of total degree at most three. The four constructors denote, respectively, the constant monomial 1, a variable xᵢ, a quadratic product xᵢ·xⱼ, and a cubic product xᵢ·xⱼ·xₖ. Variable indices are drawn from ℕ without restriction at this level; their admissibility relative to a specific equation's arity is enforced separately by the well-formedness predicate in the next section.]]@*)

(*@c.unicode@[[monomial ::= 1 ∣ xᵢ ∣ xᵢ·xⱼ ∣ xᵢ·xⱼ·xₖ (i, j, k ∈ ℕ)]]@*)

Inductive monomial : Type :=
| m_const
| m_linear (i : nat)
| m_quadratic (i j : nat)
| m_cubic (i j k : nat).

(*@c.inline@[[A term pairs a natural-number coefficient with a monomial. Restricting coefficients to ℕ is a deliberate normal-form choice: any cubic Diophantine equation with integer coefficients can be rewritten, without loss of generality, as an equality of two sums of ℕ-coefficient monomials by moving negative summands to the opposite side.]]@*)

(*@c.unicode@[[term ≔ (c, μ) with c ∈ ℕ, μ a monomial;  ⟦term⟧ ≡ c · μ]]@*)

Record term : Type := {
  coeff : nat;
  mono : monomial
}.

(*@c.inline@[[An equation is a triple consisting of a declared arity and two lists of terms representing the left- and right-hand sides. Writing equations in the form LHS = RHS, rather than P = 0, is what permits both sides to range over ℕ and is the necessary counterpart of the ℕ-coefficient normal form adopted above.]]@*)

(*@c.unicode@[[h10_nd3n_equation ≔ ⟨n, Σᵢ lᵢ, Σⱼ rⱼ⟩ with n ∈ ℕ and each lᵢ, rⱼ a degree-≤3 term over x₀, …, x_{n−1}]]@*)

Record h10_nd3n_equation : Type := {
  var_count : nat;
  lhs_terms : list term;
  rhs_terms : list term
}.

(*@c.inline@[[A valuation assigns a natural number to every variable index. Representing valuations as total functions ℕ → ℕ, rather than as finite vectors, removes the need to carry an arity proof alongside each valuation; values at indices outside the declared arity of a given equation are inaccessible to that equation by well-formedness and are therefore immaterial.]]@*)

(*@c.unicode@[[valuation ≔ ρ : ℕ → ℕ]]@*)

Definition valuation : Type := nat -> nat.

(*@box.section@[[WELL-FORMEDNESS]]@*)

(*@c.inline@[[A monomial is well-formed at arity n when every variable index occurring in it is strictly less than n; the constant monomial is vacuously well-formed. The predicate serves solely to rule out ill-scoped syntax and imposes no arithmetic constraint on valuations.]]@*)

(*@c.unicode@[[monomial_wf(n, μ) ⇔ every index occurring in μ is strictly less than n]]@*)

Definition monomial_wf (n : nat) (m : monomial) : Prop :=
  match m with
  | m_const => True
  | m_linear i => i < n
  | m_quadratic i j => i < n /\ j < n
  | m_cubic i j k => i < n /\ j < n /\ k < n
  end.

(*@c.inline@[[An equation is well-formed when every term occurring on either side uses only variable indices below its declared arity. Well-formedness is incorporated into the definition of `solves` below, so that no downstream result of this file applies to ill-formed instances.]]@*)

(*@c.unicode@[[equation_wf(⟨n, L, R⟩) ⇔ ∀ t ∈ L ⊕ R. monomial_wf(n, mono(t))]]@*)

Definition equation_wf (eqn : h10_nd3n_equation) : Prop :=
  Forall (fun t => monomial_wf (var_count eqn) (mono t)) (lhs_terms eqn) /\
  Forall (fun t => monomial_wf (var_count eqn) (mono t)) (rhs_terms eqn).

(*@box.section@[[SEMANTICS]]@*)

(*@c.inline@[[Denotational semantics of a monomial under a valuation. The constant monomial evaluates to 1, and linear, quadratic, and cubic monomials evaluate to the corresponding products of variable values. All operations are performed in ℕ, so evaluation never escapes the natural numbers.]]@*)

(*@c.unicode@[[⟦1⟧ρ = 1,  ⟦xᵢ⟧ρ = ρ(i),  ⟦xᵢxⱼ⟧ρ = ρ(i)·ρ(j),  ⟦xᵢxⱼxₖ⟧ρ = ρ(i)·ρ(j)·ρ(k)]]@*)

Definition eval_monomial (rho : valuation) (m : monomial) : nat :=
  match m with
  | m_const => 1
  | m_linear i => rho i
  | m_quadratic i j => rho i * rho j
  | m_cubic i j k => rho i * rho j * rho k
  end.

(*@c.inline@[[The value of a term under a valuation is the product of its coefficient and the value of its monomial.]]@*)

(*@c.unicode@[[⟦(c, μ)⟧ρ = c · ⟦μ⟧ρ]]@*)

Definition eval_term (rho : valuation) (t : term) : nat :=
  coeff t * eval_monomial rho (mono t).

(*@c.inline@[[A list of terms denotes the sum of the individual term values under the given valuation. The fold is initialized at 0, so that an empty list denotes the empty sum, in agreement with the standard mathematical convention.]]@*)

(*@c.unicode@[[⟦[t₁, …, tₘ]⟧ρ = Σᵢ₌₁ᵐ ⟦tᵢ⟧ρ, with ⟦[]⟧ρ = 0]]@*)

Definition eval_poly (ts : list term) (rho : valuation) : nat :=
  fold_right (fun t acc => eval_term rho t + acc)%nat 0 ts.

(*@c.inline@[[A valuation is said to solve an equation when the equation is well-formed and its two sides evaluate to the same natural number under that valuation. Well-formedness is incorporated directly into the solution relation, ensuring that solvability can never be witnessed by the evaluation of a malformed syntactic object.]]@*)

(*@c.unicode@[[solves(⟨n, L, R⟩, ρ) ⇔ equation_wf(⟨n, L, R⟩) ∧ ⟦L⟧ρ = ⟦R⟧ρ (in ℕ)]]@*)

Definition solves (eqn : h10_nd3n_equation) (rho : valuation) : Prop :=
  equation_wf eqn /\
  eval_poly (lhs_terms eqn) rho = eval_poly (rhs_terms eqn) rho.

(*@c.inline@[[An equation is solvable over ℕ when it admits at least one solving valuation. This existential closure is the semantic yes-language of the H10 fragment studied in P002.]]@*)

(*@c.unicode@[[solvable_over_nat(E) ⇔ ∃ ρ : ℕ → ℕ. solves(E, ρ)]]@*)

Definition solvable_over_nat (eqn : h10_nd3n_equation) : Prop :=
  exists rho : valuation, solves eqn rho.

(*@c.inline@[[A problem instance is, by definition, an equation in the form fixed above. The alias is introduced so that downstream files can consistently refer to `instances` at the problem-theoretic layer while `equations` remains a purely syntactic notion.]]@*)

Definition h10_nd3n_instance : Type := h10_nd3n_equation.

(*@c.inline@[[The canonical yes-predicate of the P002 problem: an instance is a yes-instance precisely when it is solvable over ℕ. Every decidability claim in the development is stated relative to exactly this predicate.]]@*)

(*@c.unicode@[[h10_nd3n_yes(E) ⇔ ∃ ρ : ℕ → ℕ. solves(E, ρ)]]@*)

Definition h10_nd3n_yes (eqn : h10_nd3n_instance) : Prop :=
  solvable_over_nat eqn.

(*@box.section@[[SOLVER OR METHOD DEFINITION]]@*)

(*@c.inline@[[The classical, extensional notion of a decider: a boolean function `solver` decides a yes-language `yes` when its set of true-answers coincides exactly with the yes-language. No computability or effectivity is asserted at this level; effectivity enters only through the coded solver model introduced below.]]@*)

(*@c.unicode@[[decides(yes, f) ⇔ ∀ x. f(x) = true ↔ yes(x)]]@*)

Definition decides {X : Type} (yes : X -> Prop) (solver : X -> bool) : Prop :=
  forall x, solver x = true <-> yes x.

(*@c.inline@[[Specialization of the extensional decider notion to the P002 yes-predicate on semantic instances.]]@*)

(*@c.unicode@[[decides_h10_nd3n(f) ⇔ decides(h10_nd3n_yes, f)]]@*)

Definition decides_h10_nd3n (solver : h10_nd3n_instance -> bool) : Prop :=
  decides h10_nd3n_yes solver.

(*@c.inline@[[Abstract Gödel decoder: a partial function from natural-number codes to semantic instances. Kept abstract so that this file remains independent of any particular encoding scheme. The concrete realization using D001's carryless pairing is the responsibility of P002_04_01_D001_API, which is the earliest file in the P002 chain that may import D001.]]@*)

(*@c.unicode@[[decode : ℕ ⇀ h10_nd3n_instance]]@*)

Parameter decode_h10_nd3n_instance : nat -> option h10_nd3n_instance.

(*@c.inline@[[Coverage axiom: every semantic instance is named by at least one natural-number code. This is the sole non-logical assumption introduced by this file. It constitutes the bridge between the semantic presentation of instances and their arithmetized presentation as natural numbers. Discharged by the concrete carryless-pairing encoder in P002_04_01_D001_API.]]@*)

(*@c.unicode@[[∀ E : h10_nd3n_instance. ∃ u ∈ ℕ. decode(u) = Some E (surjectivity of decode onto instances)]]@*)

Axiom decode_h10_nd3n_instance_surjective :
  forall eqn : h10_nd3n_instance,
  exists u, decode_h10_nd3n_instance u = Some eqn.

(*@c.inline@[[The coded yes-language: a natural number u qualifies as a yes-code precisely when it decodes to some semantic instance that is itself a yes-instance. This predicate is the pullback of `h10_nd3n_yes` along the abstract decoder.]]@*)

(*@c.unicode@[[h10_nd3n_yes_code(u) ⇔ ∃ E. decode(u) = Some E ∧ h10_nd3n_yes(E)]]@*)

Definition h10_nd3n_yes_code (u : nat) : Prop :=
  exists eqn : h10_nd3n_instance,
  decode_h10_nd3n_instance u = Some eqn /\
  h10_nd3n_yes eqn.

(*@c.inline@[[Transfer from the semantic yes-language to the coded yes-language: every yes-instance is witnessed by at least one yes-code. The proof is an immediate consequence of the coverage axiom.]]@*)

(*@c.unicode@[[h10_nd3n_yes(E) ⇒ ∃ u ∈ ℕ. h10_nd3n_yes_code(u)]]@*)

Lemma h10_nd3n_yes_implies_code_yes :
  forall eqn : h10_nd3n_instance,
  h10_nd3n_yes eqn ->
  exists u, h10_nd3n_yes_code u.
Proof.
  intros eqn Hyes.
  destruct (decode_h10_nd3n_instance_surjective eqn) as [u Hdecode].
  exists u.
  exists eqn.
  split.
  - exact Hdecode.
  - exact Hyes.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[Transfer from the coded yes-language back to the semantic yes-language: every yes-code exhibits a concrete yes-instance together with its decoding. Note that the conclusion is definitionally equal to the hypothesis — `h10_nd3n_yes_code u` unfolds to exactly the existential in the conclusion — so the proof closes by `exact`. The lemma carries no logical content beyond its definition; it exists solely to provide a stable named interface that downstream files can import without depending on the raw unfolding of `h10_nd3n_yes_code`.]]@*)

(*@c.unicode@[[h10_nd3n_yes_code(u) ⇒ ∃ E. decode(u) = Some E ∧ h10_nd3n_yes(E)   (definitional identity)]]@*)

Lemma h10_nd3n_code_yes_implies_instance_yes :
  forall u : nat,
  h10_nd3n_yes_code u ->
  exists eqn : h10_nd3n_instance,
  decode_h10_nd3n_instance u = Some eqn /\
  h10_nd3n_yes eqn.
Proof.
  intros u Hyes_code.
  exact Hyes_code.
Qed.

(*@box.section@[[CODED SOLVER NOTION]]@*)

(*@c.inline@[[A computational solver is modelled abstractly as a natural-number code together with an evaluator `eval_code` and a totality predicate `total_code`. Keeping the evaluator and the totality predicate abstract, rather than pinning them to a specific universal machine, ensures that the results of this file apply uniformly to any reasonable model of computation — Turing machines, μ-recursive functions, or Rocq's own extraction target — provided it exposes these two signatures.]]@*)

(*@c.unicode@[[decider_code ≔ ℕ, eval_code : ℕ → ℕ → Option Bool, total_code : ℕ → Prop]]@*)

Definition decider_code : Type := nat.
Definition eval_code_sig : Type := decider_code -> nat -> option bool.
Definition total_code_sig : Type := decider_code -> Prop.

(*@c.inline@[[A coded decider e is correct for a yes-language P when two conditions hold: (i) e is accepted by the totality predicate, and (ii) its evaluator returns `Some true` on exactly the yes-instances of P. The behaviour on `Some false` is not postulated separately; in conjunction with the totality condition below — under which every accepted code terminates with some boolean answer on every input — this suffices to recover an ordinary boolean decider on codes, as established by `correct_code_over_implies_decides`.]]@*)

(*@c.unicode@[[correct_code_over(P, eval, tot, e) ⇔ tot(e) ∧ ∀ s ∈ ℕ. eval(e, s) = Some true ↔ P(s)]]@*)

Definition correct_code_over
  (P : nat -> Prop)
  (eval_code : eval_code_sig)
  (total_code : total_code_sig)
  (e : decider_code) : Prop :=
  total_code e /\
  forall s, eval_code e s = Some true <-> P s.

(*@c.inline@[[Totality property of the coded solver model: every code accepted by the totality predicate terminates with some boolean output on every input. This property is precisely what distinguishes a decider code from a merely semi-deciding (partial) code, and it is the crucial ingredient that permits the conversion of a `correct_code_over` witness into an extensional `decides` witness.]]@*)

(*@c.unicode@[[coded_solver_total(eval, tot) ⇔ ∀ e, s. tot(e) ⇒ ∃ b ∈ {true, false}. eval(e, s) = Some b]]@*)

Definition coded_solver_total
  (eval_code : eval_code_sig)
  (total_code : total_code_sig) : Prop :=
  forall e input,
  total_code e ->
  exists b, eval_code e input = Some b.

(*@c.inline@[[Specialization of `correct_code_over` to the P002 coded yes-language. This is the predicate whose uninhabitation is established by the coded no-solver endpoint in the downstream development.]]@*)

(*@c.unicode@[[h10_nd3n_correct_code_over(eval, tot, e) ⇔ correct_code_over(h10_nd3n_yes_code, eval, tot, e)]]@*)

Definition h10_nd3n_correct_code_over
  (eval_code : eval_code_sig)
  (total_code : total_code_sig)
  (e : decider_code) : Prop :=
  correct_code_over h10_nd3n_yes_code eval_code total_code e.

(*@box.section@[[COMMUNICATING]]@*)

(*@c.inline@[[Projection of a code, together with an evaluator, to an ordinary boolean function ℕ → Bool. The projected function returns `true` exactly when the evaluator yields `Some true`, and `false` in every other case — whether `Some false`, `None`, or any intermediate diverging outcome. This projection constitutes the canonical bridge from the coded solver model to the extensional `decides` notion.]]@*)

(*@c.unicode@[[code_to_solver(eval, e) ≔ λ s. if eval(e, s) = Some true then true else false]]@*)

Definition code_to_solver
  (eval_code : eval_code_sig)
  (e : decider_code) : nat -> bool :=
  fun s =>
  match eval_code e s with
  | Some true => true
  | _ => false
  end.

(*@c.inline@[[Under totality of the coded solver model, any witness of `correct_code_over` for a yes-language P projects, via `code_to_solver`, to a genuine extensional decider for P. This lemma provides the precise sense in which the coded correctness notion introduced here is conservative over the classical extensional decider notion.]]@*)

(*@c.unicode@[[coded_solver_total(eval, tot) ∧ correct_code_over(P, eval, tot, e) ⇒ decides(P, code_to_solver(eval, e))]]@*)

Lemma correct_code_over_implies_decides :
  forall (P : nat -> Prop) eval_code total_code e,
  coded_solver_total eval_code total_code ->
  correct_code_over P eval_code total_code e ->
  decides P (code_to_solver eval_code e).
Proof.
  intros P eval_code total_code e Htot Hcorr.
  destruct Hcorr as [He_total He_corr].
  unfold decides, code_to_solver.
  intro s.
  split.
  - destruct (eval_code e s) eqn:Hev.
  + destruct b.
  * intros _.
  destruct (He_corr s) as [Hforward _].
  exact (Hforward Hev).
  * discriminate.
  + discriminate.
  - intro HP.
  destruct (Htot e s He_total) as [b Hb].
  rewrite Hb.
  destruct b.
  + reflexivity.
  + exfalso.
  destruct (He_corr s) as [_ Hbackward].
  specialize (Hbackward HP).
  rewrite Hb in Hbackward.
  discriminate.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[Specialization of the preceding lemma to the P002 coded yes-language. Downstream refutations show that no code can satisfy the antecedent, and the present lemma is what lifts those refutations from the coded model to an extensional undecidability statement.]]@*)

(*@c.unicode@[[coded_solver_total(eval, tot) ∧ h10_nd3n_correct_code_over(eval, tot, e) ⇒ decides(h10_nd3n_yes_code, code_to_solver(eval, e))]]@*)

Lemma h10_nd3n_correct_code_implies_decides :
  forall eval_code total_code e,
  coded_solver_total eval_code total_code ->
  h10_nd3n_correct_code_over eval_code total_code e ->
  decides h10_nd3n_yes_code (code_to_solver eval_code e).
Proof.
  intros eval_code total_code e Htot Hcorr.
  eapply correct_code_over_implies_decides.
  - exact Htot.
  - exact Hcorr.
Qed.

(*@box.section@[[TARGET STATEMENTS]]@*)

(*@c.inline@[[The three target predicates stated below fix the form of the claims that the remainder of the development must establish or refute. The neighbouring H10 variants — unrestricted and quadratic — are parameterized by abstract instance types, so that this file remains independent of any particular formalization of those problems; only the cubic single-equation normal form `h10_nd3n_instance` is defined concretely, since it is the central object of P002.]]@*)

Section targets.

  (*@c.inline@[[Abstract instance type for unrestricted Hilbert's Tenth Problem over ℕ, together with its associated yes-predicate. Leaving these abstract allows the target statement below to be matched against any specific formalization of H10 without committing to one.]]@*)
  
  (*@c.unicode@[[h10_n_instance : Type, h10_n_yes : h10_n_instance → Prop]]@*)

  Parameter h10_n_instance : Type.
  Parameter h10_n_yes : h10_n_instance -> Prop.

  (*@c.inline@[[Target: unrestricted H10 over ℕ admits no extensional decider. This is the statement of the Davis–Putnam–Robinson–Matiyasevich theorem, re-expressed in the `decides` vocabulary of this file so that reductions into and out of P002 can be phrased in a uniform idiom.]]@*)
  
  (*@c.unicode@[[h10_n_undecidable ⇔ ∀ f : h10_n_instance → Bool. ¬ decides(h10_n_yes, f)]]@*)

  Definition h10_n_undecidable : Prop :=
    forall solver : h10_n_instance -> bool,
    ~ decides h10_n_yes solver.

  (*@c.inline@[[Abstract instance type for the degree-two (quadratic) restriction of H10 over ℕ, together with its yes-predicate. As above, these are kept abstract so that the target statement is not tied to a specific syntax for quadratic Diophantine systems.]]@*)
  
  Parameter h10_quadratic_n_instance : Type.

  Parameter h10_quadratic_n_yes : h10_quadratic_n_instance -> Prop.

  (*@c.inline@[[Target: degree-two H10 over ℕ admits an extensional decider. In combination with `h10_n_undecidable` and `h10_nd3n_semantic_undecidable`, this statement locates the degree frontier of decidability for Diophantine equations over ℕ: the problem is decidable at degree two and undecidable at degree three.]]@*)
  
  (*@c.unicode@[[h10_quadratic_n_decidable ⇔ ∃ f : h10_quadratic_n_instance → Bool. decides(h10_quadratic_n_yes, f)]]@*)

  Definition h10_quadratic_n_decidable : Prop :=
    exists solver : h10_quadratic_n_instance -> bool,
    decides h10_quadratic_n_yes solver.

End targets.

(*@c.inline@[[Target: single-equation degree-three H10 over ℕ, in the normal form fixed by this file, admits no extensional decider. This is the principal claim of P002. Defined outside `Section targets` so it does not acquire spurious dependencies on the abstract h10_n / h10_quadratic Parameters.]]@*)
(*@c.unicode@[[h10_nd3n_semantic_undecidable ⇔ ∀ f : h10_nd3n_instance → Bool. ¬ decides_h10_nd3n(f)]]@*)

Definition h10_nd3n_semantic_undecidable : Prop :=
  forall solver : h10_nd3n_instance -> bool,
  ~ decides_h10_nd3n solver.

(* ---- theories/P002/P002_01__CIC.v ---- *)

(*@file@*)
(*@copyright@*)

(*@H.1@[[Proofcase / P002_01__CIC : Calculus Formant]]@*)
(*@H.2@[[Overview]]@*)
(*@p.l@[[This file is the first permanent conditional layer of the P002 development. It sees only P002_00_Premises and names the two-part split of the principal target claim `h10_nd3n_semantic_undecidable`: the semantic no-solver conclusion holds if and only if (i) no total correct coded decider exists for the cubic coded yes-language, and (ii) a fixed-point reflection bridge carries that coded obstruction into the extensional semantic statement. Both component claims are stated here as named propositions; neither is proved. The proof burden is passed to the downstream temporary file.]]@*)
(*@p.l@[[The split is the first rewrite step of the strongly sequential proof strategy. The temporary file entering Phase 2 asserts the full conclusion axiomatically. This file extracts from it the two-part conditional structure and exports the stable names that the remainder of the development will discharge layer by layer.]]@*)
(*@H.2@[[Contents]]@*)
(*@ptoclist.roman@[[CODED NO-SOLVER CLAIM]][[Named proposition: no total correct coded decider for h10_nd3n_yes_code exists.]]@*)
(*@ptoclist.roman@[[SEMANTIC BRIDGE CLAIM]][[Named proposition: coded no-solver together with the fixed-point bridge implies semantic no-solver.]]@*)
(*@ptoclist.roman@[[MAIN CONDITIONAL]][[The top-level conditional: semantic undecidability follows from the two component claims.]]@*)

From P002 Require Export P002_00_Premises.

(*@box.section@[[CODED NO-SOLVER CLAIM]]@*)

(*@c.inline@[[The coded no-solver claim: no pair (eval, tot) of evaluator and totality predicate simultaneously satisfies coded totality and admits a correct coded decider for the cubic yes-language. This is the first of the two component claims whose conjunction, via the bridge below, yields the semantic conclusion.]]@*)
(*@c.unicode@[[h10_nd3n_coded_no_solver ⇔ ∀ eval tot. coded_solver_total(eval,tot) ⇒ ¬ ∃ e. h10_nd3n_correct_code_over(eval,tot,e)]]@*)

Definition h10_nd3n_coded_no_solver : Prop :=
  forall (eval_code : eval_code_sig) (total_code : total_code_sig),
    coded_solver_total eval_code total_code ->
    ~ exists e : decider_code,
        h10_nd3n_correct_code_over eval_code total_code e.

(*@box.section@[[SEMANTIC BRIDGE CLAIM]]@*)

(*@c.inline@[[The fixed-point reflection bridge: a parameter asserting that coded no-solver implies semantic no-solver. The bridge is the classical metatheoretic step — its proof involves the Feferman reflection argument and is discharged in a later file. Here it is named as a plain proposition so that the conditional structure is visible without requiring any proof machinery.]]@*)
(*@c.unicode@[[h10_nd3n_reflection_bridge ⇔ h10_nd3n_coded_no_solver ⇒ h10_nd3n_semantic_undecidable]]@*)

Definition h10_nd3n_reflection_bridge : Prop :=
  h10_nd3n_coded_no_solver -> h10_nd3n_semantic_undecidable.

(*@box.section@[[MAIN CONDITIONAL]]@*)

(*@c.inline@[[The top-level conditional of P002. Semantic undecidability of cubic H10 over ℕ follows from the two component claims: the coded no-solver obstruction and the reflection bridge that transports it into the semantic statement. This theorem is provable here unconditionally from the two definitions above — it is pure propositional logic.]]@*)
(*@c.unicode@[[h10_nd3n_coded_no_solver ∧ h10_nd3n_reflection_bridge ⇒ h10_nd3n_semantic_undecidable]]@*)

Theorem h10_nd3n_undecidable_from_coded_and_bridge :
  h10_nd3n_coded_no_solver ->
  h10_nd3n_reflection_bridge ->
  h10_nd3n_semantic_undecidable.
Proof.
  intros h_coded h_bridge.
  exact (h_bridge h_coded).
Qed.


(* ---- theories/P002/P002_02__Object_Theory.v ---- *)

(*@file@*)
(*@copyright@*)

(*@H.1@[[Proofcase / P002_02__Object_Theory : Local Object Theory]]@*)
(*@H.2@[[Overview]]@*)
(*@p.l@[[This file is the second permanent conditional layer of the P002 development. It imports P002_01__CIC and splits the coded no-solver claim into two finer components: Σ₁-completeness of RA provability via the compiler chain, and the diagonal contradiction premise that rules out a coded decider given that completeness. The reflection bridge from P002_01 is passed through unchanged to the new temporary file.]]@*)
(*@p.l@[[The conditional proved here is: if Σ₁-completeness holds and the diagonal premise holds, then h10_nd3n_coded_no_solver holds. Together with the reflection bridge already in P002_01, this gives the full semantic undecidability conclusion.]]@*)
(*@H.2@[[Contents]]@*)
(*@ptoclist.roman@[[SIGMA-1 COMPLETENESS CLAIM]][[New named proposition: RA provability is equivalent to cubic satisfiability through the compiler chain.]]@*)
(*@ptoclist.roman@[[DIAGONAL PREMISE]][[New named proposition: sigma-completeness implies h10_nd3n_coded_no_solver.]]@*)
(*@ptoclist.roman@[[CODED NO-SOLVER FROM SIGMA]][[Conditional: sigma-completeness and diagonal premise imply h10_nd3n_coded_no_solver.]]@*)
(*@ptoclist.roman@[[FULL CONDITIONAL]][[End-to-end: sigma-completeness, diagonal premise, and reflection bridge imply semantic undecidability.]]@*)

From P002 Require Export P002_01__CIC.

(*@box.section@[[SIGMA-1 COMPLETENESS CLAIM]]@*)

(*@c.inline@[[Abstract carrier types for the compilation chain. These are opaque at this layer; their concrete realization is the responsibility of downstream discharge files.]]@*)

(*@c.unicode@[[ra_sentence : Type  (RA sentence codes)]]@*)

Parameter ra_sentence : Type.

(*@c.unicode@[[compilation_witness : Type  (proof-trace codes)]]@*)

Parameter compilation_witness : Type.

(*@c.unicode@[[compilation_stream : Type  (pre-aggregation constraint streams)]]@*)

Parameter compilation_stream : Type.

(*@c.unicode@[[compilation_aggregate : Type  (post-aggregation polynomial aggregates)]]@*)

Parameter compilation_aggregate : Type.

(*@c.inline@[[Abstract compilation predicates for the six-step chain.]]@*)

Parameter prov_ra : ra_sentence -> Prop.
Parameter check : compilation_witness -> ra_sentence -> Prop.
Parameter emit : compilation_witness -> ra_sentence -> compilation_stream -> Prop.
Parameter agg : compilation_stream -> compilation_aggregate -> Prop.
Parameter enc : compilation_aggregate -> nat -> Prop.
Parameter cubic_eq_sat : nat -> Prop.

(*@c.inline@[[Σ₁-completeness: RA provability of a sentence is equivalent to the existence of a full compilation witness chain culminating in a satisfiable cubic instance. This is the new claim introduced at this layer whose proof burden is passed downstream.]]@*)
(*@c.unicode@[[sigma_1_completeness ⇔ ∀t. prov_ra(t) ↔ ∃e,p,S,U. check(p,t) ∧ emit(p,t,S) ∧ agg(S,U) ∧ enc(U,e) ∧ cubic_eq_sat(e)]]@*)

Definition sigma_1_completeness : Prop :=
  forall t : ra_sentence,
    prov_ra t <->
    exists (e : nat) (p : compilation_witness) (S : compilation_stream) (U : compilation_aggregate),
      check p t /\
      emit p t S /\
      agg S U /\
      enc U e /\
      cubic_eq_sat e.

(*@box.section@[[DIAGONAL PREMISE]]@*)

(*@c.inline@[[The diagonal contradiction premise: sigma-completeness implies the coded no-solver conclusion. The argument is the source-toggle diagonal — if a total correct coded decider for the cubic yes-language existed, sigma-completeness would compose it into a decider for RA provability, contradicting a fixed-point construction. The concrete proof is the responsibility of downstream files.]]@*)
(*@c.unicode@[[diagonal_contradiction_premise ⇔ sigma_1_completeness ⇒ h10_nd3n_coded_no_solver]]@*)

Definition diagonal_contradiction_premise : Prop :=
  sigma_1_completeness -> h10_nd3n_coded_no_solver.

(*@box.section@[[CODED NO-SOLVER FROM SIGMA]]@*)

(*@c.inline@[[Conditional discharged by this file: sigma-completeness and the diagonal premise together imply the coded no-solver conclusion. Pure propositional logic.]]@*)
(*@c.unicode@[[sigma_1_completeness ∧ diagonal_contradiction_premise ⇒ h10_nd3n_coded_no_solver]]@*)

Theorem h10_nd3n_coded_no_solver_from_sigma :
  sigma_1_completeness ->
  diagonal_contradiction_premise ->
  h10_nd3n_coded_no_solver.
Proof.
  intros h_sigma h_diag.
  exact (h_diag h_sigma).
Qed.

(*@box.section@[[FULL CONDITIONAL]]@*)

(*@c.inline@[[End-to-end conditional: sigma-completeness, diagonal premise, and reflection bridge together imply semantic undecidability. Composes the result of this file with the bridge conditional from P002_01.]]@*)
(*@c.unicode@[[sigma_1_completeness ∧ diagonal_contradiction_premise ∧ h10_nd3n_reflection_bridge ⇒ h10_nd3n_semantic_undecidable]]@*)

Theorem h10_nd3n_semantic_undecidable_from_sigma_and_bridge :
  sigma_1_completeness ->
  diagonal_contradiction_premise ->
  h10_nd3n_reflection_bridge ->
  h10_nd3n_semantic_undecidable.
Proof.
  intros h_sigma h_diag h_bridge.
  apply h10_nd3n_undecidable_from_coded_and_bridge.
  - exact (h_diag h_sigma).
  - exact h_bridge.
Qed.

(*@H.2@[[Overview]]@*)
(*@p.l@[[D001 boundary layer for P002. All D001 vocabulary consumed by P002 enters through this single file. No P002 file may import D001 internals directly.]]@*)

From P002 Require Export P002_02__Object_Theory.
From D001 Require Export D001_99_API.
