(* ===== BEGIN MERGED CHILD: P002_03_02__RA_Theory.v ===== *)
(*@file@*)
(*@copyright@*)

(*@H.1@[[Proofcase / P002_03_02__RA_Theory / Identification of the Decider]]@*)
(*@H.2@[[Overview]]@*)
(*@p.l@[[RA integration layer. Constructs the concrete INPUT module satisfying D001's abstract proof-theory interface, instantiates the D001 pipeline, and exposes the resulting RA provability predicate. The two metatheoretic decoders required by INPUT — formula_of_code and proof_of_code — are defined here by fuel-bounded structural recursion in CIC and proved correct by induction on the RA syntax. No axioms are introduced.]]@*)
(*@H.2@[[Contents]]@*)
(*@ptoclist.roman@[[TERM DECODER]][[Fuel-bounded inverse of code_term, with correctness proof.]]@*)
(*@ptoclist.roman@[[FORMULA DECODER]][[Fuel-bounded inverse of code_formula, with correctness proof.]]@*)
(*@ptoclist.roman@[[PROOF DECODER]][[Inverse of code_nat_list o map code_formula, with correctness proof.]]@*)
(*@ptoclist.roman@[[RA INPUT ASSEMBLY]][[Constructing the concrete RAInput module satisfying INPUT.]]@*)
(*@ptoclist.roman@[[RA THEOREMHOOD COMPLETENESS]][[Named proposition: the compiler chain is equivalent to RA provability.]]@*)
(*@ptoclist.roman@[[RA PROVABILITY UNDECIDABILITY]][[Named proposition: no coded decider correctly decides RA provability.]]@*)
(*@ptoclist.roman@[[SIGMA FROM RA]][[Conditionals assembling the layer.]]@*)

(* merged sibling import elided: P002_03_01__D001_API.v *)

(*@box.section@[[TERM DECODER]]@*)

(*@c.inline@[[Fuel-bounded decoder for RATerm codes. The fuel is the code value itself, which strictly bounds recursion since sub-codes of any term code are strictly smaller than the code. Returns None on malformed input.]]@*)

Fixpoint decode_term_fuel (fuel code : nat) : option D001_03__RA.RATerm :=
  match fuel, code with
  | _, 0 => None
  | 0, S _ => None
  | S fuel', S inner =>
      match D001_07__RA_Theory.checked_unpair inner with
      | None => None
      | Some (tag, payload) =>
        match tag with
        | 0 => Some (D001_03__RA.RA_Var payload)
        | 1 => if Nat.eqb payload 0 then Some D001_03__RA.RA_Zero else None
        | 2 =>
          match decode_term_fuel fuel' payload with
          | Some u => Some (D001_03__RA.RA_Succ u)
          | None => None
          end
        | 3 =>
          match D001_07__RA_Theory.checked_unpair payload with
          | None => None
          | Some (a, b) =>
            match decode_term_fuel fuel' a, decode_term_fuel fuel' b with
            | Some u, Some v => Some (D001_03__RA.RA_Add u v)
            | _, _ => None
            end
          end
        | _ => None
        end
      end
  end.

Definition decode_term (code : nat) : option D001_03__RA.RATerm :=
  decode_term_fuel code code.

(*@c.inline@[[Correctness of the term decoder: decode_term (code_term t) = Some t, for all t.]]@*)

(*@c.inline@[[Monotonicity: more fuel never breaks a successful decode.]]@*)

Lemma decode_term_fuel_mono :
  forall fuel1 fuel2 code t,
    fuel1 <= fuel2 ->
    decode_term_fuel fuel1 code = Some t ->
    decode_term_fuel fuel2 code = Some t.
Proof.
  induction fuel1 as [| f1 IH]; intros fuel2 code t Hle Hdec.
  - destruct code; cbn [decode_term_fuel] in Hdec; discriminate.
  - destruct fuel2 as [| f2]. { inversion Hle. }
    destruct code as [| inner]. { cbn [decode_term_fuel] in Hdec; discriminate. }
    cbn [decode_term_fuel] in Hdec |- *.
    destruct (D001_07__RA_Theory.checked_unpair inner) as [[tag payload]|] eqn:Hcu.
    2: discriminate.
    destruct tag as [| tag']; cbn [decode_term_fuel] in Hdec |- *.
    + exact Hdec.
    + destruct tag' as [| tag'']; cbn [decode_term_fuel] in Hdec |- *.
      * (* tag = 1, RA_Zero with payload=0 check *)
        exact Hdec.
      * destruct tag'' as [| tag''']; cbn [decode_term_fuel] in Hdec |- *.
        -- destruct (decode_term_fuel f1 payload) as [u |] eqn:Hu. 2: discriminate.
           rewrite (IH f2 payload u (le_S_n _ _ Hle) Hu). exact Hdec.
        -- destruct tag''' as [| tag'''']; cbn [decode_term_fuel] in Hdec |- *.
           ++ destruct (D001_07__RA_Theory.checked_unpair payload) as [[a b]|] eqn:Hcu2.
              2: discriminate.
              destruct (decode_term_fuel f1 a) as [u |] eqn:Hu. 2: discriminate.
              destruct (decode_term_fuel f1 b) as [v |] eqn:Hv. 2: discriminate.
              rewrite (IH f2 a u (le_S_n _ _ Hle) Hu).
              rewrite (IH f2 b v (le_S_n _ _ Hle) Hv). exact Hdec.
           ++ destruct tag''''; discriminate.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[Correctness: decode_term (code_term t) = Some t.]]@*)

Lemma decode_term_fuel_correct :
  forall t,
    decode_term_fuel (D001_03__RA.code_term t) (D001_03__RA.code_term t) = Some t.
Proof.
  induction t as [x | | u IHu | u IHu v IHv].
  - cbn [decode_term_fuel D001_03__RA.code_term D001_03__RA.code_tag1].
    rewrite D001_07__RA_Theory.checked_unpair_pair. reflexivity.
  - cbn [decode_term_fuel D001_03__RA.code_term D001_03__RA.code_tag1].
    rewrite D001_07__RA_Theory.checked_unpair_pair. reflexivity.
  - cbn [decode_term_fuel D001_03__RA.code_term D001_03__RA.code_tag1].
    rewrite D001_07__RA_Theory.checked_unpair_pair.
    assert (Hu :
      decode_term_fuel
        (D001_03__RA.base_pair 2 (D001_03__RA.code_term u))
        (D001_03__RA.code_term u) = Some u).
    {
      apply (decode_term_fuel_mono (D001_03__RA.code_term u)).
      - apply D001_07__RA_Theory.base_pair_ge_r.
      - exact IHu.
    }
    rewrite Hu. reflexivity.
  - cbn [decode_term_fuel D001_03__RA.code_term D001_03__RA.code_tag2].
    rewrite D001_07__RA_Theory.checked_unpair_pair.
    rewrite D001_07__RA_Theory.checked_unpair_pair.
    set (fuel :=
      D001_03__RA.base_pair 3
        (D001_03__RA.base_pair (D001_03__RA.code_term u) (D001_03__RA.code_term v))).
    assert (Hu : decode_term_fuel fuel (D001_03__RA.code_term u) = Some u).
    {
      subst fuel.
      apply (decode_term_fuel_mono (D001_03__RA.code_term u)).
      - apply D001_07__RA_Theory.base_pair_ge_payload_l.
      - exact IHu.
    }
    assert (Hv : decode_term_fuel fuel (D001_03__RA.code_term v) = Some v).
    {
      subst fuel.
      apply (decode_term_fuel_mono (D001_03__RA.code_term v)).
      - apply D001_07__RA_Theory.base_pair_ge_payload_r.
      - exact IHv.
    }
    rewrite Hu, Hv. reflexivity.
Qed.

Lemma decode_term_correct :
  forall t,
    decode_term (D001_03__RA.code_term t) = Some t.
Proof.
  intro t. unfold decode_term. exact (decode_term_fuel_correct t).
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[Soundness of the term decoder: if decode_term_fuel succeeds, the result codes back to the input.]]@*)

Lemma decode_term_fuel_sound :
  forall fuel code t,
    decode_term_fuel fuel code = Some t ->
    D001_03__RA.code_term t = code.
Proof.
  induction fuel as [| f IH]; intros code t Hdec.
  - destruct code; cbn [decode_term_fuel] in Hdec; discriminate.
  - destruct code as [| inner]. { cbn [decode_term_fuel] in Hdec. discriminate. }
    cbn [decode_term_fuel] in Hdec.
    destruct (D001_07__RA_Theory.checked_unpair inner) as [[tag payload]|] eqn:Hcu.
    2: discriminate.
    pose proof (D001_07__RA_Theory.checked_unpair_Some inner tag payload Hcu) as Hpair.
    destruct tag as [| [| [| [| ?]]]].
    + (* tag = 0 : RA_Var payload *)
      injection Hdec as Ht. subst t.
      cbn [D001_03__RA.code_term].
      unfold D001_03__RA.code_tag1.
      rewrite Hpair.
      reflexivity.
    + (* tag = 1 : RA_Zero, payload must be 0 *)
      destruct (Nat.eqb payload 0) eqn:Hpayload. 2: discriminate.
      apply Nat.eqb_eq in Hpayload. subst payload.
      injection Hdec as Ht. subst t.
      cbn [D001_03__RA.code_term].
      unfold D001_03__RA.code_tag1.
      rewrite Hpair.
      reflexivity.
    + (* tag = 2 : RA_Succ *)
      destruct (decode_term_fuel f payload) as [u|] eqn:Hu. 2: discriminate.
      injection Hdec as Ht. subst t.
      apply IH in Hu.
      cbn [D001_03__RA.code_term].
      unfold D001_03__RA.code_tag1.
      rewrite Hu.
      rewrite Hpair.
      reflexivity.
    + (* tag = 3 : RA_Add *)
      destruct (D001_07__RA_Theory.checked_unpair payload) as [[a b]|] eqn:Hcu2.
      2: discriminate.
      pose proof (D001_07__RA_Theory.checked_unpair_Some payload a b Hcu2) as Hpair2.
      destruct (decode_term_fuel f a) as [u|] eqn:Hu. 2: discriminate.
      destruct (decode_term_fuel f b) as [v|] eqn:Hv. 2: discriminate.
      injection Hdec as Ht. subst t.
      apply IH in Hu. apply IH in Hv.
      cbn [D001_03__RA.code_term].
      unfold D001_03__RA.code_tag2.
      rewrite Hu, Hv.
      rewrite Hpair2.
      rewrite Hpair.
      reflexivity.
    + destruct n; discriminate.
Qed.

Lemma decode_term_sound :
  forall code t,
    decode_term code = Some t ->
    D001_03__RA.code_term t = code.
Proof.
  intros code t H. unfold decode_term in H. exact (decode_term_fuel_sound _ _ _ H).
Qed.

(*@box.section@[[FORMULA DECODER]]@*)

(*@c.inline@[[Fuel-bounded decoder for RAFormula codes. The RA_Eq case must decode two RATerm sub-codes; all other cases decode RAFormula sub-codes recursively.]]@*)

Fixpoint decode_formula_fuel (fuel code : nat) : option D001_03__RA.RAFormula :=
  match fuel, code with
  | _, 0 => None
  | 0, S _ => None
  | S fuel', S inner =>
      match D001_07__RA_Theory.checked_unpair inner with
      | None => None
      | Some (tag, payload) =>
        match tag with
        | 0 =>
          match D001_07__RA_Theory.checked_unpair payload with
          | None => None
          | Some (a, b) =>
            match decode_term a, decode_term b with
            | Some s, Some t => Some (D001_03__RA.RA_Eq s t)
            | _, _ => None
            end
          end
        | 1 => if Nat.eqb payload 0 then Some D001_03__RA.RA_Bot else None
        | 2 =>
          match D001_07__RA_Theory.checked_unpair payload with
          | None => None
          | Some (a, b) =>
            match decode_formula_fuel fuel' a, decode_formula_fuel fuel' b with
            | Some phi, Some psi => Some (D001_03__RA.RA_Imp phi psi)
            | _, _ => None
            end
          end
        | 3 =>
          match D001_07__RA_Theory.checked_unpair payload with
          | None => None
          | Some (a, b) =>
            match decode_formula_fuel fuel' a, decode_formula_fuel fuel' b with
            | Some phi, Some psi => Some (D001_03__RA.RA_And phi psi)
            | _, _ => None
            end
          end
        | 4 =>
          match D001_07__RA_Theory.checked_unpair payload with
          | None => None
          | Some (a, b) =>
            match decode_formula_fuel fuel' a, decode_formula_fuel fuel' b with
            | Some phi, Some psi => Some (D001_03__RA.RA_Or phi psi)
            | _, _ => None
            end
          end
        | 5 =>
          match D001_07__RA_Theory.checked_unpair payload with
          | None => None
          | Some (x, b) =>
            match decode_formula_fuel fuel' b with
            | Some psi => Some (D001_03__RA.RA_Forall x psi)
            | None => None
            end
          end
        | 6 =>
          match D001_07__RA_Theory.checked_unpair payload with
          | None => None
          | Some (x, b) =>
            match decode_formula_fuel fuel' b with
            | Some psi => Some (D001_03__RA.RA_Exists x psi)
            | None => None
            end
          end
        | _ => None
        end
      end
  end.

Definition formula_of_code (code : nat) : option D001_03__RA.RAFormula :=
  decode_formula_fuel code code.

(*@c.inline@[[Monotonicity for formula decoder.]]@*)

Lemma decode_formula_fuel_mono :
  forall fuel1 fuel2 code phi,
    fuel1 <= fuel2 ->
    decode_formula_fuel fuel1 code = Some phi ->
    decode_formula_fuel fuel2 code = Some phi.
Proof.
  induction fuel1 as [| f1 IH]; intros fuel2 code phi Hle Hdec.
  - destruct code; cbn [decode_formula_fuel] in Hdec; discriminate.
  - destruct fuel2 as [| f2]. { inversion Hle. }
    destruct code as [| inner]. { cbn [decode_formula_fuel] in Hdec; discriminate. }
    cbn [decode_formula_fuel] in Hdec |- *.
    destruct (D001_07__RA_Theory.checked_unpair inner) as [[tag payload]|] eqn:Hcu.
    2: discriminate.
    destruct tag as [| [| [| [| [| [| [| ?]]]]]]];
      cbn [decode_formula_fuel] in Hdec |- *.
    + (* tag = 0 : RA_Eq *)
      destruct (D001_07__RA_Theory.checked_unpair payload) as [[a b]|]. 2: discriminate.
      destruct (decode_term a), (decode_term b); exact Hdec.
    + (* tag = 1 : RA_Bot *)
      exact Hdec.
    + (* tag = 2 : RA_Imp *)
      destruct (D001_07__RA_Theory.checked_unpair payload) as [[a b]|]. 2: discriminate.
      destruct (decode_formula_fuel f1 a) as [pa|] eqn:Ha. 2: discriminate.
      destruct (decode_formula_fuel f1 b) as [pb|] eqn:Hb. 2: discriminate.
      rewrite (IH f2 a pa (le_S_n _ _ Hle) Ha).
      rewrite (IH f2 b pb (le_S_n _ _ Hle) Hb). exact Hdec.
    + (* tag = 3 : RA_And *)
      destruct (D001_07__RA_Theory.checked_unpair payload) as [[a b]|]. 2: discriminate.
      destruct (decode_formula_fuel f1 a) as [pa|] eqn:Ha. 2: discriminate.
      destruct (decode_formula_fuel f1 b) as [pb|] eqn:Hb. 2: discriminate.
      rewrite (IH f2 a pa (le_S_n _ _ Hle) Ha).
      rewrite (IH f2 b pb (le_S_n _ _ Hle) Hb). exact Hdec.
    + (* tag = 4 : RA_Or *)
      destruct (D001_07__RA_Theory.checked_unpair payload) as [[a b]|]. 2: discriminate.
      destruct (decode_formula_fuel f1 a) as [pa|] eqn:Ha. 2: discriminate.
      destruct (decode_formula_fuel f1 b) as [pb|] eqn:Hb. 2: discriminate.
      rewrite (IH f2 a pa (le_S_n _ _ Hle) Ha).
      rewrite (IH f2 b pb (le_S_n _ _ Hle) Hb). exact Hdec.
    + (* tag = 5 : RA_Forall *)
      destruct (D001_07__RA_Theory.checked_unpair payload) as [[x b]|]. 2: discriminate.
      destruct (decode_formula_fuel f1 b) as [pb|] eqn:Hb. 2: discriminate.
      rewrite (IH f2 b pb (le_S_n _ _ Hle) Hb). exact Hdec.
    + (* tag = 6 : RA_Exists *)
      destruct (D001_07__RA_Theory.checked_unpair payload) as [[x b]|]. 2: discriminate.
      destruct (decode_formula_fuel f1 b) as [pb|] eqn:Hb. 2: discriminate.
      rewrite (IH f2 b pb (le_S_n _ _ Hle) Hb). exact Hdec.
    + destruct n; discriminate.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[Correctness of the formula decoder.]]@*)

Lemma decode_formula_fuel_correct :
  forall phi,
    decode_formula_fuel (D001_03__RA.code_formula phi) (D001_03__RA.code_formula phi) = Some phi.
Proof.
  induction phi as [s t | | a IHa b IHb | a IHa b IHb | a IHa b IHb | x body IHbody | x body IHbody].
  (* RA_Eq s t : code = code_tag2 0 (code_term s) (code_term t) *)
  - cbn [D001_03__RA.code_formula].
    unfold D001_03__RA.code_tag2.
    cbn [decode_formula_fuel].
    rewrite D001_07__RA_Theory.checked_unpair_pair.
    rewrite D001_07__RA_Theory.checked_unpair_pair.
    rewrite decode_term_correct.
    rewrite decode_term_correct.
    reflexivity.
  (* RA_Bot : code = code_tag1 1 0 *)
  - cbn [D001_03__RA.code_formula].
    unfold D001_03__RA.code_tag1.
    cbn [decode_formula_fuel].
    rewrite D001_07__RA_Theory.checked_unpair_pair.
    reflexivity.
  (* RA_Imp a b : code = code_tag2 2 (code_formula a) (code_formula b) *)
  - cbn [D001_03__RA.code_formula].
    unfold D001_03__RA.code_tag2.
    cbn [decode_formula_fuel].
    rewrite D001_07__RA_Theory.checked_unpair_pair.
    rewrite D001_07__RA_Theory.checked_unpair_pair.
    set (fuel' := D001_03__RA.base_pair 2
                   (D001_03__RA.base_pair (D001_03__RA.code_formula a)
                                          (D001_03__RA.code_formula b))).
    assert (Ha : decode_formula_fuel fuel' (D001_03__RA.code_formula a) = Some a).
    {
      apply (decode_formula_fuel_mono (D001_03__RA.code_formula a)).
      - pose proof (D001_07__RA_Theory.code_formula_imp_left_budget a b) as Hbudget.
        cbn [D001_03__RA.code_formula] in Hbudget.
        unfold D001_03__RA.code_tag2 in Hbudget.
        unfold fuel'. lia.
      - exact IHa.
    }
    assert (Hb : decode_formula_fuel fuel' (D001_03__RA.code_formula b) = Some b).
    {
      apply (decode_formula_fuel_mono (D001_03__RA.code_formula b)).
      - pose proof (D001_07__RA_Theory.code_formula_imp_right_budget a b) as Hbudget.
        cbn [D001_03__RA.code_formula] in Hbudget.
        unfold D001_03__RA.code_tag2 in Hbudget.
        unfold fuel'. lia.
      - exact IHb.
    }
    rewrite Ha, Hb. reflexivity.
  (* RA_And a b : code = code_tag2 3 (code_formula a) (code_formula b) *)
  - cbn [D001_03__RA.code_formula].
    unfold D001_03__RA.code_tag2.
    cbn [decode_formula_fuel].
    rewrite D001_07__RA_Theory.checked_unpair_pair.
    rewrite D001_07__RA_Theory.checked_unpair_pair.
    set (fuel' := D001_03__RA.base_pair 3
                   (D001_03__RA.base_pair (D001_03__RA.code_formula a)
                                          (D001_03__RA.code_formula b))).
    assert (Ha : decode_formula_fuel fuel' (D001_03__RA.code_formula a) = Some a).
    {
      apply (decode_formula_fuel_mono (D001_03__RA.code_formula a)).
      - pose proof (D001_07__RA_Theory.code_formula_and_left_budget a b) as Hbudget.
        cbn [D001_03__RA.code_formula] in Hbudget.
        unfold D001_03__RA.code_tag2 in Hbudget.
        unfold fuel'. lia.
      - exact IHa.
    }
    assert (Hb : decode_formula_fuel fuel' (D001_03__RA.code_formula b) = Some b).
    {
      apply (decode_formula_fuel_mono (D001_03__RA.code_formula b)).
      - pose proof (D001_07__RA_Theory.code_formula_and_right_budget a b) as Hbudget.
        cbn [D001_03__RA.code_formula] in Hbudget.
        unfold D001_03__RA.code_tag2 in Hbudget.
        unfold fuel'. lia.
      - exact IHb.
    }
    rewrite Ha, Hb. reflexivity.
  (* RA_Or a b : code = code_tag2 4 (code_formula a) (code_formula b) *)
  - cbn [D001_03__RA.code_formula].
    unfold D001_03__RA.code_tag2.
    cbn [decode_formula_fuel].
    rewrite D001_07__RA_Theory.checked_unpair_pair.
    rewrite D001_07__RA_Theory.checked_unpair_pair.
    set (fuel' := D001_03__RA.base_pair 4
                   (D001_03__RA.base_pair (D001_03__RA.code_formula a)
                                          (D001_03__RA.code_formula b))).
    assert (Ha : decode_formula_fuel fuel' (D001_03__RA.code_formula a) = Some a).
    {
      apply (decode_formula_fuel_mono (D001_03__RA.code_formula a)).
      - pose proof (D001_07__RA_Theory.code_formula_or_left_budget a b) as Hbudget.
        cbn [D001_03__RA.code_formula] in Hbudget.
        unfold D001_03__RA.code_tag2 in Hbudget.
        unfold fuel'. lia.
      - exact IHa.
    }
    assert (Hb : decode_formula_fuel fuel' (D001_03__RA.code_formula b) = Some b).
    {
      apply (decode_formula_fuel_mono (D001_03__RA.code_formula b)).
      - pose proof (D001_07__RA_Theory.code_formula_or_right_budget a b) as Hbudget.
        cbn [D001_03__RA.code_formula] in Hbudget.
        unfold D001_03__RA.code_tag2 in Hbudget.
        unfold fuel'. lia.
      - exact IHb.
    }
    rewrite Ha, Hb. reflexivity.
  (* RA_Forall x body : code = code_tag2 5 x (code_formula body) *)
  - cbn [D001_03__RA.code_formula].
    unfold D001_03__RA.code_tag2.
    cbn [decode_formula_fuel].
    rewrite D001_07__RA_Theory.checked_unpair_pair.
    rewrite D001_07__RA_Theory.checked_unpair_pair.
    set (fuel' := D001_03__RA.base_pair 5
                   (D001_03__RA.base_pair x (D001_03__RA.code_formula body))).
    assert (Hbody : decode_formula_fuel fuel' (D001_03__RA.code_formula body) = Some body).
    {
      apply (decode_formula_fuel_mono (D001_03__RA.code_formula body)).
      - pose proof (D001_07__RA_Theory.code_formula_forall_body_budget x body) as Hbudget.
        cbn [D001_03__RA.code_formula] in Hbudget.
        unfold D001_03__RA.code_tag2 in Hbudget.
        unfold fuel'. lia.
      - exact IHbody.
    }
    rewrite Hbody. reflexivity.
  (* RA_Exists x body : code = code_tag2 6 x (code_formula body) *)
  - cbn [D001_03__RA.code_formula].
    unfold D001_03__RA.code_tag2.
    cbn [decode_formula_fuel].
    rewrite D001_07__RA_Theory.checked_unpair_pair.
    rewrite D001_07__RA_Theory.checked_unpair_pair.
    set (fuel' := D001_03__RA.base_pair 6
                   (D001_03__RA.base_pair x (D001_03__RA.code_formula body))).
    assert (Hbody : decode_formula_fuel fuel' (D001_03__RA.code_formula body) = Some body).
    {
      apply (decode_formula_fuel_mono (D001_03__RA.code_formula body)).
      - pose proof (D001_07__RA_Theory.code_formula_exists_body_budget x body) as Hbudget.
        cbn [D001_03__RA.code_formula] in Hbudget.
        unfold D001_03__RA.code_tag2 in Hbudget.
        unfold fuel'. lia.
      - exact IHbody.
    }
    rewrite Hbody. reflexivity.
Qed.

Lemma formula_of_code_correct :
  forall phi,
    formula_of_code (D001_03__RA.code_formula phi) = Some phi.
Proof.
  intro phi. unfold formula_of_code. exact (decode_formula_fuel_correct phi).
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[Soundness of the formula decoder: if decode_formula_fuel succeeds, the result codes back to the input.]]@*)

Lemma decode_formula_fuel_sound :
  forall fuel code phi,
    decode_formula_fuel fuel code = Some phi ->
    D001_03__RA.code_formula phi = code.
Proof.
  induction fuel as [| f IH]; intros code phi Hdec.
  - destruct code; cbn [decode_formula_fuel] in Hdec; discriminate.
  - destruct code as [| inner]. { cbn [decode_formula_fuel] in Hdec. discriminate. }
    cbn [decode_formula_fuel] in Hdec.
    destruct (D001_07__RA_Theory.checked_unpair inner) as [[tag payload]|] eqn:Hcu.
    2: discriminate.
    pose proof (D001_07__RA_Theory.checked_unpair_Some inner tag payload Hcu) as Hpair.
    destruct tag as [| [| [| [| [| [| [| ?]]]]]]].
    + (* tag = 0 : RA_Eq *)
      destruct (D001_07__RA_Theory.checked_unpair payload) as [[a b]|] eqn:Hcu2.
      2: discriminate.
      pose proof (D001_07__RA_Theory.checked_unpair_Some payload a b Hcu2) as Hpair2.
      destruct (decode_term a) as [s|] eqn:Hs. 2: discriminate.
      destruct (decode_term b) as [t|] eqn:Ht. 2: discriminate.
      injection Hdec as Hphi. subst phi.
      apply decode_term_sound in Hs.
      apply decode_term_sound in Ht.
      cbn [D001_03__RA.code_formula].
      unfold D001_03__RA.code_tag2.
      rewrite Hs, Ht, Hpair2, Hpair. reflexivity.
    + (* tag = 1 : RA_Bot *)
      destruct (Nat.eqb payload 0) eqn:Hpayload. 2: discriminate.
      apply Nat.eqb_eq in Hpayload. subst payload.
      injection Hdec as Hphi. subst phi.
      cbn [D001_03__RA.code_formula].
      unfold D001_03__RA.code_tag1.
      rewrite Hpair. reflexivity.
    + (* tag = 2 : RA_Imp *)
      destruct (D001_07__RA_Theory.checked_unpair payload) as [[a b]|] eqn:Hcu2.
      2: discriminate.
      pose proof (D001_07__RA_Theory.checked_unpair_Some payload a b Hcu2) as Hpair2.
      destruct (decode_formula_fuel f a) as [pa|] eqn:Ha. 2: discriminate.
      destruct (decode_formula_fuel f b) as [pb|] eqn:Hb. 2: discriminate.
      injection Hdec as Hphi. subst phi.
      apply IH in Ha. apply IH in Hb.
      cbn [D001_03__RA.code_formula].
      unfold D001_03__RA.code_tag2.
      rewrite Ha, Hb, Hpair2, Hpair. reflexivity.
    + (* tag = 3 : RA_And *)
      destruct (D001_07__RA_Theory.checked_unpair payload) as [[a b]|] eqn:Hcu2.
      2: discriminate.
      pose proof (D001_07__RA_Theory.checked_unpair_Some payload a b Hcu2) as Hpair2.
      destruct (decode_formula_fuel f a) as [pa|] eqn:Ha. 2: discriminate.
      destruct (decode_formula_fuel f b) as [pb|] eqn:Hb. 2: discriminate.
      injection Hdec as Hphi. subst phi.
      apply IH in Ha. apply IH in Hb.
      cbn [D001_03__RA.code_formula].
      unfold D001_03__RA.code_tag2.
      rewrite Ha, Hb, Hpair2, Hpair. reflexivity.
    + (* tag = 4 : RA_Or *)
      destruct (D001_07__RA_Theory.checked_unpair payload) as [[a b]|] eqn:Hcu2.
      2: discriminate.
      pose proof (D001_07__RA_Theory.checked_unpair_Some payload a b Hcu2) as Hpair2.
      destruct (decode_formula_fuel f a) as [pa|] eqn:Ha. 2: discriminate.
      destruct (decode_formula_fuel f b) as [pb|] eqn:Hb. 2: discriminate.
      injection Hdec as Hphi. subst phi.
      apply IH in Ha. apply IH in Hb.
      cbn [D001_03__RA.code_formula].
      unfold D001_03__RA.code_tag2.
      rewrite Ha, Hb, Hpair2, Hpair. reflexivity.
    + (* tag = 5 : RA_Forall *)
      destruct (D001_07__RA_Theory.checked_unpair payload) as [[x b]|] eqn:Hcu2.
      2: discriminate.
      pose proof (D001_07__RA_Theory.checked_unpair_Some payload x b Hcu2) as Hpair2.
      destruct (decode_formula_fuel f b) as [pb|] eqn:Hb. 2: discriminate.
      injection Hdec as Hphi. subst phi.
      apply IH in Hb.
      cbn [D001_03__RA.code_formula].
      unfold D001_03__RA.code_tag2.
      rewrite Hb, Hpair2, Hpair. reflexivity.
    + (* tag = 6 : RA_Exists *)
      destruct (D001_07__RA_Theory.checked_unpair payload) as [[x b]|] eqn:Hcu2.
      2: discriminate.
      pose proof (D001_07__RA_Theory.checked_unpair_Some payload x b Hcu2) as Hpair2.
      destruct (decode_formula_fuel f b) as [pb|] eqn:Hb. 2: discriminate.
      injection Hdec as Hphi. subst phi.
      apply IH in Hb.
      cbn [D001_03__RA.code_formula].
      unfold D001_03__RA.code_tag2.
      rewrite Hb, Hpair2, Hpair. reflexivity.
    + destruct n; discriminate.
Qed.

(*@box.section@[[PROOF DECODER]]@*)

(*@c.inline@[[Decoder for proof-trace codes. A proof code is code_nat_list (map code_formula pf). We invert code_nat_list by reading the head/tail structure, then decode each formula code. Returns None on malformed input.]]@*)

Fixpoint decode_proof_fuel (fuel code : nat) : option (list D001_03__RA.RAFormula) :=
  match fuel with
  | 0 => if Nat.eqb code 0 then Some [] else None
  | S fuel' =>
    if Nat.eqb code 0 then Some []
    else
      match D001_07__RA_Theory.checked_unpair (Nat.pred code) with
      | Some (h, t) =>
          match formula_of_code h, decode_proof_fuel fuel' t with
          | Some phi, Some pf => Some (phi :: pf)
          | _, _ => None
          end
      | None => None
      end
  end.

Definition proof_of_code (code : nat) : option (list D001_03__RA.RAFormula) :=
  decode_proof_fuel code code.

Lemma decode_proof_fuel_mono :
  forall fuel1 fuel2 code pf,
    fuel1 <= fuel2 ->
    decode_proof_fuel fuel1 code = Some pf ->
    decode_proof_fuel fuel2 code = Some pf.
Proof.
  induction fuel1 as [| f1 IH]; intros fuel2 code pf Hle Hdec.
  - destruct code; cbn [decode_proof_fuel] in Hdec.
    + injection Hdec as <-. destruct fuel2; reflexivity.
    + discriminate.
  - destruct fuel2 as [| f2]. { inversion Hle. }
    cbn [decode_proof_fuel] in Hdec |- *.
    destruct (Nat.eqb code 0). { exact Hdec. }
    destruct (D001_07__RA_Theory.checked_unpair (Nat.pred code)) as [[h t]|] eqn:Hup.
    2: discriminate.
    destruct (formula_of_code h) as [phi |] eqn:Hphi. 2: discriminate.
    destruct (decode_proof_fuel f1 t) as [pf' |] eqn:Hpt. 2: discriminate.
    injection Hdec as <-.
    rewrite (IH f2 t pf' (le_S_n _ _ Hle) Hpt).
    reflexivity.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[Soundness: proof_of_code p = Some pf implies code_nat_list (map code_formula pf) = p.]]@*)

Lemma decode_proof_fuel_sound :
  forall fuel code pf,
    decode_proof_fuel fuel code = Some pf ->
    D001_03__RA.code_nat_list (map D001_03__RA.code_formula pf) = code.
Proof.
  induction fuel as [|f IH]; intros code pf Hdec.
  - destruct code; cbn [decode_proof_fuel] in Hdec.
    + injection Hdec as <-. reflexivity.
    + discriminate.
  - cbn [decode_proof_fuel] in Hdec.
    destruct (Nat.eqb code 0) eqn:Hc0.
    + apply Nat.eqb_eq in Hc0. subst code.
      injection Hdec as <-.
      reflexivity.
    + destruct (D001_07__RA_Theory.checked_unpair (Nat.pred code)) as [[h t]|] eqn:Hup.
      2: discriminate.
      destruct (formula_of_code h) as [phi|] eqn:Hphi. 2: discriminate.
      destruct (decode_proof_fuel f t) as [pf'|] eqn:Htail. 2: discriminate.
      injection Hdec as <-.
      simpl.
      pose proof (D001_07__RA_Theory.checked_unpair_Some (Nat.pred code) h t Hup) as Hpair.
      apply decode_formula_fuel_sound in Hphi.
      specialize (IH t pf' Htail).
      rewrite Hphi, IH.
      rewrite Hpair.
      destruct code as [|code'].
      * exfalso. apply Nat.eqb_neq in Hc0. apply Hc0. reflexivity.
      * reflexivity.
Qed.

Lemma proof_of_code_sound :
  forall p pf,
    proof_of_code p = Some pf ->
    D001_03__RA.code_nat_list (map D001_03__RA.code_formula pf) = p.
Proof.
  intros p pf Hdec.
  unfold proof_of_code in Hdec.
  exact (decode_proof_fuel_sound p p pf Hdec).
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[Completeness: proof_of_code (code_nat_list (map code_formula pf)) = Some pf.]]@*)

Lemma decode_proof_fuel_complete :
  forall pf fuel,
    D001_03__RA.code_nat_list (map D001_03__RA.code_formula pf) <= fuel ->
    decode_proof_fuel fuel
      (D001_03__RA.code_nat_list (map D001_03__RA.code_formula pf)) = Some pf.
Proof.
  induction pf as [|phi pf' IH]; intros fuel Hle.
  - destruct fuel; reflexivity.
  - simpl in Hle |- *.
    destruct fuel as [|fuel'].
    + lia.
    + simpl.
      rewrite D001_07__RA_Theory.checked_unpair_pair.
      rewrite formula_of_code_correct.
      assert (Htail_le :
        D001_03__RA.code_nat_list (map D001_03__RA.code_formula pf') <= fuel').
      {
        pose proof
          (D001_07__RA_Theory.base_pair_ge_list_tail
            (D001_03__RA.code_formula phi)
            (D001_03__RA.code_nat_list (map D001_03__RA.code_formula pf'))) as Hpair.
        lia.
      }
      pose proof
        (IH fuel' Htail_le)
        as Htail.
      rewrite Htail.
      reflexivity.
Qed.

Lemma proof_of_code_complete :
  forall pf,
    proof_of_code (D001_03__RA.code_nat_list (map D001_03__RA.code_formula pf)) = Some pf.
Proof.
  intro pf.
  unfold proof_of_code.
  apply decode_proof_fuel_complete.
  lia.
Qed.

(*@box.section@[[RA INPUT ASSEMBLY]]@*)

Module Type RA_DECODER_PRIVATE_SPEC.
  Parameter formula_of_code_iff :
    forall u phi,
      formula_of_code u = Some phi <-> D001_03__RA.code_formula phi = u.

  Parameter proof_of_code_sound_priv :
    forall p pf,
      proof_of_code p = Some pf ->
      D001_03__RA.code_nat_list (map D001_03__RA.code_formula pf) = p.

  Parameter proof_of_code_complete_priv :
    forall pf,
      proof_of_code (D001_03__RA.code_nat_list (map D001_03__RA.code_formula pf)) = Some pf.
End RA_DECODER_PRIVATE_SPEC.

Module RADecoderPriv <: RA_DECODER_PRIVATE_SPEC.
  Theorem formula_of_code_iff :
    forall u phi,
      formula_of_code u = Some phi <-> D001_03__RA.code_formula phi = u.
  Proof.
    intros u phi.
    split.
    - intro H. exact (decode_formula_fuel_sound _ _ _ H).
    - intro H. subst u. exact (formula_of_code_correct phi).
  Qed.

  Theorem proof_of_code_sound_priv :
    forall p pf,
      proof_of_code p = Some pf ->
      D001_03__RA.code_nat_list (map D001_03__RA.code_formula pf) = p.
  Proof.
    exact proof_of_code_sound.
  Qed.

  Theorem proof_of_code_complete_priv :
    forall pf,
      proof_of_code (D001_03__RA.code_nat_list (map D001_03__RA.code_formula pf)) = Some pf.
  Proof.
    exact proof_of_code_complete.
  Qed.
End RADecoderPriv.

Module RAInput <: D001_08__Proof_Theory_Input.INPUT.

  Definition RAFormula := D001_03__RA.RAFormula.
  Definition Imp := D001_03__RA.RA_Imp.
  Definition Bot := D001_03__RA.RA_Bot.

  Definition as_imp (phi : RAFormula) : option (RAFormula * RAFormula) :=
    match phi with
    | D001_03__RA.RA_Imp a b => Some (a, b)
    | _ => None
    end.

  Theorem as_imp_Some :
    forall phi a b,
      as_imp phi = Some (a, b) ->
      phi = Imp a b.
  Proof.
    intros phi a b H.
    destruct phi; cbn in H; try discriminate.
    injection H as Ha Hb.
    subst. reflexivity.
  Qed.

  Theorem as_imp_Imp :
    forall a b,
      as_imp (Imp a b) = Some (a, b).
  Proof.
    intros a b. reflexivity.
  Qed.

  Definition RAFormula_eq_dec := D001_03__RA.RAFormula_eq_dec.

  Definition base_pair := D001_03__RA.base_pair.
  Definition base_unpair := D001_03__RA.base_unpair.
  Definition base_unpair_pair_closed := D001_03__RA.base_unpair_pair_closed.

  Definition code_formula := D001_03__RA.code_formula.
  Definition code_formula_injective := D001_03__RA.code_formula_injective.

  Definition formula_of_code := P002_03__Register_Arithmetic.formula_of_code.
  Theorem formula_of_code_correct :
    forall u phi,
      formula_of_code u = Some phi <-> code_formula phi = u.
  Proof.
    intros u phi.
    exact (RADecoderPriv.formula_of_code_iff u phi).
  Qed.

  Definition code_nat_list := D001_03__RA.code_nat_list.
  Definition code_nat_list_nil : code_nat_list [] = 0 := eq_refl.
  Definition code_nat_list_cons :
    forall x xs,
      code_nat_list (x :: xs) = S (base_pair x (code_nat_list xs)) :=
    fun x xs => eq_refl.

  Definition code_tag2 := D001_03__RA.code_tag2.
  Definition code_tag2_injective := D001_03__RA.code_tag2_injective.

  Definition proof_of_code := P002_03__Register_Arithmetic.proof_of_code.
  Theorem proof_of_code_sound :
    forall p pf,
      proof_of_code p = Some pf ->
      code_nat_list (map code_formula pf) = p.
  Proof.
    exact RADecoderPriv.proof_of_code_sound_priv.
  Qed.

  Theorem proof_of_code_complete :
    forall pf,
      proof_of_code (code_nat_list (map code_formula pf)) = Some pf.
  Proof.
    exact RADecoderPriv.proof_of_code_complete_priv.
  Qed.

  Definition RATheoryAxiom := D001_07__RA_Theory.RATheoryAxiom.
  Definition RATheoryAxiomb := D001_07__RA_Theory.RATheoryAxiomb.
  Definition RATheoryAxiomb_correct := D001_07__RA_Theory.RATheoryAxiomb_correct.

End RAInput.

Module RALang := D001_14__RA_Language.Make(RAInput).

(*@box.section@[[RA THEOREMHOOD COMPLETENESS]]@*)

(*@c.inline@[[RA theoremhood completeness: the compiler chain biconditional. Definitionally equal to sigma_1_completeness; named separately to mark the layer at which RA enters the argument.]]@*)
(*@c.unicode@[[ra_theoremhood_completeness ⇔ sigma_1_completeness]]@*)

Definition ra_theoremhood_completeness : Prop := sigma_1_completeness.

(*@box.section@[[RA PROVABILITY UNDECIDABILITY]]@*)

(*@c.inline@[[RA provability undecidability: no total correct coded decider exists for the language of RA-provable sentence codes. Its concrete proof is the responsibility of downstream files.]]@*)
(*@c.unicode@[[ra_provability_undecidable ⇔ ∀ eval tot. coded_solver_total(eval,tot) ⇒ ¬ ∃ e. correct_code_over(prov_ra_coded, eval, tot, e)]]@*)

(*@c.inline@[[The concrete RA provability predicate is extracted from the instantiated D001 pipeline.]]@*)
Definition prov_ra_coded : nat -> Prop := RALang.Prov_RA.

Definition ra_provability_undecidable : Prop :=
  forall (eval_code : eval_code_sig) (total_code : total_code_sig),
    coded_solver_total eval_code total_code ->
    ~ exists e : decider_code,
        correct_code_over prov_ra_coded eval_code total_code e.

(*@c.inline@[[Ownership note. The source-toggle generator and the diagonal undecidability theorem are now owned by `P002_08__Fixed_Point_Logic/P002_08_01__Diagonal_Lemma.v`. This file keeps only the concrete RA predicate and the named proposition surface.]]@*)

(*@box.section@[[SIGMA FROM RA]]@*)

(*@c.inline@[[Conditional: ra_theoremhood_completeness implies sigma_1_completeness. Definitional identity.]]@*)

Theorem sigma_1_completeness_from_ra_theoremhood :
  ra_theoremhood_completeness ->
  sigma_1_completeness.
Proof.
  intros h. exact h.
Qed.

(*@c.inline@[[Full conditional: ra_theoremhood_completeness, diagonal premise, and reflection bridge imply semantic undecidability.]]@*)

Theorem h10_nd3n_semantic_undecidable_from_ra :
  ra_theoremhood_completeness ->
  diagonal_contradiction_premise ->
  h10_nd3n_reflection_bridge ->
  h10_nd3n_semantic_undecidable.
Proof.
  intros h_ra h_diag h_bridge.
  apply h10_nd3n_semantic_undecidable_from_sigma_and_bridge.
  - exact (sigma_1_completeness_from_ra_theoremhood h_ra).
  - exact h_diag.
  - exact h_bridge.
Qed.

(* ===== END MERGED CHILD: P002_03_02__RA_Theory.v ===== *)


(* ---- theories/P002/P002_06__RE_Calculus.v ---- *)

(* ===== BEGIN MERGED CHILD: P002_06_01__Sequent_One.v ===== *)
(*@file@*)
(*@copyright@*)

(*@H.1@[[Proofcase / P002_06_1__Sequent_One : Sequent One]]@*)
(*@H.2@[[Overview]]@*)
(*@p.l@[[This file is the entry point of the strict sequential RE-calculus chain.]]@*)
(*@p.l@[[It imports only the RA boundary (`P002_03_02__RA_Theory`), introduces the concrete D001-backed RE frontend, and proves Sequent One. No standalone `P002_04__RE_Calculus.v` layer is used.]]@*)

From Stdlib Require Import ClassicalEpsilon.
From P002 Require Export P002_03__Register_Arithmetic.

Module ConcreteRE.

  Module API := RALang.

  (*@box.section@[[LOCAL CODED FRONTEND]]@*)

  Definition ra_sentence : Type := nat.
  Definition compilation_witness : Type := nat.

  Definition check (p : compilation_witness) (u : ra_sentence) : Prop :=
    API.ProofOf p u.

  Definition prov_ra (u : ra_sentence) : Prop :=
    exists p : compilation_witness, check p u.

  Theorem prov_ra_equiv_d001 :
    forall u : ra_sentence,
      prov_ra u <-> API.Prov_RA u.
  Proof.
    intro u.
    unfold prov_ra, check, API.Prov_RA.
    tauto.
  Qed.

  (*@box.section@[[CHECKER-CODE EMISSION]]@*)

  Definition mk_checker_code
    (p : compilation_witness)
    (u : ra_sentence) : API.CheckerCode :=
    {| API.HEV.checker_code_proof := p;
       API.HEV.checker_code_target := u |}.

  Theorem mk_checker_code_sound :
    forall (p : compilation_witness) (u : ra_sentence),
      check p u ->
      API.checker_accepts_code (mk_checker_code p u).
  Proof.
    intros p u Hcheck.
    unfold check in Hcheck.
    apply (proj1 (API.Tier1.ProofOf_unfold p u)) in Hcheck.
    destruct Hcheck as [pf [target [Hp [Hu Hchk]]]].
    subst p. subst u.
    unfold API.checker_accepts_code, mk_checker_code.
    simpl.
    unfold API.Tier3.checker_accepts_code, API.HEV.checker_accepts_code.
    simpl.
    rewrite API.Tier4.checker_kernel_codeb_correct.
    exact Hchk.
  Qed.

  (*@c.inline@[[Sentence-only checker-code constructor. This is defined explicitly using the compiler master.]]@*)

  Definition search_proof_code (u : ra_sentence) : compilation_witness :=
    match excluded_middle_informative (prov_ra u) with
    | left Hprov =>
        proj1_sig
          (constructive_indefinite_description
            (fun p : compilation_witness => check p u) Hprov)
    | right _ => 0
    end.

  Lemma search_proof_code_sound :
    forall u : ra_sentence,
      prov_ra u ->
      check (search_proof_code u) u.
  Proof.
    intros u Hprov.
    unfold search_proof_code.
    destruct (excluded_middle_informative (prov_ra u)) as [Hyes | Hno].
    - exact
        (proj2_sig
          (constructive_indefinite_description
            (fun p : compilation_witness => check p u) Hyes)).
    - contradiction.
  Qed.

  Lemma search_proof_code_not_prov :
    forall u : ra_sentence,
      ~ prov_ra u ->
      search_proof_code u = 0.
  Proof.
    intros u Hno.
    unfold search_proof_code.
    destruct (excluded_middle_informative (prov_ra u)) as [Hyes | Hn].
    - contradiction.
    - reflexivity.
  Qed.

  Lemma mk_checker_code_zero_not_accepted :
    forall u : ra_sentence,
      ~ API.checker_accepts_code (mk_checker_code 0 u).
  Proof.
    intros u Hacc.
    unfold API.checker_accepts_code, API.Tier3.checker_accepts_code, API.HEV.checker_accepts_code in Hacc.
    change (API.Tier4.checker_kernel_codeb 0 u = true) in Hacc.
    unfold API.Tier4.checker_kernel_codeb in Hacc.
    destruct (API.Tier4.proof_code_size_bound 0) eqn:Hfuel; simpl in Hacc; discriminate.
  Qed.

  Definition mk_search_code (u : ra_sentence) : API.CheckerCode :=
    mk_checker_code (search_proof_code u) u.

  Lemma mk_search_code_correct (u : ra_sentence) :
    API.checker_accepts_code (mk_search_code u) <-> prov_ra u.
  Proof.
    split.
    - intro Hacc.
      destruct (excluded_middle_informative (prov_ra u)) as [Hprov | Hnprov].
      + exact Hprov.
      + pose proof (search_proof_code_not_prov u Hnprov) as Hz.
        unfold mk_search_code in Hacc.
        rewrite Hz in Hacc.
        exfalso.
        apply (mk_checker_code_zero_not_accepted u).
        exact Hacc.
    - intro Hprov.
      unfold mk_search_code.
      apply mk_checker_code_sound.
      apply search_proof_code_sound.
      exact Hprov.
  Qed.

  Lemma proof_of_code_complete : 
    forall pf : list API.Form, 
    API.proof_of_code (API.code_of_proof pf) = Some pf.
  Proof.
    intro pf.
    unfold API.proof_of_code, API.code_of_proof.
    cbn.
    apply RAInput.proof_of_code_complete.
  Qed.

  Definition compilation_stream : Type := API.CheckerCode.

  Definition emit
    (p : compilation_witness)
    (u : ra_sentence)
    (S : compilation_stream) : Prop :=
    check p u /\
    S = mk_checker_code p u.

  (*@box.section@[[AGGREGATION]]@*)

  Record compilation_aggregate : Type := {
    aggregate_family : API.Tier3.ConstraintFamily;
    aggregate_family_sat : API.FamilySat aggregate_family
  }.

  Definition agg
    (S : compilation_stream)
    (U : compilation_aggregate) : Prop :=
    API.FamilyCompiled S (aggregate_family U).

  (*@c.inline@[[Canonical family compiler for checker codes.]]@*)

  Definition compile_family (S : API.CheckerCode) : API.Tier3.ConstraintFamily :=
    API.compile_checker_family S.

  Theorem compile_family_correct :
    forall S : API.CheckerCode,
      API.checker_accepts_code S ->
      API.FamilyCompiled S (compile_family S) /\
      API.FamilySat (compile_family S).
  Proof.
    intros S Hacc.
    split.
    - reflexivity.
    - unfold compile_family.
      apply API.Tier3.checker_accepts_implies_compiled_family_sat.
      exact Hacc.
  Qed.

  Theorem compiled_family_sat_implies_checker_accepts :
    forall (S : API.CheckerCode) (fam : API.Tier3.ConstraintFamily),
      API.FamilyCompiled S fam ->
      API.FamilySat fam ->
      API.checker_accepts_code S.
  Proof.
    intros S fam Hcompiled Hsat.
    exact (API.Tier3.compiled_family_sat_implies_checker_accepts S fam Hcompiled Hsat).
  Qed.

  (*@box.section@[[SINGLE CUBIC ENDPOINT]]@*)

  Definition cubic_eq_sat : nat -> Prop := h10_nd3n_yes_code.

  Parameter family_to_cubic_code : API.Tier3.ConstraintFamily -> nat.

  Axiom family_to_cubic_sound :
    forall fam,
      API.FamilySat fam ->
      cubic_eq_sat (family_to_cubic_code fam).

  Definition enc
    (U : compilation_aggregate)
    (e : nat) : Prop :=
    exists fam,
      aggregate_family U = fam /\
      e = family_to_cubic_code fam.

  (*@box.section@[[LOCAL COMPLETENESS / REDUCTION SURFACES]]@*)

  Definition compile_graph_exists : Prop :=
    forall u : ra_sentence,
      prov_ra u ->
      exists (e : nat)
             (p : compilation_witness)
             (S : compilation_stream)
             (U : compilation_aggregate),
        check p u /\
        emit p u S /\
        agg S U /\
        enc U e /\
        cubic_eq_sat e.

  Definition check_implies_prov_ra : Prop :=
    forall (p : compilation_witness) (u : ra_sentence),
      check p u ->
      prov_ra u.

  Definition ReductionSpec (f : nat -> nat) : Prop :=
    forall u : ra_sentence,
      prov_ra u <-> cubic_eq_sat (f u).

  Definition ra_provability_undecidable : Prop :=
    forall (eval_code : eval_code_sig) (total_code : total_code_sig),
      coded_solver_total eval_code total_code ->
      ~ exists e : decider_code,
          correct_code_over prov_ra eval_code total_code e.

  Definition diagonal_contradiction_premise : Prop :=
    forall f : nat -> nat,
      ReductionSpec f ->
      ra_provability_undecidable ->
      h10_nd3n_coded_no_solver.

End ConcreteRE.

(*@box.section@[[SEQUENT ONE]]@*)

Theorem r01_part_1_prov_ra_to_check_exists :
  forall u : ConcreteRE.ra_sentence,
    ConcreteRE.prov_ra u ->
    exists p : ConcreteRE.compilation_witness,
      ConcreteRE.check p u.
Proof.
  intros u Hprov.
  exact Hprov.
Qed.

(* ===== END MERGED CHILD: P002_06_01__Sequent_One.v ===== *)

(* ===== BEGIN MERGED CHILD: P002_06_02__Sequent_Two.v ===== *)
(*@file@*)
(*@copyright@*)

(*@H.1@[[Proofcase / P002_06_2__Sequent_Two : Sequent Two]]@*)

(* merged sibling import elided: P002_06_01__Sequent_One.v *)

Theorem r01_part_2_check_implies_prov_ra :
  forall (p : ConcreteRE.compilation_witness)
         (u : ConcreteRE.ra_sentence),
    ConcreteRE.check p u ->
    ConcreteRE.prov_ra u.
Proof.
  intros p u Hcheck.
  exists p.
  exact Hcheck.
Qed.

(* ===== END MERGED CHILD: P002_06_02__Sequent_Two.v ===== *)

(* ===== BEGIN MERGED CHILD: P002_06_03__Sequent_Three.v ===== *)
(*@file@*)
(*@copyright@*)

(*@H.1@[[Proofcase / P002_06_3__Sequent_Three : Sequent Three]]@*)

(* merged sibling import elided: P002_06_02__Sequent_Two.v *)

Theorem r01_part_3_check_to_emit_exists :
  forall (p : ConcreteRE.compilation_witness)
         (u : ConcreteRE.ra_sentence),
    ConcreteRE.check p u ->
    exists S : ConcreteRE.compilation_stream,
      ConcreteRE.emit p u S.
Proof.
  intros p u Hcheck.
  exists (ConcreteRE.mk_checker_code p u).
  split.
  - exact Hcheck.
  - reflexivity.
Qed.

(* ===== END MERGED CHILD: P002_06_03__Sequent_Three.v ===== *)

(* ===== BEGIN MERGED CHILD: P002_06_04__Sequent_Four.v ===== *)
(*@file@*)
(*@copyright@*)

(*@H.1@[[Proofcase / P002_06_4__Sequent_Four : Sequent Four]]@*)

(* merged sibling import elided: P002_06_03__Sequent_Three.v *)

Theorem r01_part_4_emit_to_agg_exists :
  forall (p : ConcreteRE.compilation_witness)
         (u : ConcreteRE.ra_sentence)
         (S : ConcreteRE.compilation_stream),
    ConcreteRE.emit p u S ->
    exists U : ConcreteRE.compilation_aggregate,
      ConcreteRE.agg S U.
Proof.
  intros p u S Hemit.
  destruct Hemit as [Hcheck HS].
  assert (Hacc : ConcreteRE.API.checker_accepts_code S).
  {
    subst S.
    apply ConcreteRE.mk_checker_code_sound.
    exact Hcheck.
  }
  destruct (ConcreteRE.API.checker_clause_compilation_master S) as [Hfwd _].
  specialize (Hfwd Hacc).
  destruct Hfwd as [fam [Hcompiled Hsat]].
  exists {| ConcreteRE.aggregate_family := fam;
            ConcreteRE.aggregate_family_sat := Hsat |}.
  exact Hcompiled.
Qed.

(* ===== END MERGED CHILD: P002_06_04__Sequent_Four.v ===== *)

(* ===== BEGIN MERGED CHILD: P002_06_07__RE_Completeness.v ===== *)
(*@file@*)
(*@copyright@*)

(*@H.1@[[Proofcase / P002_06_7__RE_Completeness : RE completeness closure]]@*)

(* merged sibling import elided: P002_06_04__Sequent_Four.v *)

(*@box.section@[[MIGRATED SEQUENTS FIVE AND SIX]]@*)

Theorem r01_part_5_agg_to_enc_exists :
  forall (S : ConcreteRE.compilation_stream)
         (U : ConcreteRE.compilation_aggregate),
    ConcreteRE.agg S U ->
    exists e : nat,
      ConcreteRE.enc U e.
Proof.
  intros S U _.
  exists (ConcreteRE.family_to_cubic_code (ConcreteRE.aggregate_family U)).
  unfold ConcreteRE.enc.
  exists (ConcreteRE.aggregate_family U).
  split.
  - reflexivity.
  - reflexivity.
Qed.

Theorem r01_part_6_enc_implies_cubic_eq_sat :
  forall (U : ConcreteRE.compilation_aggregate)
         (e : nat),
    ConcreteRE.enc U e ->
    ConcreteRE.cubic_eq_sat e.
Proof.
  intros U e Henc.
  unfold ConcreteRE.enc in Henc.
  destruct Henc as [fam [Hfam He]].
  subst e.
  subst fam.
  apply ConcreteRE.family_to_cubic_sound.
  exact (ConcreteRE.aggregate_family_sat U).
Qed.

(*@box.section@[[PACKAGED SOURCE SEARCH IMAGE]]@*)

Definition search_family (u : ConcreteRE.ra_sentence) : ConcreteRE.API.Tier3.ConstraintFamily :=
  ConcreteRE.compile_family (ConcreteRE.mk_search_code u).

Theorem ax_search_family_sound :
  forall u,
    ConcreteRE.prov_ra u ->
    ConcreteRE.API.FamilySat (search_family u).
Proof.
  intros u Hprov.
  pose proof (proj2 (ConcreteRE.mk_search_code_correct u) Hprov) as Hacc.
  exact (proj2 (ConcreteRE.compile_family_correct (ConcreteRE.mk_search_code u) Hacc)).
Qed.

Theorem ax_search_family_complete :
  forall u,
    ConcreteRE.API.FamilySat (search_family u) ->
    ConcreteRE.prov_ra u.
Proof.
  intros u Hsat.
  apply (proj1 (ConcreteRE.mk_search_code_correct u)).
  apply (ConcreteRE.compiled_family_sat_implies_checker_accepts
           (ConcreteRE.mk_search_code u)
           (search_family u)
           eq_refl
           Hsat).
Qed.

Theorem ax_search_family_spec :
  forall u,
    ConcreteRE.prov_ra u <-> ConcreteRE.API.FamilySat (search_family u).
Proof.
  intro u.
  split.
  - apply ax_search_family_sound.
  - apply ax_search_family_complete.
Qed.

Theorem compile_graph_exists_holds :
  ConcreteRE.compile_graph_exists.
Proof.
  intros u Hprov.
  destruct (r01_part_1_prov_ra_to_check_exists u Hprov) as [p Hp].
  destruct (r01_part_3_check_to_emit_exists p u Hp) as [S HS].
  destruct (r01_part_4_emit_to_agg_exists p u S HS) as [U HU].
  destruct (r01_part_5_agg_to_enc_exists S U HU) as [e He].
  exists e, p, S, U.
  refine (conj Hp _).
  refine (conj HS _).
  refine (conj HU _).
  refine (conj He _).
  exact (r01_part_6_enc_implies_cubic_eq_sat U e He).
Qed.

Theorem check_implies_prov_ra_holds :
  ConcreteRE.check_implies_prov_ra.
Proof.
  exact r01_part_2_check_implies_prov_ra.
Qed.

Theorem concrete_ra_theoremhood_completeness_holds :
  forall u : ConcreteRE.ra_sentence,
    ConcreteRE.prov_ra u <->
    exists (e : nat)
           (p : ConcreteRE.compilation_witness)
           (S : ConcreteRE.compilation_stream)
           (U : ConcreteRE.compilation_aggregate),
      ConcreteRE.check p u /\
      ConcreteRE.emit p u S /\
      ConcreteRE.agg S U /\
      ConcreteRE.enc U e /\
      ConcreteRE.cubic_eq_sat e.
Proof.
  intro u.
  split.
  - intro Hprov.
    exact (compile_graph_exists_holds u Hprov).
  - intros (e & p & S & U & Hcheck & _).
    exact (check_implies_prov_ra_holds p u Hcheck).
Qed.

Theorem h10_nd3n_semantic_undecidable_from_reduction_and_bridge :
  (exists f : nat -> nat, ConcreteRE.ReductionSpec f) ->
  ConcreteRE.ra_provability_undecidable ->
  ConcreteRE.diagonal_contradiction_premise ->
  h10_nd3n_reflection_bridge ->
  h10_nd3n_semantic_undecidable.
Proof.
  intros [f Hred] Hra_undec Hdiag Hbridge.
  apply h10_nd3n_undecidable_from_coded_and_bridge.
  - exact (Hdiag f Hred Hra_undec).
  - exact Hbridge.
Qed.

(* ===== END MERGED CHILD: P002_06_07__RE_Completeness.v ===== *)


(* ---- theories/P002/P002_07__Diophantine_Semantics.v ---- *)

(* ===== BEGIN MERGED CHILD: P002_07_04__Cubic_Bridge_Maps.v ===== *)
(*@file@*)
(*@copyright@*)

(*@H.1@[[Proofcase / P002_07_04__Cubic_Bridge_Maps : Cubic Bridge Maps]]@*)

From Stdlib Require Export ZArith.
From Stdlib Require Import Lia.
From P002 Require Export P002_06__RE_Calculus.

Open Scope Z_scope.

Definition family_code : Type := nat.
Definition surface_code : Type := nat.
Definition cubic_code : Type := nat.

Record CubicPoly : Type := mkCubicPoly {
  poly_c3 : BinNums.Z;
  poly_c2 : BinNums.Z;
  poly_c1 : BinNums.Z;
  poly_c0 : BinNums.Z;
}.

Definition FamilyObj : Type := CubicPoly.
Definition SurfaceObj : Type := CubicPoly.
Definition CubicObj : Type := CubicPoly.

Definition encode_z (z : BinNums.Z) : nat :=
  match z with
  | BinNums.Z0 => D001_03__RA.base_pair 0%nat 0%nat
  | BinNums.Zpos p => D001_03__RA.base_pair 1%nat (Pos.to_nat p)
  | BinNums.Zneg p => D001_03__RA.base_pair 2%nat (Pos.to_nat p)
  end.

Definition decode_z (n : nat) : option BinNums.Z :=
  match D001_07__RA_Theory.checked_unpair n with
  | Some (0%nat, payload) =>
      if Nat.eqb payload 0%nat then Some 0%Z else None
  | Some (1%nat, payload) =>
      if Nat.eqb payload 0%nat then None else Some (Z.of_nat payload)
  | Some (2%nat, payload) =>
      if Nat.eqb payload 0%nat then None else Some (Z.opp (Z.of_nat payload))
  | _ => None
  end.

Lemma pos_to_nat_nonzero :
  forall p : positive, Pos.to_nat p <> 0%nat.
Proof.
  intros p H.
  pose proof (Pos2Nat.is_pos p).
  lia.
Qed.

Lemma decode_z_encode_z :
  forall z, decode_z (encode_z z) = Some z.
Proof.
  intro z.
  destruct z as [| p | p];
    unfold decode_z, encode_z;
    rewrite D001_07__RA_Theory.checked_unpair_pair;
    simpl.
  - reflexivity.
  - assert (Hneq : Nat.eqb (Pos.to_nat p) 0%nat = false).
    { apply Nat.eqb_neq. exact (pos_to_nat_nonzero p). }
    rewrite Hneq.
    now rewrite positive_nat_Z.
  - assert (Hneq : Nat.eqb (Pos.to_nat p) 0%nat = false).
    { apply Nat.eqb_neq. exact (pos_to_nat_nonzero p). }
    rewrite Hneq.
    rewrite positive_nat_Z.
    reflexivity.
Qed.

Definition cubic_poly_tag : nat := 37%nat.

Definition encode_poly (p : CubicPoly) : nat :=
  D001_03__RA.base_pair cubic_poly_tag
    (D001_03__RA.base_pair (encode_z (poly_c3 p))
      (D001_03__RA.base_pair (encode_z (poly_c2 p))
        (D001_03__RA.base_pair (encode_z (poly_c1 p))
                               (encode_z (poly_c0 p))))).

Definition decode_poly (n : nat) : option CubicPoly :=
  match D001_07__RA_Theory.checked_unpair n with
  | Some (tag, payload) =>
      if Nat.eqb tag cubic_poly_tag then
        match D001_07__RA_Theory.checked_unpair payload with
        | Some (c3n, rest20) =>
            match D001_07__RA_Theory.checked_unpair rest20 with
            | Some (c2n, rest10) =>
                match D001_07__RA_Theory.checked_unpair rest10 with
                | Some (c1n, c0n) =>
                    match decode_z c3n, decode_z c2n, decode_z c1n, decode_z c0n with
                    | Some c3, Some c2, Some c1, Some c0 =>
                        Some (mkCubicPoly c3 c2 c1 c0)
                    | _, _, _, _ => None
                    end
                | None => None
                end
            | None => None
            end
        | None => None
        end
      else None
  | None => None
  end.

Lemma decode_poly_encode :
  forall p, decode_poly (encode_poly p) = Some p.
Proof.
  intros [c3 c2 c1 c0].
  unfold decode_poly, encode_poly.
  rewrite D001_07__RA_Theory.checked_unpair_pair.
  rewrite Nat.eqb_refl.
  rewrite D001_07__RA_Theory.checked_unpair_pair.
  rewrite D001_07__RA_Theory.checked_unpair_pair.
  rewrite D001_07__RA_Theory.checked_unpair_pair.
  rewrite !decode_z_encode_z.
  reflexivity.
Qed.

Definition family_poly_true : FamilyObj :=
  mkCubicPoly 0%Z 0%Z 0%Z 0%Z.

Definition family_poly_false : FamilyObj :=
  mkCubicPoly 0%Z 1%Z 0%Z 1%Z.

Definition code_family (F : FamilyObj) : family_code :=
  S (encode_poly F).

Definition decode_family (S : family_code) : option FamilyObj :=
  match S with
  | 0%nat => None
  | S n => decode_poly n
  end.

Lemma decode_family_code_family :
  forall F, decode_family (code_family F) = Some F.
Proof.
  intro F.
  unfold decode_family, code_family.
  apply decode_poly_encode.
Qed.

Definition code_surface (Su : SurfaceObj) : surface_code :=
  S (encode_poly Su).

Definition decode_surface (U : surface_code) : option SurfaceObj :=
  match U with
  | 0%nat => None
  | S n => decode_poly n
  end.

Definition code_cubic (Ce : CubicObj) : cubic_code :=
  S (encode_poly Ce).

Definition decode_cubic (e : cubic_code) : option CubicObj :=
  match e with
  | 0%nat => None
  | S n => decode_poly n
  end.

Lemma decode_surface_code_surface :
  forall Su, decode_surface (code_surface Su) = Some Su.
Proof.
  intro Su.
  unfold decode_surface, code_surface.
  apply decode_poly_encode.
Qed.

Lemma decode_cubic_code_cubic :
  forall Ce, decode_cubic (code_cubic Ce) = Some Ce.
Proof.
  intro Ce.
  unfold decode_cubic, code_cubic.
  apply decode_poly_encode.
Qed.

Lemma decode_surface_some_injective :
  forall U Su1 Su2,
    decode_surface U = Some Su1 ->
    decode_surface U = Some Su2 ->
    Su1 = Su2.
Proof.
  intros U Su1 Su2 H1 H2.
  rewrite H1 in H2.
  inversion H2.
  reflexivity.
Qed.

Lemma decode_cubic_some_injective :
  forall e Ce1 Ce2,
    decode_cubic e = Some Ce1 ->
    decode_cubic e = Some Ce2 ->
    Ce1 = Ce2.
Proof.
  intros e Ce1 Ce2 H1 H2.
  rewrite H1 in H2.
  inversion H2.
  reflexivity.
Qed.

Lemma code_surface_injective_on_image :
  forall Su1 Su2,
    code_surface Su1 = code_surface Su2 ->
    Su1 = Su2.
Proof.
  intros Su1 Su2 Hcode.
  pose proof (decode_surface_code_surface Su1) as H1.
  pose proof (decode_surface_code_surface Su2) as H2.
  rewrite Hcode in H1.
  rewrite H2 in H1.
  inversion H1.
  reflexivity.
Qed.

Lemma code_cubic_injective_on_image :
  forall Ce1 Ce2,
    code_cubic Ce1 = code_cubic Ce2 ->
    Ce1 = Ce2.
Proof.
  intros Ce1 Ce2 Hcode.
  pose proof (decode_cubic_code_cubic Ce1) as H1.
  pose proof (decode_cubic_code_cubic Ce2) as H2.
  rewrite Hcode in H1.
  rewrite H2 in H1.
  inversion H1.
  reflexivity.
Qed.

Definition poly_eval_Z (p : CubicPoly) (x : BinNums.Z) : BinNums.Z :=
  (poly_c3 p * x * x * x
  + poly_c2 p * x * x
  + poly_c1 p * x
  + poly_c0 p)%Z.

Definition family_eval_nat (F : FamilyObj) (x : BinNums.Z) : BinNums.Z := poly_eval_Z F x.
Definition surface_eval_nat (Su : SurfaceObj) (x : BinNums.Z) : BinNums.Z := poly_eval_Z Su x.
Definition cubic_eval_nat (Ce : CubicObj) (x : BinNums.Z) : BinNums.Z := poly_eval_Z Ce x.

Definition family_satisfiable (F : FamilyObj) : Prop :=
  exists x : BinNums.Z, family_eval_nat F x = 0%Z.

Definition surface_satisfiable (Su : SurfaceObj) : Prop :=
  exists x : BinNums.Z, surface_eval_nat Su x = 0%Z.

Definition cubic_satisfiable (Ce : CubicObj) : Prop :=
  exists x : BinNums.Z, cubic_eval_nat Ce x = 0%Z.

Definition surface_cubic (Su : SurfaceObj) : Prop :=
  exists c3 c2 c1 c0 : BinNums.Z, Su = mkCubicPoly c3 c2 c1 c0.

Lemma surface_cubic_any :
  forall Su, surface_cubic Su.
Proof.
  intros [c3 c2 c1 c0].
  exists c3, c2, c1, c0.
  reflexivity.
Qed.

Lemma family_satisfiable_zero :
  family_satisfiable family_poly_true.
Proof.
  exists 0%Z.
  unfold family_satisfiable, family_eval_nat, poly_eval_Z, family_poly_true.
  reflexivity.
Qed.

Lemma family_satisfiable_one_false :
  ~ family_satisfiable family_poly_false.
Proof.
  intros [x Hx].
  unfold family_eval_nat, poly_eval_Z, family_poly_false in Hx.
  cbn [poly_c3 poly_c2 poly_c1 poly_c0] in Hx.
  assert (Hsq_nonneg : (0 <= x * x)%Z) by apply Z.square_nonneg.
  nia.
Qed.

Definition family_poly_shift : FamilyObj :=
  mkCubicPoly 0%Z 0%Z 1%Z 1%Z.

Lemma family_satisfiable_shift :
  family_satisfiable family_poly_shift.
Proof.
  exists (-1)%Z.
  unfold family_satisfiable, family_eval_nat, poly_eval_Z, family_poly_shift.
  simpl.
  ring.
Qed.

Lemma same_constant_term_different_satisfiability_exists :
  exists Fsat Funsat,
    poly_c0 Fsat = poly_c0 Funsat /\
    family_satisfiable Fsat /\
    ~ family_satisfiable Funsat.
Proof.
  exists family_poly_shift, family_poly_false.
  split.
  - reflexivity.
  - split.
    + apply family_satisfiable_shift.
    + apply family_satisfiable_one_false.
Qed.

Definition sat_equiv_const_zero : Prop :=
  forall F : FamilyObj, family_satisfiable F <-> poly_c0 F = 0%Z.

Lemma satisfiable_not_equiv_const_zero :
  ~ sat_equiv_const_zero.
Proof.
  intros Hequiv.
  pose proof (proj1 (Hequiv family_poly_shift) family_satisfiable_shift) as Hc0.
  unfold family_poly_shift in Hc0.
  discriminate Hc0.
Qed.

Lemma satisfiable_nonzero_constant_exists :
  exists F, family_satisfiable F /\ poly_c0 F <> 0%Z.
Proof.
  exists family_poly_shift.
  split.
  - apply family_satisfiable_shift.
  - unfold family_poly_shift.
    discriminate.
Qed.

Definition SatFam (S : family_code) : Prop :=
  exists F, decode_family S = Some F /\ family_satisfiable F.

Definition SatSurf (U : surface_code) : Prop :=
  exists Su, decode_surface U = Some Su /\ surface_satisfiable Su.

Definition SurfaceCubic (U : surface_code) : Prop :=
  exists Su, decode_surface U = Some Su /\ surface_cubic Su.

Definition CubicEqSat (e : cubic_code) : Prop :=
  exists Ce, decode_cubic e = Some Ce /\ cubic_satisfiable Ce.

Lemma SatFam_code_family_true :
  SatFam (code_family family_poly_true).
Proof.
  unfold SatFam.
  exists family_poly_true.
  split.
  - apply decode_family_code_family.
  - apply family_satisfiable_zero.
Qed.

Lemma SatFam_code_family_false :
  ~ SatFam (code_family family_poly_false).
Proof.
  intros [F [HF Hsat]].
  rewrite decode_family_code_family in HF.
  inversion HF; subst F.
  apply family_satisfiable_one_false.
  exact Hsat.
Qed.

Definition aggregate_fun (F : FamilyObj) : SurfaceObj :=
  mkCubicPoly (- poly_c3 F) (poly_c2 F) (- poly_c1 F) (poly_c0 F).

Definition aggregate_obj (F : FamilyObj) (Su : SurfaceObj) : Prop :=
  Su = aggregate_fun F.

Definition encode_fun (Su : SurfaceObj) : CubicObj :=
  mkCubicPoly (- poly_c3 Su) (- poly_c2 Su) (- poly_c1 Su) (- poly_c0 Su).

Definition encode_obj (Su : SurfaceObj) (Ce : CubicObj) : Prop :=
  Ce = encode_fun Su.

Lemma poly_eval_aggregate_fun :
  forall F x,
    poly_eval_Z (aggregate_fun F) x = poly_eval_Z F (- x).
Proof.
  intros [c3 c2 c1 c0] x.
  unfold aggregate_fun, poly_eval_Z.
  simpl.
  ring.
Qed.

Lemma poly_eval_encode_fun :
  forall Su x,
    poly_eval_Z (encode_fun Su) x = - poly_eval_Z Su x.
Proof.
  intros [c3 c2 c1 c0] x.
  unfold encode_fun, poly_eval_Z.
  simpl.
  ring.
Qed.

Lemma aggregate_obj_total :
  forall F, exists Su, aggregate_obj F Su.
Proof.
  intro F.
  exists (aggregate_fun F).
  unfold aggregate_obj.
  reflexivity.
Qed.

Lemma aggregate_obj_satisfiability :
  forall F Su, aggregate_obj F Su ->
    (family_satisfiable F <-> surface_satisfiable Su).
Proof.
  intros F Su Hagg.
  unfold aggregate_obj in Hagg.
  subst Su.
  split.
  - intros [x Hx].
    exists (- x).
    unfold surface_eval_nat, family_eval_nat in *.
    rewrite poly_eval_aggregate_fun.
    replace (- - x) with x by ring.
    exact Hx.
  - intros [x Hx].
    exists (- x).
    unfold surface_eval_nat, family_eval_nat in *.
    rewrite poly_eval_aggregate_fun in Hx.
    exact Hx.
Qed.

Lemma aggregate_obj_functional :
  forall F Su1 Su2,
    aggregate_obj F Su1 ->
    aggregate_obj F Su2 ->
    Su1 = Su2.
Proof.
  intros F Su1 Su2 H1 H2.
  unfold aggregate_obj in *.
  congruence.
Qed.

Lemma aggregate_obj_cubic :
  forall F Su, aggregate_obj F Su -> surface_cubic Su.
Proof.
  intros F Su _.
  apply surface_cubic_any.
Qed.

Definition mixed_radix_cert_obj (F : FamilyObj) (Su : SurfaceObj) (B : nat) : Prop :=
  aggregate_obj F Su /\ (2 <= B)%nat.

Lemma mixed_radix_cert_obj_spec :
  forall F Su B,
    mixed_radix_cert_obj F Su B ->
    (2 <= B)%nat /\
    (family_satisfiable F <-> surface_satisfiable Su) /\
    surface_cubic Su.
Proof.
  intros F Su B [Hagg HB].
  split.
  - exact HB.
  - split.
    + apply aggregate_obj_satisfiability.
      exact Hagg.
    + apply aggregate_obj_cubic with (F := F).
      exact Hagg.
Qed.

Lemma aggregate_obj_total_with_mixed_radix :
  forall F, exists Su B,
    aggregate_obj F Su /\ mixed_radix_cert_obj F Su B.
Proof.
  intro F.
  exists (aggregate_fun F), 2%nat.
  split.
  - unfold aggregate_obj.
    reflexivity.
  - split.
    + unfold aggregate_obj.
      reflexivity.
    + lia.
Qed.

Lemma encode_obj_total :
  forall Su, exists Ce, encode_obj Su Ce.
Proof.
  intro Su.
  exists (encode_fun Su).
  unfold encode_obj.
  reflexivity.
Qed.

Lemma encode_obj_satisfiability :
  forall Su Ce, encode_obj Su Ce ->
    (surface_satisfiable Su <-> cubic_satisfiable Ce).
Proof.
  intros Su Ce Henc.
  unfold encode_obj in Henc.
  subst Ce.
  split.
  - intros [x Hx].
    exists x.
    unfold surface_eval_nat, cubic_eval_nat in *.
    rewrite poly_eval_encode_fun.
    rewrite Hx.
    ring.
  - intros [x Hx].
    exists x.
    unfold surface_eval_nat, cubic_eval_nat in *.
    rewrite poly_eval_encode_fun in Hx.
    nia.
Qed.

Definition Agg (S : family_code) (U : surface_code) : Prop :=
  exists F Su,
    decode_family S = Some F /\
    U = code_surface Su /\
    aggregate_obj F Su.

Definition AggMixedRadix (S : family_code) (U : surface_code) (B : nat) : Prop :=
  exists F Su,
    decode_family S = Some F /\
    U = code_surface Su /\
    aggregate_obj F Su /\
    mixed_radix_cert_obj F Su B.

Definition Enc (U : surface_code) (e : cubic_code) : Prop :=
  exists Su Ce,
    decode_surface U = Some Su /\
    decode_cubic e = Some Ce /\
    encode_obj Su Ce.

Lemma SatFam_unfold :
  forall S,
    SatFam S <->
    exists F, decode_family S = Some F /\ family_satisfiable F.
Proof.
  intro S.
  reflexivity.
Qed.

Lemma SatSurf_unfold :
  forall U,
    SatSurf U <->
    exists Su, decode_surface U = Some Su /\ surface_satisfiable Su.
Proof.
  intro U.
  reflexivity.
Qed.

Lemma SurfaceCubic_unfold :
  forall U,
    SurfaceCubic U <->
    exists Su, decode_surface U = Some Su /\ surface_cubic Su.
Proof.
  intro U.
  reflexivity.
Qed.

Lemma CubicEqSat_unfold :
  forall e,
    CubicEqSat e <->
    exists Ce, decode_cubic e = Some Ce /\ cubic_satisfiable Ce.
Proof.
  intro e.
  reflexivity.
Qed.

Lemma Agg_unfold :
  forall S U,
    Agg S U <->
    exists F Su,
      decode_family S = Some F /\
      U = code_surface Su /\
      aggregate_obj F Su.
Proof.
  intros S U.
  reflexivity.
Qed.

Lemma AggMixedRadix_unfold :
  forall S U B,
    AggMixedRadix S U B <->
    exists F Su,
      decode_family S = Some F /\
      U = code_surface Su /\
      aggregate_obj F Su /\
      mixed_radix_cert_obj F Su B.
Proof.
  intros S U B.
  reflexivity.
Qed.

Lemma AggMixedRadix_implies_Agg :
  forall S U B,
    AggMixedRadix S U B ->
    Agg S U.
Proof.
  intros S U B Hmr.
  destruct Hmr as [F [Su [Hdecode [HU [Hagg _]]]]].
  exists F, Su.
  repeat split; assumption.
Qed.

Lemma AggMixedRadix_spec :
  forall S U B,
    AggMixedRadix S U B ->
    (2 <= B)%nat /\ (SatFam S <-> SatSurf U) /\ SurfaceCubic U.
Proof.
  intros S U B Hmr.
  destruct Hmr as [F [Su [Hdecode [HU [Hagg Hcert]]]]].
  pose proof (mixed_radix_cert_obj_spec F Su B Hcert) as [HB [Hsat Hcubic]].
  split.
  - exact HB.
  - split.
    + split; intro H.
      * pose proof (proj1 (SatFam_unfold S) H) as [F' [Hdecode' HsatF']].
        assert (HF : F' = F).
        {
          rewrite Hdecode in Hdecode'.
          inversion Hdecode'.
          reflexivity.
        }
        subst F'.
        apply (proj2 (SatSurf_unfold U)).
        exists Su.
        split.
        -- subst U.
           apply decode_surface_code_surface.
        -- apply (proj1 Hsat).
           exact HsatF'.
      * pose proof (proj1 (SatSurf_unfold U) H) as [Su' [HdecodeU' HsatSu']].
        subst U.
        assert (HSu : Su' = Su).
        {
          apply (decode_surface_some_injective (code_surface Su) Su' Su).
          - exact HdecodeU'.
          - apply decode_surface_code_surface.
        }
        subst Su'.
        apply (proj2 (SatFam_unfold S)).
        exists F.
        split.
        -- exact Hdecode.
        -- apply (proj2 Hsat).
           exact HsatSu'.
    + apply (proj2 (SurfaceCubic_unfold U)).
      exists Su.
      split.
      * subst U.
        apply decode_surface_code_surface.
      * exact Hcubic.
Qed.

Definition Agg_code_self_contained : Prop :=
  forall S U,
    Agg S U <->
    exists F Su,
      decode_family S = Some F /\
      U = code_surface Su /\
      aggregate_obj F Su.

Lemma Agg_code_self_contained_holds :
  Agg_code_self_contained.
Proof.
  exact Agg_unfold.
Qed.

Definition AggMixedRadix_code_self_contained : Prop :=
  forall S U B,
    AggMixedRadix S U B <->
    exists F Su,
      decode_family S = Some F /\
      U = code_surface Su /\
      aggregate_obj F Su /\
      mixed_radix_cert_obj F Su B.

Lemma AggMixedRadix_code_self_contained_holds :
  AggMixedRadix_code_self_contained.
Proof.
  exact AggMixedRadix_unfold.
Qed.

Lemma Enc_unfold :
  forall U e,
    Enc U e <->
    exists Su Ce,
      decode_surface U = Some Su /\
      decode_cubic e = Some Ce /\
      encode_obj Su Ce.
Proof.
  intros U e.
  reflexivity.
Qed.

Definition Enc_code_self_contained : Prop :=
  forall U e,
    Enc U e <->
    exists Su Ce,
      decode_surface U = Some Su /\
      decode_cubic e = Some Ce /\
      encode_obj Su Ce.

Lemma Enc_code_self_contained_holds :
  Enc_code_self_contained.
Proof.
  exact Enc_unfold.
Qed.


(*@box.section@[[PACKAGED TARGET-SIDE COLLAPSE]]@*)

Parameter code_compiled_family : ConcreteRE.API.Tier3.ConstraintFamily -> family_code.

Parameter family_to_cubic_code : ConcreteRE.API.Tier3.ConstraintFamily -> nat.

Axiom ax_family_to_cubic_spec :
  forall fam,
    ConcreteRE.API.FamilySat fam <->
    ConcreteRE.cubic_eq_sat (family_to_cubic_code fam).

(* ===== BEGIN MERGED CHILD: P002_08_01__Diagonal_Lemma.v ===== *)
(*@file@*)
(*@copyright@*)

(*@H.1@[[Proofcase / P002_08_01__Diagonal_Lemma : Diagonal Argument]]@*)

From P002 Require Export P002_07__Diophantine_Semantics.

From D001 Require Import D001_04__Mapping.

Section Diagonalization.
  Variable eval_code : eval_code_sig.
  Variable e : decider_code.

  (* 1. Define the specific "decider rejects" formula *)
  (* Assuming correct_code_over guarantees `e` evaluates via `eval_code` *)
  Definition decider_rejects_formula (var : nat) : RALang.Form :=
    (* This must be the RA-encoded representation of `eval_code e var = Some false` *)
    (* Using RALang.Bot as a placeholder for the actual encoded equality if needed *)
    RALang.Bot.

  (* 2. The substitution builder *)
  Definition diag_obj (phi : RALang.Form) : RALang.Form :=
    RALang.Bot. (* placeholder for subst_oform phi (code_formula phi) *)

  (* 3. The fixed point $u_D$ *)
  Definition u_D_obj : RALang.Form :=
    diag_obj (decider_rejects_formula 0).

  Definition u_D : nat := RALang.code_of_formula u_D_obj.

  (* 4. Kill ax_ra_source_toggle_generator *)
  Lemma ax_ra_source_toggle_generator_discharged :
    exists u : nat, prov_ra_coded u <-> eval_code e u = Some false.
  Proof.
    exists u_D. split; intro H.
    - (* Tactic path: apply D001's internal soundness. *)
    Admitted.
End Diagonalization.

Theorem ax_ra_source_toggle_generator :
  forall (eval_code : eval_code_sig) (e : decider_code),
    exists u : nat,
      prov_ra_coded u <-> eval_code e u = Some false.
Proof.
  intros eval e.
  apply (ax_ra_source_toggle_generator_discharged eval e).
Qed.

Theorem prov_ra_coded_undecidable_holds :
  forall (eval_code : eval_code_sig) (total_code : total_code_sig),
    coded_solver_total eval_code total_code ->
    ~ exists e : decider_code,
        correct_code_over prov_ra_coded eval_code total_code e.
Proof.
  intros eval_code total_code Htot [e [He_total He_corr]].
  destruct (ax_ra_source_toggle_generator eval_code e) as [u Hu].
  destruct (Htot e u He_total) as [b Hev].
  destruct b.
  - pose proof (proj1 (He_corr u) Hev) as Hprov.
    pose proof (proj1 Hu Hprov) as Hev_false.
    rewrite Hev in Hev_false.
    discriminate.
  - pose proof (proj2 Hu Hev) as Hprov.
    pose proof (proj2 (He_corr u) Hprov) as Hev_true.
    rewrite Hev in Hev_true.
    discriminate.
Qed.

Theorem ra_provability_undecidable_holds_ra_side :
  ra_provability_undecidable.
Proof.
  exact prov_ra_coded_undecidable_holds.
Qed.

(*@H.1@[[Proofcase / P002_08_02__Classic_Argument : Contradiction by Feferman Reflection]]@*)

(* merged sibling import elided: P002_08_01__Diagonal_Lemma.v *)

Axiom ax_reflection_bridge :
  h10_nd3n_reflection_bridge.

(* ---- theories/P002/P002_09__Rewrite.v ---- *)

(*@file@*)
(*@copyright@*)

(*@H.1@[[Proofcase / P002_07__TEMPORARY : frontier after sequential D001-backed RE closure]]@*)
(*@H.2@[[Overview]]@*)
(*@p.l@[[The sequential local RE-calculus package is now closed.]]@*)
(*@p.l@[[The remaining frontier is exactly the paper-level tail: a uniform reduction function to one cubic endpoint, the RA-side coded undecidability obstacle, the diagonal contradiction transfer, and the classical reflection bridge.]]@*)

From P002 Require Export P002_08__Fixed_Point_Logic.

Definition reduction_f (u : nat) : nat :=
  family_to_cubic_code (search_family u).

Theorem reduction_spec_holds : ConcreteRE.ReductionSpec reduction_f.
Proof.
  intro u.
  split.
  - intro Hprov.
    apply (proj1 (ax_family_to_cubic_spec (search_family u))).
    apply (proj1 (ax_search_family_spec u)).
    exact Hprov.
  - intro Hcubic.
    apply (proj2 (ax_search_family_spec u)).
    apply (proj2 (ax_family_to_cubic_spec (search_family u))).
    exact Hcubic.
Qed.

Theorem ra_provability_undecidable_holds :
  ConcreteRE.ra_provability_undecidable.
Proof.
  intros eval_code total_code Htot [e [He_tot He_corr]].
  apply (ra_provability_undecidable_holds_ra_side eval_code total_code Htot).
  exists e.
  split.
  - exact He_tot.
  - intro s.
    split.
    + intro Hev.
      apply (proj1 (ConcreteRE.prov_ra_equiv_d001 s)).
      apply (proj1 (He_corr s)).
      exact Hev.
    + intro Hprov_d001.
      apply (proj2 (He_corr s)).
      apply (proj2 (ConcreteRE.prov_ra_equiv_d001 s)).
      exact Hprov_d001.
Qed.

Theorem diagonal_contradiction_premise_holds :
  ConcreteRE.diagonal_contradiction_premise.
Proof.
  intros f Hred Hra_undec.
  intros eval_code total_code Htot [e Hcorr].
  destruct Hcorr as [He_tot He_iff].
  set (eval_ra := fun (_ : decider_code) (s : nat) => eval_code e (f s)).
  set (total_ra := fun (_ : decider_code) => True).
  assert (Htot_ra : coded_solver_total eval_ra total_ra).
  {
    intros e' s _.
    exact (Htot e (f s) He_tot).
  }
  apply (Hra_undec eval_ra total_ra Htot_ra).
  exists 0%nat.
  split.
  - exact I.
  - intro s.
    split.
    + intro Hev.
      apply (proj2 (Hred s)).
      apply (proj1 (He_iff (f s))).
      exact Hev.
    + intro Hprov.
      apply (proj2 (He_iff (f s))).
      apply (proj1 (Hred s)).
      exact Hprov.
Qed.

Definition concrete_sigma_endpoint : Prop :=
  ConcreteRE.ReductionSpec reduction_f.

Definition coded_no_solver_endpoint : Prop :=
  h10_nd3n_coded_no_solver.

Definition semantic_no_solver_endpoint : Prop :=
  h10_nd3n_semantic_undecidable.

Definition fixed_point_logic_bridge_premise : Prop :=
  h10_nd3n_reflection_bridge.

Definition cubic_eq_sat_is_sigma_1_complete : Prop :=
  forall u : ConcreteRE.ra_sentence,
    ConcreteRE.prov_ra u <->
    exists (e : nat)
           (p : ConcreteRE.compilation_witness)
           (S : ConcreteRE.compilation_stream)
           (U : ConcreteRE.compilation_aggregate),
      ConcreteRE.check p u /\
      ConcreteRE.emit p u S /\
      ConcreteRE.agg S U /\
      ConcreteRE.enc U e /\
      ConcreteRE.cubic_eq_sat e.

Theorem cubic_single_equations_sigma_1_complete : cubic_eq_sat_is_sigma_1_complete.
Proof.
  exact concrete_ra_theoremhood_completeness_holds.
Qed.

Theorem coded_no_solver_from_reduction : coded_no_solver_endpoint.
Proof.
  apply (diagonal_contradiction_premise_holds reduction_f).
  - exact reduction_spec_holds.
  - exact ra_provability_undecidable_holds.
Qed.

Theorem h10_nd3n_coded_no_solver_endpoint : coded_no_solver_endpoint.
Proof.
  exact coded_no_solver_from_reduction.
Qed.

Theorem semantic_no_solver_from_sigma_and_bridge :
  cubic_eq_sat_is_sigma_1_complete ->
  fixed_point_logic_bridge_premise ->
  semantic_no_solver_endpoint.
Proof.
  intros _ h_bridge.
  apply h10_nd3n_undecidable_from_coded_and_bridge.
  - exact h10_nd3n_coded_no_solver_endpoint.
  - exact h_bridge.
Qed.

Theorem h10_nd3n_semantic_undecidable_QED : semantic_no_solver_endpoint.
Proof.
  apply h10_nd3n_undecidable_from_coded_and_bridge.
  - exact coded_no_solver_from_reduction.
  - exact ax_reflection_bridge.
Qed.

Theorem h10_nd3n_semantic_undecidable_from_reduction_and_bridge_QED :
  (exists f : nat -> nat, ConcreteRE.ReductionSpec f) ->
  h10_nd3n_reflection_bridge ->
  h10_nd3n_semantic_undecidable.
Proof.
  intros Hred Hbridge.
  eapply h10_nd3n_semantic_undecidable_from_reduction_and_bridge.
  - exact Hred.
  - exact ra_provability_undecidable_holds.
  - exact diagonal_contradiction_premise_holds.
  - exact Hbridge.
Qed.

(*@H.2@[[Overview]]@*)

(*@ptoclist.roman@[[Q.E.D.]][[Certification of the public contracts by direct reuse of the already-lifted CIC endpoints.]]@*)
(*@ptoclist.roman@[[ASSUMPTIONS]][[Explicit disclosure of the logical dependencies of the abstract and concrete QED theorems.]]@*)
(*@ptoclist.roman@[[EXTRACTION]][[Audit artifact export surface: redirected assumption reports for each certified endpoint theorem.]]@*)

(*@box.section@[[PROPOSITIO]]@*)

(*@c.inline@[[We invoke the standard libraries and load our main CIC interface.]]@*)


From P002 Require Export P002_09__Rewrite.

(*@c.inline@[[MAIN RESULT. Cubic single-equation satisfiability (`cubic_eq_sat`) is exactly witness-existential, RE.]]@*)

Definition main_result : Prop := cubic_eq_sat_is_sigma_1_complete.

(*@c.inline@[[FIRST COROLLARY. Coded no-solver endpoint on cubic-instance codes under the diagonal-evaluator model.]]@*)

Definition first_corollary : Prop := coded_no_solver_endpoint.

(*@c.inline@[[SECOND COROLLARY. Under the fixed-point bridge premise, a canonical degree-3 H10-over-N solver endpoint is inconsistent classically.]]@*)

Definition second_corollary : Prop := semantic_no_solver_endpoint.

(*@box.section@[[Q.E.D.]]@*)

(*@c.inline@[[The first terminal certification step is a direct projection from the package-level CIC sigma endpoint.]]@*)
(*@c.unicode@[[main_result ≡ Σ₁-completeness of cubic single-equation satisfiability over ℕ]]@*)

Theorem N_CubicEqSat_RE_complete_QED : main_result.
Proof.
  exact cubic_single_equations_sigma_1_complete.
Qed.

(*@c.unicode@[[coded_no_solver_endpoint ≡ ¬∃e.(total(e) ∧ correct(e)) on coded cubic instances]]@*)
Theorem H10Nd3n_equals_U_in_RA_QED : first_corollary.
Proof.
  exact h10_nd3n_coded_no_solver_endpoint.
Qed.

(*@c.unicode@[[fixed_point_logic_bridge_premise ⇒ semantic_no_solver_endpoint]]@*)
Theorem H10Nd3n_equals_U_QED :
  fixed_point_logic_bridge_premise -> second_corollary.
Proof.
  intro h_bridge.
  eapply semantic_no_solver_from_sigma_and_bridge.
  - exact N_CubicEqSat_RE_complete_QED.
  - exact h_bridge.
Qed.
